<1.1.1>     oṃ mahanme voco bhargo me voco yaśo me vocaḥ stomaṃ me voco bhuktiṃ me vocaḥ sarvaṃ me vocastanmāvatu tanmāviśatu tena bhukṣiṣīya
<1.1.2>     devo devametu somaḥ somametvṛtasya pathā
<1.1.3>     vihāya dauṣkṛtyam
<1.1.4>     badvā nāmasi sṛtiḥ somasaraṇī somaṃ gameyam
<1.1.5>     pitaro bhūḥ pitaro bhūḥ pitaro bhūḥ
<1.1.6>     nṛmaṇa ūrdhvabharasaṃ tvordhvabharā dṛśeyam
<1.1.7>     mṛdā śithirā devānāṃ tīrthaṃ vedirasi mā mā hiṃsīḥ
<1.1.8>     viṣṇoḥ śiro'si yaśodhā yaśo mayi dhehi
<1.1.9>     iṣa ūrja āyuṣe varcase ca

<1.2.1>     yunajmi te pṛthivīmagninā saha yunajmi vācaṃ saha sūryeṇa yukto vāto'ntarikṣeṇa te saha yuktāstisro vimṛjaḥ sūryasya
<1.2.2>     ṛtasya sadane sīdāmi
<1.2.3>     ṛtapātramasi
<1.2.4>     vānaspatyo'si bārhaspatyo'si prājāpatyo'si prajāpatermūrdhāsyatyāyupātramasīdamahaṃ māṃ prāñcaṃ prohāmi tejase brahmavarcasāya
<1.2.5>     maruto napāto'pāṅkṣayāḥ parvatānāṅkakubhaḥ śyenā ajirā endraṃ vagnunā vahata ghoṣeṇāmīvāṃ cātayadhvaṃ yuktāstha vahata
<1.2.6>     idamahamamuṃ yajamānaṃ paśuṣvadhyūhāmi paśuṣu ca māṃ brahmavarcase ca
<1.2.7>     vasavastvā gāyatreṇa chandasā saṃ mṛjantu rudrāstvā traiṣṭubhena chandasā saṃ mṛjantvādityāstvā jāgatena chandasā saṃ mṛjantu
<1.2.8>     pavitraṃ te vitataṃ brahmaṇaspate prabhurgātrāṇi paryeṣi viśvato'taptatanūrna tadāmo aśnute śṛtāsa idvahantaḥ saṃ tadāśataḥ
<1.2.9>     praśukraitu devīmanīṣāsmadrathaḥ sutaṣṭo na vājyāyuṣe me pacasva varcase me pavasva viduḥ pṛthivyā divo janitrācchṛṇvantvāpo'dhaḥ kṣarantīḥ somehodgāya māmāyuṣe mama brahmavarcasāya yajamānasyarddhyā amuṣya rājyāya

<1.3.1>     vekurānāmāsi juṣṭā devebhyo namo vāce namo vācaspataye devi vāgyat te vāco madhumat tasminmā dhāḥ sarasvatyai svāhā
<1.3.2>     sūryo mā divyābhyo nāṣṭrābhyaḥ pātu vāyurantarikṣābhyo'gniḥ pārthivābhyaḥ svāhā
<1.3.3>     yo'dya saumyo vadho'ghāyūnāmudīrate viṣūkuhasya dhanvanāpa tān varuṇo'pa dhamatu
<1.3.4>     yo ma ātmā yā me prajā ye me paśavastairahaṃ mano vācaṃ pra sīdāmi
<1.3.5>     agnestejasendrasyendriyeṇa sūryasya varcasā bṛhaspatistvā yunaktu devebhyaḥ prāṇāyāgniryunaktu tapasā somaṃ yajñāya voḍhave dadhātvindra indriyaṃ satyāḥ kāmā yajamānasya santu
<1.3.6>     annaṃ kariṣyāmyannaṃ praviṣyāmyannaṃ janayiṣyāmi
<1.3.7>     annamakaramannamabhūdannamajījanam
<1.3.8>     śyeno'si gāyatracchandā anu tvā rabhe svasti mā saṃ pārayā mā stotrasya stotraṃ gamyādindravanto vanemahi bhakṣīmahi prajāmiṣam
<1.3.9>     saṃ varcasā payasā saṃ tapobhiraganmahi manasā saṃ śivena saṃ vijñānena manasaśca satyairyathā vo'haṃ cārutamaṃ vadānīndro vo dṛśe bhūyāsaṃ sūryaścakṣuṣe vātaḥ prāṇāya somo gandhāya brahma kṣatrāya
<1.3.10>     namo gandharvāya viṣvagvādine varcodhā asi varco mayi dhehi

<1.4.1>     adhvanāmadhvapate svasti me'dyāsmin devayāne pathi bhūyāt
<1.4.2>     samrāḍasi kṛśānuḥ
<1.4.3>     tutho'si janadhāyo nabho'si pratakvāsaṃmṛṣṭo'si havyasūdanaḥ
<1.4.4>     viburasi pravāhaṇaḥ
<1.4.5>     vahnirasi havyavāhanaḥ
<1.4.6>     śvātro'si pracetāḥ
<1.4.7>     tutho'si viśvavedā uśigasi kaviraṅghārirasi bambhāriravasyurasi duvasvān
<1.4.8>     śundhyurasi mārjālīyaḥ
<1.4.9>     ṛtadhāmāsi svarjyotiḥ
<1.4.10>     samudro'si viśvavyacāḥ
<1.4.11>     ahirasi budhnyaḥ
<1.4.12>     ajo'syekapāt
<1.4.13>     sagarā asi budhnyaḥ
<1.4.14>     kavyo'si kavyavāhanaḥ
<1.4.15>     pāta māgnayo raudreṇānīkena piṣṭata mā namo vo'stu mā mā hiṃsiṣṭa

<1.5.1>     ṛtasya dvārau stho mā mā saṃ tāptam
<1.5.2>     namaḥ sakhibhyaḥ pūrvasadbhyo namo parasadbhyaḥ
<1.5.3>     śyeno nṛcakṣā agneṣṭvā cakṣuṣāva paśyāmi
<1.5.4>     indavindrapītasya ta indriyāvato gāyatracchandasaḥ sarvagaṇasya sarvagaṇa upahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi
<1.5.5>     ūrdhvaḥ sapta ṛṣīnupa tiṣṭhasvendrapīto vācaspate saptartvijo'bhyucchrayasva juṣasva lokaṃ mārvāgava gāḥ
<1.5.6>     soma rārandhi no hṛdi pitā no'si mama tanmā mā hiṃsīḥ
<1.5.7>     soma gīrbhiṣṭvā vayaṃ vardhayāmo vācovidaḥ sumṛḍīko na ā viśa
<1.5.8>     ā pyāyasva sametu te viśvataḥ soma vṛṣṇyam | bhavā vājasya saṃgathe
<1.5.9>     avamaista ūrdhvaiste kāvyaiste pitṛbhirbhakṣitasya madhumato nārāśaṃsasya sarvagaṇasya sarvagaṇa upahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi
<1.5.10>     dīkṣāyai varṇena tapaso rūpeṇa manaso mahimnā vāco vibhūtyā prajāpatistvā yunaktu prajābhyo'pānāya
<1.5.11>     vāyuryunaktu manasā stomaṃ yajñāya voḍhave dadhātvindra indriyaṃ satyāḥ kāmā yajamānasya santu
<1.5.12>     vṛṣako'si triṣṭupchandā anu tvā rabhe svasti mā saṃ pārayā mā stotrasya stotraṃ gamyādindravanto vanemahi bhakṣīmahi prajāmiṣam
<1.5.13>     indavindrapītasya ta indriyāvato triṣṭupchandasaḥ sarvagaṇasya sarvagaṇa upahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi
<1.5.14>     sūryo yunaktu vācā stomaṃ yajñāya voḍhave dadhātvindra indriyaṃ satyāḥ kāmā yajamānasya santu
<1.5.15>     svaro'si gayo'si jagacchandā anu tvā rabhe svasti mā saṃ pārayā mā stotrasya stotraṃ gamyādindravanto vanemahi bhakṣīmahi prajāmiṣam
<1.5.16>     indavindrapītasya ta indriyāvato triṣṭupchandasaḥ sarvagaṇasya sarvagaṇa upahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi
<1.5.17>     āyurme prāṇo mano'si me prāṇa āyupatnyāmṛci yanme mano yamaṃ gataṃ yadvā me aparāgataṃ rājñā somena tadvayaṃ punarasmāsu dadhnasi
<1.5.18>     yanme yamaṃ vaivasvataṃ mano jagāma dūragāstanma āvartayā punarjīvātave na martave'tho ariṣṭatātaye
<1.5.19>     yenāhyājimajayadvicakṣya yena śyenaṃ śakunaṃ suparṇaṃ yadāhuścakṣuraditāvanantaṃ somo nṛcakṣā mayi taddadhātu

<1.6.1>     aindraṃ saho'sarji tasya ta indavindrapītasyendriyāvato'nuṣṭupchandaso harivataḥ sarvagaṇasya sarvagaṇa upahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi
<1.6.2>     indavindrapītasya ta indriyāvato'nuṣṭupchandasaḥ sarvagaṇasya sarvagaṇa upahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi
<1.6.3>     stutasya stutamasyūrjasvat payasvadā mā stotrasya strotraṃ. gamyādindravanto vanemahi bhakṣīmahi prajāmiṣam
<1.6.4>     iṣṭayajuṣaste deva soma stutastomasya śastokthasya tirohnyasya yo'śvasanirgosanirbhakṣastasyopahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi
<1.6.5>     ṛtasya tvā deva stomapade viṣṇordhāmāni vi muñcāmyetat tvaṃ deva stomānavakaramagannaśīmahi vayaṃ pratiṣṭhām
<1.6.6>     somehānu mehi soma saha sadasa indriyeṇa
<1.6.7>     subhūrasi śreṣṭho raśmirdevānāṃ saṃsaddevānāṃ yāturyayā tanvā brahma jinvasi tayā mā jinva tayā mā janaya prakāśaṃ mā kuru
<1.6.8>     apāṃ puṣpamasyoṣadhīnāṃ rasa indrasya priyatamaṃ haviḥ svāhā
<1.6.9>     hāriyojanasya te deva someṣṭayajuṣaḥ stutastomasya śastokthasya yo'śvasanirgosanirbhakṣastasyopahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi
<1.6.10>     devakṛtasyainaso'vayajanamasi pitṛkṛtasyainaso'vayajanamasi manuṣyakṛtasyainaso'vayajanamasyasmatkṛtasyainaso'vayajanamasi yaddivā ca naktaṃ cainaścakṛma tasyāvayajanamasi yat svapantaśca jāgrataścainaścakṛma tasyāvayajanamasi yadvidvāṃsaścāvidvāṃsaścainaścakṛma tasyāvayajanamasyenasaenasāvayajanamasi
<1.6.11>     apsu ghautasya te deva soma nṛbhiḥ sutasya
<1.6.12>     madhumantaṃ bhakṣaṃ karomi
<1.6.13>     samadbhya oṣadhībhyaḥ
<1.6.14>     kāmakāmaṃ ma ā vartaya
<1.6.15>     ūrgasyūrjaṃ mayi dhehi
<1.6.16>     prāṇa somapīthe me jāgṛhi
<1.6.17>     dadhikrāvṇo akāriṣaṃ jiṣṇoraśvasya vājinaḥ | surabhi no mukhā karat pra na āyūṃṣi tāriṣat

<1.7.1>     aśvo'syatyo'si mayo'si hayo'si vājyasi saptirarvāgsi vṛṣāsi
<1.7.2>     ādityānāṃ patmānvihi namaste'stu mā mā hiṃsīḥ
<1.7.3>     vāyoṣṭvā tejasā prati gṛhṇāmi nakṣatrāṇāṃ tvāṃ rūpeṇa prati gṛhṇāmi sūryasya tvā varcasā prati gṛhṇāmi
<1.7.4>     rathantaramasi vāmadevyamasi bṛhadasi
<1.7.5>     aṅkānyaṅkū abhito rathaṃ yau dhvāntaṃ vātāgramabhisaṃcaratau dūre hetirindriyavān patatrī te no'gnayaḥ paprayaḥ pārayantu
<1.7.6>     vaiśvānaraḥ pratnathā nākamā ruha divaḥ pṛṣṭhe mandamānaḥ sumanmabhiḥ sapūrvavajjantave dhanāḥ samānamayman paryeti jāgṛviḥ
<1.7.7>     gidaiṣa te ratha eṣa vāmaśvinā ratho'riṣṭo  viśvabheṣajaḥ
<1.7.8>     kṛśāno savyānā yaccha
<1.7.9>     dāsāno dakṣiṇānava gṛhāṇa

<1.8.1>     devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāṃ prati gṛhṇāmi
<1.8.2>     varuṇastvā nayatu devi dakṣiṇe varuṇāyāśvaṃ tenāmṛtatvamaśīya vayo dātre bhūyānmayo mahyaṃ pratigrahītre
<1.8.3>     varuṇastvā nayatu devi dakṣiṇe rudrāya gāṃ tayāmṛtatvamaśīya vayo dātre bhūyānmayo mahyaṃ pratigrahītre
<1.8.4>     varuṇastvā nayatu devi dakṣiṇe'gnaye'jam
<1.8.5>     agnaye hiraṇyam
<1.8.6>     agnīṣomābhyāmajānmayāmṛtatvamaśīya vayo dātre bhūyānmayo mahyaṃ pratigrahītre
<1.8.7>     annasyānnapatiḥ prādādanamīvasya śuṣmiṇo namo viśvajanasya kṣāmāya bhuñjati mā mā hiṃsīḥ
<1.8.8>     varuṇastvā nayatu devi dakṣiṇe tvaṣṭre'viṃ tayāmṛtatvamaśīya vayo dātre bhūyānmayo mahyaṃ pratigrahītre
<1.8.9>     gnāstvā'kṛntannapaso'tanvata vayitryo'vayan
<1.8.10>     varuṇastvā nayatu devi dakṣiṇe bṛhaspataye vāsastenāmṛtatvamaśīya vayo dātre bhūyānmayo mahyaṃ pratigṛhītre
<1.8.11>     varuṇastvā nayatu devi dakṣiṇe uttānāyāṅgirasāyāprāṇat tenāmṛtatvamaśīya vayo dātre bhūyānmayo mahyaṃ pratigrahītre
<1.8.12>     varuṇastvā nayatu devi dakṣiṇe pūṣṇa uṣṭram
<1.8.13>     vāyave mṛgaṃ tenāmṛtatvamaśīya vayo dātre bhūyānmayo mahyaṃ pratigrahītre
<1.8.14>     jāpataye hastinaṃ prajāpataye varāhaṃ prajāpataye vrīhiyavāṃstairamṛtatvamaśīya vayo dātre bhūyānmayo mahyaṃ pratigrahītre
<1.8.15>     varuṇastvā nayatu devi dakṣiṇe kṣetrapataye tilamāṣāstairamṛtatvamaśīya vayo dātre bhūyānmayo mahyaṃ pratigrahītre
<1.8.16>     varuṇastvā nayatu devi dakṣiṇe savitre'śvataraṃ vāśvatarīṃ vā tayāmṛtatvamaśīya vayo dātre bhūyānmayo mahyaṃ pratigrahītre
<1.8.17>     ka idaṃ kasmā adāt kāmaḥ kāmāyādāt kāmo dātā kāmaḥ pratigrahītā kāmaḥ samudramāviśat kāmena tvā prati gṛhṇāmi kāmaitat te

<1.9.1>     raśmirasi kṣayāya tvā kṣayaṃ jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.2>     pretirasi dharmaṇe tvā dharmaṃ jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.3>     anvitirasi dive tvā divaṃ jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.4>     saṃdhirasyantarikṣāya tvāntarikṣaṃ jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.5>     pratidhirasi pṛthivyai tvā pṛthivīṃ jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.6>     viṣṭambho'si vṛṣṭyai tvā vṛṣṭiṃ jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.7>     prāco'syahne tvāharjinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.8>     anvāsi rātryai rātriṃ jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.9>     uśigasi vasubhyastvā vasūn jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.10>     praketo'si rudrebhyastvā rudrān jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.11>     sudītirasyādityebhyastvādityān jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.9.12>     ojo'si pitṛbhyastvā pitṝn jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta

<1.10.1>     tanturasi prajābhyastvā prajā jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.2>     rodasyasyoṣadhībhyastvauṣadhīrjinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.3>     pṛtanāṣāḍasi paśubhyastvā paśūn jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.4>     abhijidasi yuktagrāvendrāya tvendraṃ jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.5>     adhipatirasi prāṇāya tvā prāṇān jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.6>     dharuṇo'syapānāya tvāpānān  jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.7>     saṃsarpo'si cakṣuṣe tvā cakṣurjinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.8>     vayodhā asi kṣetrāya tvā kṣetraṃ jinva savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.9>     trivṛdasi trivṛte tvā savṛdasi savṛte tvā pravṛdasi pravṛte tvānuvṛdasyanuvṛte tvā savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.10>     niroho'si nirohāya tvā saṃroho'si saṃrohāya tvā praroho'si prarohāya tvānuroho'syanurohāya tvā savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.11>     vasuko'si vasyaṣṭirasi veṣaśrīrasi savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta
<1.10.12>     ākramo'syākramāya tvā saṃkramo'si saṃkramāya tvotkramo'syutkramāya tvotkrāntirasyutkrāntyai tvā savitṛprasūtā bṛhaspataye stuta

<2.1.1>     tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa prathamayā tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa madhyamayā tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa uttamayodyatī trivṛto viṣṭutiḥ
<2.1.2>     jyeṣṭho jyaiṣṭhineya stuvīta
<2.1.3>     agrādagraṃ rohatyabhikrāmantī viṣṭutirabhikrāmantyā evābhikrāntena hi yajñasyardhnoti tasmādetayā stotavyamṛddhyā eva
<2.1.4>     pāpavasīyaso vidhṛtirvipāpmanā vartate ya etayā stute nāvagato'pa rudhyate nāparuddho'va gacchati | na śreyāṃsaṃ pāpīyānabhyā rohati na janatā janatāmabhyeti nānyo'nyasya prajā ā dadate yathākṣetraṃ kalpante | avarṣukastu parjanyo bhavatīme hi lokāstṛcastān hiṅkāreṇa vyeti
<2.1.5>     eṣā vai pratiṣṭhitā trivṛto viṣṭutiḥ prati tiṣṭhati ya etayā stute

<2.2.1>     tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa parācībhistisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa parācībhistisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa parācībhiḥ parivartinī triviṣṭutiḥ
<2.2.2>     praparīvartamāpnoti ya etayā stute saṃtatā viṣṭutiḥ prāṇo'pāno vyānasta ṛcastān hiṅkāreṇa saṃ tanoti | sarvamāyureti na purāyuṣaḥ pra mīyate ya etayā stute varṣukaḥ parjanyo bhavatīme hi lokāstṛcastān hiṅkāreṇa saṃ dadhāti
<2.2.3>     ślakṣṇeva tu vā īśvarā paśūnnirmṛjaḥ saiṣā ca parācota śreyān bhavatyuta yādṛṅeva tādṛṅnet tu pāpīyān
<2.2.4>     tāmetāṃ bhāllavaya upāsate tasmāt te pratigṛṇantaḥ parīvartānna cyavante

<2.3.1>     tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa parācībhistisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti yā madhyamā sā prathamā yottamā sā madhyamā yā prathamā sottamā tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti yottamā sā prathamā yā prathamā sā madhyamā yā madhyamā sottamā kulāyinī trivṛto viṣṭutiḥ
<2.3.2>     prajākāmo vā paśukāmo vā stuvīta vai kulānāyapaśavaḥ kulāyaṃkulāyameva bhavati
<2.3.3>     etāmevānujāvarāya kuryādetāsāmevāgraṃ pariyatīnāṃ prajānā[m] agraṃ paryeti
<2.3.4>     etāmeva bahubhyo yajamānebhyaḥ kuryāt yat sarvā agriyā bhavanti sarvā madhye sarvā uttamāḥ sarvānevainān samāvadbhājaḥ karoti nānyo'nyamapa ghnate sarve samāvadindriyā bhavanti
<2.3.5>     varṣukaḥ parjanyo bhavatīme hi lokāstṛcastān hiṅkāreṇa vyati ṣajati
<2.3.6>     pāpavasīyasaṃ tu bhavati
<2.3.7>     adharottaramapāvagato rudhyateva gacchatyaparuddhaḥ pāpīyān śreyāṃsamabhyā rohati janatā janatāmabhyetyanyo'nyasya prajā ā dadate na yathākṣetraṃ kalpante

<2.4.1>     pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā sa ekayā pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ pañcapañcinī pañcapañcadaśasya viṣṭutiḥ
<2.4.2>     pāṅktaḥ puruṣaḥ pāṅktāt paśavastayā puruṣaṃ ca paśūṃścāpnoti vajro vai pancapaśo yat pañcapañca vyūhati vajrameva tadvyūhati śāntyā eṣā vai pratiṣṭhitā pañcadaśasya viṣṭutiḥ prati tiṣṭhati ya etayā stute

<2.5.1>     pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa parācībhiḥ saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhis
<2.5.2>     trīn stomān prativihitā brahmavarcasakāmaḥ stuvīta
<2.5.3>     pañcabhiḥ pañcadaśaṃ tisṛbhistrivṛtaṃ saptabhiḥ saptadaśam
<2.5.4>     vīryaṃ vai stomā vīryameva tadekadhā samūhate brahmavarcasyāvaruddhyai tejasvī brahmavarcasī bhavati ya etayā stute

<2.6.1>     tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa parācībhiḥ pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhirudyatī pañcadaśasya viṣṭutiḥ
<2.6.2>     etayā vai devāḥ svargaṃllokamāyan svargakāmaḥ stuvīta svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<2.6.3>     abhikrāmantī viṣṭutirabhikrāmantyā evābhikrāntena hi yajñasyardhnoti tasmādetayā stotavyamṛddhyā eva

<2.7.1>     pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā sa ekayā pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhirdaśasaptā saptadaśasya viṣṭutiḥ
<2.7.2>     etayā vai devā asurānatyakrāmannati pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ krāmati ya etayā stute
<2.7.3>     abhikrāmantī viṣṭutirabhikrāmantyā evābhikrāntena hi yajñasyardhnoti tasmādetayā stotavyamṛddhyā eva
<2.7.4>     garbhiṇī viṣṭutiḥ pa prajayā pra paśubhirjāyate ya etayā stute
<2.7.5>     viḍvai saptadaśastasyā rājā garbho viśa eva tadrājānaṃ garbhaṃ karoti
<2.7.6>     nāvagato'pa rudhyate nāparuddho'va gacchati
<2.7.7>     annaṃ vai saptadaśo yat sapta madhye bhavanti pañcapañcābhito'nnameva tanmadhyato dhīyate'naśanāyuko yajamāno bhavatyanaśanāyukāḥ prajāḥ
<2.7.8>     vairājo vai puruṣaḥ sapta grāmyāḥ paśavo yaddaśapūrvā bhavanti saptottamā yajamānameva tat paśuṣu prati ṣṭhāpayati
<2.7.9>     eṣā vai pratiṣṭhitā saptadaśasya viṣṭutiḥ prati tiṣṭhati ya etayā stute

<2.8.1>     eṣa eva vyūhaḥ saptaikamadhyā
<2.8.2>     brahmaṇo vā āyatanaṃ prathamā kṣatrasya madhyamā viśa uttamā yat prathamā bhūyiṣṭhā bhājayati brahmaṇyeva tadojo vīryaṃ dadhāti brahmaṇa eva tat kṣatraṃ ca viśaṃ cānuge karoti kṣatrasyāsya prakāśo bhavati ya etayā stute
<2.8.3>     tāmetāṃ trikharvā upāsate tasmāt te spardhamānā na vlīyante
<2.9.1>     saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti parācībhiḥ saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ saptāsthitā
<2.9.2>     bhrātṛvyaṃ stuvīta yathā saptāsthitena matyena samī karotyevaṃ pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ pra rujati
<2.9.3>     etāmeva bahubhyo yajamānebhyaḥ kuryādyaḥ prathamo hiṅkāraḥ sa prathamāyā yattāṃ saptabhyo hiṅkaroti tena sā sapta bhajate yat saptaiva madhye sapadyante tena sā sapta bhajate ya uttamo hiṅkāraḥ sa uttamayā yattāṃ saptabhyo hiṅkaroti tena sā sapta bhajate sarvānevainān samāvadbhājaḥ karoti nānyo'nyamapa ghnate samāvadindriyā bhavanti
<2.9.4>     tāmetāmabhipratāriṇa upāsate tasmāt ta ojiṣṭhāsvānām

<2.10.1>     eṣa eva vyūha ubhayaḥ saptaikamadhyā nirmadhyā
<2.10.2>     ānujāvara stuvītāloko vā eṣa yadānujāvaro yat saptaprathamāḥ saptottamāstisro madhye tryakṣaraḥ puruṣo lokamevāsmai tanmadhyataḥ karoti tasmiṃlloke prati tiṣṭhati
<2.10.3>     etāmeva prajākāmāya kuryānmadhyato vā eṣa saṃrūḍho yaḥ prajāṃ na vindate lokamevāsmai taṃ madhyataḥ karoti taṃ lokaṃ prajayā ca paśubhiścānu pra jāyate
<2.10.4>     etāmevāparuddharājanyāya kuryādviḍvai saptadaśastasyā rājā garbho viśa eva tadrājānaṃ nirhantyapāvagato'pa rudhyate'va gacchatyaparuddhaḥ
<2.10.5>     etāmevābhicaryamāṇāya kuryāt prajāpatirvai saptadaśaḥ prajāpatimeva madhyattaḥ pra viśantyastṛtyai

<2.11.1>     pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā sa ekayā tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa parācībhirnavabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ
<2.11.2>     caturastomān prativihitā brahmavarcasakāmāḥ stuvīta pañcabhiḥ pañcadaśaṃ tisṛbhistrivṛtaṃ navabhistriṇavaṃ svayaṃ saptadaśaḥ sampanno vīryaṃ vai stomā vīryameva tadekadhā samūhate brahmavarcasyāvaruddhyai tejasvī brahmavarcasī bhavati ya etayā stute

<2.12.1>     tisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa parācībhiḥ pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā navabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhirudyatīsaptadaśasya viṣṭutiḥ
<2.12.2>     etayā vai devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāyan svargakāmaḥ stuvīta svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvāno'bhikrāmantī viṣṭutirabhikrāntyā evābhikrāntena hi yajñasyardhnoti tasmādetayā stotavyamṛddhyā eva

<2.13.1>     saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa ekayā sa tisṛbhirbhastrāvācīnabilā
<2.13.2>     yaṃ dviṣyāt tasya kuryādyathāvācīnabilayā bhastrayā pradhūnuyādevaṃ yajamānasya paśūn pradhūnotyapakrāmantī viṣṭutistayā yajamānasya paśavo'pakrāmanti pāpīyān bhavati ya etayā stute

<2.14.1>     saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ saptasaptinyekaviṃśasya viṣsṭutiḥ
<2.14.2>     sapta grāmyāḥ paśavastānetayā spṛṇoti sapta śirasi prāṇāḥ prāṇā indriyāṇīndriyāṇyevaitayāpnoti
<2.14.3>     eṣā vai pratiṣthitaikaviṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ pratitiṣṭhati ya etayā stute

<2.15.1>     pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā sa ekayā saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhirnavabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhirudyatyekaviṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ
<2.15.2>     etayā vai devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāyan svargakāmaḥ stuvīta svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvāno'bhikrāmantī viṣṭutirabhikrāntyā evābhikrāntena hi yajñasyardhnoti tasmādetayā stotavyamṛddhyā eva
<2.15.3>     saiṣā trivṛt prāyaṇā trivṛdudayanā yat trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ bhavati navaitā ekaviṃśasyottamā bhavanti prāṇā vai trivṛt prāṇāneva tadubhayato dadhāti tasmādayamardhabhāgavāk(?) prāṇa uttareṣāṃ prāṇānāṃ sarvamāyureti na purāyuṣaḥ pramīyate ya etayā stute
<2.15.4>     tāmetāṃ kradviṣa upāsate tasmāt te sarvamāyuryanti

<2.16.1>     navabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ pañcabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ pratiṣṭutiḥ
<2.16.2>     navabhistrivṛtaṃ pratiṣṭauti pañcabhiḥ pañcabhiḥ pañcadaśaṃ saptabhiḥ saptadaśaṃ svayamekaviṃśaḥ saṃpannaḥ
<2.16.3>     sa eva stomā yajñaṃ vahanti tānuttame stotre saṃtarpayati yathānaḍuho vāśvān vāśvatarān vohuṣaḥ saṃtarpayedevametaduttame stotre stomān saṃtarpayati tṛpyati prajayā paśubhirya etayā stute
<2.16.4>     etāmeva purodhākāmāya kuryādbrahma vai trivṛt kṣatramekaviṃśo yat trivṛtaikaviṃśaṃ pratipadyate brahma tat kṣatrasya purastānnidadhāti gacchati purodhāṃ na purodhāyāścyavate ya etayā stute
<2.16.5>     tāmetāṃ prāvāhaṇya upāsate tasmāt te purodhāyā na cyavanta

<2.17.1>     navabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhistisṛbhyo hiṅkaroti sa parācībhirnavabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sūrmyubhayata ādīptā
<2.17.2>     brahmavarcasakāmaḥ stuvīta tejo vai trivṛt tryakṣaraḥ puruṣo yat trivṛtāvabhito bhavatastisro madhye yathā hi hiraṇyaṃ niṣṭapedevamenaṃ trivṛtau niṣṭapatastejase brahmavarcasāya
<2.17.3>     apaśavyeva tu vā īśvarā paśūnnirdahaḥ kilāsattvānnūbhayamati hi niṣṭapataḥ
<2.17.4>     etāmevābhiśasyamānāya kuryācchamalaṃ vā etamṛcchati yamaślīlā vāgṛcchati yaivainamasāvaślīlaṃ vāgvadati tāmasya trivṛtau niṣṭapatastejasvī bhavati ya etayā stute

<3.1.1>     navabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa pañcabhiḥ sa ekayā navabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa pañcabhirnavabhyo hiṅkaroti sa pañcabhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ
<3.1.2>     vajro vai triṇavo vajrameva tadvyūhati śāntyai
<3.1.3>     pañcabhirvihitaikā paricarā pāṅktāḥ paśavo yajamānaḥ paricarā yat pañcabhirvidadhātyekā paricarā bhavati yajamānameva tat paśuṣu pratiṣṭhāpayatyeṣā vai pratiṣṭhitā triṇavasya viṣṭutiḥ pratitiṣṭhati ya etayā stute

<3.2.1>     saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā navabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa pañcabhirekādaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa pañcabhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhirudyatī triṇavasya viṣṭutiḥ
<3.2.2>     etayā vai devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāyan svargakāmaḥ stuvīta svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvāno'bhikrāmantī viṣṭutirabhikrāntyā evābhikrāntena hi yajñasyardhnoti tasmādetayā stotavyamṛddhyā eva

<3.3.1>     ekādaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa saptabhiḥ sa ekayaikādaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa saptabhirekādaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa saptabhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ
<3.3.2>     anto vai trayastriṃśaḥ paramo vai trayastriṃśa stomānāṃ saptabhirvihitaikā paricarā sapta grāmyāḥ paśavo yajamānaḥ paricarā yat saptabhirvidadhātyekā paricarā bhavati yajamānameva tadantataḥ paśuṣu pratiṣṭhāpayatyeṣā vai pratiṣṭhitā trayastriṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ pratitiṣṭhati ya etayā stute

<3.4.1>     ekādaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa pañcabhiḥ sa tisṛbhirekādaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa pañcabhirekādaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa pañcabhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhirnedīyaḥsaṃkramā
<3.4.2>     anto vai trayastriṃśo yathā mahāvṛkṣasyāgraṃ sṛptvā nedīyaḥsaṃkramāt saṃkrāmatyevametannedīyaḥsaṃkramayā nedīyaḥsaṃkramāt saṃkrāmati
<3.4.3>     pañcabhirvihitāstisraḥ paricarāḥ pāṅktāḥ paśava etāvān puruṣo yadātmā prajā jāyā yat pañcabhirvidadhāti tisraḥ paricarā bhavanti yajamānameva tat paśuṣu pratiṣṭhāpayati paśumān bhavati ya etayā stute

<3.5.1>     navabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa pañcabhiḥ sa ekayaikādaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa saptabhistrayodaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa saptabhiḥ sa tisṛbhirudyatī trayastriṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ
<3.5.2>     etayā vai devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāyan svargakāmaḥ stuvīta svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvāno'grādagraṃ rohatyabhikrāmantī viṣṭutirabhikrāntyā evābhikrāntena hi yajñasyardhnoti tasmādetayā stotavyamṛddhyā eva

<3.6.1>     trayodaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa pañcabhiḥ sa pañcabhirekādaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa pañcabhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhirnavabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ pratyavarohiṇītrayastriṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ
<3.6.2>     yathā mahāvṛkṣasyāgraṃ sṛptvā śākhāyāḥ śākhāmālambhamupāvarohedevametayemaṃ lokamupāvarohati pratiṣṭhityai
<3.6.3>     trivṛtā praiti trivṛtodeti prāṇā vai trivṛt prāṇenaiva praiti prāṇamabhyudeti sarvamāyureti na purāyuṣaḥ pramīyate ya etayā stute
<3.6.4>     tāmetāṃ karadviṣa upāsate tasmāt te sarvamāyuryanti

<3.7.1>     pañcadaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa pañcabhirekādaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa pañcabhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ saptabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ
<3.7.2>     yo vai trayastriṃśamekaviṃśe pratiṣṭhitaṃ veda pratitiṣṭhati pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśaḥ  stomānāṃ yadetāḥ sapta trayastriṃśasyottamā bhavanti saptavidhaikaviṃśasya viṣṭutirekaviṃśa eva tat trayastriṃśaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati pratitiṣṭhati ya etayā stute

<3.8.1>     aṣṭābhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa catasṛbhiḥ sa ekayāṣṭābhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa catasṛbhiraṣṭābhyo hiṅkaroti sa catasṛbhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ
<3.8.2>     paśavo vai chandogā yadaṣṭābhyo'ṣṭābhyo hiṅkaroti aṣṭāśaphāḥ paśavaḥ śaphaśastat paśūnāpnoti
<3.8.3>     catasṛbhirvihitaikā paricarā catuṣpādāḥ paśavo yajamānaḥ paricarā yaccatasṛbhirvidadhātyekā paricarā bhavati yajamānameva tat paśuṣu pratiṣṭhāpayatyeṣā vai pratiṣṭhitā caturviṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ pratitiṣṭhati ya etayā stute

<3.9.1>     pañcadaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekādaśabhiḥ sa ekayā caturdaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa daśabhiḥ pañcadaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekādaśabhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ
<3.9.2>     brahmaṇo vā āyatanaṃ prathamā kṣatrasya madhyamā viśa uttamā yat pañcadaśinyau pūrve bhavataścaturdaśottamā brahmaṇi caiva tat kṣatre caujo vīryaṃ dadhāti brahmaṇe caiva tat kṣatrāya ca viśamanugāṃ karoti kṣatrasyevāsya prakāśo bhavati ya etayā stute
<3.9.3>     astomā vā ete yacchandogā ayujo hi stomā yugmanti chandāṃsi yadeṣā yujinī catuścatvāriṃśasya viṣṭutistenāstomāḥ
<3.9.4>     eṣā vai pratiṣṭhitā catuścatvāriṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ pratitiṣṭhait ya etayā stute

<3.10.1>     caturdaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa daśabhiḥ sa ekayā pañcadaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekādaśabhiḥ pañcadaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekādaśabhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhirnirmadhyā
<3.10.2>     astīva vā ayaṃ loko'stīvāsau chidramivedamantarikṣaṃ yadeṣā nirmadhyā bhavatīmāneva lokānanu prajāyate pra prajayā pra paśubhirjāyate ya etayā stute
<3.11.1>     pañcadaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekādaśabhiḥ sa ekayā pañcadaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekādaśabhiḥ caturdaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa daśabhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ
<3.11.2>     ājyānāṃ prathamā pṛṣṭhānāṃ dvitīyokthānāṃ tṛtīyā
<3.11.3>     yājyānāṃ sā hoturyā pṛṣṭhānāṃ sā maitrāvaruṇasya yokthānāṃ sā brāhmaṇācchaṃsino yaiva hotuḥ sācchāvākasya yā pṛṣṭhānāṃ sahoturyokthānāṃ sā maitrāvaruṇasya yājyānāṃ sā brāhmaṇācchaṃsino yaiva hotuḥ sācchāvākasya yokthānāṃ sā hoturyājyānāṃ sā maitrāvaruṇasya yā pṛṣṭhānāṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsino yaiva hotuḥ sācchāvākasya sarvā ājyeṣu sarvāḥ pṛṣtheṣu sarvā uktheṣu
<3.11.4>     paśavo vai samīṣantī yadeṣā sarvāṇi savanānyanusaṃcaratyanusavanamevainaṃ paśubhiḥ samardhayati paśuman bhavati ya etayā stute

<3.12.1>     ṣoḍaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa dvadaśabhiḥ sa ekayā ṣoḍaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa dvādaśabhiḥ ṣoḍaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa dvādaśabhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ
<3.12.2>     anto vā aṣṭācatvāriṃśaḥ paśavaśchandogā yat ṣoḍaśabhyaḥṣoḍaśabhyo hiṅkaroti ṣoḍaśakalāḥ paśavaḥ kalāśastat paśūnāpnoti
<3.12.3>     dvādaśabhirvihitaikā paricarā dvādaśamāsāssaṃvvatsaro yajamānaḥ paricarā yaddvādaśabhirvidadhātyekā paricarā bhavati yajamānameva tadantatassaṃvvatsare paśuṣu pratiṣṭhāpayatyeṣā vai pratiṣṭhitāṣṭācatvāriṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ pratitiṣṭhati ya etayā stute

<3.13.1>     ṣoḍaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa daśabhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ ṣoḍaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa daśabhiḥ ṣoḍaśabhyo hiṅkaroti sa daśabhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhirnedīyaḥsaṃkramā
<3.13.2>     anto vā aṣṭācatvāriṃśo yathā mahāvṛkṣasyāgraṃ sṛptvā nedīyaḥsaṃkramāt saṃkrāmatyevametannedīyaḥsaṃkramayā nedīyaḥsaṃkramāt saṃkrāmati
<3.13.3>     daśabhirvihitā tisraḥ paricarā daśākṣarā virāḍetāvān puruṣo yadātmā prajā jāyā yaddaśabhirvidadhātt tisraḥ paricarā bhavanti yajamānameva tadvirājyannādye'ntataḥ pratiṣṭhāpayatyannādo bhavati ya etayā stute

<4.1.1>     gāvo vā etat sattramāsata tāsāṃ daśasu māssu śṛṅgaṇyajāyanta tā abruvannarāḥ smottiṣṭhāmopaśā no'jñateti tā udatiṣṭhan
<4.1.2>     tāsāṃ tvevābruvannāsāmahā evemau dvādaśau māsau saṃsaṃvvatsaramāpayāmeti tāsāṃ dvādaśasu māḥsu śṛṅgāṇi prāvartanta tāḥ sarvamannādyamāpnuvaṃstā etāstūparāstasmāt tāḥ sarvān dvādaśamāsaḥ prerate sarvaṃ hi tā annādyamāpnuvan
<4.1.3>     sarvamannādyamāpnoti ya evaṃvveda
<4.1.4>     prajāpatirvā idameka āsīt so'kāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti sa etamatirātramapaśyat tamāharat tenāhorātre prājanayat
<4.1.5>     yadeṣo'tirātro bhavatyahorātre eva prajanayantyahorātrayoḥ pratitiṣṭhantyetāvān vāva saṃvvatsaro yadahasca rātriścāhorātrābhyāmeva tat saṃvvatsaramāpnuvanti
<4.1.6>     yajjyotiṣṭomo bhavati yajñamukhaṃ tadṛdhnuvanti yaduktho yajñakratoranantarayāya yadrātriḥ sarvasyāptyai
<4.1.7>     sa etān stomānapaśyat jyotirgaurauritīme vai lokā ete stomā ayameva jyotirayaṃ madhyamo gaurasāvuttama āyuḥ
<4.1.8>     yadete stomā bhavantīmāneva lokān prajanayantyeṣu lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.1.9>     sa etaṃ tryahaṃ punaḥ prāyauṅkta tena ṣaḍahena ṣaṭkratūn prājanayat
<4.1.10>     yadeṣa ṣaḍaho bhavati ṛtūneva prajanayanti ṛtuṣu pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.1.11>     sa etaṃ ṣaḍahaṃ punaḥ prāyuṅkta tābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ ṣaḍahābhyāṃ dvādaśamasaḥ prājanayat
<4.1.12>     yadetau ṣaḍahau bhavato māsāneva prajanayanti māseṣu pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.1.13>     sa etau dvau ṣaḍahau punaḥ prayuṅkta taiścaturbhiḥ ṣaḍahaiścaturviṃśatimardhamāsān prājanayat
<4.1.14>     yadete catvāraḥ ṣaḍahā bhavantyardhamāsāneva prajanayantyardhamāseṣu pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.1.15>     sa idaṃ bhuvanaṃ prajanayitva pṛṣṭhyena ṣaḍahena vīryamātmanyadhatta
<4.1.16>     yadeṣaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaho bhavati vīrya evāntataḥ pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.1.17>     tena māsān saṃvvatsaraṃ prājanayadyadeṣa māso bhavati saṃvvatsarameva prajanayanti saṃvvatsare pratitiṣṭhanti

<4.2.1>     prāyaṇīyametadaharbhavati
<4.2.2>     prāyaṇīyena vā ahnā devāḥ svargaṃ lokaṃ prāyan yat prāyaṃstat prāyaṇīyasya prāyaṇīyatvam
<4.2.3>     tasmāt prāyaṇīyasyāhna ṛtvijā bhavitavyametaddhi svargasya lokasya nediṣṭhaṃ ya etasyartviṅna bhavati hīyate svargāllokāt
<4.2.4>     caturviṃśaṃ bhavati
<4.2.5>     caturviṃśatyakṣarā gāyatrī tejo brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī teja eva brahmavarcasamārabhya prayanti
<4.2.6>     caturviṃśaṃ bhavati caturviṃśo vai saṃvvatsaraḥ sākṣādeva saṃvvatsaramārabhante
<4.2.7>     yāvatyaścaturviṃśasyokthasya stotrīyāstāvatyaḥ saṃvvatsarasya rātrayaḥ stotrīyābhireva tat saṃvvatsaramāpnuvanti
<4.2.8>     pañcadaśastotrāṇi bhavanti pañcadaśārdhamāsasya rātrayo'rdhamāsaśa eva tat saṃvvatsaramāpnuvanti
<4.2.9>     pañcadaśa stotrāṇi pañcadaśaśastrāṇi samāso māsaśa eva tat saṃvvatsaramāpnuvanti
<4.2.10>     tadāhurīrma iva vā eṣā hotrā yadacchāvākyā yadacchāvākamanusaṃtiṣṭhata īśvarermā bhavitoriti yadyukthaṃ smātraikakubhañcodvaṃśīyañcāntataḥ pratiṣṭhāpye vīryaṃ vā ete samanī vīrya evāntataḥ pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.2.11>     atho khalvāhuragniṣṭomameva kāryameṣa vai yajñaḥ svargyo yadagniṣṭoma ūrdhvo hi hotāramanusaṃtiṣṭhate
<4.2.12>     dvādaśastotrāṇyagniṣṭomo dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvvatsarastena saṃvvatsarasaṃmito dvādaśa stotraṇi dvādaśa śastrāṇi taccaturviṃśatiścaturviṃśatirardhamāsāḥ saṃvvatsarastena saṃvvatsarasaṃmitaḥ
<4.2.13>     atho khalvāhurukthameva kāryamahnaḥ samṛddhyai
<4.2.14>     sarvāṇi rūpāṇi kriyante sarvaṃ hyetāhnāpyate
<4.2.15>     pavante vājasātaye somāḥ sahasrapājasa iti sahasravatī pratipat kāryā
<4.2.16>     saṃvvatsarasya rūpaṃ sarvanevainānetayā punāti sarvānabhivadati
<4.2.17>     atho khalvāhuḥ pavasva vāco agriya ityeva kāryā mukhaṃ vā etat saṃvvatsarasya yadvāco'graṃ mukhata eva tat saṃvvatsaramārabhante
<4.2.18>     mithunamiva vā eṣā vyāhṛtiḥ pavasveti puṃso rūpaṃ vāca iti striyāḥ someti puṃso rūpaṃ citrābhiriti striyā mithunamevaibhyo yajñamukhe dadhāti prajananāya
<4.2.19>     agne yuṅkṣvā hi ye taveti jarābodhīyamagniṣṭoma sāma kāryaṃ yuktenaiva saṃvvatsareṇa prayanti caturviṃśatyakṣarāsu bhavati caturviṃśasya rūpam
<4.2.20>     atho khalvāhuryajñāyajñīyameva kāryam
<4.2.21>     panthā vai yajñāyajñīyaṃ patha eva tanna yanti

<4.3.1>     abhīvarto brahmasāma bhavati
<4.3.2>     abhīvartena vai devāḥ svargaṃ lokamabhyavartanta yadabhīvarto brahmasāma bhavati svargasya lokasyābhivṛttyai
<4.3.3>     ekākṣaraṇidhano bhavatyekākṣarā vai vāgvācaiva tadārabhya svargaṃ lokaṃ yanti
<4.3.4>     sāmneto yantyṛcā punarāyanti
<4.3.5>     sāma vai asau loka ṛgayaṃ yaditaḥ sāmnā yanti svargaṃ lokamārabhya yanti yadṛcā punarāyantyasmin loke pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.3.6>     yat sāmāvasṛjeyurava svargāllokāt padyeran yadṛcamanusṛjeyurnaśyeyurasmāllokāt
<4.3.7>     samānaṃ sāma bhavatyanyo'nyaḥ pragātho'nyadanyaddhi citramadhvānamavagacchanneti
<4.3.8>     vṛṣā vā eṣa retodhā yadabhīvartaḥ pragātheṣu reto dadhadeti yaditaḥ samānaṃ sāma bhavatyanyo'nyaḥ pragātho reta eva taddadhati yat parastāt samānaḥ pragātho bhavatyanyadanyat sāma reta eva taddhitaṃ prajanayanti
<4.3.9>     sāmnetaḥ pragāthāṃ dugdhe pragāthena parastāt sāmāni dugdhe salomatvāya
<4.3.10>     yo vā adhvānaṃ punarnivartayanti nainaṃ te gacchanti ye'punarnivartaṃ yanti te gacchati
<4.3.11>     ya āstutaṃ kurvate yathā dugdhāmupasīdedevaṃ tat
<4.3.12>     ye nāstutaṃ yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayante tamabhyaśnuvate
<4.3.13>     brahmavādino vadanti yātayāmāḥ saṃvvatsarā3 ayātayāmā3 iti te nāyātayāmeti vaktavyaṃ punaranyāni stotrāṇi nivartanta ūrdhvameva brahmasāmaiti

<4.4.1>     pañcasu māḥsu bārhatāḥ pragāthā āpyante
<4.4.2>     teṣvāpteṣu chandasī saṃyujyaitavyam
<4.4.3>     tisra uṣṇihaḥ syurekā gāyatrī tāstisro bṛhatyo bhavanti
<4.4.4>     tadāhuḥ saṃśara iva vā eṣa cchandasāṃ yaddve chandasī saṃyuñjantīti
<4.4.5>     caturuttaraireva cchandobhiretavyam
<4.4.6>     paśavo vai caturuttarāṇi cchandasāṃsi paśubhireva tat svargaṃ lokamākramamānayanti
<4.4.7>     ekaṃ gāyatrīmekāhamupeyurekāmuṣṇihamekāhamekāmanuṣṭubhamekāhaṃ bṛhatyā pañcamāsa iyuḥ paṅktim  ekāhamupeyustriṣṭubhā ṣaṣṭhaṃ māsamīyuḥ śvo viṣuvān bhaviteti jagatīmupeyuḥ
<4.4.8>     tadāhuranavakḷptāni vā etāni cchandāṃsi madhyandine bṛhatyā caiva triṣṭubhā caitavyam
<4.4.9>     ete vai chandasī vīryavatī ete pratyakṣaṃ madhyandinasya rūpam
<4.4.10>     rathantare'hani bṛhatī kāryaitadvai rathantarasya svamāyatanaṃ yadbṛhatī sva eva tadāyatane rathantaraṃ pratitiṣṭhati bārhate'hani triṣṭupkāryaitadvai bṛhataḥ svamāyatanaṃ yat triṣṭupsva eva tadāyatane bṛhadrathantare pratitiṣṭhantī itaḥ
<4.4.11>     trayastriṃśatā pragāthairetavyaṃ trayastriṃśaddevatā devatāsveva pratitiṣṭhanto yanti caturviṃśatyaitavyaṃ caturviṃśatirardhamāsāḥ saṃvvatsaraḥ saṃvvatsara eva pratitiṣṭhanto yanti dvādaśabhiretavyaṃ dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvvatsaraḥ saṃvvatsara eva pratitiṣṭhanto yanti ṣaḍbhiretavyaṃ ṣaḍṛtava ṛtuṣveva pratitiṣṭhanto yanti caturbhiretavyaṃ catuṣpādāḥ paśavaḥ paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhanto yanti tribhiretavyaṃ traya ime lokā eṣveva lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhanto yanti dvābhyāmetavyaṃ dvipādyajamānaḥ svargasya lokasyākrāntyā anyenānyena hi padā puruṣaḥ pratitiṣṭhanneti

<4.5.1>     svarasāmāna ete bhavante
<4.5.2>     svarbhānurvā āsurādityaṃ tamasāvidhyat taṃ devāḥ svarairaspṛṇvan yat svarasāmāno bhavantyādityasya spṛtyai
<4.5.3>     parairvai devā ādityaṃ svargaṃ lokamapārayan yadaparayaṃstat parāṇāṃ paratvaṃ
<4.5.4>     pārayantyenaṃ parāṇi ya evaṃvveda
<4.5.5>     saptadaśā bhavanti
<4.5.6>     prajāpatirvai saptadaśaḥ prajāpatimevopayanti
<4.5.7>     anuṣṭupchandaso bhavantyānuṣṭubho vai prajāpatiḥ sākṣādeva prajāpatimārabhante
<4.5.8>     trayaḥ purastāt trayaḥ parastādbhavanti
<4.5.9>     devā vā ādityasya svargāllokādavapādādabibhayustametaiḥ stomaiḥ saptadaśairadṛṃhan yadete stomābhavantyādityasya dhṛtyai
<4.5.10>     catustriṃśā bhavanti varṣma vai catustriṃśo varṣmaṇaivainaṃ saṃmimate
<4.5.11>     tasya parācīnātipādādabibhayustaṃ sarvaiḥ stomaiḥ +paryārṣan viśvajidabhijidbhyāṃ vīryaṃ vā etau stomau vīryeṇaiva tadādityaṃ paryuṣanti dhṛtyai
<4.5.12>     anavapādāyānatipādāya
<4.5.13>     tadāhuḥ kartapraskanda iva vā eṣa yat trayastriṃśata+ḥ saptadaśamupayantīti pṛṣṭhyo'ntaraḥ kāryaḥ
<4.5.14>     tasya yat saptadaśamahastaduttamaṃ kāryaṃ salomatvāya
<4.5.15>     tadāhurudaraṃ vā eṣa stomānāṃ yat saptadaśo yat saptadaśaṃ madhyato nirhareyuraśanāyavaḥ prajāḥ syuraśanāyavaḥ sattriṇaḥ
<4.5.16>     trayastriṃśādeva saptadaśa upetyo varṣma vai trayastriṃśo varṣma saptadaśo varṣmaṇa eva tadvarṣmābhisaṃkrāmanti
<4.5.17>     nānāhurukthāḥ kāryā3 iti yadyukthāḥ syuḥ
<4.5.18>     paśavo vā ukthāni śāntiḥ paśavaḥ śāntenaiva tadviṣuvantamupayanti
<4.5.19>     tadāhurvivīvadhamiva vā etadyadagniṣṭomo viṣuvānagniṣṭomau viśvajidabhijitāvathetara ukthāḥ syuriti
<4.5.20>     agniṣṭomā eva sarve kāryāḥ
<4.5.21>     vīryaṃ vā agniṣṭomo vīrya eva madhyataḥ pratitiṣṭhanti nava saṃstutā bhavanti nava prāṇāḥ praṇeṣveva pratitiṣṭhanti

<4.6.1>     viṣuvāneṣa bhavati
<4.6.2>     devaloko vā eṣa yadviṣuvān devaokameva tadabhyārohanti
<4.6.3>     ekaviṃśo bhavatyekaviṃśo vā asya bhuvanasyāditya ādityalokameva tadabhyārohanti
<4.6.4>     dvādaśa māsāḥ pañcartavastraya ime lokā asāvāditya ekaviṃśaḥ
<4.6.5>     madhyata eva yajñasya pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.6.6>     vāyo śukro ayāmi ta iti śukravatī pratipadbhavatyādityasya rūpam
<4.6.7>     vāyurvā etaṃ devatānāmānaśe'nuṣṭupchandasaṃ yadato'nyā pratipat syāt pradahet
<4.6.8>     yanti vā ete prāṇādityāhurye gāyatryāḥ pratipado yantīti yadvayavyā bhavanti tena prāṇānna yanti prāṇo hi vāyuḥ
<4.6.9>     atho śamayantyevainametayā śantirhi vāyuḥ
<4.6.10>     ā yāhi somapītaya iti saumī pāvamānī
<4.6.11>     niyutvatī bhavati paśavo vai niyutaḥ śāntiḥ paśavaḥ śāntenaiva tadādityamupayanti
<4.6.12>     divākīrtyasāmā bhavati
<4.6.13>     svarbhānurvā āsurādityaṃ tamasāvidhyat tasya devā divākīrtyaistamo'pāghnan yaddivākīrtyāni bhavanti tama evāsmādapaghnanti raśmayo vā eta ādityasya yaddivākīrtyāni raśmibhireva tadādityaṃ sākṣādārabhante
<4.6.14>     bhrājābhrāje pavamānamukhe bhavato mukhata evāsya tābhyāṃ tamo'paghnanti
<4.6.15>     mahādivākīrtyaṃ ca vikarṇaṃ ca madhyato bhavato madhyata evāsya tābhyāṃ tamo'paghnanti bhāsamantato bhavati patta evāsya tena tamo'paghnanti
<4.6.16>     daśastobhaṃ bhavti daśākṣarā virāḍvirājyeva pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.6.17>     yat tvityāhuḥ ṣaḍbhirito māsairadhvānaṃ yanti ṣaḍbhiḥ punarāyanti kva tarhi svargo loko yasya kāmāya sattramāsata iti
<4.6.18>     mūrdhānaṃ diva iti svargaṃ lokamārabhante
<4.6.19>     aratiṃ pṛthivyā ityasmiṃ loke pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.6.20>     vaiśvānaramṛta ājātamagnimiti viṣuvata eva tadrūpaṃ kriyate
<4.6.21>     kaviṃ samrājamatitihiṃ janānāmityannādyamevopayanti
<4.6.22>     āsannāpātraṃ janayanta devā iti jāyanta eva
<4.6.23>     tat triṣṭubjagatīṣu bhavati traiṣṭubhjāgato vā ādityo yadato'nyāsu syādava svargāllokāt padyeran
<4.6.24>     samrāḍvatīṣu bhavati sāmrājyaṃ vai svargo lokaḥ svarga eva loke pratitiṣṭhanti

<4.7.1>     ātmā vā eṣa saṃvvatsarasya yadviṣuvān pakṣāvetāvabhito bhavato yena ceto'bhīvartena yanti yaśca parasmāt pragātho bhavati tāvubhau viṣuvati kāryau pakṣāveva tadyajñasyātman pratidadhati svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
<4.7.2>     indra kratuṃ na ābhareti pragātho bhavati
<4.7.3>     vasiṣṭho vā etaṃ putrahato'paśyat sa prajayā paśubhiḥ prājāyata yadeṣa pragātho bhavati prajātyai
<4.7.4>     jīvā jyotiraśīmahīti ye vai svasti saṃvvatsaraṃ saṃtaranti te jīvā jyotiraśnuvate
<4.7.5>     māno ajñātā vṛjanā durādhyo māśivāso'vakramuriti ye vai stenā ripavaste durādhyastāneva tadatikrāmati
<4.7.6>     tvayā vyayaṃ pravataḥ śaśvatīrapo'ti śūra tarāma so'tisaṃvvatsaro vai pravataḥ śaśvatīrapastameva tat taranti
<4.7.7>     adyādyāśvaḥ śvastvāmidā hyo naro vayamenamidā hya iti saṃtanayaḥ pragāthā bhavanti teṣāmekaḥ kāryaḥ salomatvāya śvastanamevābhisaṃtanvanti
<4.7.8>     atho khalvāhurindra kratuṃ na ābharetyeva kāryaṃ samṛddhyai
<4.7.9>     pratyavarohiṇo māsā bhavanti
<4.7.10>     yathā vā ito vṛkṣaṃ rohantyevamenaṃ pratyavarohanti svargameva lokaṃ rūḍhvāsmiṃlloke pratitiṣṭhanti

<4.8.1>     gauścāyuśca stomau bhavataḥ
<4.8.2>     dvipādyajamānaḥ pratiṣṭhityai
<4.8.3>     ūnātiriktau bhavata ūnātiriktaṃ vā anu prajāḥ prajāyante prajātyai
<4.8.4>     vairājau bhavato'nnaṃ virāḍannādya eva pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.8.5>     dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni bhavanti
<4.8.6>     virāḍvā eṣā samṛddhā yaddaśāhāni virājyeva samṛddhāyāṃ pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.8.7>     pṛṣthāni bhavanti vīryaṃ vai pṛṣṭhāni vīrya eva pratitiṣṭhanti cchandomā bhavanti paśavo vai cchandomāḥ paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhantyathaitaddaśamamaharāptastomamāptacchanda āptavibhaktikamaniruktaṃ prājāpatyam
<4.8.8>     yadadhyāhurati tadrecayanti tasmānna vyucyam
<4.8.9>     parokṣamanuṣṭubhamupayanti prajāpatirvā anuṣṭubyat pratyakṣamupeyuḥ prajāpatimṛccheyuḥ
<4.8.10>     yo vai sattrasya sadveda sadbhavati vāmadevyaṃ vai sāmnāṃ sadagnirdevatānāṃ virāṭchandasāṃ trayastriṃśaḥ stomānāṃ tānyeva tadekadhā saṃbhṛtyottiṣṭhanti
<4.8.11>     brahmavādino vadanti yataḥ sattrādudasthātā3 thitā3 iti
<4.8.12>     ye rathantareṇa stutvottiṣṭhanti te yata uttiṣṭhanti tān brūyādapratiṣṭhānā bhaviṣyantīti ye bṛhatā stutyottiṣṭhanti te sthitāduttiṣṭhanti tān brūyāt sthāyukaiṣāṃ śrīrbhaviṣyati na vasīyāṃso bhaviṣyantīti
<4.8.13>     ye vāmadevyena stutvottiṣṭhanti te sataḥ sadabhyuttiṣṭhanti pūrṇāt pūrṇamāyatanādāyatanamantarikṣāyatanā hi prajā
<4.8.14>     trayastriṃśadakṣarāsu bhavati trayastriṃśaddevatāsveva pratiṣṭhāyottiṣṭhanti
<4.8.15>     prājāpatyaṃ vai vāmadevyaṃ prajāpatāveva pratiṣṭhāyottiṣṭhanti paśavo vai vāmadevyaṃ paśuṣveva pratiṣṭhāyottiṣṭhanti
<4.9.1>     patnīḥ saṃyājya prāñca udetyāyaṃ sahasramānava ityaticchandasāhavanīyamupatiṣṭhante
<4.9.2>     ime vai lokā aticchandā eṣveva lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.9.3>     goriti nidhanaṃ bhavati virājo vā etadrūpaṃ yadgaurvirājyeva pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.9.4>     pratyañcaḥ prapadya sārparājñyā ṛgbhiḥ stuvanti
<4.9.5>     arbudaḥ sarpa etābhirmṛtāṃ tvacamapāhata mṛtāmevaitābhirstvacamapaghnate
<4.9.6>     iyaṃ vai sārparājñyasyāmeva pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.9.7>     tisṛbhiḥ stuvanti traya ime lokā eṣveva pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.9.8>     manasopāvartayati
<4.9.9>     manasā hiṅkaroti manasā prastauti manasodgāyati manasā pratiharati manasā nidhanamupayantyasamāptasya samāptyai
<4.9.10>     yadvai vācā na samāpnuvanti manasā tat samāpnuvanti
<4.9.11>     pariśrite stuvanti brahmaṇaḥ parigṛhītyai
<4.9.12>     brahmodyaṃ vadanti brahmavarcasa eva pratitiṣṭhanti
<4.9.13>     caturhotāraṃ hotā vyācaṣṭe stutameva tadanuśaṃsati nahi tat stutaṃ yadananuśastam
<4.9.14>     prajāpatiṃ parivadantyāptvevainaṃ tadvyācakṣate tāvadāpāmainamiti
<4.9.15>     gṛhapatiraudumbarīṃ dhārayati gṛhapatirvā ūrjo yantorjamevaibhyo yacchati
<4.9.16>     vācaṃ yacchanti
<4.9.17>     dugdhānīva vai tarhi chandasāṃsi yātayāmānyantargatāni tānyeva tadrasenāpyāyayanti
<4.9.18>     atho śvastanamevābhisaṃtanvanti
<4.9.19>     ātmadakṣiṇaṃ vā etadyat sattram
<4.9.20>     yadā vai puruṣa ātmano'vadyati yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate tamabhyaśnute
<4.9.21>     dvābhyāṃ lomāvadyati dvābhyāṃ tvacaṃ dvābhyāṃ māṃsaṃ dvābhyāmasthi dvābhyāṃ majjānaṃ dvābhyāṃ pīvaśca lohitaṃ ca
<4.9.22>     śikhā anupravapante pāpmānameva tadapaghnate laghīyāṃsaḥ svargaṃ lokamayāmeti
<4.9.23>     atho gavāmevānurūpā bhavanti sarvasyānnādyasyāvaruddhyai

<4.10.1>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata so'ricyata so'padyata taṃ devā abhisamagacchanta te'bruvanmahadasmai vrataṃ saṃbharāma yadimaṃ dhinavaditi tasmai yat saṃvvatsaramannaṃ pacyate tat samabharaṃstadasmai prāyacchaṃstadavrajayat tadenamadhinonmahanmaryā vrataṃ yadimamadhinvīditi tanmahāvratasya mahāvratatvam
<4.10.2>     prajāpatirvāva mahāṃstasyaitadvratamannameva
<4.10.3>     tadāhurmadhyaḥ saṃvvatsarasyopetyaṃ madhyato vā annaṃ jagdhaṃ dhinoti
<4.10.4>     tadvāhuryanmadhyata upayantyardhamannādyasyāpnuvantyardhaṃ sambaṭkurvantītyupariṣṭādeva saṃvvatsarasyopetyaṃ saṃvvatsare vā annaṃ sarvaṃ pacyate
<4.10.5>     caturviṃśaṃ bhavati caturviṃśo vai saṃvvatsaro'nnaṃ pañcaviṃśam
<4.10.6>     yadvā adaścaturviṃśaṃprāyaṇīyaṃ tadetadudayanīyam
<4.10.7>     yat saṃvvatsaramannaṃ saṃbharanti saiṣā pañcaviṃśyupajāyate

<5.1.1>     vāmadevyaṃ mahāvrataṃ kāryam
<5.1.2>     trivṛcchiro bhavati
<5.1.3>     trivṛddhyeva śiro loma tvagasthi
<5.1.4>     pāṅkta itara ātmā loma tvaṅmāṃsamasthi majjā
<5.1.5>     sakṛddhiṅkṛtena śirasā pārācā stuvate
<5.1.6>     tasmācchiro'ṅgāni medyanti nānumedyati na kṛśyantyanukṛśyati
<5.1.7>     punarabhyāvartamitareṇātmanā stuvate tasmāditara ātmā medyati ca kṛśyati ca
<5.1.8>     arkavatīṣu gāyatrīṣu śiro bhavati
<5.1.9>     annaṃ vā arko brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatryannādyaṃ caivaibhyo brahmavarcasaṃ ca mukhato dadhāti
<5.1.10>     pañcadaśasaptadaśau pakṣau bhavataḥ prakṣābhyāṃ vai yajamāno vayo bhūtvā svargaṃ lokameti
<5.1.11>     tāvāhuḥ samau kāryau pañcadaśau vā saptadaśau vā savīvadhatvāya
<5.1.12>     tadvāhuryat samau bhavata ekavīryau tarhi bhavata iti pañcadaśasaptadaśāveva kāryau sācīva vai vayaḥ pakṣau kṛtvāpatīyaḥ patati
<5.1.13>     dakṣiṇato bṛhat kāryaṃ dakṣiṇo vā ardha ātmano vīryavattaraḥ
<5.1.14>     atho khalvāhuruttarata eva kāryaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsino'rdhāt traiṣṭubhaṃ vai bṛhat traiṣṭubho vai brāhmaṇācchaṃsī traiṣṭubhaḥ pañcadaśastomaḥ
<5.1.15>     dakṣiṇato rathantaraṃ kāryaṃ maitrāvaruṇasyārdhādgāyatraṃ vai rathantaraṃ gāyatro maitrāvaruṇo gāyatraḥ saptadaśastomaḥ
<5.1.16>     ekaviṃśaṃ pucchaṃ bhavati
<5.1.17>     ekaviṃśo vai stomānāṃ pratiṣṭhā tasmādvayaḥ pucchena pratiṣṭhāyotpatati pucchena pratiṣṭhāya niṣīdati
<5.1.18>     yajñāyajñīyaṃ pucchaṃ kāryaṃ yajñāyajñīyaṃ hyeva mahāvratasya puccham
<5.1.19>     atho khalvāhuratiśayaṃ vai dvipadāṃ yajñāyajñīyaṃ bhadraṃ kāryaṃ samṛddhyai

<5.2.1>     vāmadevyaṃ mahāvrataṃ kāryaṃ tasya gāyatraṃ śiro bṛhadrathantare pakṣau yajñāyajñīyaṃ puccham
<5.2.2>     yo vai mahāvrate sahasraṃ protaṃ veda pra sahasraṃ paśūnāpnoti
<5.2.3>     tasya prācī dikśirastacchandobhiḥ sahasramasāvanyataraḥ pakṣaḥ sanakṣattraiḥ sāhasro'yamanyataraḥ pakṣaḥ sa oṣadhibhiśca vanaspatibhiśca sāhasro'ntarikṣamātmā tadvayobhiḥ sāhasraṃ pratīcī dikpucchaṃ tadagnibhiśca raśmibhiśca sāhasraṃ pra sasasraṃ paśūnāpnoti ya evaṃvveda
<5.2.4>     tadāhurapṛṣṭhaṃ vai vāmadevyamanidhanaṃ hīti
<5.2.5>     anāyatanaṃ vā etat sāma yadanidhanam
<5.2.6>     rājanaṃ mahāvrataṃ kāryam
<5.2.7>     etadvai sākṣādannaṃ yadrājanaṃ pañcavidhaṃ bhavati pāṅktaṃ hyannam
<5.2.8>     hiṅkāravadbhavati tena vāmadevyasya rūpam
<5.2.9>     nidhanavadbhavati tena prṣṭhasya rūpam
<5.2.10>     aticchandaḥsu pañcanidhanaṃ vāmadevyaṃ brahmasāma kāryam
<5.2.11>     ati vā eṣānyāni cchandāṃsi yadaticchandā atyetadanyānyahānyaharyanmahāvratam
<5.2.12>     brahmasāmnaiva tadanyānyahānyatimedayati
<5.2.13>     pañcanidhanaṃ bhavati pāṅktaṃ hyannam
<5.3.1>     ilāndamagniṣṭomasāma kāryam
<5.3.2>     etadvai sākṣādannaṃ yadilāndamirānnaṃ etadirāyāmevānnādye'ntataḥ pratitiṣṭhanti
<5.3.3>     samudro vā etacchandaḥ salilaṃ lomaśaṃ samudra iva khalu vai sa bhavati salila iva lomaśa iva yo bhavati
<5.3.4>     tasmādetāsu kāryaṃ samṛddhyai
<5.3.5>     vratamiti nidhanaṃ bhavati mahāvratasyaiva tadrūpaṃ kriyate svariti bhavati svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai śakuna iti bhavati śakuna iva vai yajamāno vayo bhūtvā svargaṃllokameti
<5.3.6>     yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma kāryam
<5.3.7>     yadvā anyā vāṅnātivadet tadagniṣṭomasāma kāryaṃ na vai vāgvācamativadati vāgyajñāyajñīyaṃ vācyevāntataḥ pratitiṣṭhanti
<5.3.8>     vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma kāryam
<5.3.9>     agnirvā idaṃ vaiśvānaro'dahannaitasmāddevā abibhayustaṃ varaṇaśākhayāvārayanta yadavārayanta tasmādvāravantīyam
<5.3.10>     tasmādvaraṇo bhiṣajya etena hi devā ātmānamatrāyanta
<5.3.11>     tasmādbrāhmaṇo vāraṇena na pibedvaiśvānaraṃ necchamayā iti
<5.3.12>     paśavo vai vāravantīyaṃ śāntiḥ paśavaḥ śāntādeva tat saṃvvatsarāduttiṣṭhanti

<5.4.1>     prāṇena purastādāhavanīyamupatiṣṭhante prāṇameva tajjayanti
<5.4.2>     apānena paścāt pucchamupatiṣṭhante apānameva tajjayanti
<5.4.3>     vratapakṣābhyāṃ pakṣāvupatiṣṭhante diśa eva tajjayanti
<5.4.4>     prajāpaterhṛdayenāpikakṣamupatiṣṭhante jyaiṣṭhyameva tajjayanti
<5.4.5>     vasiṣṭhasya nihavena cātvālamupatiṣṭhante svargameva tallokamāotvā śriyaṃ vadante
<5.4.6>     vaiśvadevyāmṛci bhavati viśvarūpaṃ vai paśūnāṃ rūpaṃ paśūneva tajjayanti
<5.4.7>     sattrasyarddhyāgnīdhramupatiṣṭhanta ṛddhāveva pratitiṣṭhanti
<5.4.8>     caturakṣarāṇidhanaṃ bhavati catuṣpādāḥ paśavaḥ paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhanti
<5.4.9>     ātamitornidhanamupayantyāyureva sarvamāpnuvanti
<5.4.10>     ślokānuślokābhyāṃ havirdhāne upatiṣṭhante kīrtimeva tajjayanti
<5.4.11>     yāmena mārjālīyamupatiṣṭhante pitṛlokameva tajjayanti
<5.4.12>     āyurṇavastobhābhyāṃ sada upatiṣṭhante brahma caiva tat kṣatraṃ ca jayanti
<5.4.13>     ṛśyasya sāmnā gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhante
<5.4.14>     indraṃ sarvāṇi bhūtānyastuvaṃstasyarśya ekamaṅgamastutamacāyat tadasyaitenāstaut tenāsya priyaṃ dhāmopāgacchat priyamevāsyaitena dhāmopagacchanti
<5.4.15>     yat paro'kṣaṃ nidhanamupeyurhrītamukhaṃ pratimuñceran pratyakṣamupayanti hrītamukhamevāpajayanti

<5.5.1>     āsandīmāruhyodgāyati devasākṣya eva tadupariṣadyaṃ jayati
<5.5.2>     audumbarī bhavatyūrgudumbara ūrjamevāvarunddhe
<5.5.3>     prādeśamātrī bhavatyasya lokasyānuddhānāya
<5.5.4>     chandobhirārohati svargameva tallokamārohati
<5.5.5>     chandobhirupāvarohatyasmiṃ loke pratitiṣṭhati
<5.5.6>     sarveṇātmanā samuddhṛtyodgeyameṣu lokeṣu nedvyāhito'sānīti
<5.5.7>     ekasyāṃ stotrīyāyāmastutāyāṃ pādāvupāharati
<5.5.8>     saha nidhanena pratiṣṭhāmupayantyeṣeṣveva lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhante
<5.5.9>     preṅkhāmāruhya hotā śaṃsati mahasa eva tadrūpaṃ kriyate
<5.5.10>     yadā vai prajā maha āviśati preṅkhāstarhyārohanti
<5.5.11>     phalakamāruhyādhvaryuḥ pratigṛṇāti
<5.5.12>     kūrcānitare'dhyāsata ūrdhvā eva tadutkrāmanto yanti
<5.5.13>     abhigarāpagarau bhavato nindatyenānanyaḥ prānyaḥ śaṃsati ya enānnindati pāpmānameṣāṃ so'pahanti yaḥ praśaṃsati yadevaiṣāṃ suṣṭutaṃ suśastaṃ tat so'bhigṛṇāti
<5.5.14>     śūdrāryau carmaṇi vyāyacchete tayorāryaṃ varṇamujjāpayanti
<5.5.15>     devāśca vā asurāścāditye vyāyacchantastaṃ devā abhyajayaṃstato devā abhavan | parāsurā abhavannātmanā parāsya bhrātṛvyo bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<5.5.16>     yadāryaṃ varṇamujjāpayantyātmānameva tadujjāpayanti
<5.5.17>     parimaṇḍalaṃ carma bhavatyādityasyaiva tadrūpaṃ kriyate
<5.5.18>     sarvāsu sraktiṣu dundubhayo vadanti yā vanaspatiṣu vāktāmeva tajjayanti
<5.5.19>     bhūmidundubhirbhavati yā pṛthivyāṃ vāktāmeva tajjayanti
<5.5.20>     sarvā vāco vadanti yeṣu lokeṣu vāktāmeva tajjayanti
<5.5.21>     saṃnaddhāḥ kavacinaḥ pariyantīndriyasyaiva tadrūpaṃ kriyate'tho mahāvratameva mahayanti

<5.6.1>     sarve sahartvijo mahāvratena stuvīran
<5.6.2>     adhvaryuḥ śirasodgāyanmaitrāvaruṇo dakṣiṇena pakṣeṇa brāhmaṇācchaṃsyuttareṇa gṛhapatiḥ pucchenodgātātmanā
<5.6.3>     tadyadyevaṃ kuryurekaikayā stotrīyayāstutayodgātāramabhisameyuḥ
<5.6.4>     tisṛbhirudgātātmana udgīyātha yā śirasaḥ stotrīyā tāṃ dadhyādaparābhistisṛbhirudgīyātha yā dakṣiṇasya pakṣasya stotrīyā tāṃ dadhyādaparābhistisṛbhirudgīyātha yottarasya pakṣasya stotrīyā tāṃ dadhyāt tisṛbhirvaikayā vā stutaṃ syādatha yā pucchasya stotrīyā tāṃ dadhyāt
<5.6.5>     ātmanyeva tadaṅgāni pratidadhati svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
<5.6.6>     atho khalvāhuḥ kathamadhvaryurbahvṛcaḥ sāma gāyedityudgātaiva sarveṇodgāyet tadeva samṛddhaṃ samṛddhāveva pratitiṣṭhanti
<5.6.7>     havirdhāne śirasā stutvā saṃrabdhāḥ pratyañca eyuste dakṣiṇena dhiṣṇyān parītya paścānmaitrāvaruṇasya dhiṣṇyasyopaviśya rathantareṇa pañcadaśena stuvīraṃsta udañcaḥ saṃsarpeyurjaghanena hoturdhiṣṇyaṃ paścādbrāhmaṇācchaṃsino dhiṣṇyasyopaviśya bṛhatā saptadaśena stuvīraṃste yenaiva prasarpeyustena punarniḥsṛpyottareṇāgnīdhraṃ parītya paścādgārhapatyasyopaviśya pucchenaikaviṃśena stuvīraṃste yenaiva niḥsarpeyustena punaḥ prasṛpya yathāyatanamupaviśyāsandīmāruhyodgātātmanodgāyati
<5.6.8>     taṃ patnyo'paghāṭilābhirupagāyantyārtvijyameva tat patnyaḥ kurvanti saha svargaṃllokamayāmeti
<5.6.9>     kulekule'nnaṃ kriyate tadyat pṛccheyuḥ kimidaṃ kurvantītīme yajamānā annamatsyantīti brūyuḥ
<5.6.10>     yo vai dīkṣitānāṃ pāpaṃ kīrtayati tṛtīyamevāṃśaṃ pāpmano haratyannādastṛtīyaṃ pipīlikāstṛtīyam
<5.6.11>     parimādbhiścaranti tvakca vā etalloma ca mahāvratasya yat parimādastvacaṃ caiva talloma ca mahāvratasyāptvāvarundhate
<5.6.12>     vāṇaṃ vitanvantyanto vai vāṇo'nto mahāvratamantenaiva tadantamabhivādayanti
<5.6.13>     śatatantrīko bhavati śatāyurvai puruṣaḥ śatavīryaḥ
<5.6.14>     tamullikhet prāṇāya tvāpānāya tvā vyānāya tveti prāṇāpānavyānāneva tadāptvāvarundhate
<5.6.15>     pari kumbhinyo mārjālīyaṃ yantīdaṃ madhvidaṃ madhviti saghoṣā eva tadvayo bhūtvā saha svargaṃllokaṃ yanti

<5.7.1>     devā vai vācaṃ vyabhajanta tasyā yo raso'tyaricyata tadgaurīvitamabhavadanuṣṭubhamanu pariplavate vāganuṣṭubvāco raso gaurīvitam
<5.7.2>     yadgaurīvitenānvahaṃsstuvate vācyeva tadvācā rasaṃ dadhati
<5.7.3>     rasavadvācā vadati ya evaṃvvedeti
<5.7.4>     dvyudāsaṃ bhavati svargasya vā etau lokasyāvasānadeśau pūrveṇaiva pūrvamahaḥ saṃsthāpayantyuttareṇottaramaharabhyativadanti
<5.7.5>     etadvai yajñasya śvastanaṃ yadgaurīvitaṃ yadgaurīvitamanusṛjeyuraśvastanā aprajasaḥ syuḥ
<5.7.6>     saṃ vā anyo yajñastiṣṭhata ityāhurvāgeva na saṃtiṣṭhata iti yadgaurīvitamanvahaṃ bhavati vācameva tat punaḥ prayuñjate
<5.7.7>     svarṇidhanamanvahaṃ bhavati
<5.7.8>     devakṣetraṃ vā ete'bhyārohanti ye svarṇidhanamupayanti sa u vai sattriṇaḥ sattramupanayedityāhurya enān devakṣetramabhyārohediti na vai devakṣetra āsīna ārtimārchati yat svarṇidhanamanvahaṃ bhavati naiva kāṃ canārtimārchanti
<5.7.9>     cyavante vā ete'smāllokādityāhurye svarṇidhanamupayantīti yadṛcā svarūpaṃ  yantyasmiṃ loke pratitiṣṭhanti yadekāro'tarikṣe yat sāmnāmuṣmint sarveṣu lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhanti svarṇidhanena tuṣṭuvānāḥ
<5.7.10>     sujñānaṃ bhavati
<5.7.11>     devā vai svargaṃllokaṃ yanto'jñātādabibhayusta etat saṃjñānamapaśyaṃstena jñātramagacchan yat sujñānamanvahaṃ bhavati jñātrameva gacchanti

<5.8.1>     ye vai vācamannamādayantyannādā bhavanti ye vitarṣayanti rukṣā bhavanti
<5.8.2>     gaurīvitaṃ śyāvāśvaṃ nihava etāni vai sāmāni vāco'nnameteṣāṃ vāgannaṃ yadetāni na cyavante vācameva tadannamādayanti tena sarve'nnādā bhavanti
<5.8.3>     abhikrāntāpakrāntāni bhavantyabhikrāntāpakrāntaṃ vai vāco rūpam
<5.8.4>     plavo'nvahaṃ bhavati
<5.8.5>     samudraṃ vā ete prasnānti ye saṃvvatsaramupayanti yo vā aplavaḥ samudraṃ prasnāti na sa tata udeti yat plavo bhavati svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
<5.8.6>     ati viśvāni duritā taremeti yadevaiṣāṃ duṣṭutaṃ duḥśastaṃ tadetena taranti
<5.8.7>     okonidhanaṃ ṣaḍahamukhe bhavati
<5.8.8>     parāṃ vā ete parāvataṃ gacchanti ye ṣaḍahasyāntaṃ gacchanti yadokonidhanaṃ ṣaḍahamukhe bhavati prajñātyai
<5.8.9>     yadā vai puruṣaḥ svamoka āgacchati sarvaṃ tarhi prajānāti sarvamasmai divā bhavati

<5.9.1>     ekāṣṭakāyāṃ dīkṣeran
<5.9.2>     eṣā vai saṃvvatsarasya patnī yadekāṣṭakaitasyāṃ vā gatāṃ rātriṃ vasati sākṣādeva tat saṃvvatsaramārabhya dīkṣante
<5.9.3>     tasya sāniryā yadapo' nabhinandanto'bhyavayanti
<5.9.4>     vicchinnaṃ vā ete saṃvvatsarasyābhidīkṣante ya ekāṣṭakāyāṃ dīkṣante'ntanāmānāvṛtū bhavete
<5.9.5>     ārtaṃ vā ete saṃvvatsarasyābhidīkṣante ye'ntanāmānāvṛtū abhidīkṣante
<5.9.6>     tasmādekāṣṭakāyāṃ na dīkṣyam
<5.9.7>     phālgune dīkṣeran
<5.9.8>     mukhaṃ vā etat saṃvvatsarasya yat phālguno mukhata eva tat saṃvvatsaramārabhya dīkṣante
<5.9.9>     tasya sāniryā yat saṃmeghe viṣuvān saṃpadyate
<5.9.10>     citrāpūrṇamāse dīkṣeran
<5.9.11>     cakṣurvā etat saṃvvatsarasya yaccitrāpūrṇamāso mukhato vai cakṣurmukhata eva tat saṃvvatsaramārabhya dīkṣante tasya na niryāsti
<5.9.12>     caturahe purastāt paurṇamāsyā dīkṣeran
<5.9.13>     teṣāmakāṣṭakāyāṃ krayaḥ saṃpadyate tenaikāṣṭakāṃ na saṃbaṭkurvanti
<5.9.14>     teṣāṃ pūrvapakṣe sutyā saṃpadyate pūrvapakṣe māsāḥ saṃtiṣṭhamānā yanti pūrvapakṣa uttiṣṭhanti tānuttiṣṭhataḥ paśava oṣadhayo'nūttiṣṭhanti tān kalyāṇī vāgabhivadaryarātsurime sattriṇa iti te rādhnuvanti

<5.10.1>     ā vā ete saṃvvatsaraṃ pyāyayanti ya utsṛjanti
<5.10.2>     yathā vai dṛtirādhmāta evaṃ saṃvvatsaro'nutsṛṣṭo yannotsṛjeyuramehena pramāyukāḥ syuḥ
<5.10.3>     prāṇo vai saṃvvatsara udānā māsā yadutsṛjanti prāṇa evodānān dadhati yo dīkṣitaḥ pramīyate yā saṃvvatsarasyānutsṛṣṭasya śuksā tamṛcchati
<5.10.4>     tadāhurutsṛjyā3ṃ notsṛjyā3 iti
<5.10.5>     yadyutsṛjeyurukthānyutsṛjeyustadevotsṛṣṭaṃ tadanutsṛṣṭam
<5.10.6>     atho khalvāhurekatrikaṃ kāryaṃ tadeva sākṣādutsṛṣṭamabhyutṣuṇvanti
<5.10.7>     chidro vā eteṣāṃ saṃvvatsara ityāhurye stomamutsṛjantīti
<5.10.8>     paśumālabhante stomameva tadālabhante stomo hi paśuḥ
<5.10.9>     śva utsṛṣṭāḥ sma iti vatsānapākurvanti prātaḥ paśumālabhante tasya vapayā pracaranti tatassavanīyenāṣṭākapālena tata āgneyenāṣṭākapālena tato dadhnaindreṇa tataścaruṇā vaiśvadevena tat prātaḥsavanaṃ saṃtiṣṭhante
<5.10.10>     tataḥ paśupuroḍāśenaikādaśakapālena tataḥ savanīyenaikādaśakapālena tato marutvatīyenaikādaśakapālena tataścaruṇaindreṇa tanmādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ saṃtiṣṭhante
<5.10.11>     paśunā pracaranti tataḥ savanīyena dvādaśakapālena tato vaiśvadevena dvādaśakapālena tataścaruṇāgnimārutena tat tṛtīyaṃ savanaṃ saṃtiṣṭhante
<5.10.12>     pṛṣadājyena pracarya patnīssaṃyājayanti

<6.1.1>     prajāpatirakāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti sa etamagniṣṭomamapaśyat tamāharat tenemāḥ prajā asṛjata
<6.1.2>     ekādaśena ca vai satā stotreṇāgniṣṭomasyāsṛjataikādaśena ca māsā saṃvvatsarasya tā dvādaśena ca stotreṇāgniṣṭomasya paryagṛhṇāddvādaśena ca māsā saṃvvatsarasya
<6.1.3>     tasmāt prajā daśamāso garbhaṃ bhṛtvaikādaśamanu prajāyante tasmāddvādaśaṃ nābhyatiharanti dvādaśena hi parigṛhītāstadya evaṃvveda pari jātāḥ prajā gṛhṇāti pra jātā janayanti
<6.1.4>     tāsāṃ parigṛhītānāmaśvataryatyakrāmat tasyā anuhāya reta ādattā tadvaḍavāyāṃ nyamāḍyasmādvaḍavā dviretāstasmādaśvataryaprajā āttaretā hi
<6.1.5>     tasmādvadakṣiṇīyāti hi sā yajñamaricyatātiriktasya dakṣiṇā syāt salomatvāya ṣoḍaśinaḥ stotredeyātirikto vai ṣoḍaśyatirikta evātiriktāṃ dadāti
<6.1.6>     so'kāmayata yajñaṃ sṛjeyeti sa mukhata eva trivṛtamasṛjata taṃ gāyatrī cchando'nyasṛjyatāgnirdevatā brāhmaṇo manuṣyo vasanta ṛtustasmāt trivṛt stomānāṃ mukhaṃ gāyatrī cchandasāmagnirdevatānāṃ brāhmaṇo manuṣyāṇāṃ vasanta ṛtūnāṃ tasmādbrāhmaṇo mukhena vīryaṃ karoti mukhato hi sṛṣṭaḥ
<6.1.7>     karoti mukhena vīryaṃ ya evaṃvveda
<6.1.8>     sa urasta eva bāhubhyāṃ pañcadaśamasṛjata taṃ triṣṭupchando'nvasṛjyatendro devatā rājanyo manuṣyo grīṣma ṛtustasmādrājanyasya pañcadaśa stomastriṣṭupchanda indro devatā grīṣma ṛtustasmādu bāhuvīryo bāhubhyāṃ hi sṛṣṭaḥ
<6.1.9>     karoti bāhubhyāṃ vīryaṃ ya evaṃvveda
<6.1.10>     sa madhyata eva prajananāt saptadaśamasṛjata taṃ jagatī cchando'nvasṛjyata viśve devā devatā vaiśyo manuṣyo varṣā ṛtustasmādvaiśyo'dyamāno na kṣīyate prajananāddhi sṛṣṭastasmādu bahupaśurvaiśvadevo  hi jāgato varṣā hyasyartustasmādbrāhmaṇasya ca rājanyasya cādyo'dharo hi sṛṣṭaḥ
<6.1.11>     sa patta eva pratiṣṭhāyā ekaviṃśamasṛjata tamanuṣṭupchando'nvasṛjyata na kā cana devatā śūdro manuṣyastasmācchūdra uta bahupaśurayajñiyo videvo hi na hi taṃ kā cana devatā'nvasṛjyata tasmāt pādāvanejyaṃ nāti vardhate patto hi sṛṣṭastasmādekaviṃśastomānāṃ pratiṣṭhā pratiṣṭhāyā hi sṛṣṭastasmādanuṣṭubhaṃ chandāṃsi nānu vyūhanti
<6.1.12>     pāpavasīyaso vidhṛtyai
<6.1.13>     vidhṛtiḥ pāpavasīyaso bhavati ya evaṃvveda

<6.2.1>     yo vai stomānupadeśanavato vedopadeśanavān bhavati
<6.2.2>     prāṇo vai trivṛdardhamāsaḥ pañcadaśaḥ saṃvvatsaraḥ saptadaśa āditya ekaviṃśa ete vai stomā upadeśanavanta upadeśanavān bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<6.2.3>     ime vai lokāstriṇavastriṇavasya vai brāhmaṇeneme lokāstriṣpunarnavā bhavanti
<6.2.4>     eṣu lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati ya evaṃvveda
<6.2.5>     devatā vāva trayastriṃśo'ṣṭau vasava ekādaśa rudrā dvādaśādityāḥ prajāpatiśca vaṣaṭkāraśca trayastriṃśau
<6.2.6>     sa devena yajñena yajate ya evaṃvveda
<6.2.7>     yo vā adhipatiṃ vedādhipatirbhavati trayastriṃśo vai stomānamadhipatiḥ puruṣaḥ paśumān
<6.2.8>     tasmānnyañco'nye paśavo'dantyūrdhvaḥ puruṣo'dhipatirhi saḥ
<6.2.9>     adhipatiḥ samānānāṃ bhavati ya evaṃvveda

<6.3.1>     eṣa vāva yajño yadagniṣṭomaḥ
<6.3.2>     ekasmā anyo yajñaḥ kāmāyāhriyate sarvebhyo'gniṣṭomaḥ
<6.3.3>     dvādaśa stotrāṇyagniṣṭomo dvādaśamāsāḥ saṃvvatsaraḥ saṃvvatsaraṃ paśavo'nu prajāyante tena paśavyaḥ samṛddhaḥ
<6.3.4>     dvādaśa stotrāṇi dvādaśa śastrāṇi taccaturviṃśatiścaturviṃśatirardhamāsāḥ saṃvvatsaraḥ saṃvvatsaraṃ paśavo'nuprajāyante tena paśavyaḥ samṛddhaḥ
<6.3.5>     agnau stotramagnau śastraṃ pratitiṣṭhati tena brahmavarcasasya
<6.3.6>     kiṃ jyotiṣṭomasya jyotiṣṭomatvamityāhurvirājaṃ saṃstutaḥ saṃpadyate virāḍvai cchandasāṃ jyotiḥ
<6.3.7>     jyotiḥ samānānāṃ bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<6.3.8>     jyeṣṭhayajño vā eṣa yadagniṣṭomaḥ
<6.3.9>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata tā asmai śraiṣṭhyāya nātiṣṭhanta sa etamagniṣṭomamapaśyat tamāharat tato'smai prajāḥ śraiṣṭhyāyātiṣṭhanta
<6.3.10>     tiṣṭhante'smai samānāḥ śraiṣṭhyāya ya evaṃvveda
<6.3.11>     yat tvityāhurgāyatraṃ prātaḥsavanaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ jāgataṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ kva tarhi turīyaṃ cchando'nuṣṭubiti
<6.3.12>     chandasāṃ vā anvavaluptiṃ yajamāno'nvavalupyate
<6.3.13>     aṣṭākṣarā gāyatrī hiṅkāro navama ekādaśākṣarā triṣṭubdvādaśākṣarā jagatī cchandobhirevānuṣṭubhamāpnoti yajamānasyānavalopāya
<6.3.14>     yo vā anuṣṭubhaṃ sarvatrāpiṃ savanānyanvāyattāṃ veda sarvatrāsyāpirbhavatyeṣā vā anuṣṭupsarvatrāpiḥ savanānyanvāyattā tadya evaṃ vveda sarvatrāpirbhavati
<6.3.15>     yadvai rājāno'dhvānaṃ dhāvayanti ye'śvānāṃvvīryavattamāstān yuñjate trivṛt pañcadaśa ekaviṃśa ete vai stomānāṃvvīryavattamāstāneva yuṅkte svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
<6.3.16>     catuṣṭomo bhavati pratiṣṭhā vai catuṣṭomaḥ pratiṣṭhityai

<6.4.1>     prajāpatirdevebhya ūrjaṃ vyabhajat tata udumbaraḥ samabhavat prājāpatyo vā udumbaraḥ prājāpatya udgātā yadudgātaudumbarīṃ prathamena karmaṇānvārabhate svayaiva taddevatayātmānamārtvijyāya vṛṇīte
<6.4.2>     tāmucchrayati dyutānastvā māruta ucchrayatūddivaṃ stabhānāntarikṣaṃ pṛṇa dṛṃha pṛthivīm
<6.4.3>     tāmanvārabhata āyoṣṭvā sadane sādayāmyavataśchāyāyāṃ samudrasya hṛdaya iti
<6.4.4>     yajño vā  āyustasya tat sadanaṃ kriyate
<6.4.5>     yajño vā avati tasya sā cchāyā kriyate
<6.4.6>     madhyato vā ātmano hṛdayaṃ tasmānmadhye sadasa audumbarī mīyate
<6.4.7>     namaḥ samudrāya namaḥ samudrasya cakṣuṣa ityāha vāgvai samudro manaḥ samudrasya cakṣustābhyāmeva tannamaskaroti
<6.4.8>     sāmā yūnarvāhāsīdityāha sāma vai yūnarvā sāmna eva tannamaskarotyārtvijyaṃ kariṣyan
<6.4.9>     yo vā evaṃ sāmne namaskṛtya sāmnārtvijyaṃ karoti na sāmno hīyate nainaṃ sāmāpahate
<6.4.10>     ya enamanuvyāharati sa ārtimārchati
<6.4.11>     ūrgasyūrjodā ūrjaṃ me dehyūrjaṃ me dhehyannaṃ me dehyannaṃ me dhehi prajāpatervā etadudaraṃ yat sada ūrgudumbaro yadaudumbarī madhye sadaso mīyate madhyata eva tat prajābhyo'nnamūrjaṃ dadhāti
<6.4.12>     tasmādyatraiṣā yātayāmā kriyate tat prajā aśanāyavo bhavanti
<6.4.13>     sāma devānāmannaṃ sāmanyeva taddevebhyo'nna ūrhaṃ dadhāti sa eva tadūrji śritaḥ prajābhya ūrjaṃ vibhajati
<6.4.14>     udaṅṅāsīna udgāyatyudīcīṃ tādṛśamūrjā bhājayati pratyaṅṅāsīnaḥ prastauti pratīcīṃ tādṛśamūrjā bhājayati dakṣiṇāsīnaḥ pratiharati dakṣiṇāṃ tādṛśamūrjā bhājayati prāñco'nya ṛtvija ārtvijyaṃ kurvanti tasmādeṣā diśāṃ vīryavattamaitāṃ hi bhūyiṣṭhāḥ prīṇanti
<6.4.15>     brahmavādino vadanti kasmāt satyāt prāñco'nya ṛtvija ārtvijyaṃ kurvantīti viparikramyodgātāra iti diśāmabhīṣṭyai diśāmabhiprītyā iti brūyāt tasmāt sarvāsu dikṣvannaṃvvidyate sarvā hyabhīṣṭāḥ prītāḥ

<6.5.1>     prajāpatirakāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti so'śocat tasya śocata ādityo mūrdhno'sṛjyata so'sya mūrdhānamudahan sa droṇakalaśo'bhavat tasmin devāḥ śukramagṛhṇata tāṃ vai sa āyuṣārtimatyajīvat
<6.5.2>     āyuṣārtimatijīvati ya evaṃvveda |
<6.5.3>     raṃ prohedvānaspatyo'si bārhaspatyo'si prājāpatyo'si prajāpatermūrdhāsyatyāyupātramasīdamahaṃ māṃ prāñcaṃ prohāmi tejase brahmavarcasāyeti
<6.5.4>     yadāha vānaspatya iti satyenaivainaṃ tat prohati
<6.5.5>     yadāha bārhaspatya iti bṛhaspatirvai devānāmudgātā tameva tadyunakti
<6.5.6>     yadāha prājāpatya iti prājāpatyo hyeṣa devatāyā yaddroṇakalaśo yadāha prajāpatermūrdheti prajāpaterhyeṣa mūrdhna udahanyata
<6.5.7>     yadāhātyāyupātramityati hyetadanyāni pātrāṇi yaddroṇakalaśo devapātraṃ droṇakalaśaḥ
<6.5.8>     devapātrī bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<6.5.9>     brāhmaṇaṃ pātre na mīmāṃseta yaṃ brāhmaṇamiva manyate pra devapātramāpnoti na manuṣyapātrācchidyate
<6.5.10>     vāgvai devebhyo'pakrāmat sāpaḥ prāviśat tāṃ devāḥ punarayācaṃstā abruvan yat punardadyāma kiṃ nastataḥ syāditi yat kāmayadhva ityabruvaṃstā abruvan yadevāsmāsu manuṣyā apūtaṃ praveśayāṃstenāsaṃsṛṣṭā asāmeti
<6.5.11>     śuddhā asmā āpaḥ pūtā bhavanti ya evaṃvveda
<6.5.12>     sā punartātyakrāmat sā vanaspatīn prāviśat tān devāḥ punarayācaṃstāṃ na punaradadustānaśapan svena vaḥ kiṣkuṇā vajreṇa vṛścāniti tasmādvanaspatīn svena kiṣkuṇā vajreṇa vṛścanti devaśaptā hi
<6.5.13>     tāṃvvanaspatayaścaturdhā vācaṃvvinyadadhurdundubhau vīṇāyāmakṣe tūṇave tasmādeṣā vadiṣṭhaiṣā valgutamā vāgvā vanaspatīnāṃ devānāṃ hyeṣā vāgāsīt
<6.5.14>     adho'dho'kṣaṃ droṇakalaśaṃ prohanti tasyā vāco'varudhyā uparyuparyakṣaṃ pavitraṃ prayacchantyubhayata eva vācaṃ parigṛhṇanti
<6.5.15>     yasya kāmayetāsuryamasya yajñaṃ kuryāṃvvācaṃvvṛñjīyeti droṇakalaśaṃ prohan bāhubhyāmakṣanupaspṛśedasuryamasya yajñaṃ karoti vācaṃvvṛṅkte yo'sya priyaḥ syādanupaspṛśannakṣaṃ prohet prāṇā vai droṇakalaśaḥ prāṇānevāsya kalpayati
<6.5.16>     yannvityāhurvācānyānṛtvijo vṛṇate kasmādudgātāro vṛtā ārtvijyaṃ kurvantīti
<6.5.17>     yaddroṇakalaśamupasīdanti tenodgātāro vṛtāḥ
<6.5.18>     prājāpatyā vā udgātāraḥ prājāpatyo droṇakalaśo droṇakalaśa evaināmārtvijyāya vṛṇīte
<6.5.19>     prāñca upasīdanti prāñco yajñasyāgre karavāmeti
<6.5.20>     anabhijitā vā eṣodgātṝṇāṃ digyat prācī yaddroṇakalaśaṃ prāñcaṃ prohanti diśo'bhijityai
<6.5.21>     yannvityāhurantarāśvaḥ prāsevau yujyate'ntarā śamya anaḍvān ka udgātṝṇāṃ yoga iti yaddroṇakalaśamupasīdanti sa eṣāṃ yogastasmādyuktairevopasadyaṃ na hyayukto vahati

<6.6.1>     grāvṇaḥ saṃsādya droṇakalaśamadhyūhanti viḍvai grāvāṇo'nnaṃ somo rāṣṭraṃ droṇakalaśo yadgrāvasu droṇakalaśamadhyūhanti viśyeva tadrāṣṭramadhyūhanti
<6.6.2>     yaṃ dviṣyādvimukhān grāvṇaḥ kṛtvedamahamamumamuṣyāyaṇamamuṣyāḥ putramamuṣyā viśo'muṣmādannādyānnirūhāmīti nirūhedviśa evainamannādyānnirūhati
<6.6.3>     yo'sya priyaḥ syāt saṃmukhān grāvṇaḥ kṛtvedamahamamuṣyāyaṇamamuṣyāḥ putramamuṣyāṃ viśyamuṣinnannādye'dhyūhāmītyadhyūhedviśyevainamannādye'dhyūhati
<6.6.4>     atho tadubhayamanādṛtyedamahaṃ māṃ tejasi brahmavarcasse'dhyūhāmītyadhyūhet tejasyeva brahmavarcasa ātmānamadhyūhati
<6.6.5>     yaḥ kāmayeta viśā rāṣṭraṃ hanyāmiti vyūhya grāvṇo'dho droṇakalaśaṃ sādayitvopāṃśusavanamupariṣṭādabhinidadhyādidamahamamuyā viśā'do rāṣṭraṃ hanmīti viśaiva tadrāṣṭraṃ hanti
<6.6.6>     yo vai daivāni pavitrāṇi veda pūto yajñiyo bhavati cchandāṃsi vai daivāni pavitrāṇi tairdroṇakalaśaṃ pāvayanti
<6.6.7>     vasavastvā gāyatreṇa cchandasā punantu rudrāstvā traiṣṭubhena cchandasā punantvādityāstvā jāgatena cchandasā punantvetāni vai daivāni pavitrāṇi pūto yajñiyo bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<6.6.8>     svarbhānurvā āsura ādityaṃ tamasāvidhyat taṃ devā na vyajānaṃste'trimupādhāvaṃstasyātrirbhāsena tamo'pāhan yat prathamamapāhan sā kṛṣṇāvirabhavadyaddvitīyaṃ sā rajatā yat tṛtīyaṃ sā lohinī yayā varṇamabhyatṛṇat sā śuklāsīt
<6.6.9>     tasmācchuklaṃ pavitraṃ śukraḥ somaḥ sa śukratvāya
<6.6.10>     yaṃ dviṣyāt tasyaiteṣāṃ varṇānāmapi pavitre kuryāt pāpmanaivainaṃ tamasā vidhyati kṛṣṇamiva hi tamo yo'sya priyaḥ syādāsaktiśuklaṃ kuryājjyotirvai hiraṇyaṃ jyotirevāsmin dadhāti
<6.6.11>     tasmādātreyaṃ candreṇecchantyatrirhi tasya jyotiḥ
<6.6.12>     abhyatṛṇat pavitraṃvvigṛhṇanti hastakāryameva tadyajñasya kriyata etadvā udgātṝṇāṃ hastakāryaṃ yat pavitrasya vigrahaṇam
<6.6.13>     na hastaveṣyānnirṛcchati ya evaṃvveda
<6.6.14>     yo'pi na vigṛhṇāti prāṇādenamantaryanti
<6.6.15>     taṃ brūyādvepamānaḥ prameṣyasa iti vepamāna eva pramīyate
<6.6.16>     pra śukraitu devī manīṣāsmadrathaḥ sutaṣṭo na vājītyudgātā dhārāmanumantrayate
<6.6.17>     āyuṣe me pavasva varcase me pavasva viduḥ pṛthivyā divo janitrācchṛṇvantvāpo'dhaḥ kṣarantīḥ somehodgāyetyāha mahyaṃ tejase brahmavarcasāyeti
<6.6.18>     eṣa vai somasyodgītho yat pavate somodgīthameva sāma gāyati
<6.6.19>     ācchinnaṃ pāvayanti yajñaṃ caiva prāṇāṃśca saṃtanvanti saṃtataṃ pāvayanti yajñasya saṃtatyai

<6.7.1>     bṛhaspatirvai devānāmudagāyat taṃ rakṣāṃsyajighāṃsan sa ya eṣāṃ lokānāmadhipatayastān bhāgadheyenopādhāvat
<6.7.2>     sūryo mā divābhyo nāṣṭrābhyaḥ pātu vāyurantarikṣābhyo'gniḥ pārthivābhyaḥ svāheti juhoti
<6.7.3>     ete vā eṣāṃ lokānāmadhipatayastān bhāgadheyenopāsarat
<6.7.4>     karoti vācā vīryaṃ na sadasyāmārtimārcchati ya evaṃvveda
<6.7.5>     vāgvai devebhyo'pākrāmat tāṃ devā anvamantrayanta sā'bravīdabhāgāsmi bhāgadheyaṃ me'stviti kaste bhāgadheyaṃ kuryādityudgātāra ityabravīdudgātāro vai vāce bhāgadheyaṃ kurvanti
<6.7.6>     tasyai juhuyādbekurānāmāsi juṣṭā devebhyo namo vāce namo vācaspataye devi vāgyat te vāco madhumat tasminmā dhāḥ sarasvatyai svāheti
<6.7.7>     vāhvai sarasvatī tāmeva tadbhāgadheyenārabhate
<6.7.8>     yaṃ dviṣyāt tasyaitāmāhutiṃ juhuyādvācaṃ manasā dhyāyedvācamevāsya vṛṅkte
<6.7.9>     bahiṣpavamānaṃ sarpanti svargameva tallokaṃ sarpanti
<6.7.10>     prakvāṇā iva sarpanti pratikūlamiva hītaḥ svargo lokaḥ tsaranta iva sarpanti mṛgadharmo vai yajño yajñasya śāntyā apratrāsāya
<6.7.11>     vācaṃ yacchanti yajñameva tadyacchanti yadvyavavadeyuryajñaṃ nirbrūyustasmānna vyavavadyam
<6.7.12>     pañcartvijaḥ saṃrabdhāḥ sarpanti pānkto yajño yāvān yajñastameva saṃtanvanti
<6.7.13>     yadi prastotāvacchidyate yajñasya śiraśchidyate brahmaṇe varaṃ dattvā sa eva punarvartavyaśchinnameva tat pratidadhāti
<6.7.14>     yadyudgātāvacchidyate yajñena yajamāno vyṛdhyate'dakṣiṇaḥ sa yajñakratuḥ saṃsthāpyo'thānya āhṛtyastasmin deyaṃ yāvaddāsyan syāt
<6.7.15>     yadi pratihartā'vacchidyate paśubhiryajamāno vyṛdhyate paśavo vai pratihartā sarvavedasaṃ deyaṃ yadi sarvavedasaṃ na dadāti sarvajyāniṃ jīyate
<6.7.16>     adhvaryuḥ prastaraṃ harati
<6.7.17>     yajamāno vai prastaro yajamānameva tat svargaṃllokaṃ harati
<6.7.18>     yajño vai devebhyo'śvo bhūtvāpākrāmat taṃ devāḥ prastareṇāsamayaṃstasmādaśvaḥ prastareṇa saṃmṛjyamāna upāvarabhate yadadhvaryuḥ prastaraṃ harati yajñasya śāntyā apratrāsāya
<6.7.19>     prajāpatiḥ paśunasṛjata te'smāt sṛṣṭā aśanāyanto'pākrāmaṃstebhyaḥ prastaramannaṃ prāyacchat enamupāvartanta tasmādadhvaryuṇā prastara īṣadiva vidhūyo vidhūtimiva hi tṛṇaṃ paśava upāvartante
<6.7.20>     upainaṃ paśava āvartante ya evaṃvveda
<6.7.21>     prastaramāsadyodgāyeddhaviṣo'skandāya
<6.7.22>     yajamānaṃ tu svargāllokā avagṛhṇāti
<6.7.23>     aṣṭhīvatopaspṛśatodgeyaṃ tenāsya haviraskannaṃ bhavati na yajamānaṃ svargāllokādavagṛhṇāti
<6.7.24>     cātvālamavekṣya bahiṣpavamānaṃ stuvantyatra vā asāvāditya āsīt ta devā bahiṣpavamānena svargaṃllokamaharan yaccātvālamavekṣya bahiṣpavamānaṃ stuvanti yajamānameva tat svargaṃllokaṃ haranti

<6.8.1>     sa tu vai yajñena yajetetyāhuryasya virājaṃ yajñamukhe dadhyuriti
<6.8.2>     navabhiḥ stuvanti hiṅkāro daśamo daśākṣarā virāḍvirājamevāsya yajñamukhe dadhāti
<6.8.3>     navabhiḥ stuvanti prāṇāḥ prāṇairevainaṃ samarddhayanti hiṅkāro daśamastasmānnābhiranavatṛṇṇā daśamī prāṇānām
<6.8.4>     navabhiḥ stuvanti navādhvaryuḥ prātaḥsavane grahān gṛhṇāti tāneva tat pāvayanti teṣāṃ prāṇānutsṛjanti
<6.8.5>     prajāpatirvai hiṅkārastrayo bahiṣpavamānyo yaddhiṅkṛtya prastauti mithunamevāsyā yajñamukhe dadhāti prajananāya
<6.8.6>     eṣa vai stomasya yogo yaddhiṅkāro yaddhiṅkṛtya prastauti yuktenaiva stomena prastauti
<6.8.7>     eṣa vai sāmnāṃ raso yaddhiṅkāro yaddhiṅkṛtya prastauti rasenaivaitā abhyudya prastauti
<6.8.8>     āraṇyebhyo vā etat paśubhyaḥ stuvanti yadbahiṣpavamānamekārūpābhiḥ stuvanti tasmādekarūpā āraṇyāḥ paśavaḥ
<6.8.9>     parācībhiḥ stuvanti tasmāt parāñcaḥ prajāyante parāñco vitiṣṭhante
<6.8.10>     apariśrite stuvanti tasmādaparigṛhītā āraṇyāḥ paśavaḥ
<6.8.11>     bahiḥ stuvantyantaranuśaṃsanti tasmādgrāmamāhṛtairbhuñjate
<6.8.12>     grāmebhyo vā etat paśubhyaḥ stuvanti yadājyairnānārūpaiḥ stuvanti tasmānnānārūpā grāmyāḥ paśavaḥ
<6.8.13>     punarabhyāvartaṃ stuvanti tasmāt pretvaryaḥ pretya punarāyanti
<6.8.14>     pariśrite stuvanti tasmāt parigṛhītā grāmyāḥ paśavaḥ
<6.8.15>     amuṣmai vā etallokāya stuvanti yadbahiṣpavamānaṃ sakṛddhiṅkṛtābhiḥ parācībhiḥ stuvanti sakṛddhīto'sau parāṅlokaḥ
<6.8.16>     asmai vā etallokāya stuvanti yadājyaiḥ punarabhyāvartaṃ stuvanti tasmādayaṃllokaḥ punaḥpunaḥ prajāyate
<6.8.17>     parāñco vā eteṣāṃ prāṇā bahvantītyāhurye parācībhirbahiṣpavamānībhiḥ stuvata ityāvatīmuttamāṃ gāyet prāṇānāṃ dhṛtyai
<6.8.18>     cyavante vā ete'smāllokādityāhurye parācībhirbahiṣpavamānībhiḥ stuvata iti rathantaravarṇamuttamāṃ gāyediyaṃ vai rathantaramasyāmeva pratitiṣṭhati

<6.9.1>     upāsmai gāyata nara iti grāmakāmāyapratipadaṃ kuryāt
<6.9.2>     naro vai devānāṃ grāmo grāmamevāsmā upākaḥ
<6.9.3>     upa vā annamannamevāsmā upākaḥ
<6.9.4>     upoṣu jātamapturamiti prajākāmāya pratipadaṃ kuryāt
<6.9.5>     upa vai prajā tāṃ jātamityevājījanat
<6.9.6>     sa naḥ pasvasya śaṃ gava iti pratipadaṃ kuryāt
<6.9.7>     yāṃ samāṃ mahādevaḥ paśūn hanyāt sa naḥ pavasva śaṃ gava iti catuṣpade bheṣajaṃ karoti
<6.9.8>     śaṃ janāyeti dvipade śamarvata ityekaśaphāya
<6.9.9>     viṣeṇa vai tāṃ samāmoṣadhayo'ktā bhavanti yāṃ samāṃ mahādevaḥ paśūn hanti yacchaṃ rājannoṣadhībhya ityāhauṣadhīrevāsmai svadayatyubhayyo'smai svaditāḥ pacyante'kṛṣṭapacyāśca kṛṣṭapacyāśca
<6.9.10>     pavasva vāco agriya iti pratipadaṃ kuryādyaṃ kāmayeta samānānāṃ śīreṣṭhaḥ syāditi
<6.9.11>     pavasva vāco agriya ityagramevainaṃ pariṇayati
<6.9.12>     śrīrvai vāco'graṃ śriyamevāsmin dadhāti
<6.9.13>     ete asṛgramindava iti bahubhyaḥ pratipadaṃ kuryāt
<6.9.14>     eta iti sarvānevainānṛddhyai bhūtyā abhivadati
<6.9.15>     eta iti vai prajāpatirdevānasṛjatāsṛgramiti manuṣyānindava iti pitṝṃstiraḥ pavitra iti grahānāśava iti stotraṃvviśvānīti śastramabhi saubhagetyanyāḥ prajāḥ
<6.9.16>     yadeta iti tasmādyāvanta evāgre devāstāvanta idānīm
<6.9.17>     sarvān vṛddhimārdhnuvaṃ sthiteva hyeṣā vyāhṛtiḥ
<6.9.18>     yadasṛgramiti tasmānmanuṣyāḥ śvaḥśvaḥ sṛjyante
<6.9.19>     yadindava itīndava iva hi pitaraḥ
<6.9.20>     mana iva
<6.9.21>     yāntāḥ prajāḥ sṛṣṭā ṛddhimārdhnuvaṃstāmṛdhnuvanti yeṣāmevaṃvvidvānetāṃ pratipadaṃ karoti
<6.9.22>     chandāṃsi vai somamāharaṃstaṃ gandharvo viśvāvasuḥ paryamuṣṇāt. tenāpaḥ prāviśat taṃ devatā anvaicchaṃstaṃ viṣṇurapsu paryapaśyat sa vyakāṅkṣadayaṃ nū3 nā3 iti taṃ padā prāsphurat tasmāt pṛthagindavo'sṛjyanta sa devatābhyo'bhitastiṣṭhantībhya ete asṛgramindava iti prābravīdbahiṣpavamānena vai yajñaḥ sṛjyate yadete asṛgramindava iti prastauti yajñameva tat sṛṣṭaṃ devatābhyaḥ prāha
<6.9.23>     vyṛddhaṃ vā etadapaśavyaṃ yat prātaḥsavanamaniḍaṃ hi yadiḍāmasmabhyaṃ saṃyatamityāha prātaḥsavanameva tadiḍāvat paśumat karoti
<6.9.24>     davidyūtatyā ruceti vrātāya pratipadaṃ kuryāt
<6.9.25>     davidyūtatyā receti vai gāyatryā rūpaṃ pariṣṭobhantyeti triṣṭubhaḥ kṛpetyanuṣṭubhaḥ somāḥ śukrā gavāśira iti jagatyāḥ sarveṣāṃ vā eṣā cchandasāṃ rūpaṃ cchandāṃsīva khalu vai vratopadeṣā pratipadbhavati svenaivaināṃstadrūpeṇa samardhayati
<6.9.26>     vṛddhā vā eta indriyeṇa vīryeṇa yadvrāta indriyaṃ vīryaṃ chandāṃsīndriyeṇaivainān vīryeṇa samardhayati

<6.10.1>     agna āyūṃṣi pavasa iti pratipadaṃ kuryādyeṣāṃ dīkṣitānāṃ pramīyate
<6.10.2>     apūtā iva vā ete yeṣāṃ dīkṣitānāṃ pramīyate yadyeṣāgnipāvamānī pratipadbhavatyagnirevainānniṣṭapati pavamānaḥ punāti
<6.10.3>     yadāyūṃṣītyāha ya eva jīvanti teṣvāyurdadhāti
<6.10.4>     ā no mitrāvaruṇeti jyogāmayāvine pratipadaṃ kuryāt
<6.10.5>     apakrāntau vā etasya prāṇāpānau yasya jyogāmayati prāṇāpānau mitrāvaruṇau prāṇāpānāvevāsmin dadhāti
<6.10.6>     apaghnan pavate mṛdho'pa somo arāvṇa ityanṛtamabhiśasyamānāya pratipadaṃ kuryāt
<6.10.7>     arāvāṇo vā ete ye'nṛtamabhiśaṃsanti tānevāsmādapahanti
<6.10.8>     gacchannindrasya niṣkṛtamiti pūtamevainaṃ yajñiyamindrasya niṣkṛtaṃ gamayati
<6.10.9>     vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti rājanyāya pratipadaṃ kuryādvṛṣā vai rājanyo vṛṣāṇamevainaṃ karoti
<6.10.10>     marutvate ca matsara iti maruto vai devānāṃ viśo viśamevāsmā anu niyunaktyanapakrāmukāsmādviḍbhavati
<6.10.11>     viśvā dadhāna ojasetyojasaivāsmai vīryeṇa viśaṃ purastāt parigṛhṇātyanapakrāmukāsmādviḍbhavati
<6.10.12>     pavasvendo vṛṣāsuta iti pratipadaṃ kuryādyaḥ kāmayeta jane ma ṛdhyeteti
<6.10.13>     kṛdhī no yaśaso jana iti janatāyāmevāsmā ṛdhyate
<6.10.14>     yuvaṃ hi sthaḥ svaḥ patī iti dvābhyāṃ pratipadaṃ kuryāt samāvadbhājāvevainau yajñasya karotyubhau yajñayaśasenārpayati
<6.10.15>     prāsya dhārā akṣaranniti vṛṣṭikāmāya pratipadaṃ kuryāt
<6.10.16>     prāsya dhārā akṣaranniti divo vṛṣṭiṃ cyāvayati vṛṣṇaḥ sutasyaujasa ityantarikṣāt
<6.10.17>     devāṃ anu prabhūṣata ityasmin loke pratiṣṭhāpayati
<6.10.18>     ojasā vā etadvīryeṇa pradīyate yadaprattaṃ bhavati yadvṛṣṇaḥ sutasyaujasa ityāhaujasaivāsmai vīryeṇa divo vṛṣṭiṃ prayacchati
<6.10.19>     tayā pavasva dhārayā yayā gāva ihāgamañjanyāsa upno gṛhamiti pratipadaṃ kuryādyaḥ kāmayetopa mā janyā gāvo nameyurvindeta me janyā gā rāṣṭramiti yadeṣā pratipadbhavatyupainaṃ janyā gāvo namanti vindate'sya janyā gā rāṣṭram

<7.1.1>     ime vai lokā gāyatraṃ tryāvṛdgeyaṃ trayo hīme lokā yat tryāvṛdgāyatyebhirevainaṃ lokaiḥ saṃmimīte
<7.1.2>     dviravanardeddhiṅkuryāt tṛtīyam
<7.1.3>     yat triravanardatyati tadgāyatraṃ recayati
<7.1.4>     yo vai gāyatreṇāpratihṛtenodgāyatyapratiṣṭhāno bhavati hiṅkāro vai gāyatrasya pratihāraḥ
<7.1.5>     sa manasā dhyeyaḥ pratihṛtena gāyatreṇodgāyati pratitiṣṭhati
<7.1.6>     yo vā ebhyo lokebhyo gāyatraṃ gāyati naibhyo lokebhya āvṛścyata ima enaṃ lokā ūrjjābhisaṃvasate
<7.1.7>     mandramivāgra ādadītātha tārataramatha tāratamaṃ tadebhyo lokebhyo'gāsīt
<7.1.8>     aniruktaṃ geyametadvai gāyatrasya krūraṃ yanniruktaṃ yadaniruktaṃ gāyati krūramevāsya parivṛṇakti
<7.1.9>     prāṇo gāyatraṃ na vyavānyāt prāṇasyāvicchedāya yadi vyavāniti pramāyuko bhavati yadi na vyavāniti sarvamāyureti
<7.1.10>     yadi vyavānyānmadhya ṛco vyavānyāt prāṇo vai gāyatraṃ prāṇaḥ svaraḥ prāṇameva tanmadhyata ātman dadhāti sa sarvamāyureti
<7.1.11>     iḍāṃ paśukāmāya nidhanaṃ kuryāt svaḥ svargakāmāya yaśo brahmavarcasakāmāyāyurāmayāvine haṃsītyabhicarate
<7.1.12>     ete vai gāyatrasya dohāḥ
<7.1.13>     brahmavarcasasī paśumān bhavati ya evaṃvveda

<7.2.1>     prajāpatirdevebhya ātmānaṃ yajñaṃ kṛtvā prāyacchat te'nyo'nyasmā agrāya nātiṣṭhanta tānabravīdājimasminniteti ta ājimāyan yadājimāyaṃstadājyānāmājyatvam
<7.2.2>     sa indro'vedagnirvā idamagra ujjyeṣyatīti so'bravīdyataro nāvidamagra ujjayat tannau saheti so'gniragra udajayadatha mitrāvaruṇāvathendro'thaiṣaikā hotrānujjitāsīt sa indro'gnimabravīdyat sahāvocāvahī yannau taditi saiṣaindrāgnyadhyardhamagne stotramadhyardhamindrasya
<7.2.3>     catvāri santi ṣaḍdevatyāni
<7.2.4>     ṣaḍdhā vihito yajño yāvān yajñastamevārabhate
<7.2.5>     sarvāṇi svārāṇyājyāni tajjāmi nānādevatyaiḥ stuvantyajāmitāyai
<7.2.6>     grāmyebhyo vā etat paśubhyaḥ stuvanti yadājyaiḥ punarabhyāvartaṃ stuvanti tasmāt parāñcaḥ prājyante pratyañcaḥ prajāyante tasmādu pretya punarāyanti

<7.3.1>     etāvatā vāva mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ pupuve
<7.3.2>     tribhiśca cchandobhiḥ pañcabhiśca sāmabhiḥ
<7.3.3>     yanmādhyandinena pavamānena stuvanti mādhyandinameva tat savanaṃ pāvayanti
<7.3.4>     etāvanti vāva sarvāṇi sāmāni yāvanti mādhyandine pavamāne
<7.3.5>     gāyatraṃ nidhanavadanidhanamaiḍam
<7.3.6>     yanmādhyandinena pavamānena stuvanti sarvaireva tat sāmabhiḥ stuvanti
<7.3.7>     ātmā vai yajñasya pavamāno mukhaṃ gāyatrī prāṇo gāyatraṃ yadgāyatryāṃ gāyatreṇa stuvanti mukhata eva tat prāṇān dadhati
<7.3.8>     prāṇāpānā vā etāni cchandāṃsi prāṇo gāyatrī vyāno bṛhatyapānastriṣṭubyadetaiśchandobhiḥ stuvanti prāṇāpānānāmavicchedāya
<7.3.9>     ime vai lokā etāni cchandāṃsyayameva gāyatryayaṃ madhyamo bṛhatyasāvuttamastriṣṭubyadetaśchandobhiḥ saṃhitaiḥ stuvantyeṣāṃ lokānāmavicchedāya
<7.3.10>     yadanyacchando'ntarā vyaveyādimāṃllokān vicchindyāt
