<7.3.11>     gāyatreṇa stutvā nidhanavatā stuvantīyaṃ vai gāyatryasyāmeva tadāyatanaṃ kriyate
<7.3.12>     yadanidhanenāgre stuyuranāyatano yajamānaḥ syāt
<7.3.13>     nidhanavatā stuvanti vīryaṃ vai gāyatrī vīryaṃ nidhanaṃ vīryeṇaiva tadvīryaṃ samardhayati
<7.3.14>     aiḍena bṛhatīmārabhante
<7.3.15>     paśavo vā iḍā paśavo bṛhatī paśuṣveva tat paśūn dadhāti
<7.3.16>     bṛhatyāṃ bhūyiṣṭhāni sāmāni bhavanti
<7.3.17>     tatrāpi triṇidhanam
<7.3.18>     abaliṣṭha iva vā ayaṃ madhyami lokastasyaiva tadāyatanaṃ kriyate
<7.3.19>     triṇidhanaṃ bhavati trīṇi savanānāṃ chidrāṇi tāni tenāpidhīyante
<7.3.20>     triṇidhanaṃ bhavati traya ime lokā eṣveva lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<7.3.21>     triṇidhanaṃ bhavatyetena vai mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ yat triṇidhanaṃ yat triṇidhanaṃ na syādapratiṣṭhitaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ syāt
<7.3.22>     dvyakṣarāṇi nidhanāni bhavanti dvipādyajamāno yajamānameva yajñe paśuṣu pratiṣthāpayati
<7.3.23>     anidhanamantato bhavati svargasya lokasyānatipādāya
<7.3.24>     yannidhanavat syādyajamānaṃ svargāllokānnirhanyāt
<7.3.25>     svāraṃ bhavati
<7.3.26>     svareṇa vai devebhyo'ntato'nnādyaṃ pradīyate svareṇaiva taddevebhyo'ntato'nnādyaṃ prayacchati
<7.3.27>     gāyatraṃ purastādbhavati svāramantataḥ
<7.3.28>     prāṇo vai gāyatraṃ prāṇaḥ svaraḥ prāṇānevatadubhayato dadhāti tasmādubhayataḥ prāṇāḥ paśavaḥ
<7.3.29>     yadgāyatraṃ purastādbhavati svāramantataḥ prāṇaireva praityapāne pratitiṣṭhati

<7.4.1>     etadvai yajñasya svargyaṃ yanmādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ mādhyandinasya pavamānaḥ pavamānasya bṛhatī yadbṛhatyāḥ stotre dakṣiṇā dīyante svargasyaiva tallokasyāyatane dīyante
<7.4.2>     devā vai cchandāṃsyabruvan yuṣmābhiḥ svargyaṃllokamayāmeti te gāyatrīṃ prāyuñjata tayā na vyāpnuvaṃstriṣṭubhaṃ prāyuñjata tayā na vyāpnuvañjagatīṃ prāyuñjata tayā na vyāpnuvannanuṣṭubhaṃ prāyuñjata tayālpakādi na vyāpnuvaṃsta āsāṃ diśāṃ rasān prabṛhya catvāryakṣarāṇyupādadhuḥ sā bṛhatyabhavat tayemāṃllokān vyāpnuvan
<7.4.3>     bṛhatī maryā yayemāṃllokān vyāpāmeti tadbṛhatyā bṛhattvam
<7.4.4>     paśūn vā asyāntānupādadhuḥ paśavo vai bṛhatī yadbṛhatyāḥ stotre dakṣiṇā dīyante sva eva tadāyatane dīyante
<7.4.5>     yannvityāhuranyāni cchandāṃsi varṣīyāṃsi kasmādbṛhatyucyata eṣā hīmāṃllokān vyāpnonnānyacchandaḥ kiṃ cana yāni sapta caturuttarāṇi cchandāṃsi tāni bṛhatīmabhisaṃpadyante tasmādbṛhatyucyate
<7.4.6>     yannvityāhurgāyatraṃ prātaḥsavanaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ jāgataṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ kasmādbṛhatyā madhyandine stuvantīti
<7.4.7>     bahiṣpavamānena vai devā ādityaṃ svargaṃllokamaharan sa nādhriyata taṃ bṛhatyā madhyandine'stabhnuvaṃstasmādbṛhatyā madhyandinaṃ stuvantyādityaṃ hyeṣā madhyandine dādhāra
<7.4.8>     yairu kaiśca cchandobhirmadhyandine stuvanti tāni triṣṭubhamabhi saṃpadyante tasmāt triṣṭubho nayanti mādhyandināt savanāt

<7.5.1>     prajāpatirakāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti sa śocannamahīyamāno'tiṣṭhat sa etadāmahīyavamapaśyat tenemāḥ prajā asṛjata tāḥ sṛṣṭā amahīyanta yadamahīyanta tasmādāmahīyavam
<7.5.2>     tā asmāt sṛṣṭā apākrāmaṃstāsāṃ divi sadbhūmyādada iti prāṇānādatta tā enaṃ prāṇeṣvātteṣu punarupāvartanta tābhya ugraṃ śarma mahi śrava iti punaḥ prāṇān prāyacchat tā asmādudevāyodhaṃstāsāṃ stauṣa iti manyūnavāśṛṇāt tato vai tasmai tāḥ śraiṣṭhyāyātiṣṭhanta
<7.5.3>     tiṣṭhante'smai samānāḥ śraiṣṭhyāya ya evaṃvveda
<7.5.4>     prajānāṃ ca vā eṣā sṛṣṭiḥ pāpavasīyaśca vidhṛtiryadāmahīyavam
<7.5.5>     vidhṛtiḥ pāpavasīyaso bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<7.5.6>     devā vai yaśaskāmāḥ sattramāsatāgnirindro vāyurmakhaste'bruvsn yanno yaśa ṛcchāt tannaḥ sahāsaditi teṣā makhaṃ yaśa ārcchat tadādāyāpākrāmat tadasya prāsahāditsanta taṃ paryayatanta svadhanuḥ pratiṣṭhābhyātiṣṭhat tasya dhanurārtnirūrdhvā patitvā śiro'cchinat sa pravargyo'bhavadyajño vai makho yat pravargyaṃ pravṛñjanti yajñasyaiva tacchiraḥ pratidadhati
<7.5.7>     taddevā yaśo vyabhajanta tasyāgnī rauravaṃ prābṛhata
<7.5.8>     tadvai sa paśuvīryaṃ prābṛhata paśavo vai rauravam
<7.5.9>     paśumān bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<7.5.10>     agnirvai rūrastasyaitadrauravam
<7.5.11>     asurā vai devān paryayatanta tata etāvagnī rūrau viṣvañcau stobhāvapaśyat tābhyāmenān pratyauṣat te pratyuṣyamāṇā aravanta yadaravanta tasmādrauravam
<7.5.12>     athendro yaudhājayaṃ prābṛhata tadvai sa vajraṃ prābṛhata vajro vai yaudhājayam
<7.5.13>     vajraṃ bhrātṛvyāya praharati ya evaṃvveda
<7.5.14>     indro vai yudhājit tasyaitadyaudhājayam
<7.5.15>     yudhā maryā ajaiṣmeti tasmādyaudhājayam
<7.5.16>     atha vāyurauśanaṃ prābṛhata
<7.5.17>      tadvai sa prāṇvīryaṃ prābṛhata prāṇā vā auśanam
<7.5.18>     sarvamāyureti ya evaṃvveda
<7.5.19>     vāyurvā uśanaṃstasyaitadauśanam
<7.5.20>     uśanā vai kāvyo'surāṇāṃ purohita āsīt taṃ devāḥ kāmadughābhirapāmantrayanta tasmā etānyauśanāni prāyacchan kāmadughā vā auśanāni
<7.5.21>     kāmadughā enamupatiṣṭhante ya evaṃvveda

<7.6.1>     prajāpatirakāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti sa tūṣṇīṃ manasādhyāyat tasya yanmanasyāsīt tadbṛhat samabhavat
<7.6.2>     sa ādadhīta garbho vai me'yamantarhitastaṃvvācā prajanayā iti
<7.6.3>     sa vācaṃ vyasṛjata sā vāgrathantaramanvapadyata
<7.6.4>     rathamaryāḥ kṣeplātārīditi tadrathantarasya rathantaratvam
<7.6.5>     tato bṛhadanu prājāyata bṛhanmaryā idaṃ sa jyogantarabhūditi tadbṛhato bṛhattvam
<7.6.6>     yathā vai putro jyeṣṭha evaṃ bṛhat prajāpateḥ
<7.6.7>     jyeṣṭhabrāhmaṇaṃ vā etat
<7.6.8>     pra jyaiṣṭhyamāpnoti ya evaṃvveda
<7.6.9>     yannvityāhurbṛhat pūrvaṃ prajāpatau samabhavat kasmādrathantaraṃ pūrvaṃ yogamānaśa iti
<7.6.10>     bṛhadeva pūrvaṃ samabhavadrathantaraṃ tu pūrvaṃ sṛṣṭyāsṛjata tasmāt pūrvaṃ yogamānaśe
<7.6.11>     tayoḥ samānaṃ nidhanamāsīt tasminnātiṣṭhetāṃ ta ājimaitāṃ tayorhasiti bṛhat prāṇamudajayadasiti rathantaramapānamabhisamaveṣṭata
<7.6.12>     prāṇāpānau vai bṛhadrathantare jyogāmayāvina ubhe kuryādapakrāntau vā etasya prāṇāpānau yasya jyogāmayati prāṇāpānāvevāsmin dadhāti
<7.6.13>     yannvityāhurubhe bṛhadrathantare bahirṇidhane kasmādbṛhadbahirṇidhanāni bhajate'ntarṇidhanāni rathantaramiti
<7.6.14>     prāṇo bṛhat tasmādbahirṇidhanāni bhajate bahirhi prāṇo'pāno rathantaraṃ tasmādantarṇidhanāni bhajate'ntarhyapānaḥ
<7.6.15>     mahāvṛkṣau vai bṛhadrathantare nidhanena samarpye
<7.6.16>     yadvai mahāvṛkṣau samṛcchete bahu tatra vibhagnaṃ prabhagnaṃ śete
<7.6.17>     airaṃ vai bṛhadaiḍaṃ rathantaraṃ mano vai bṛhadvāgrathantaraṃ sāma vai bṛhadṛgrathantaraṃ prāṇo vai bṛhadapāno rathantaramasau vai loko bṛhadayaṃ rathantarametāni manasānvīkṣyodgāyet kḷptābhyāmevābhyāmudgāyati

<7.7.1>     paśavo vai bṛhadrathantare aṣṭākṣareṇa prathamāyā (?) ṛcaḥ prastautyaṣṭāśaphāṃstat paśūnavarundhe
<7.7.2>     dvyakṣareṇottarayorṛcoḥ prastauti dvipādyajamāno yajamānameva yajñe paśuṣu pratiṣṭhāpayati
<7.7.3>     pañcākṣareṇa rathantarasya pratiharati pāṅktāṃstat paśūnavarundhe
<7.7.4>     caturakṣareṇa bṛhataḥ pratiharati catuṣpadastat paśūnavarundhe
<7.7.5>     na vai bṛhanna rathantaramekaṃ chando'yacchat tataḥ kakubhāvuttare upādadhustasmādbṛhatī prathamā kakubhāvuttare tasmādbṛhadrathantare ekarcena kurvanti na hi te ekaṃ chando'yacchat
<7.7.6>     nava bṛhato rohān rohati nava prāṇāḥ prāṇānevāvarundhe
<7.7.7>     trīn prathamāyāṃ rohati bhūtaṃ bhavadbhaviṣyat tānevāvarundhe trīnmadhyamāyāṃ rohatyātmānaṃ prajāṃ paś+ūṃstānevāvarundhe trīnuttamāyāṃ rohati traya ime lokā eṣveva lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<7.7.8>     sarvān kāmānavarundhe ya evaṃvvidvān bṛhato rohān rohati
<7.7.9>     vajreṇa vā etat prastotodgātāramabhipravartayati yadrathantaraṃ prastauti samudramantardhāyodgāyedvāgityādeyaṃvvāgvai samudraḥ samudramevāntardadhātyahiṃsāyai
<7.7.10>     balavadgeyaṃ vajramevaṃ pravṛttaṃ pratyudgṛhṇāti
<7.7.11>     balvalā kurvatā geyamabhilobhayateva vajramevābhilobhayati
<7.7.12>     kṣipraṃ geyaṃ svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
<7.7.13>     devaratho vai rathantaramakṣareṇākṣareṇa pratiṣṭhāpayatodgeyamareṇāreṇa hi rathaḥ pratitiṣṭhati
<7.7.14>     yo vai devarathamananvālabhyātiṣṭhatyavāsmāt padyata iyaṃ vai devaratha imāmālabhyodgāyennāsmādavapadyate
<7.7.15>     īśvaraṃ vai rathantaramudgātuścakṣuḥ pramathitoḥ prastuyamāne saṃmīlet svardṛśaṃ prativīkṣeta nainaṃ cakṣurjahāti
<7.7.16>     prajananaṃ vai rathantaraṃ yat tasthuṣa ityāhāsthāyukodgāturvāgbhavatyapi prajananaṃ hantyasthuṣa iti vaktavyaṃ susthuṣa iti vā sthāyukodgāturvāgbhavati na prajananamapi hanti
<7.7.17>     pṛṣṭhāni vā asṛjyanta tairdevāḥ svargaṃllokamāyaṃsteṣāṃ rathantaraṃ mahimnā nāśaknodutpatat
<7.7.18>     tasya vaśiṣṭho mahimno vinidhāya tena stutvā svargaṃllokamait tān saṃbhṛtyodgāyet
<7.7.19>     yaste goṣu mahimā yaste apsu rathe vā te stanayitnau ya u te yaste agnau mahimā tena saṃbhava rathantara draviṇavanna edhi

<7.8.1>     apo vā ṛtvyamārcchat tāsāṃvvāyuḥ pṛṣṭhe vyavartata tato vasu vāmaṃ samabhavat tasminmitrāvaruṇau paryapaśyatāṃ tāvabrūtāṃvvāmaṃ maryā idaṃ deveṣvājānīti tasmādvāmadevyam
<7.8.2>     tat parigṛhṇantāvabrūtāmidamavidāvedaṃ nau mābhyartiḍhvamiti tat prajāpatirabravīnmadvā etaddhyajani mama vā etaditi tadagnirabravīnmāṃ (?) vā etadannamajani mama vā etaditi tadindro'bravīcchreṣṭhasthā vā etadahaṃ vaḥ śreṣṭho'smi mama vā etaditi tadviśve devā abruvannasmaddevatyaṃ vā etadyadadbhyo'dhi samabhūdasmākaṃ vā etaditi tat prajāpatirabravīt sarveṣāṃ na idamastu sarva idamupajīvāmeti tat pṛṣṭheṣu nyadadhuḥ sarvadevatyaṃ vai vāmadevyam
<7.8.3>     yat kavatīṣu tena prājāpatyaṃ ko hi prajāpatiryadaniruktāsu tena prājāpatyamanirukto hi prajāpatiḥ
<7.8.4>     yadgāyatrīṣu tenāgneyaṃ gāyatracchandā hyagniḥ
<7.8.5>     yat pṛṣṭheṣu nyadadadhustenaindraṃ sarvāṇi hi pṛṣṭhānīndrasya niṣkevalyāni
<7.8.6>     yanmaitrāvaruṇo'nuśaṃsati tena maitrāvaruṇam
<7.8.7>     yadbahudevatyamuttamaṃ padaṃ tena vaiśvadevaṃ sarveṣveva rūpeṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<7.8.8>     prajāpatirvā etāṃ gāyatrīṃ yonimapaśyat sa ādīdhītāsmādyoneḥ pṛṣṭhāni sṛjā iti
<7.8.9>     sa rathantaramasṛjata tadrathasya ghoṣo'nvasṛjyata
<7.8.10>     sa bṛhadasṛjata tat stanayitnorghoṣo'nvasṛjyata sa vairūpamasṛjata tadvā tasya ghoṣo'nvasṛjyata
<7.8.11>     sa vairūpamasṛjata tadagnerghoṣo'nvasṛjyata
<7.8.12>     sa śakvarīrasṛjata tadapāṃ ghoṣo'nvasṛjyata
<7.8.13>     sa revatīrasṛjata tadgavāṃ ghoṣo'nvasṛjyata
<7.8.14>     etairvā etāni saha ghoṣairasṛjyanta
<7.8.15>     sarve'smin ghoṣāḥ sarvāḥ puṇyā vāco vadanti ya evaṃvveda

<7.9.1>     pitā vai vāmadevyaṃ putrāḥ pṛṣṭhāni
<7.9.2>     etasmādvā etāni yonerasṛjyanta
<7.9.3>     tasmāt pṛṣṭhānāṃ stotraṃ vāmadevyenānuṣṭuvanti śāntyai
<7.9.4>     yaddhi putro'śāntaṃ carati pitā tacchamayati
<7.9.5>     ayaṃ vai loko madhyamo vāmadevyametasmādvā imau lokau viṣvañcāvasṛjyetāṃ bṛhacca ratantaraṃ ca
<7.9.6>     yadrathantareṇa stuvanti ye rathantarāḥ paśavo'ntarīkṣaṃ ta upaśrayanti yadbṛhatā stuvanti ye bārhatāḥ paśavo'ntarīkṣaṃ ta upaśrayanti te vāmadevyasya stotreṇāvaruddhāḥ
<7.9.7>     dhruva āsīno vāmadevyenodgāyet paśūnāmupavṛtyai
<7.9.8>     upainaṃ paśava āvartante ya evaṃvveda
<7.9.9>     antarikṣaṃ vai vāmadevyamadhūnvatevodgeyamadhūtamivāntarikṣaṃ paśavo vai vāmadevyamahiṃsatevodgeyaṃ paśūnāmahiṃsāyai
<7.9.10>     kathamiva vāmadevyaṃ geyamityāhuḥ
<7.9.11>     yathāṅkulī putrān saṃdaśyāsaṃbhindantī harati yathā vāto'psu śanairvāti
<7.9.12>     svadhūrvāmadevyaṃ geyam
<7.9.13>     yo vai svadhūrvāmadevyaṃ gāyati svadhūrbhavati
<7.9.14>     yātyasyānyo niyānena nānyasya niyānena yāti
<7.9.15>     na bṛhato na rathantarasyānurūpaṃ geyaṃ svenaivāyatanena geyamāyatanavān bhavati
<7.9.16>     devā vai paśūn vyabhajanta te rudramantarāyaṃstān vāmadevyasya stotra upekṣate
<7.9.17>     aniruktaṃ geyam
<7.9.18>     yannirāha rudrāya paśūnapidadhāti rudrastāṃ samāṃ paśūn dhātuko bhavati
<7.9.19>     revatīṣu vāmadevyena paśukāmaḥ stuvīta
<7.9.20>     āpo vai revatyaḥ paśavo vāmadevyamadbhya evāsmai paśūn prajanayati
<7.9.21>     anavartiḥ paśuto bhavati prajā svasya mīliteva bhavati
<7.9.22>     kavatībhyo hyeti prajāpateḥ

<7.10.1>     imau vai lokau sahāstāṃ tau viyantāvabrūtāṃvvivāhaṃvvivahāvahai saha nāvastviti
<7.10.2>     tayorayamamuṣmai śyaitaṃ prāyacchannaudhasamasāvasmai
<7.10.3>     tata enayornidhane viparyakrāmatāṃ devavivāho vai śyaitanaudhase
<7.10.4>     pravasīyāṃsaṃ vivāhamāpnoti ya evaṃvveda
<7.10.5>     ito vā ime lokā ūrdhvāḥ kalpamānā yantyamuto'rvāñcaḥ kalpamānā āyanti
<7.10.6>     yadrathantareṇa stuvantīmaṃllokaṃ tena yunaktyantarikṣaṃvvāmadevyena naudhasenāmuṃ yadbṛhatā stuvantyamuṃllokaṃ tena yanaktyantarikṣaṃvvāmadevyena śyaitenemam
<7.10.7>     kḷptānimāllokānupāste ya evaṃvveda
<7.10.8>     bṛhadrathantare vai śaitanaudhase yadrathantarāya naudhasaṃ prati prayuñjanti bṛhadevāsmai tat prati prayuñjanti bṛhaddhyetat paro'kṣaṃ yannaudhasaṃ yadbṛhate śyaitaṃ prati prayuñjanti rathantaramevāsmai tat prati prayuñjanti rathantaraṃ hyetat paro'kṣaṃ yacchyaitam
<7.10.9>     ubhabhyāṃ bṛhadrathantarābhyāṃ stute ya evaṃvveda
<7.10.10>     devā vai brahma vyabhajanta tāṃ nodhāḥ kākṣīvata āgacchat te'bruvannṛṣirna āgaṃstasmai brahma dadāmeti tasmā etat sāma prāyacchaṃstasmānnaudhasaṃ brahma vai naudhasam
<7.10.11>     brahmavarcasakāma etena stuvīta brahmavarcasī bhavati
<7.10.12>     athaitacchyaitam
<7.10.13>     prajāpatiḥ paśūnasṛjata te'smāt sṛṣṭā apākrāmaṃstānetena sāmnābhivyāharat te'smā atiṣṭhanta te śetyā abhavan yacchetyā abhavaṃstasmācchyaitaṃ paśavo vai śyaitam
<7.10.14>     paśukāma etena stuvīta paśumān bhavati
<7.10.15>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata tāḥ sṛṣṭā aśocaṃstāḥ śyaitena huṃmā ityabhyajighrat tato vai tāḥ samaidhanta samedhante tāṃ samāṃ prajā yatraivaṃ vidvāñchyaitenodgāyati
<7.10.16>     eṣa vai yajamānasya prajāpatiryadudgātā yacchyaitena hiṅkaroti prajāpatireva bhūtvā prajā abhijihgrati
<7.10.17>     vasunidhanaṃ bhavati paśavo vai vasu paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhati

<8.1.1>     āṣkāraṇidhanaṃ kāṇvaṃvvaṣaṭkāraṇidhanamabhicaraṇīyasya brahmasāma kuryādabhinidhanaṃ mādhyandine pavamāne
<8.1.2>     deveṣurvā eṣā yadvaṣatkāro'bhīti vā indro vṛtrāya vajraṃ prāharadabhītyevāsmai vajraṃ prahṛtya deveṣvā vaṣaṭkāreṇa vidhyati
<8.1.3>     traikakubhaṃ paśukāmāya brahmasāma kuryāt tvamaṅga praśaṃsiṣa ityetāsu
<8.1.4>     indro yatīn sālāvṛkebhyaḥ prāyacchat teṣāṃ traya udaśiṣyanta rāyovājo bṛhadgiriḥ pṛthuraśmiste'bruvan ko naḥ putrān bhariṣyantītyahamintīndro'bravīt tāṃstrikakubadhinidhāyācarat sa etat sāmāpaśyadyat trikakubapaśyat tasmāt traikakubham
<8.1.5>     sa ātmānameva punarupādhāvat tvamaṅga praśaṃsiṣo devaḥ śaviṣṭha martyaṃ na tvadanyo maghavannasti ca marḍitendra bravīmi te vaca iti sa etena ca pragāthenaitena sāmnā sahasraṃ paśūnasṛjata tānebhyaḥ prāyacchat te pratyatiṣṭhan
<8.1.6>     yaḥ paśukāmaḥ syādyahpratiṣṭhākāma etasmin pragātha etena sāmnā stuvīta pra sahasraṃ paśūnāpnoti pratitiṣṭhati
<8.1.7>     trivīryaṃ vā etat sāma trīndriyamaindrya ṛca aindraṃ sāmaindreti nidhanamindriya eva vīrye pratitiṣṭhati
<8.1.8>     traiśokaṃ jyogāmayāvine brahmasāma kuryāt
<8.1.9>     ime vai lokāḥ sahāsaṃste'śocantaṃsteṣāmindra etena sāmnā śucamapāhan yat trayāṇāṃ śocatāmapāhaṃstasmāt traiśokam
<8.1.10>     yāsmādapāhan sā puṃścalīṃ prāviśadyāmantarikṣāt sā klībaṃ yāmamuṣmāt sainasvinam
<8.1.11>     tasmāt teṣāṃ nāśaitavyā ya eṣāmāśāmeti tasmā eva śuco'pabhajate
<8.1.12>     śucā vā eṣa viddho yasya jyogāmayati yat traiśokaṃ brahmasāma bhavati śucamevāsmādapahanti
<8.1.13>     diveti nidhanamupayanti vyuṣṭirvai divā vyevāsmai vāsayati

<8.2.1>     āṣkāraṇidhanaṃ kāṇvaṃ pratiṣṭhākāmāya brahmasāma kuryāt
<8.2.2>     kaṇvo vā etat sāmarte nidhanamapaśyat sa na pratyatiṣṭhat sa vṛṣadaṃśasyāṣiti kṣuvata upāśṛṇot sa tadeva nidhanamapaśyat tato vai sa pratyatiṣṭhadyadetat sāma bhavati pratiṣṭhityai
<8.2.3>     vasiṣṭhasya janitraṃ prajākāmāya brahmasāma kuryāt
<8.2.4>     vasiṣṭho vā etat putrahataḥ sāmāpaśyat sa prajayā paśubhiḥ prājāyata yadetat sāma bhavati prajātyai
<8.2.5>     ātharvaṇaṃ lokakāmāya brahmasāma kuryāt
<8.2.6>     atharvāṇo vā etallokākāmāḥ sāmāpaśyaṃstenāmartyaṃllokamapaśyan yadetat sāma bhavati svargasya lokasya prajātyai
<8.2.7>     abhīvartaṃ bhrātṛvyavate brahmasāma kuryāt
<8.2.8>     abhīvartena vai devā asurānabhyavartanta yadabhīvarto brahmasāma bhavati bhrātṛvyasyābhivṛtyai
<8.2.9>     śrāyantīyaṃ yajñavibhraṣṭāya brahmasāma kuryāt
<8.2.10>     prajāpatiruṣasamadhyait svāṃ duhitaraṃ tasya retaḥ parāpatat tadasyāṃ nyaṣicyata tadaśrīṇādidaṃ me mā duṣaditi tat sadakarot paśūneva
<8.2.11>     yacchrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma bhavati śrīṇāti caivenaṃ sacca karoti

<8.3.1>     devāśca vā asurāścaiṣu lokeṣvaspardhanta te devāḥ prajāpatimupādhāvaṃstebhya etat sāma prāyacchadetenainān kālayiṣyaddhamiti tenainānebhyo lokebhyo'kālayanta yadakālayanta tasmāt kāleyam
<8.3.2>     ebhyo vai lokebhyo bhrātṛvyaṃ kālayate ya evaṃvveda
<8.3.3>     stomo vai deveṣu taro nāmāsīdyajño'sureṣu vidadvasuste devāstarobhirvo vidadvasumiti stomena yajñamasurāṇāmāvṛñjata
<8.3.4>     stomena yajñaṃ bhrātṛvyasya vṛṅkte ya evaṃvveda
<8.3.5>     sāhdyā vai nāma devā āsaṃste'vacchidya tṛtīyasavanaṃ mādhyandinena savanena saha svargaṃllokamāyaṃstaddevāḥ lokeyena samatanvan yat kāleyaṃ bhavati tṛtīyasavanasya saṃtatyai
<8.3.6>     vidadvasu vai tṛtīyasavanaṃ yat tarobhirvo vidadvasumiti prastauti tṛtīyasavanameva tadabhyativadati
<8.3.7>     sarvāṇi vai rūpāṇi kāleyaṃ yat padaprastāvaṃ tena rāthantaraṃ yadbṛhato rohān rohati tena bārhataṃ yat stobhavān pratihārastena bārhataṃ yaddravadiḍaṃ tena rāthantaraṃ sarveṣveva rūpeṣu pratitiṣṭhati

<8.4.1>     sādhyā vai nāma devā āsaṃste sarveṇa yajñena saha svargaṃllokamāyaṃste devāśīchandāṃsyabruvan somamāharateti te jagatīṃ prāhiṇvan sā trīṇyakṣarāṇi hitvaikākṣarā bhūtvāgacchat triṣṭubhaṃ prāhiṇvan saikamakṣaraṃ hitvā tryakṣarā bhūtvāgacchadgāyatrīṃ prāhiṇvaṃścaturakṣarāṇi vai tarhi cchandāṃsyāsan sā tāni cākṣarāṇi harantyāgacchadaṣṭākṣarā bhūtvā trīṇi ca savanāni hastābhyāṃ dve savane dantairdaṃṣṭvā tṛtīyasavanaṃ tasmāddve aṃśumatī savane dhītaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ dantairhi taddaṃṣṭvā dhayantyaharat tasya ye hriyamāṇasyāṃśavaḥ parāpataṃste pūtīkā abhavan yāni puṣpāṇyavāśīyanta tānyarjjunāni yat prāprothat te praprothāstasmāt tṛtīyasavana āśiramavanayanti yameva taṃ gāvaḥ somamadanti tasya taṃ rasamavanayanti sasomatvāya
<8.4.2>     te triṣṭubjagatyai gāyatrīmabrūtāmupa tvāyāveti sābravīt kiṃ me tataḥ syāditi yat kāmayasa ityabrūtāṃ sābvravīnmama sarvaṃ prātaḥsavanamahamuttare savane praṇayānīti tasmādgāyatraṃ prātaḥsavanaṃ gāyatryuttare savane praṇayati
<8.4.3>     tān triṣṭuptribhirakṣarairupait saikādaśākṣarā bhūtvā prājāyata tāṃ jagatyekenākṣareṇopait sā dvādaśākṣarā bhūtvā prājāyata
<8.4.4>     tasmādāhurgāyatrī vāva sarvāṇi cchandāṃsi gāyatrī hyetān poṣān puṣyantyaiditi
<8.4.5>     indrastṛtīyasavanādbībhatsamāna udakrāmat taddevāḥ svādiṣṭhayeti asvadayanmadiṣṭhayeti madvadakurvan pavasva soma dhārayetyapāvayannindrāya pātave suta iti tato vai tadindra upāvartata yat svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti prastauti tṛtīyasavanasya sendratvāya
<8.4.6>     svādiṣṭhā vai deveṣu paśava āsanmadiṣṭhā asureṣu te devāḥ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti paśūnasurāṇāmavṛñjata
<8.4.7>     paśūn bhrātṛvyasya vṛṅkte ya evaṃvveda
<8.4.8>     tāsu saṃhitam
<8.4.9>     sādhyā vai nāmadevā āsaṃste'vacchidya tṛtīyasavanaṃ mādhyandinena savanena saha svargaṃllokamāyaṃstaddevāḥ saṃhitena samadadhuryat samadadhustasmāt saṃhitam
<8.4.10>     kāleyaṃ purastādbhavati saṃhitamupariṣṭādetābhyāṃ hi tṛtīyasavanaṃ saṃtāyate
<8.4.11>     sarvāṇi vai rūpāṇi saṃhitaṃ yat padaprastāvaṃ tena rāthantaraṃ yadbṛhato rohān rohati tena bārhataṃ yadpadanidhanaṃ tena rāthantaraṃ sarveṣveva rūpeṣu pratitiṣṭhati

<8.5.1>     uṣṇikkakubhāvete bhavataḥ
<8.5.2>     uṣṇikkakubbhyāṃ vā indro vṛtrāya vajraṃ prāharat kakubhi parākramatoṣṇihā prāharat tasmāt kakubho madhyamaṃ padaṃ bhūyiṣṭhākṣaraṃ parākramaṇaṃ hi tadabhi samauhat tasmāduṣṇiha uttamaṃ padaṃ bhūyiṣṭhākṣaraṃ puro gururiva hi vajraḥ
<8.5.3>     vajraṃ bhrātṛvyāya praharati ya evaṃvveda
<8.5.4>     nāsike vā ete yajñasya yaduṣṇikkakubhau tasmāt samānaṃ chandaḥ satī nānā yajñaṃvvahatastasmāt samānāyā nāsikāyāḥ satyā nānā prāṇāvuccarataḥ
<8.5.5>     prāṇā vā uṣṇikkakubhau tasmāt tābhyāṃ na vaṣaṭkurvanti yadvaṣaṭkuryuḥ prāṇānagnau pradadhyuḥ
<8.5.6>     tāsu saphaṃ viphamiva vai tṛtīyasavanaṃ tṛtīyasavanasya saphatvāyāthauṣkalametena vai prajāpatiḥ puṣkalān paśūnasṛjata teṣu rūpamadadhādyadetat sāma bhavati paśuṣveva rūpaṃ dadhāti
<8.5.7>     purojitī vo andhasa iti padyā cākṣaryā ca virājau bhavataḥ padyayā vai devāḥ svargaṃllokamāyannakṣaryayā ṛṣayo nu prājānān yadete padyā cākṣaryā ca virājau bhavataḥ svargasya lokasya prajñātyai
<8.5.8>     tāsu śyāvāśvam
<8.5.9>     śyāvāśvamārvanānasaṃ sattramāsīnaṃ dhanvodavahan sa etat sāmāpaśyat tena vṛṣṭimasṛjata tato vai sa pratyatiṣṭhat tato gātumavindata gātuvidvā etat sāma
<8.5.10>     vindate gātuṃ pratitiṣṭhatyetena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<8.5.11>     indrastṛtīyasavanādbībhatsamāna udakrāmat taṃ devāḥ śyāvāślvenaihāyi ehiyetyanvāhvayan sa upāvartata yadetat sāma bhavati tṛtīyasavanasya sendratvāya
<8.5.12>     athaitadāndhīgavamandhīgurvā etat paśukāmaḥ sāmāpaśyat tena sahasraṃ paśūnasṛjata yadetat sāma bhavati paśūnāṃ puṣṭyai madhye nidhanamaiḍaṃ bhavatyetena vai tṛtīyasavanaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ yanmadhye nidhanamaiḍaṃ na syādapratiṣṭhitaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ syāt
<8.5.13>     daśākṣaraṃ madhyato nidhanamupayanti daśākṣarā virāḍvirājyeva pratitiṣṭhati
<8.5.14>     abhi priyāṇi pavata iti kāvaṃ prājāpatyaṃ sāma
<8.5.15>     prajā vai priyāṇi paśavaḥ priyāṇi prajāyāmeva paśuṣu pratitiṣtśuṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<8.5.16>     raśmī vā etau yajñasya yadauśanakāve devakośo vā eṣa yajñamabhisamubjito yadete antato bhavato yajñasyāriṣṭyai

<8.6.1>     devā vai brahma vyabhajanta tasya yo raso'tyaricyata tadyajñāyajñīyamabhavat
<8.6.2>     brahmaṇo vā eṣa raso yadyajñāyajñīyaṃ yadyajñāyajñīyena stuvanti brahmaṇa eva rase yajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<8.6.3>     yonirvai yajñāyajñīyametasmādvai yoneḥ prajāpatiryajñamasṛjata tasmādyajñāyajñīyam
<8.6.4>     tasmādvā etena purā brāhmaṇā bahiṣpavamānamastoṣata yoneryajñaṃ pratanavāmahā iti yajñaṃ tataḥ stuvanti yonau yajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<8.6.5>     asureṣu vai sarvo yajña āsīt te devā yajñāyajñīyamapaśyaṃsteṣāṃ yajñāyajñā vo agnaya ityagnihotramavṛñjata girāgirā ca dakṣasa iti darśapūrṇamāsau prapra vayamamṛtaṃ jātavedasamiti cāturmāsyāni priyaṃ mitraṃ na śaṃsiṣamiti saumyamadhvaram
<8.6.6>     yajñā vo agnaye girā ca dakṣase pra vayamamṛtaṃ jātavedasaṃ priyaṃ mitraṃ na śaṃsiṣamiti vai tarhi cchandāṃsyāsaṃste devā abhyārambhamabhinivartyaṃ cchandobhiryajñamasurāṇāmavṛñjata
<8.6.7>     chandobhiryajñaṃ bhrātṛvyasya vṛṅkte ya evaṃvveda
<8.6.8>     etaddha sma vā āha kūśāmbaḥ svāyavo brahmā lātavyaḥ kaṃ svidadya śiśumārī yajñapathe'pyastā gariṣyati
<8.6.9>     eṣā vai śiśumārī yajñapathe'pyastā yajñāyajñīyaṃ yadgirāgiretyāhātmānaṃ tadudgātā girati
<8.6.10>     airaṃ kṛtvodgeyamirāyāṃ yajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayatyapramāyuka udgātā bhavati
<8.6.11>     vaiśvānare vā etadudgātātmānaṃ pradadhāti yat prapra vayamityāha praprīṃ vayamiti vaktavyaṃvvaiśvānarameva parikrāmati
<8.6.12>     yo vai nihnuvānaṃ cchanda upaiti pāpīyānujjagivān bhavatyetadvai nihnuvānaṃ cchando yanna śaṃsiṣamiti nu śaṃsiṣamiti vaktavyaṃ suśaṃsiṣamiti vā na nihnuvānaṃ chanda upaiti vasīyānujjagivān bhavati
<8.6.13>     yasya vai yajñā vāgantā bhavanti vācaśchidreṇa sravantyete vai yajñā vāgantā ye yajñāyajñīyāntā etadvācaśchidraṃ yadanṛtaṃ yadagniṣṭomayājyanṛtamāha tadanvasya yajñaḥ sravatyakṣareṇāntataḥ pratiṣṭhāpyamakṣareṇaiva yajñasya cchidramapi dadhāti
<8.6.14>     virājo vā etadrūpaṃ yadakṣaraṃ virājyevāntataḥ pratitiṣṭhati

<8.7.1>     ito vai prātarūrdhvāṇi cchandāṃsi yujyante'muto'vāñci yajñāyajñīyasya stotre yujyante yajñā vo agnaye girā ca dakṣasa iti dvādaśākṣaraṃ pra vayamamṛtaṃ jātavedasamityekādaśākṣaraṃ priyaṃ mitraṃnna śaṃsiṣamityaṣṭākṣaram
<8.7.2>     anuṣṭubhamuttamāṃ saṃpādayatīyaṃ vā anuṣṭubasyāmeva pratitiṣṭhati
<8.7.3>     vāgvā anuṣṭubvācyeva pratitiṣṭhati jyaiṣṭhyaṃ vā anuṣṭubjaiṣṭhya eva pratitiṣṭhati
<8.7.4>     kathamiva yajñāyajñīyaṃ geyamityāhuryathānaḍvān prasrāvayamāṇa itthamiva cetthamiva ceti
<8.7.5>     vaiśvānaraṃ vā etadudgātānu prasīdannetītyāhuryadyajñāyajñīyasyarcaṃ saṃpratyāheti parikrāmatevodgeyaṃvvaiśvānarameva parikrāmati
<8.7.6>     vaiśvānare vā etadadhvaryuḥ sadasyānabhisṛjati yadyajñāyajñīyasya stotramupāvartayati prāvṛtenodgeyaṃvvaiśvānareṇānabhidāhāya
<8.7.7>     na ha tu vai pitaraḥ prāvṛtaṃ jānanti yajñāyajñīyasya vai stotre pitaro yathāyathaṃ jijñāsanta ākarṇābhyāṃ prāvṛtyaṃ tadeva prāvṛtaṃ tadaprāvṛtaṃ jānanti pitaro na vaiśvānaro hinasti
<8.7.8>     apaḥ paścāt patnya upasṛjanti vaiśvānarameva tacchamayantyāpo hi śāntiḥ
<8.7.9>     atho reta eva tat siñcantyāpo hi retaḥ
<8.7.10>     dakṣiṇānūrūnabhiṣiñcanti dakṣiṇato hi retaḥ sicyate
<8.7.11>     mahadiva pratyūhyaṃ mana evāsya tajjanayanti
<8.7.12>     udgātrā patnīḥ saṃkhyāpayanti retodheyāya
<8.7.13>     hiṅkāraṃ prati saṃkhyāpayanti hiṅkṛtāddhi reto'dhīyata
<8.7.14>     ātṛtīyāyāḥ saṃkhyāpayanti trivaddhi retaḥ

<8.8.1>     devā vā agniṣṭomamabhijityokthāni nāśaknuvannabhijetuṃ te'gnimabruvaṃstvayā mukhenedaṃ cayāmeti so'bravīt kiṃ me tataḥ syāditi yat kāmayasa ityabruvan so'bravīnmaddevatyāsūkthāni praṇayāniti
<8.8.2>     tasmādāgneyīṣūkthāni praṇayanti
<8.8.3>     tasmādu gāyatrīṣu gāyatracchandā hyagniḥ
<8.8.4>     te'gniṃ mukhaṃ kṛtvā sākamaśvenābhyakrāman yat sākamaśvenābhyakrāmaṃstasmāt sākamaśvam
<8.8.5>     tasmāt sākamaśvenokthāni praṇayantyetena hi tānyagre'bhyajayan
<8.8.6>     sa indro'bravīt kaścāhaṃ cedamanvavaiṣyāva ityahaṃ ceti varuṇastaṃvvaruṇo'nvatiṣṭhadindra āharat tasmādaindrāvaruṇamanuśasyate
<8.8.7>     sa evābravīt kaścāhaṃ cedamanvavaiṣyāva ityahaṃ ceti bṛhaspatistaṃ bṛhaspatiranvatiṣṭadindra āharat tasmādaindrābārhaspatyamanuśasyate sa evābravīt kaścāhaṃ cedamanvavaiṣyāva ityahaṃ ceti viṣṇustaṃ viṣṇuranvatiṣṭadindra āharat tasmādaindrāvaiṣṇavamanuśasyate
<8.8.8>     paśūn vā ebhyastānāharat paśavo vā ukthāni paśukāma ukthena stuvīta paśumān bhavati
<8.8.9>     bṛhatā vā indro vṛtrāya vajraṃ prāharat tasya tejaḥ parāpatat tat saubharamabhavat
<8.8.10>     jāmi vā etadyajñe kriyata ityāhuryadrathantaraṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ rathantaraṃ saṃdhirnāntarā bṛhatā stuvantīti yat saubhareṇa stuvanti bṛhataiva tadantarā stuvanti bṛhato hyetat tejo yat saubharam
<8.8.11>     yadi bṛhat sāmātirātraḥ syāt saubharamukthānāṃ brahma sāma kāryaṃ bṛhadeva tat tejasā samardhayati
<8.8.12>     yadi rathantarasāmnā saubharaṃ kuryādajāmitāyai
<8.8.13>     devānāṃ vai svargaṃllokaṃ +yatāṃ diśo'vlīyanta tāḥ saubhareṇo ityudastaṃbhuvaṃstato vai tā adṛṃhanta tataḥ pratyatiṣṭhaṃstataḥ svargaṃllokaṃ prājānan yaḥ svargakāmaḥ syādyaḥ pratiṣṭhākāmaḥ saubhareṇa stuvīta pra svargaṃllokaṃ jānāti pratitṣṭhati
<8.8.14>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata tāḥ sṛṣṭā āśanāyaṃstābhyaḥ saubhareṇorgityannaṃ prāyacchat tato vai tāḥ samaidhanta
<8.8.15>     samedhante tāṃ samāṃ prajā yatraivaṃvvidvān saubhareṇodgāyati
<8.8.16>     tā abruvan subhṛtaṃ no'bhārṣīriti tasmāt saubharam
<8.8.17>     vṛṣṭiṃ vā abhyastāṃ prāyacchadannameva
<8.8.18>     yo vṛṣṭikāmaḥ syādyo'nnādyakāmo yaḥ svargakāmaḥ saubhareṇa stuvīta
<8.8.19>     hīṣiti vṛṣṭikāmāya nidhanaṃ kuryādūrgityannādyakāmāyo iti svargakāmāya
<8.8.20>     sarve vai kāmāḥ saubharaṃ sarveṣveva kāmeṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<8.8.21>     athaitannārmedham
<8.8.22>     nṛmedhasamāṅgirasaṃ sattramāsīnaṃ śvabhirabhyāhvayan so'gnimupādhāvat pāhi no agna ekayeti taṃvvaiśvānaraḥ paryudatiṣṭhat tato vai sa pratyatiṣṭhat tato gātumavindata
<8.8.23>     gātuvidvā etat sāma vindate gātuṃ pratitiṣṭhatyetena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<8.8.24>     naiva hyetadahno rūpaṃ na rātreryadukthānām
<8.8.25>      kakupprathamāthoṣṇigatha purauṣṇiganuṣṭuptenānuṣṭubho nayantyacchāvākasāmnaḥ

<8.9.1>     hārivarṇaṃ bhavati
<8.9.2>     asurā vā eṣu lokeṣvāsaṃstān devā hariśriyamityasmāllokāt prāṇudanta virājasītyantarikṣāddivodiva ityamuṣmāt
<8.9.3>     tadya evaṃ vedaibhyo lokebhyo bhrātṛvyaṃ praṇudyemāṃllokānabhyārohati
<8.9.4>     harivarṇo vā etat paśukāmaḥ sāmāpaśyat tena sahasraṃ paśūnasṛjata yadetat sāma bhavati paśūnāṃ puṣṭaiḥ
<8.9.5>     aṅgirasaḥ svargaṃllokaṃ yato rakṣāṃsyanvasacanta tānyetena harivarṇo'pāhanta yadetat sāma bhavati rakṣasāmapahatyai
<8.9.6>     pṛṣṭhāni vā asṛjyanta teṣāṃ yat tejo raso'tyaricyata taddevāḥ samabharaṃstadudvaṃśīyamabhavat
<8.9.7>     sarveṣāṃ vā etat pṛṣṭhānāṃ tejo yadudvaṃśīyaṃ tasmādvā etat purā sajātāya nākran pāpavasīyaso vidhṛtyai
<8.9.8>     eṣa hyeva pṛṣṭhaistuṣṭuvāno ya udvaṃśīyena stuvate
<8.9.9>     sarvāṇi vai rūpāṇyudvaṃśīyam
<8.9.10>     gāyanti tvā gāyatriṇa eti rathantarasya rūpameti hi rathantaram
<8.9.11>     ādirbṛhata ūrdhvamiva hi bṛhat
<8.9.12>     pariṣṭobho vairūpasya pariṣṭubhaṃ hi vairūpam
<8.9.13>     anutodo vairājasyānutunnaṃ hi vairājam
<8.9.14>     ardheḍā śakvarīṇāmatisvāro revatīnām
<8.9.15>     ardheḍayā vai devā asurānavahatyātisvāreṇa svargaṃllokamārohan
<8.9.16>     tadya evaṃvvedārdheḍayaiva bhrātṛvyamavahatyātisvāreṇa svargaṃllokamārohati
<8.9.17>     ardheḍayā vai pūrvaṃ yajñaṃ saṃsthāpayantyatisvāreṇottramārabhante
<8.9.18>     upainamuttaro yajño namati ya evaṃvveda
<8.9.19>     pāṅktaṃ vā etat sāma pāṅkto yajñaḥ pāṅktāḥ paśavo yajña eva paśuṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<8.9.20>     āṣṭādaṃṣṭre ṛddhikāmāya kuryāt
<8.9.21>     aṣṭādaṃṣṭro vairūpo'putro'prajā ajīryat sa imāṃllokān vicicchidvāṃ amanyata sa ete jarasi sāmanī apaśyat tayoraprayogādabibhet so'bravīdṛdhnavadyobhe sāmabhyāṃ stavatā iti
<8.9.22>     ṛṣervā etat prāśodbhūtaṃ yadaṣṭādaṃṣṭre bhavata ṛdhyā eva

<8.10.1>     gāyatrīṣu brahmavarcasakāmāyokthāni praṇayeyurgāyatryāṃ brahmasāmānuṣṭubhyacchāvākasāma saiṣā gāyatrī saṃpadyate
<8.10.2>     tejo brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī teja eva brahmavarcasamavarundhe
<8.10.3>     gāyatrīṣu paśukāmāyokthāni praṇayeyuruṣṇihi brahmasāmānuṣṭubhyacchāvākasāma saiṣoṣṇiksaṃpadyate
<8.10.4>     paśavo vā uṣṇikpaśūnevāvarundhe
<8.10.5>     gāyatrīṣu puruṣakāmāyokthāni praṇayeyuḥ kakubhi brahmasāmānuṣṭubhyacchāvākasāma saiṣā kakupsaṃpadyate
<8.10.6>     puruṣo vai kakuppuruṣānevāvarundhe
<8.10.7>     virāṭsvannādyakāmāyokthāni praṇayeyuruṣṇihi brahmasāmānuṣṭubhyacchāvākasāma saiṣā virāṭsaṃpadyate
<8.10.8>     annaṃ virāḍannādyamevāvarundhe
<8.10.9>     akṣarapaṅktiṣu jyaiṣṭhyakāmāyokthāni praṇayeyuruṣṇihi brahmasāmānuṣṭubhyacchāvākasāma saiṣānuṣṭupsaṃpadyate
<8.10.10>     jyaiṣṭhyaṃ vā anuṣṭubjyaiṣṭhyamavāvarundhe

<9.1.1>     devā vā ukthānyabhijitya rātriṃ nāśaknuvannabhijetuṃ te'surān rātriṃ tamaḥ praviṣṭāṃ nānuvyapaśyaṃsta etamanuṣṭupśirasaṃ pragāthmapaśyan virājaṃ jyotiḥ tān virājā jyotiṣānupaśyanto'nuṣṭubhā vajreṇa rātrernirāghnan
<9.1.2>     yadeṣo'nuṣṭupśirāḥ pragātho bhavati virāḍeva jyotiṣānupaśyannanuṣṭubhā vajreṇa rātrerbhrātṛvyaṃ nirhanti
<9.1.3>     tān samantaṃ paryāyaṃ prāṇudanta yat paryāyaṃ prāṇudanta tat paryāyāṇāṃ paryāyatvam
<9.1.4>     prathamāni padāni punarādīni bhavanti prathamasya paryāyasya
<9.1.5>     prathamairhi padaiḥ punarādāya prathamarātrāt prāṇudanta
<9.1.6>     pāntamā vo andhasa iti prastauti
<9.1.7>     aharvai pāntamandho rātrirahnaiva tadrātrimārabhante
<9.1.8>     tāsu vaitahavyam
<9.1.9>     vītahavyaḥ śrāyaso jyogniruddha etat sāmāpaśyat so'vagacchat pratyatiṣṭhadavagacchati pratitiṣṭhatyetena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<9.1.10>     tamiva vā ete praviśantiye rātrimupayanti yadeko nidhanaṃ rātrermukhe bhavati prajñātyai
<9.1.11>     yadā vai puruṣaḥ svamoka āgacchati sarvaṃ tarhi prajānāti sarvamasmai divā bhavati
<9.1.12>     te madhyamaṃ paryāyamaśrayanta teṣāmaurdhvasadmanena vācamavṛñjata
<9.1.13>     vācaṃ bhrātṛvyasya vṛṅkte ya evaṃvveda
<9.1.14>     triṇidhanaṃ bhavati
<9.1.15>     yathā vā ahno mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ triṇidhanāyatanamevameṣa rātrermadhyamaḥ paryāyastriṇidhanāyatanaḥ salokatvāya
<9.1.16>     madhyamāni padāni punarādīni bhavanti madhyamasya paryāyasya madhyamairhi padaiḥ punarādāyaṃ madhyamarātrāt prāṇudanta
<9.1.17>     ta uttamaṃ paryāyamaśrayanta teṣāṃ ghṛtaścyunnidhanena paśūnavṛñjata paśavo vai ghṛtaścyutaḥ
<9.1.18>     paśūn bhrātṛvyasya vṛṅkte ya evaṃvveda
<9.1.19>     uttamāni padāni punarādīni bhavantyuttamasya paryāyasyottamairhi padaiḥpunarādāyamuttamarātrāt prāṇudanta
<9.1.20>     tān sandhinābhipalāyanta
<9.1.21>     tānāśvinenāsaṃhāyyamagamayan
<9.1.22>     asaṃhāyyaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ gamayati ya evaṃvveda
<9.1.23>     eṣā vā agniṣṭomasya saṃmā yadrātriḥ
<9.1.24>     dvādaśa stotrāṇyagniṣṭomo dvādaśa strotrāṇi rātriḥ
<9.1.25>     eṣā vā ukthasya saṃmā yadrātriḥ
<9.1.26>     trīṇyukthāni tridevatyaḥ sandhiḥ
<9.1.27>     yathā vā ahna ukthānyevameṣa rātreḥ sandhirnānārūpāṇyahna ukthāni nānārūpā ete tṛcā bhavanti
<9.1.28>     rathantaraṃ pratiṣṭhākāmāya sandhiṃ kuryāt
<9.1.29>     iyaṃ vai rathantaramasyāmeva pratitiṣṭhati
<9.1.30>     bṛhat svargakāmāya sandhiṃ kuryāt
<9.1.31>     svargo loko bṛhat svarga eva loke pratitiṣṭhati
<9.1.32>     vāravantīyaṃ vā vāmadevyaṃ vā śruddhyaṃ vai teṣāmekaṃ paśukāmāya sandhiṃ kuryāt
<9.1.33>     paśavo vā etāni sāmāni paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhati
<9.1.34>     āśvinaṃ hotānuśaṃsati
<9.1.35>     prajāpatirvā etat sahasramasṛjata taddevebhyaḥ prāyacchat tasminna samarādhayaṃste sūryaṃ kāṣṭhāṃ kṛtvājimadhāvan
<9.1.36>     teṣāmaśvinau prathamāvadhāvatāṃ tāvanvavadan saha no'stviti tāvabrūtāṃ kiṃ tataḥ syāditi yat kāmayethe ityabruvaṃstāvabrūtāmasmaddevatyamidamukthamucyātā iti tasmādāśvinamucyate
<9.1.37>     sarvāḥ khalu devatāḥ śasyante
<9.1.38>     kṣipraṃ śasyamājimiva hyete dhāvantyā sūryasyodetoḥ śaṃset sūryaṃ hi kāṣṭhāmakurvata

<9.2.1>     pāntamā vo andhasa iti vaitahavyamanyakṣetraṃvā ete prayanti ye rātrimupayanti yadokonidhanaṃ rātrermukhe bhavatyokaso'pracyāvāya
<9.2.2>     pra va indrāya mādanamiti gaurīvitam
<9.2.3>     brahma yaddevā vyakurvata tato yadatyaricyata tadgaurīvitamabhavat
<9.2.4>     atiriktaṃ gaurīvitamatiriktametat stotraṃ yadrātriratirikta evātiriktaṃ dadhāti
<9.2.5>     vayamu tvā tadidarthā iti kāṇvam
<9.2.6>     etena vai kaṇva indrasya sāṃvidyamagacchadindrasyaivaitena sāṃvidyaṃ gacchati
<9.2.7>     indrāya madvane sutamiti śrautakakṣaṃ kṣatrasāma pra kṣatramevaitena bhavati
<9.2.8>     ayaṃ ta indra soma iti daivodāsam
<9.2.9>     agniṣṭomena vai devā imaṃllokamabhyajayannantarikṣamukthenātirātreṇāmuṃ. ta imaṃllokaṃ punarabhyakāmayanta ta ihetyasmiṃlloke pratyatiṣṭhan yadetat sāma bhavati pratiṣṭhityai
<9.2.10>     ūrdhvasadmanamapi śarvarīṣu prohanti
<9.2.11>     asurā vā eṣu lokeṣvāsaṃstān devā ūrdhvasadmanenaibhyo lokebhyaḥ prāṇudanta
<9.2.12>     tadya evaṃvvedaibhyo lokebhyo bhrātṛvyaṃ praṇudya sva āyatane sattramāste
<9.2.13>     ā tū na indra kṣumantamityākūpāram
<9.2.14>     akūpārāṅgirasasyāsīt tasyā yathā godhāyāstvageva tvagāsīt tāmetena triḥsāmnendraḥ pūtvā sūryatvacasamakarot tadvāva sā tarhyakāmayata yatkāmā etena sāmnā stuvate sa ebhyaḥ kāmaḥ samṛdhyate
<9.2.15>     abhi tvā vṛṣabhā sutaṃ ityārṣabhaṃ kṣatrasāma kṣatramevaitena bhavati
<9.2.16>     idaṃvvaso sutamandha iti gārametena vai gara indramaprīṇāt prīta evāsyaitenendro bhavati
<9.2.17>     idaṃ hyanvojaseti mādhucchandasaṃ prajāpatervā eṣā tanūrayātayāmnī prayujyate
<9.2.18>     ā tvetā niṣīdateti daivātitham
<9.2.19>     devātithiḥ saputro'śanāyaṃścarannaraṇya urvārūṇyavindat tānyetena sāmnopāsīdat tā asmai gāvaḥ pṛśnayo bhūtvodatiṣṭhan yadetat sāma bhavati paśūnāṃ puṣṭyai
<9.2.20>     yogeyoge tavastaramiti saumedhaṃ rātriṣāma rātrereva samṛdhyai
<9.2.21>     indra suteṣu someṣviti kautsam
<9.2.22>     kutsaśca luśaścendraṃ vyahvayetāṃ sa indraḥ kutsamupāvartata taṃ śatena vārdhrībhirāṇḍayorabadhnāt taṃ luśo'bhyavadat pramucyasva pari kutsādihāgahi kimu tvāvānāṇḍayorbaddha āsātā iti tāssaṃcchidya prādravat sa etat kutsaḥ sāmāpaśyat tenainamanvavadat sa upāvartata
<9.2.23>     yadetat sāma bhavati sendratvāya

<9.3.1>     yadi sattrāya dīkṣerannatha sāmyuttiṣṭhet somamapabhajya viśvajitātirātreṇa yajeta sarvavedasena sarvasmā eva dīkṣate sarvamāpnoti
<9.3.2>     yā iddakṣiṇā dadāti tābhiriti prayuṅkte
<9.3.3>     yadi paryāyairastutamabhivyucchet pancadaśabhirhotre stuyuḥ pañcabhiḥpañcabhiritarebhyaḥ
<9.3.4>     agne vivasvaduṣasa iti sandhinā stuyuḥ prāṇā vai trivṛt stomānāṃ pratiṣṭhā rathantaraṃ sāmnāṃ prāṇāṃscaivopayanti pratiṣṭhāṃ ca
<9.3.5>     ṣaṣṭiṃ ca trīṇi ca śatāni hotā śaṃsati
<9.3.6>     tāvatyaḥ saṃvatsarasya rātrayaḥ saṃvatsarasaṃmitābhireva tadṛgbhirāśīvinamāpnoti
<9.3.7>     yadarvākstuvanti tadastutaṃ yat samprati stuvanti tat stutaṃ yadatiṣṭuvanti tat suṣṭutam
<9.3.8>     yadyarvākstuyuryāvatībhirna stuyustāvatībhirvātiṣṭuyurbhūyo'kṣarābhirvā
<9.3.9>     yadyatiṣṭuyuryāvatībhiratiṣṭuyustāvatībhirvā na stuyuḥ kanīyo'kṣarābhirvā
<9.3.10>     yadyarvākstuyustrīḍamagniṣṭomasāma kāryaṃ nidhanamekeḍayā ye dve tābhyāmeva tat samaṃ kriyate
<9.3.11>     yadyatiṣṭuyuḥ svāramagniṣṭomasāma kāryamūnamiva vā etat sāmno yat svarastenaiva tat samaṃ kriyate

<9.4.1>     yadi somau saṃsutau syātāṃ mahati rātreḥ prātaranuvākamupākuryāt
<9.4.2>     pūrvo vācaṃ pūrvaśchandāṃsi pūrvo devatā vṛṅkte
<9.4.3>     vṛṣaṇvatīṃ pratipadaṃ kuryādindro vai vṛṣā prātaḥsavanādevaiṣāmindraṃvvṛṅkte
<9.4.4>     atho khalvāhuḥ savanamukhesavanamukhe kāryā savanamukhātsavanamukhādevaiṣāmindraṃvvṛṅkte
<9.4.5>     susamiddhe hotavyamagnirvai sarvā devatāḥ sarvā eva devatāḥ paśyañjuhoti
<9.4.6>     saṃveśāyopaveśāya gāyatryai cchandase'bhibhūtaye svāhā saṃveśāyopaveśāya triṣṭubhe cchandase'bhibhūtaye svāhā saṃveśāyopaveśāya jagatyai cchandase'bhibhūtaye svāheti juhoti
<9.4.7>     cchandāṃsi vā abhibhūtayastairevainānabhibhavatyubhe bṛhadrathantare kārye
<9.4.8>     yatra vā indrasya harī tadindraḥ. indrasya vai harī bṛhadrathantare yadubhe bṛhadrathantare bhavataḥ pūrva evendrasya harī ārabhante
<9.4.9>     tairaśravase kārye
<9.4.10>     turaśravasaśca vai pārāvatānāṃ ca somau saṃsutāvāstāṃ tata ete turaśravāḥ sāmanī apaśyat tābhyāmasmā indraḥ śalmalināṃ yamunāyā havyaṃ nirāvahat yat tairaśravase bhavato havyamevaiṣāṃvvṛṅkte
<9.4.11>     pūrve'bhiṣuṇuyuḥ
<9.4.12>     yā vai pūrvāḥ prasnānti tāḥ pūrvāstīrthaṃ jayanti pūrva evendramārabhante
<9.4.13>     vihavyaṃ śasyam
<9.4.14>     jamadagneśca vā ṛṣīṇāṃ ca somau saṃsutāvāstāṃ tata etajgamadagnirvihavyamapaśyat tamindra upāvartata yadvihavyaṃ hotā śaṃsatīndramevaiṣāṃvvṛṅkte
<9.4.15>     yadītaro'gniṣṭomaḥ syādukthaḥ kāryo yadyuktho'tirātro yo vai bhūyān yajñakratuḥ sa indrasya priyo bhūyasaivaiṣāṃ yajnakratunendraṃvvṛṅkte
<9.4.16>     atho khalvāhurduṣprāpa iva vai paraḥ panthā yamevāgre yajñakratumārabheta tasmānneyāditi
<9.4.17>     sajanīyaṃ śasyamagastyasya kayāśubhīyaṃ śasyam
<9.4.18>     asyā amuṣyā adyaśvānmithunādahorātrābhyāmevainānnirbhajati

<9.5.1>     yadi somamakrītamapahareyuranyaḥ kretavyaḥ
<9.5.2>     yadi krītaṃ yo'nyo'bhyāśaṃ syāt sa āhṛtyaḥ somavikrayaṇe tu kiṃ ciddadyāt
<9.5.3>     yadi somaṃ na vindeyuḥ pūtīkānabhiṣuṇuyuryadi na pūtīkānarjjunāni
<9.5.4>     gāyatrī somamāharat tasyā anu visṛjya somarakṣiḥ parṇamacchinat tasya yo'ṃśuḥ parāpatat sa pūtīko'bhavat tasmin devā ūtimavindannūtīko vā eṣa yat pūtīkānabhiṣuṇvantyūtimevāsmai vindanti
<9.5.5>     pratidhukca prātaḥ pūtīkāśca śṛtaṃ ca madhyandine pūtīkāśca dadhi cāparāhṇe pūtīkāśca
<9.5.6>     somapītho vā etasmādapakrāmatītyāhuryasya somamapaharantīti sa oṣadhīśca paśūṃśca praviśati tamoṣadhibhyaśca paśubhyaścāvarundhe
<9.5.7>     indro vṛtramahaṃstasya yo nastaḥ somaḥ samadhāvat tāni babhrutūlānyarjunāni yo vapāyā utkhinnāyāstāni lohitatūlāni yāni babhrutūlānyarjunāni tānyabhiṣuṇuyādetadvai brahmaṇo rūpaṃ sākṣādeva somamabhiṣuṇoti
<9.5.8>     śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma kāryaṃ sadevainaṃ karoti
<9.5.9>     yajñāyajñīyamanuṣṭubhi prohedvācaivainaṃ samardhayati vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma kāryamindriyasya vīryasya parigṛhītyai
<9.5.10>     pañca dakṣiṇā deyāḥ
<9.5.11>     pāṅkto yajño yāvān yajñastamevārabhate
<9.5.12>     avabhṛthādudetya punardīkṣeta
<9.5.13>     tatra taddadyādyaddāsyaṃ syāt

<9.6.1>     yadi kalaśo dīryeta vaṣaṭkāraṇidhanaṃ brahmasāma kuryāt
<9.6.2>     avaṣaṭkṛto vā etasya somaḥ parāsicyate yasya kalaśo dīryate yadvaṣaṭkāraṇīdhanaṃ brahmasāma bhavati vaṣaṭkṛta evāsya somo bhavati
<9.6.3>     vidhuṃ dadrāṇaṃ samane bahūnāmityetāsu kāryam
<9.6.4>     eṣa hi bahūnāṃ samane dīryate yat kalaśaḥ
<9.6.5>     tadāhurna vā ārtyārtiranūdyārtyā vā eṣa ārtimanuvadati yaḥ kalaśo dīrṇe dadrāṇavatīṣu karotīti
<9.6.6>     śrāyantīyameva kāryam
<9.6.7>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata sa dugdho riricāno'manyata sa etacchrāyantīyamapaśyat tenātmānaṃ samaśrīṇāt prajayā paśubhirindriyeṇa
<9.6.8>     dugdha iva eṣa riricāno yasya kalaśo dīryate yacchrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma bhavati punarevātmānaṃ saṃśrīṇāti prajayā paśubhirindriyeṇa
<9.6.9>     yadi śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma syādvaiṣṇavīṣvanuṣṭupsu vaṣaṭkāraṇidhanaṃ kuryāt
<9.6.10>     yadvai yajñasya sravati vācaṃ pratisravati vāganuṣṭupyajño viṣṇurvācaiva yajñasya cchidramapidadhāti
<9.6.11>     yadvai yajñasya sravatyantataḥ sravati vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma kāryaṃ yajñasyaiva cchidraṃ vārayate

<9.7.1>     yadi prātassavanāt somo'tiricyeta asti somo ayaṃ suta iti marutvatīṣu gāyatreṣu stuyuḥ
<9.7.2>     mādhyandinaṃ vā eṣa savanaṃ nikāmayamāno'bhyatiricyate yaḥ prātassavanādatiricyate tasmānmarutvatīṣu stuvanti marutvaddhi mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ tasmādu gāyatrīṣu gāyatraṃ hi prātassavanam
<9.7.3>     yasmāt stomādatiricyate sa eva stomaḥ kāryaḥ salomatvāya
<9.7.4>     aindrāvaiṣṇavaṃ hotānuśaṃsati
<9.7.5>     vīryaṃ vā indro yajño viṣṇurvīrya eva yajñe pratitiṣṭhati
<9.7.6>     yadi mādhyandināt savanādatiricyeta baṇmahāṃ asi sūryetyādityavatīṣu gaurīvitena stuyuḥ
<9.7.7>     tṛtīyasavanaṃ vā eṣa nikāmayamāno'bhyatiricyate yo mādhyandināt savanādatiricyate tasmādādityavatīṣu stuvantyādityaṃ hi tṛtīyasavanaṃ tasmādu bṛhatīṣu bārhataṃ hi mādhyandinaṃ savanam
<9.7.8>     yasmāt stomādatiricyeta sa eva stomaḥ kāryaḥ salomatvāyaindrāvaiṣṇavaṃ hotānuśaṃsati vīryaṃ vā indro yajño viṣṇurvīrya eva yajñe pratitiṣṭhati
<9.7.9>     yadi tṛtīyasavanādatiricyeta viṣṇoḥ śipiviṣṭavatīṣu gaurīvitena stuyuḥ
<9.7.10>     yajño vai viṣṇuśśipiviṣṭo yajña eva viṣṇau pratitiṣṭhatyatiriktaṃ gaurīvitamatirikta evātiriktaṃ dadhāti
<9.7.11>     etadanyat kuryurukthāni praṇayeyurukthāni vā eṣa nikāmayamāno'bhyatiricyate yo'gniṣṭomādatiricyate yadyukthyebhyo'tiricyetātirātraḥ kāryo rātriṃ vā eṣa nikāmayamāno'bhyatiricyate ya ukthyebhyo'tiricyate yadi rātreratiricyeta viṣṇoḥ śipiviṣṭavatīṣu bṛhatā stuyureṣu tu vā atiricyata ityāhuryo rātreratiricyata iti
<9.7.12>     amuṃ vā eṣa lokaṃ nikāmayamāno'bhyatiricyate yo rātreratiricyate bṛhatā stuvanti bṛhadamuṃ lokamāptumarhati tamevāpnoti

<9.8.1>     yadi dīkṣitānāṃ pramīyate dagdhvāsthīnyupanahya yo nediṣṭhī syāt taṃ dīkṣayitvā saha yajeran
<9.8.2>     etadanyat kuryurabhiṣutyānyat somamagṛhītvā grahān yāsau dakṣiṇā sraktistadvā stuyurmārjālīye vā
<9.8.3>     api vā etasya yajñe yo dīkṣitaḥ pramīyate tametena niravadayante
<9.8.4>     yāmena stuvanti yamalokamevainaṃ gamayanti
<9.8.5>     tisṛbhiḥ stuvanti tṛtīye hi loke pitaraḥ
<9.8.6>     parācībhiḥ stuvanti parāṅhīto'sau lokaḥ
<9.8.7>     sarparājñyā ṛgbhiḥ stuvanti
<9.8.8>     arbudaḥ sarpa etābhirmṛtāṃ tvacamapāhata mṛtāmevaitābhistvacamapaghnate
<9.8.9>     tā ṛco'nubruvantastrirmārjālīyaṃ pariyanti savyānūrūnāghnānāḥ
<9.8.10>     stutamanuśaṃsatyamuṣminnevainaṃ loke nidhnuvanti
<9.8.11>     yanti vā ete patha ityāhurye mṛtāya kurvantītyaindravāyavāgrān grahān gṛhṇate punaḥ panthānamapiyanti
<9.8.12>     agna āyūṃṣi pavasa iti pratipatkāryā ya eva jīvanti teṣvāyurdadhāti
<9.8.13>     saṃvatsare'sthīni yājayeyuḥ saṃvvatsaro vai sarvasya śāntiryat purā saṃvvatsarādyājayeyurvācamaruṣkṛtāṃ krūrāmṛccheyuḥ
<9.8.14>     vṛtaḥ pavamānāḥ syuḥ saptadaśamitarat sarvam
<9.8.15>     yat trivṛtaḥ pavamānā bhavanti prāṇā vai trivṛt prāṇānevopayanti yat saptadaśamitarat sarvaṃ prajāpatirvai saptadaśaḥ prajatimevopayanti
<9.8.16>     prāṇāpānairvā ete vyṛdhyanta ityāhurye mṛtāya kurvantīti maitrāvaruṇāgrān grahān gṛhṇate prāṇāpānau mitrāvaruṇau prāṇāpānaireva samṛdhyante

<9.9.1>     yasya kalaśa upadasyati kalaśamevāsyopadasyantaṃ prāṇo'nūpadasyati prāṇo hi somaḥ
<9.9.2>     tadāhuḥ payo'vanayediti
<9.9.3>     atho khalvāhurantarhitamiva vā etadyat payo hiraṇyamevāpo'bhyavanayeddhiraṇyamabhyunnayediti
<9.9.4>     prāṇā vā āpo'mṛtaṃ hiraṇyamamṛta evāsya prāṇān dadhāti sa sarvamāyureti
<9.9.5>     yasya nārāśaṃsa upavāyati nārāśaṃsamevāsyopa+vāyantaṃ prāṇo'nūpadasyati prāṇo hi somaḥ
<9.9.6>     yamadhvaryurantato grahaṃ gṛhṇīyāt tasyāptumavanayet
<9.9.7>     prāyaścityai vai graho gṛhyate prāyaścityevāsmai prāyaścittiṃ karoti
<9.9.8>     yadi pītāpītau somau saṃgaccheyātāmantaḥ paridhyaṅgārānnirvartya juhuyāt hutasya cāhutasya cāhutasya hutasya ca | pītāpītasya somasyendrāgnī pibataṃ sutaṃ svāheti saiva tasya prāyaścittiḥ
<9.9.9>     prajāpataye svāhetyabhakṣaṇīyasya juhuyāduttarārdhyapūrvārdhya uparavaḥ
<9.9.10>     indurindumavāgādityavavṛṣṭasya bhakṣayet
<9.9.11>     tasya ta indravindrapītasyendriyāvatassarvagaṇasya sarvagaṇa upahūta upahūtasya bhakṣayāmi
<9.9.12>     hiraṇyagarbhaḥ samavartatāgra ityājyenābhyupākṛtasya juhuyādagnīdhraṃ paretya bhūtānāṃ jātaḥ patireka āsīt sa dādhāra pṛthivīṃ dyāmutemāṃ tasmai ta indo haviṣā vidhema svāheti saiva tasya prāyaścittiḥ
<9.9.13>     yadi grāvāpi śīryate paśubhiryajamāno vyṛdhyate paśavo vai grāvāṇo dyutānasya mārutasya sāmnā stuyuḥ
<9.9.14>     mārutā vai grāvāṇaḥ svenaivaināṃstadrūpeṇa samardhayati
<9.9.15>     yadi somamabhidahedgrahānadhvaryuḥ spāśayeta stotrāṇyudgātā śastrāṇi hotātha yathāpūrvaṃ yajñena careyuḥ pañca dakṣiṇā deyāḥ pāṅkto yajño yāvān yajñastamārabhate'vabhṛthādudetya punardīkṣate tatra taddadyādyaddāsyaṃ syāt purā dvādaśyā dīkṣeta yaddvādaśīmatinayedantardhīyeta

<9.10.1>     yadi mahāvīro bhidyeta taṃ bhinnamabhimṛśedya ṛte cidabhiśriṣaḥ purā jatrubhya ātṛdaḥ | saṃdhātā saṃdhiṃ maghavā purūvasurniṣkartā vihrutaṃ punaḥ mā bhema niṣṭyā ivendra tvadaraṇā iva | vanāni na prajahitānyadrivo duroṣāso amanmahi | amanmahīdanāśavo'nugrābhaśca vṛtrahan | sakṛt sute mahatā śūra rādhasānu stomaṃ mademahīti mahāvīraṃ bhinnamabhimṛśet saiva tasya prāyaścittiḥ
<9.10.2>     asuryaṃ vā etasmādvarṇaṃ kṛtvā teja indriyaṃ vīryamannādyaṃ prajāḥ paśavo'pakrāmanti yasya yūpo virohati sa īśvaraḥ pāpīyān bhavitoḥ
<9.10.3>     tvāṣṭraṃ paśuṃ bahurūpamālabheta tvaṣṭā vai paśūnāṃ rūpāṇāṃ vikartā tameva tadupadhāvati sa enaṃ tejaseindriyeṇa vīryeṇānnādyena prajayā paśubhiḥ punassamardhayati saiva tasya prāyaścittiḥ

<10.1.1>     agninā pṛthivyauṣadhibhistenāyaṃ lokastrivṛdvāyunāntarikṣeṇa vayobhistenaiṣa lokāstrivṛdyo'yamantarādityena divā nakṣatraistenāsau lokastrivṛdetadeva trivṛta āyatanameṣāsya bandhutā
<10.1.2>     āyatanavān bandhumān bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<10.1.3>     tamu pratiṣṭhetyāhustrivṛddhyevaiṣu lokeṣu pratiṣṭhitaḥ
<10.1.4>     ardhamāsa eva pañcadaśasyāyatanameṣāsya bandhutā
<10.1.5>     āyatanavān bandhumān bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<10.1.6>     taṃ caujo balamityāhurardhamāsaśo hi prajāḥ paśava ojo balaṃ puṣyanti
<10.1.7>     saṃvatsara eva saptadaśasyāyatanaṃ dvādaśa māsāḥ pañcadartava etadeva saptadaśasyāyatanameṣāsya bandhutā
<10.1.8>     āyatanavān bandhumān bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<10.1.9>     tamu prajāpatirityāhuḥ saṃvatsaraṃ hi prajāḥ paśavo'nuprajāyante
<10.1.10>     āditya evaikaviṃsasyāyatanaṃ dvādaśa māsāḥ pañcavartastraya ime lokā asāvāditya ekaviṃśa etadevaikaviṃśasyāyatanameṣāsya bandhutā
<10.1.11>     āyatanavān bandhumān bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<10.1.12>     tamu devatalpa ityāhuḥ pra devatalpamāpnoti ya evaṃvveda
<10.1.13>     trivṛdeva triṇavasyāyatanameṣāsya bandhutā
<10.1.14>     āyatanavān bandhumān bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<10.1.15>     tamu puṣṭirityāhustrivṛddhyevaiṣa puṣṭaḥ
<10.1.16>     devatā eva trayastriṃśasyāyatanaṃ trayastriṃśaddevatāḥ prajāpatiścatustriṃśa etadeva trayastriṃśasyāyatanameṣāsya bandhutā
<10.1.17>     āyatanavān bandhumān bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<10.1.18>     tamu nāka ityāhurna hi prajāpatiḥ kasmai canākam
<10.1.19>     chandāṃsyeva chandomānāmāyatanameṣaiṣāṃ bandhutā
<10.1.20>     āyatanavān bandhumān bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<10.1.21>     tānu puṣṭirityāhuḥ paśavo hi chandomāḥ

<10.2.1>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata so'tāmyat tasmai vājjyotirudagṛhṇāt  so'bravīt ko me'yaṃ jyotirudagṛhṇāditi svaiva te vāgityabravīt tāmabravīdvirājaṃ tvā cchandasāṃ jyotiṣkṛtvā yajāntā iti
<10.2.2>     tasmādyo virājaṃ stomaṃ saṃpadyate taṃ jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭoma ityācakṣate virāḍḍhi chandasāṃ jyotiḥ
<10.2.3>     jyotiḥ samānānāṃ bhavati ya evaṃvveda
<10.2.4>     anuṣṭupca vai saptadaśaśca samabhavatāṃ sānuṣṭupcaturuttarāṇi chandāṃsyasṛjata ṣaḍuttarān stomān saptadaśastāvetānmadhyataḥ prājanayatām
<10.2.5>     trivṛcca triṇavaśca rāthantarau tāvajaścāśvaścānvasṛjyetāṃ tasmāt tau rāthantaraṃ prācīnaṃ pradhūnutaḥ
<10.2.6>     pañcadaśaścaikaviṃśaśca bārhatau tau gauścāviścānvasṛjyetāṃ tasmāt tau bārhataṃ prācīnaṃ bhāskurutaḥ
<10.2.7>     evaṃ vai vidvāṃsamāhurapi grāmyāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ vāca ājānāti

<10.3.1>     prajāpatirakāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti sa ātmannṛtvyamapaśyat tata ṛtvijo'sṛjata yadṛtvyādasṛjata tadṛtvijāmṛtviktvaṃ tairetaṃ dvādaśāhamupāsīdat so'rādhnot
<10.3.2>     pitā no'rātsīditi māsā upāsīdaṃste dīkṣayaivārādhnuvannupasatsu trayodaśamadīkṣayan so' nuvyamabhavat tasmādupasatsu didīkṣāṇo'nuvyaṃ bhavatyeva ca hi trayodaśaṃ māsaṃ cakṣate naiva ca
<10.3.3>     eko dīkṣetaiko hi prajāpatirarādhnoddvādaśa dīkṣeran dvādaśa hi māsā arādhnuvaṃścaturviṃśatirdīkṣeraṃścaturviṃśatirhyardhamāsā arādhnuvan
<10.3.4>     yadi pañcadaśo dīkṣeteme pañceme pañceme pañceme pañceme catvāro'sāveka iti nirdiśeyuryasmā arāddhikāmāḥ syustamevārāddhiranveti sarva itare rādhnuvanti
<10.3.5>     yo vai devānāṃ gṛhapatiṃ vedāśnute gārhapataṃ pra gārhapatamāpnoti
<10.3.6>     saṃvatsaro vai devānāṃ gṛhapatiḥ sa eva prajāpatistasya māsā eva saha dīkṣiṇaḥ
<10.3.7>     vindate saha dīkṣiṇo'śnute gārhapatyaṃ pra gārhapatyaṃ āpnoti ya evaṃvveda |
<10.3.8>     yo vai chandasāṃ svarājaṃ vedāśnute svārājyaṃ pra svārājyaṃ āpnoti bṛhatī vāva chandasāṃ svarāḍaśnute svārājyaṃ pra svārājyaṃ āpnoti ya evaṃvveda
<10.3.9>     tāmetāmannādyāya vyāvṛjyāsate yadetaṃ dvādaśāhaṃ dvādaśa dīkṣā svādaśopasado dvādaśa prasutaḥ ṣaṭriṃśadetā rātrayo bhavanti ṣaṭtriṃśadakṣarā bṛhatī
<10.3.10>     jāyate vāva dīkṣayā punīta upasadbhirdevalokameva sutyayāpyeti
<10.3.11>     etāvadvāva saṃvatsara indriyaṃ vīryaṃ yadetā rātrayo dvādaśa pūṛnamāsyo dvādaśaikaṣṭakā dvādaśāmāvāsyā yāvadeva saṃvatsara indriyaṃ vīryaṃ tadetenāptvāvarundhe dvādaśāhena
<10.3.12>     triṃśadakṣarā vā eṣā virāḍṣaḍṛtava ṛtuṣveva virājā pratitiṣṭhatyṛtubhirvirāji
<10.3.13>     dvātriṃśadakṣarā vā eṣānuṣṭubvāganuṣṭupcatuṣpādaḥ paśavo vācā paśūn dādhāra tasmādvācā siddhā vācāhūtā āyanti tasmādu nāma jānate

<10.4.1>     bhūtaṃ pūrvo'tirātro bhaviṣyaduttaraḥ pṛthivī pūrvo'tirātro dyauruttaro'gniḥ pūrvo'tirātra āditya uttaraḥ prāṇaḥ pūrvo'tirātra udāna uttaraḥ
<10.4.2>     cakṣuṣī atirātrau kanīnike agniṣṭomau yasmādantarā agniṣṭomāvatirātrābhyāṃ tasmādantare satyau kanīnike bhuṅktaḥ
<10.4.3>     saṃvatsarasya vā etau daṃṣṭrau yadatirātrau tayorna svaptavyaṃ saṃvatsarasya daṃṣṭrayorātmānaṃ nedapidadhānīti
<10.4.4>     tadāhuḥ ko'svaptumarhati yadvāva prāṇo +jāgāra tadeva jāgaritamiti
<10.4.5>     gāyatrīṃ vā etāṃ jyotiḥpakṣāmāsate yadetaṃ dvādaśāhamaṣṭau madhya ukthā agniṣṭomāvabhito māsā svargṃ lokametyājarasaṃ brahmādyamannamatti dīpyamānaḥ
<10.4.6>     triṣpurastādrathantaraṃ upayanti tryāvṛdvai vāksarvāmeva vācamavarudhya sarvamannādyaṃ dvādaśāhaṃ tanvate
<10.4.7>     jāmi vā etadyajñe kriyata ityāhuryat triṣpurastādrathantaramupayantīti saubharamukthānāṃ brahmasāma bhavati tenaiva tadajāmi
<10.4.8>     pratnavatyaḥ prāyaṇīyasyāhnaḥ pratipado bhavanti teno eva tadajāmi
<10.4.9>     trirvevopariṣṭādrathantaramupayanti trāvṛdvai vāksarvāmeva vācamavarudhya sarvamannādyaṃ dvādaśāhāduttiṣṭhanti

<10.5.1>     trayo vā ete trirātrā yadeṣa dvādaśāho gāytramukhaḥ prathamo gāyatramadhyo dvitīyo gāyatrottamastṛtīyaḥ
<10.5.2>     yasmādgāyatramukhaḥ prathamastasmādūrdhvo'gnirdīdāya yasmādgāyatramadhyo dvitīyastasmāt tiryaṅvāyuḥ pavate
<10.5.3>
<10.5.4>
<10.5.5>     tejasā vā ete prayanti tejo madhye dadhati tejo'bhyudyanti jyotiṣā vā ete prayanti jyotirmadhye dadhati jyotirabhyudyanti cakṣuṣā vā ete prayanti cakṣurmadhye dadhati cakṣurabhyudyanti prāṇena vā ete prayanti prāṇaṃ madhye dadhati prāṇamabhyudyanti ye gāyatryā prayanti gāyatrīṃ madhye dadhati gāyatrimabhyudyanti
<10.5.6>     tantraṃ vā etadvitāyate yadeṣa dvādaśāhastasyetair(?) mayūkhā yadgāyatryasaṃvyāthāya
<10.5.7>     girikṣidauccāmanyaveti hovācābhipratārī kākṣaseniḥ kathaṃ dvādaśāha iti yathārānnemiḥ paryetyevamenaṃ gāyatrī paryeti avisraṃsāya yathārā nābhau dhṛtā evamasyāṃ dvādaśāho dhṛtaḥ
<10.5.8>     anuṣṭubhaṃ vā etāmannādyāya vyāvṛjyāsate yadetaṃ dvādaśāham
<10.5.9>     aṣṭābhirvā akṣairanuṣṭupprathamaṃ dvādaśāhasyāharduyacchatyekādaśabhirdvitīyaṃ dvādaśābhistṛtīyam
<10.5.10>     akṣaraṃ tryakṣaramucchiṣyate tadevottaraṃ trirātramanuvidadhāti
<10.5.11>     chandāṃsyevāsyāstṛtīyaṃ trirātraṃ vahanti
<10.5.12>     tāṃ vā etāṃ pratīcīṃ tiraścīṃ parācīmāsate'nnādyāya tasmāt pratyañcaṃ tiryañcaṃ parāñcaṃ prajāḥ paśumupajīvanti
<10.5.13>     chandāṃsi vā anyo'nyasya lokamabhadhyāyan gāyatrī triṣṭubhastriṣṭubjagatyā jagatī gāyatryāstāni vyauhan yathālokaṃ tato vai tāni yaṃyaṃ kāmamakāmayanta tamasanvan
<10.5.14>     yatkāmo vyūḍhacchandasā dvadaśāhena yajate so'smai kāmaḥ samṛdhyate
<10.5.15>     oko vai devānāṃ dvādaśāho yatho vai manuṣyā imaṃ lokamāviṣṭā evaṃ devatā dvādaśāhamāviṣṭā devatā ha vā etena yajate ya evaṃvvidvān dvādaśāhena yajate
<10.5.16>     gṛhā vai devānāṃ dvādaśāho nāgṛhatāyā bhayyam
<10.5.17>     yo vai dvādaśāhamagniṣṭomena kalpamānaṃ veda kalpate'smai prātaḥsavanenaiva prathamastrirātraḥ kalpate mādhyandinena dvitīyastṛtīyasavanena tṛtīyo'gniṣṭomasāmnaiva daśamamahaḥ kalpate
<10.5.18>     kalpate'smai ya evaṃvveda

<10.6.1>     eti pretyāśumadvītimadrukmat tejasvadyuñjānaṃ prathamasyāhno rūpaṃ trivṛtaḥ stomasya gāyatrasya chandaso rathantarasya sāmnaḥ
<10.6.2>     vṛṣavadvṛtravadrayimadviśvavadupasthitaṃ dvitīyasyāhno rūpaṃ pañcadaśasya stomasya traiṣṭubhasya chandaso bṛhataḥ sāmnaḥ
<10.6.3>     udvat trivaddigvat gomaṭṭaṣabhavat tṛtīyasyāhno rūpaṃ saptadaśasya stomasya jāgatasya chandaso vairūpasya sāmnaḥ
<10.6.4>     rājanvajjanavadvat sūryavadvirāḍanutodavaccaturthasyāhno rūpamekaviṃśasya stomasyānuṣṭubhasya chandaso (?) rājasya vai sāmnaḥ
<10.6.5>     citravacchiśumat paṅktiḥ śakvarī dyūnākṣarā gomadṛṣabhavadvajryabhimat pañcamasyāhno rūpaṃ triṇavasya stomasya pāṅktena chandaso śakvarīṇāṃ sāmnaḥ
<10.6.6>     parivat prativat saptapadā dvipadā vinārāśaṃsā gomadṛṣabhavat ṣaṣṭhasyāhno rūpaṃ trayastriṃśasya stomasya sarveṣāṃ chandasāṃ rūpaṃ (?) revatīnāṃ sāmnaḥ
<10.6.7>     yasmādeṣā samānā satī ṣaḍahavibhaktirnānārūpā tasmādvirūpaḥ saṃvvatsaraḥ
<10.6.8>     virūpamenamanuprajāyate ya evaṃvveda

<10.7.1>     agna iti prathamasyāhno rūpamagnivibhakteragnimiti dvitīyasyāgnineti tṛtīyasyāgniriti caturthasya
<10.7.2>     devā vai śriyamaicchaṃstānna prathame'hanyavindanna dvitīye na tṛtīye tāñcaturthe'hanyavindan vindate śriyaṃ ya evaṃ vedāgneriti pañcamasya teno śrīḥ pratyupoditetyāhuḥ
<10.7.3>     aprativādyenaṃ bhrātṛvyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<10.7.4>     agna iti ṣaṣṭhasya yenaiva rūpeṇa prayanti tadabhyudyanti
<10.7.5>     yasmādeṣā samānā satyagnivbhaktirnānārūpā tasmādyathartvādityastapati

<10.8.1>     indreti prathamasyāhno rūpamindravibhakterindramiti dvitīyasyendreṇeti tṛtīyasyendra iti caturthasyendrāditi pañcamasyendreti ṣaṣṭhasya yenaiva rūpeṇa prayanti tadabhyudyanti yasmādeṣā samānā satīndravibhaktirnānārūpā tasmādyathartvoṣadhayaḥ pacyante

<10.9.1>     yadṛcā prastauti tat prathamasyāhno rūpaṃ svaravibhakteryat purastāt stobhaṃ taddvitīyasya yadubhayataḥ stobhaṃ tat tṛtīyasya yadanutunnaṃ taccaturthasya yadabhyārabdhaṃ tat pañcamasya yadihakāreṇābhyastaṃ tat ṣaṣṭhasyāhno rūpam
<10.9.2>     svarāṇāṃ yasmādeṣā samānā satī svaravibhaktirnnānārūpā tasmādyathartu vāyuḥ pavate

<10.10.1>     padanidhanaṃ prathamasyāhno rūpaṃ nidhanavibhakterbahirṇidhanaṃ dvitīyasya niṃnidhanaṃ tṛtīyasyenidhanañcaturthasyāthakāraṇidhanaṃ pañcamasya yadihākāreṇābhyastaṃ tat ṣaṣṭhasyāhno rūpaṃ nidhanānāṃ yasmādeṣā samānā satī nidhanavibhaktirnnānārūpā tasmādime lokāḥ saha santo nānaiva

<10.11.1>     dravadiḍaṃ prathamasyāhno rūpamiḍāvibhakterūrdhveḍaṃ dvitīyasya pariṣṭubdheḍaṃ tṛtīyasyeḍābhiraiḍaṃ caturthasyādhyardheḍaṃ pañcamasya yadihākāreṇābhyastaṃ tat ṣaṣṭhasyāhno rūpamiḍānāṃ yasmādeṣā samānā satīḍāvibhaktirnnānārūpā tasmāt samānāḥ santaḥ paśavo nānārūpāḥ

<10.12.1>     bharadvājāyanā vai sattramāsata tānapṛcchan kiṃ prathamenāhnā kuruteti praivaimeti kiṃ dvitīyenetyavasamevākurmahīti kiṃ tṛtīyeneti paryevāplavāmahīti kiṃ caturtheneti sataiva sadapyadadhmeti kiṃ pañcameneti prāṇāneva vicchindanta aimeti kiṃ ṣaṣṭhenetīdamevāgacchāmeti iti
<10.12.2>     yasya padena prastautyatha svāramabhi vāva tena devāḥ paśūnapaśyan yat purastāt stobhaṃ atha svāramudeva tenāsṛjanata yadubhayataḥ stobhamatha svāramebhya eva tena lokebhyo devāḥ paśubhyo'nnādyaṃ prāyacchan yadanutunnamatha svāramupaiva tenāśikṣan yasya madhye nidhanamatha svāraṃ garbhāṃstenādadhata tāni havatāsvāreṇa prājanayan
<10.12.3>     imaṃ vāva devā lokaṃ padanidhanenābhyajayannamuṃ bahirṇidhanenāntarikṣaṃ diṅnidhanenāmṛtatvamīnidhanenāgacchan brahmavarcasamathanidhanenāvārundhatāsminneva loka ihanidhanena pratyatiṣṭhan
<10.12.4>     imaṃ vāva devā lokaṃ dravadiḍenābhyajayannamumūrdhveḍenāntarikṣaṃ pariṣṭubdheḍena pratiṣṭhāmiḍābhiraiḍenāvārundhata pratiṣṭhāyādhyardheḍena vyajayantāsminneva loka iheḍena pratyatiṣṭhan
<10.12.5>     na vāksaṃvatsaramativadatīḍaiva saṃvatsaramativadati garbheṇa saṃvatsare paryāvṛtya prajāyete tenātivadati
<10.12.6>     tā vā etāścatasraḥ ṣaḍahaṃ parācya iḍā atiyantyeṣā nūtaiṣā viṣūcyeṣā pratīcyetadvīḍam
<10.12.7>     saṃvatsaro'gnirvāksamvatsaro yadagnirvibhajyate vācameva tadvibhajanti
<10.12.8>     dve dve akṣare vibhajanti dvau dvau hi māsāvṛturatho māsānāmeva tadrūpaṃ kriyate
<10.12.9>     ṣaḍahāni vibhajanti ṣaḍṛtava ṛtūnāṃ dhṛtyā ṛtūnāṃ pratiṣṭhityā atho ṛtūnāmeva tadrūpaṃ kriyate ṣaḍu puruṣā yānagniranuvihriyate
<10.12.10>     yadidaṃ bahudhāgnirvihriyate tadasāvādityaḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ pratyaṅtasmādete devate vibhaktimānaśāte nāto'nyā kā cana
<11.1.1>     stomo yujyate sattriyebhyo' harbhyaḥ pratnavatībhiścopavatībhiśca
<11.1.2>     yat pratnavatyo upavatībhyaḥ pūrvā yujyante brahma tat pūrvaṃ kṣatrādyujyate brahma hi pūrvaṃ kṣatram
<11.1.3>     manastat pūrvaṃ vāco yujyate mano hi yaddhi manasābhigacchati tadvācā vadati
<11.1.4>     bṛhat tat pūrvaṃ rathantarādyujyate bṛhaddhi pūrvaṃ rathantarādvijityā tu vai rathantaraṃ pūrvaṃ yogamānaśe
<11.1.5>     sambhāryāstṛcā bhavanti yathāśiṣṭhān vahiṣṭhān sambharedevamevaitān sambharanti gatyai
<11.1.6>     nava bhavanti navāhasya yuktyā ṛcarcaivāharyunakti yathāprārthasya śamyā avadadhyādevamevaitannavāhasya śamyā avadadhāti gatyai
<11.1.7>     trivṛdeva stomo bhavati tejase brahmavarcasāya

<11.2.1>     ubhābhyāṃ vai rūpābhyāṃ bahiṣpavamānyo yujyante yatsāmā stomo bhavati tadājyeṣu
<11.2.2>     nirāhāvantyājyāni bhavanti yuktameva tairāhvayati
<11.2.3>     agna āyāhi vītaya ā no mitrāvaruṇāyāhi suṣamā hi ta indrāgnī āgataṃ sutamiti rāthantarameva tadrūpaṃ nirdyotayati stomaḥ iti

<11.3.1>     pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatrī bhavati pretyā abhidroṇāni babhrava ityabhikrāntyai stutā indrāya vāyava iti saṃskṛtyai pra soma deva vītaya iti pretyai pra tu draveti pretyai pra vā etenāhnā yanti
<11.3.2>     gāyatraṃ bhavati
<11.3.3>     yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<11.3.4>     āśvaṃ bhavati
<11.3.5>     aśvo vai bhūtvā prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata sa prājāyata bahurabhavat prajāyate bahurbhavatyāśvena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.3.6>     ekākṣaraṃ nidhanamupayanti rathantarasyānativādāya
<11.3.7>     anativādyenaṃ bhrātṛvyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<11.3.8>     somasāma bhavati
<11.3.9>     yathā vā imā anyā oṣadhayaḥ evaṃ soma āsīt sa tapo'tapyata sa etat somasāmāpaśyat tena rājyamādhipatyamagacchadyaśo'bhavadrājyamādhipatyaṃ gacchati yaśo bhavati somasāmnā tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.3.10>     yaudhājayaṃ bhavati yadeva yaudhājayasya brāhmaṇam
<11.3.11>     auśanaṃ yadauśanasya stomaḥ

<11.4.1>     abhi tvā śūra no nu ma ityabhīti rathantarasya rūpaṃ rathantaraṃ hyetadahaḥ
<11.4.2>     kayā naścitra ābhuvaditi kavatyastena prājāpatyāḥ ko hi prajāpatiḥ prajāpaterāptyai
<11.4.3>     taṃ vo dasmamṛtīṣahaṃ vasormandānamandhaso'bhivatsaṃ na svasareṣu dhenava ityabhīti rathantarasya rūpaṃ rathantaraṃ hyetadahaḥ
<11.4.4>     indraṃ gīrbhirhavāmaha iti havanta evainam
<11.4.5>     tarobhirvo vidadvasumiti stomo vai taro yajño vidadvasuḥ stomena vai yajño yujyate yat tarobhirvo vidadvasumityāha yajñameva tadyunakti
<11.4.6>     rathantaraṃ bhavati brahma vai rathantaraṃ brahma prāyaṇīyamaharbrahmaṇa eva tadbrahmākramya prayanti
<11.4.7>     iyaṃ vai rathantaramasyāmeva pratiṣṭhāya sattramāsate
<11.4.8>     vāmadevyaṃ bhavati paśavo vai vāmadevyaṃ paśūnāmavaruddhyai prājāpatyaṃ vai vāmadevyaṃ prajāpatāveva pratiṣṭhāya sattramāsate
<11.4.9>     naudhasaṃ bhavati brahma vai naudhasaṃ brahma prāyaṇīyamaharbrahmaṇa eva tadrahmākramante
<11.4.10>     kāleyaṃ bhavati samānaloke vai kāleyañca rathantarañceyaṃ vai rathantaraṃ paśavaḥ kāleyamasyāñcaiva paśuṣu ca pratiṣṭhāya sattramāsate
<11.4.11>     dravadiḍaṃ tathā hyetasyāhno rūpaṃ stomaḥ

<11.5.1>     pra somāso madacyuta iti gāyatrī bhavati madavadvai rasavṛt tṛtīyasavanaṃ madameva tadrasaṃ dadhātyayā pasvasva devayurityeti rathantarasya rūpaṃ rāthantaraṃ hyetadahaḥ pavate'haryato haririti bṛhato rūpaṃ bṛhadeva tadetasminnahani yunakti tadyuktaṃ śva ārabhante pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti pravatyo bhavanti praṇinīṇyamiva hyetadaharabhi priyāṇi pavate ca nohita ityabhīti rathantarasya rūpaṃ rāthantaraṃ hyetadahaḥ
<11.5.2>     yajñā yajñā vo agnaya ityagnervai yajño yajña eva tadyajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<11.5.3>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<11.5.4>     saṃhitaṃ bhavati vyakṣaraṇidhanaṃ pratiṣṭhāyai pratiṣṭhāyaiva sattramāsate
<11.5.5>     saphaṃ bhavati
<11.5.6>     saphena vai devā imān lokān samāpnuvvan yat samāpnuvaṃstat saphasya saphatvamimānevaitena lokān samāpya sattramāsate
<11.5.7>     ākṣāraṃ bhavati
<11.5.8>     aṣṭau vā etāḥ kādughā āsaṃstāsām (?) ekā samaśīryata sā kṛṣirabhavadṛdhyate'smai kṛṣau ya evaṃ veda
<11.5.9>     tāsu devāsurā aspardhanta te devā asurān kāmadughābhya ākṣāreṇānudanta nudate bhrātṛvyaṃ kāmadughābhya ākṣāreṇa tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.5.10>     ebhyo vai lokebhyo raso'pākrāmat taṃ prajāpatirākṣāreṇākṣārayadyadākṣārayat tadākṣārasyākṣāratvam
<11.5.11>     tasmādyaḥ purā puṇyo bhūtvā paścāt pāpīyān syādākṣāraṃ brahma sāma kurvītātmanyevedindriyaṃ vīryaṃ rasamākṣārayati
<11.5.12>     januṣaikarco bhavato'hno dhṛtyai yadvā etasyāhno'dhṛtaṃ tadetābhyāṃ dādhāra
<11.5.13>     gaurīvitaṃ bhavati
<11.5.14>     gaurīvitirvā etacchāktyo brāhmaṇo'tiriktamapaśyat tadgaurīvitamabhavat
<11.5.15>     atiriktaṃ vā etadatiriktena stuvanti yadgaurīvitenāhīnāñchavastanavadbhavatyapi prajāyā upakḷptam
<11.5.16>     vṛṣā vā etadvājisāma vṛṣabho retodhā adya stuvanti śvaḥ prajāyate
<11.5.17>     anuṣṭubhi chandasāṃ kriyate'nuṣṭubbhi chandasāṃ yoniḥ svāyāmeva tadyonau reto dhatte prajātyai
<11.5.18>     prajāyate bahurbhavati ya evaṃ veda
<11.5.19>     dvyudāsaṃ bhavatyetau vā udāsau svargasya lokasyāvasānadarśau pūrveṇa pūrvamahaḥ saṃsthāpayantyuttareṇottaramaharabhyativadanti
<11.5.20>     tadyathādaḥ pūrvedyuḥ spaṣṭaṃ tṛṇodakamanvavasyanto yantyevameva tābhyāṃ svargaṃ lokamanvavasyanto yanti
<11.5.21>     gautamaṃ bhavati
<11.5.22>     sāmārṣeyavat svargāya yujyate svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.5.23>     yadu caivānuṣtṭubhasya madhye nidhanasya brāhmaṇaṃ taddu caitasya
<11.5.24>     kāvaṃ bhavati
<11.5.25>     lokabinduḥ sāma vindate lokaṃ kāvena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.5.26>     svārasu svareṇa svareṇa hi devebhyo'ntato'nnādyaṃ pradīyate svareṇaiva taddevebhyo'ntato'nnādyaṃ pra yacchati
<11.5.27>     yajñāyajñīyaṃ bhavati
<11.5.28>     vāgyajñāyajñīyaṃ vāci yajñaḥ pratiṣṭhito vācyeva tadyajñamantataḥ pratiṣṭhāpayanti taṃ vāco'dhi śva ārabhante
<11.5.29>     trivṛdeva stomo bhavati tejase brahmavarcasāya

<11.6.1>     pratipadbhavati
<11.6.2>     samārabhate dvirātrasyāvrisraṃsāya
<11.6.3>     pavasvendo vṛṣāsuta ityanurūpyo bhavati vṛṣāvadvā eta aindraṃ traiṣṭubhamaharyat dvitīyaṃ tadeva tadabhivadati
<11.6.4>     pūrvamu tadrūpamapareṇa rūpeṇānuvadati yat pūrvaṃ rūpamapareṇa rūpeṇānuvadati tadanurūpasyānurūpatvam
<11.6.5>     anurūpa enaṃ putro jāyate ya evaṃ veda
<11.6.6>     stotrīyānurūpau tṛcau bhavataḥ prāṇāpānānāmavarudhyai
<11.6.7>     vṛṣaṇvantastṛcā bhavatīndriyasya vīryasyāvarudhyai
<11.6.8>     tṛca uttamo bhavati
<11.6.9>     yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyanti
<11.6.10>     pañdadaśa eva stomo bhavati
<11.6.11>     ojasyeva tadvīrye pratitiṣṭhati ojo vīryaṃ pañcadaśaḥ

<11.7.1>     ubhābhyāṃ vai rūpābhyāṃ bahiṣpavamānyo yujyante yatsāmā stomo bhavati tadājyeṣu
<11.7.2>     nirāhopasthitānyājyāni bhavanti
<11.7.3>     agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmaha indramidgāthino bṛhadindro agnā namo bṛhaditi bārhatameva tadrūpaṃ nirdyotayati stomaḥ

<11.8.1>     vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatrī bhavatyahno dhṛtyai
<11.8.2>     vṛṣaṇvatyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa traiṣṭubhaṃ hyetadahaḥ
<11.8.3>     punānassoma dhārayeti dhṛtyai
<11.8.4>     vṛṣā śoṇo abhikanikradadgā iti
<11.8.5>     vṛṣaṇvatyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa samṛddhā vṛṣaṇvadvā etadaindraṃ traiṣṭubhamaharyat dvitīyaṃ tadeva tadabhivadati
<11.8.6>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<11.8.7>     yauktāśvaṃ bhavati
<11.8.8>     yuktāśvo va āṅgirasaḥ śiśū jātau viparyaharat tasmānmantro'pākrāmat sa tapo'tapyata sa etadyauktāśvamapaśyat taṃ mantra upāvartata tadvāva sa tarhyakāmayata kāmasani sama yauktāśvaṃ kāmamevaitenāvarundhe
<11.8.9>     āyāsye bhavataḥ
<11.8.10>     ayāsyo vā āṅgirasa ādityānāṃ dīkṣitānāmannādyamāśnāt taṃ śugārcchat sa tapo'tapyata sa ete āyāsye apaśyat tābhyāṃ śucamapāhatāpa śucaṃ hata āyāsyābhyāṃ tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.8.11>     ebhyo vai lokebhyo vṛṣṭirapākrāmat tāmayāsya āyāsyābhyāmacyāvayat cyāvayati vṛṣṭimāyāsyābhyāṃ tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.8.12>     annādyaṃ vāva tadebhyo lokebhyo'pākrāmat tadayāsya āyāsyābhyāmacyāvayat cyāvayatyannādyamāyāsyābhyāṃ tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.8.13>     vāsiṣṭhaṃ bhavati
<11.8.14>     vasiṣṭho vā etena vaiḍavaḥ stutvāñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ stomaḥ

<11.9.1>     tvāmiddhi havāmaha iti tvamiti bṛhato rūpaṃ bārhataṃ hyetadahaḥ
<11.9.2>     abhipravaḥ surādhasamiti yuñjate vai pūrveṇāhnā hyetena prayanti
<11.9.3>     tvāmidā hyo nara ityadya caiva hyaśca samārabhate dvirātrasyāvisraṃsāya
<11.9.4>     bṛhadbhavati varṣma vai bṛhadvarṣma dvitīyamaharvarṣmaṇa eva tadvarṣmākramante
<11.9.5>     śyetaṃ bhavati sāmnorvāho yajñasya santatyai
<11.9.6>     mādhucchandasaṃ bhavati sāmārṣeyavat svargāya yujyate svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.9.7>     ūrdhveḍaṃ tathā hyetasyāhno rūpaṃ stomaḥ

<11.10.1>     yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatrī bhavati
<11.10.2>     madavadvai rasavat tṛtīyasavanaṃ madameva tadrasaṃ dadhāti
<11.10.3>     pavasva madhumattama iti pavanta iva hyetenāhnā madhumattama ityannaṃ vai madhvannādyameva tadyajamāne dadhāti
<11.10.4>     indramaccha sutā ima itīndriyasya vīryasyāvarudhyai
<11.10.5>     ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti
<11.10.6>     anuṣṭubhaḥ satyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa traiṣṭubhaṃ hyetadahaḥ
<11.10.7>     vṛṣāmatīnāṃ pavate vicakṣaṇa iti jagatyaḥ satyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa traiṣṭubhaṃ hyetadahaḥ
<11.10.8>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<11.10.9>     hāviṣmataṃ bhavati
<11.10.10>     haviṣmāṃśca vai haviṣkṛccāṅgirasāvāstāṃ dvitīye'hani haviṣmānarādhnonnavame'hani haviṣkṛt
<11.10.11>     ayaṃ haviṣmānityeva jātamaharjātaṃ somaṃ prāha devebhyaḥ sāmnaivāsmā āśiṣamāśāste sāma hi satyāśīḥ
<11.10.12>     śaṅku bhavatyahno dhṛtyai yadvā adhṛtaṃ śaṅkunā taddādhāra
<11.10.13>     tadu sīdantīyamiyāhuretena vai prajāpatirūrdhva imān lokānasīdadyadasīdat tat sīdantīyasya sīdantīyatvamūrdhva imān lokān sīdati sīdantīyena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.10.14>     sujñānaṃ bhavati
<11.10.15>     svarvadvai rāthantaraṃ rūpaṃ svarṇadhanaṃ bārhatam
<11.10.16>     svarṇadhanaṃ bhavati tathā hyetasyāhno rūpam
<11.10.17>     pluvau vā etāvupohante svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
<11.10.18>     gaurīvitaṃ bhavati yadeva gaurīvitasya brāhmaṇam
<11.10.19>     krauñcaṃ bhavati
<11.10.20>     vāgvai krauñcaṃ vāgdvādaśāho vācyeva tadvācā stuvate yajñasya prabhūtyai
<11.10.21>     yāmaṃ bhavati
<11.10.22>     etena vai yamo'napajayyamamuṣya lokasyādhipatyamāśnutānapajayyamamuṣya lokasyādhipatyamaśnute yāmena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<11.10.23>     etena vai yamī yamaṃ svargaṃ lokamagamayat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ stomaḥ

<11.11.1>     ehyū ṣu bravāṇi ta ityehivatyai bhavanti tṛtīyasyāhna upahavāya santatyai
<11.11.2>     apacchidiva vā etadyajñakāṇḍaṃ yadukthāni yadeti salomatvāya
<11.11.3>     evāhyasi vīrayuriti samānaṃ vadantīdamitthamasaditi
<11.11.4>     indraṃ viśvā avīvṛdhannityavardhanta hyetarhi yajamānamevaitayā vardhanti
<11.11.5>     sākamaśvaṃ bhavatyukthānāmabhijityā abhikrāntyai
<11.11.6>     etena hyagra ukthānyadhyajayannetenābhyakrāman
<11.11.7>     āmahīyavaṃ bhavati kḷptiścānnādyañca samānaṃ vadantīṣu kriyata idamitthamasaditi
<11.11.8>     kṣatraṃ vā etadaharabhinirvadati yat pañcadaśaṃ yadgāyatrīṣu brahmasāma bhavati brahma caiva tat kṣatrañca sayujī karoti brahmaiva kṣatrasya purastānnidadhati brahmaṇe kṣatrañca viśañcānuge karoti
<11.11.9>     brahma vai pūrvamahaḥ kṣatraṃ dvitīyaṃ yadgāyatrīṣu brahma sāma bhavati brahma tadyaśasārdhayati brahma hi gāyatrī
<11.11.10>     tadu samānaṃ vadantīṣu kriyate samṛddhyai
<11.11.11>     āṣṭādaṃṣṭre bhavataḥ
<11.11.12>     aiyāhā iti vā indro vṛtramahannaiyādohoveti nyagṛhṇādvārtraghne sāmanī vīryavatī
<11.11.13>     ojo evaitābhyāṃ vīryamavarundhe
<11.11.14>     pañcadaśa eva stomo bhavatyojasyeva tadvīrye pratitiṣṭhatyojo vīryaṃ pañcadaśaḥ

<12.1.1>     davidyutatyāruceti tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ pratipadbhavati
<12.1.2>     davidyutatī vai gāyatrī pariṣṭobhantī triṣṭubgavāśīrjagatī trīṇi rūpāṇi samārabhate trirātrasyāvisraṃsāya
<12.1.3>     ete asṛgramindava ityanurūpo bhavati
<12.1.4>     eta iti vai prajāpatirdevānasṛjatāsṛgramiti manuṣyānindava iti pit.ṛṃstadeva tadabhivadati
<12.1.5>     pūrvamu caiva tadrepamapareṇa rūpeṇānuvadati yat pūrvaṃ rūpamapareṇa rūpaṇo'nuvadati tadanurūpasyānurūpatvamanurūpa enaṃ putro jāyate ya evaṃ veda
<12.1.6>     stotrīyānurūpau tṛco bhavataḥ prāṇāpānānāmavarudhyai
<12.1.7>     rājā medhābhirīyate pavamāno manāvadhyantarikṣeṇa yātava iti
<12.1.8>     antarikṣadevatyastṛco bhavatyantarikṣadevatyametadaharyat tṛtīyaṃ tadeva tadabhivadati
<12.1.9>     pañcarcau bhavati pañcapadā paṅktiḥ pāṅktamannamannādyasyāvarudhyai
<12.1.10>     tṛca uttamo bhavati yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyupayānti
<12.1.11>     saptadaśa eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai prajātyai

<12.2.1>     agnināgniḥ samidhyata ityāgreyamājyaṃ bhavati
<12.2.2>     pūrve eva tadahanī samiddhe tṛtīyamaharabhisamindhe
<12.2.3>     mitraṃ huve pūtadakṣamiti rāthantaraṃ maitrāvaruṇam
<12.2.4>     huva iti vai rāthantaraṃ rūpam
<12.2.5>     rathantarametat parokṣaṃ yadvairūpaṃ rāthantarameva tadrūpaṃ nirdyotayati
<12.2.6>     indreṇa saṃ hi dṛkṣusa ityaindram
<12.2.7>     samiva vā ime lokā dadṛśire'ntarikṣadevatyametadaharyat tṛtīyaṃ tadeva tadabhivadati
<12.2.8>     tā huve yayoridamiti rāthantaramaindrāgnam
<12.2.9>     huva iti vai rāthantaraṃ rūpaṃ rathantarametat parokṣaṃ yadvairūpaṃ rāthantarameva tadrūpaṃ nirdyotayati stomaḥ

<12.3.1>     uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatrī bhavati
<12.3.2>     udvadvā etadaharyat tṛtīyaṃ tadeva tadabhivadati
<12.3.3>     andhasvatī bhavatyaharvā andho'hna ārambhaḥ
<12.3.4>     abhi somāsa āyava iti
<12.3.5>     abhīti rathantarasya rūpaṃ bṛhaditi bṛhata ubhayoḥ saha rūpamupaityubhau hi varṇāvetadahaḥ
<12.3.6>     tisro vāca īrayati pravahniriti tṛtīyasyāhno rūpaṃ tena tṛtīyamaharārabhante
<12.3.7>     triṣṭubhaḥ satyo jagatyo rūpeṇa jāgataṃ hyetadahaḥ
<12.3.8>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<12.3.9>     vaiṣṭambhaṃ bhavati
<12.3.10>     aharvā etadavlīyata taddevā vaiṣṭambhairvyaṣṭabhnuvaṃstadvaiṣṭambhasya vaiṣṭambhatvam
<12.3.11>     diśa iti nidhanamupayanti diśāṃ dhṛtyai
<12.3.12>     paurūmadgaṃ bhavati
<12.3.13>     aharvā etadavlīyamānaṃ tadrakṣāṃsyanvasacanta tasmāddevāḥ paurūmadgena rakṣāṃsyapāghnannapa pāpmānaṃ hate paurūmadgena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<12.3.14>     devāśca vāsurāścāspardhanta te devā asurāṇāṃ paurūmadgena puro'majjayan yat puro'majjayaṃstasmāt paurūmadgaṃ pāpmānamevaitena bhrātṛvyaṃ majjayati
<12.3.15>     gautamaṃ bhavati
<12.3.16>     yadeva gautamasya brāhmaṇam
<12.3.17>     ubhayataḥ stotraṃ tathā hyetasyāhno rūpam
<12.3.18>     antarikṣaṃ bhavati
<12.3.19>     antarikṣadevatyametadaharyat tṛtīyamantarikṣa eva tadantarikṣeṇa stuvate pratiṣṭhāyai
<12.3.20>     āṣkāraṇidhanaṃ kāṇvaṃ bhavati
<12.3.21>     asiti vai rāthantaraṃ rūpaṃ hasiti bārhataṃ tṛtīyameva tadrūpamupayanti samṛdhyai
<12.3.22>     aṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo bhavati
<12.3.23>     etena vā aṅgirasaḥ saṃkrośamānāḥ svargaṃ lokamāpan svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ stomaḥ

<12.4.1>     yadyāva indra te śatamiti śatavatyo bhavanti
<12.4.2>     śatavadvai paśūnāṃ rūpaṃ sahasravat paśūnāmevaitābhī rūpamavarūndhe
<12.4.3>     vayaṅghatvā sutāvanta iti satobṛhatyo varṣīyaśchanda ākramate'napabhraṃśāya
<12.4.4>     taraṇirit siṣāsati vājaṃ purandhyā yujā| āva indraṃ puruhūtaṃ na me giretyāvadakṣaramuddhatamiva vai tṛtīyamaharyadeva tāvadakṣaraṃ bhavatyaharevaitena pratiṣṭhāpayati
<12.4.5>     pañcanidhanaṃ vairūpaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavati diśāṃ dhṛtyai
<12.4.6>     pañcapadā paṅktiḥ pāṅktamannamannāghasyāvarūdhyai
<12.4.7>     diśāṃ vā etat sāma yadvairūpaṃ diśo hyevaitenābhivadati
<12.4.8>     atha yat pañcanidhanaṃ tenartūnāṃ pañca hyṛtavaḥ
<12.4.9>     ṛtubhiśca vā ime lokā digbhiścāvṛtāsteṣvevobhayeṣu yajamānaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati yajamānaṃ vā anupratitiṣṭhantamudgātā pratitiṣṭhati ya evaṃ vidvān vairūpeṇodgāyati
<12.4.10>     digvadbhavati bhrātṛvyasyāpanutyai
<12.4.11>     diśaṃ viśamiti nidhanamupayanti diśāṃ dhṛtyai
<12.4.12>     hasityupariṣṭāddiśāṃ nidhanamupayanti tena bārhatam
<12.4.13>     rāthantaro vā ayaṃ loko bārhato'sāvubhe eva tadbṛhadrathantarayo rūpeṇāparādhnoti
<12.4.14>     anaḍvāhau vā etau devayānau yajamānasya yadbṛhadrathantare tāveva tadyunakti svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
<12.4.15>     aśvavadbhavati prajātyai
<12.4.16>     yathā maṇḍūka āṭkarotyevaṃ nidhanamupayantyayātayāmatāyai
<12.4.17>     dvādaśa vairūpāṇi bhavanti dvādaśamāsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsara eva pratitiṣṭhati
<12.4.18>     virūpaḥ saṃvatsaro virūpamannādyasyāvarudhyai
<12.4.19>     mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ brahmasāma bhavatyannādyasyāvarudhyai
<12.4.20>     yadā vai puruṣo'nnamattyathāntarato viṣṭabdhaḥ
<12.4.21>     diśa iti nidhanamupayanti diśāṃ dhṛtyai
<12.4.22>     sato bṛhatīṣu stuvanti pūrvayorahnoḥ pratyudyamāya
<12.4.23>     rauravamacchāvākasāma bhavati
<12.4.24>     agnirvai rūro rūdro'gniḥ
<12.4.25>     agnirvā etasya paśūnapakramayati yasya paśavo'pakrāmantyagnireva tasya paśūnabhikramayati yasya paśavo'bhikrāmanti
<12.4.26>     abhyabhyevāsya paśavaḥ krāmanti ya evaṃ vidvān rauraveṇa stuvate
<12.4.27>     pariṣṭubdheḍaṃ tathā hyetasyāhno rūpaṃ stomaḥ

<12.5.1>   "tisro vāca udīrata"iti tṛtīyasyāhno rūpaṃ tena tṛtīyamaharārabhante
<12.5.2>     udvadvā etat trivadaharyat tṛtīyaṃ tadeva tadabhivadati
<12.5.3>   "ā sotā pariṣiñcata"iti parivatyo bhavanti
<12.5.4>     anto vai tṛtīyamahastasyaitāḥ paryāptyai
<12.5.5>   "sakhāya āniṣīdata"ityuddhattamiva vai tṛtīyamaharyadāha niṣīdatetyaharevaitena pratiṣṭhāpayati
<12.5.6>   "sutāso madhumattamā"ityanuṣṭubhaḥ satyo jagatyo rūpeṇa jāgataṃ hyetadahaḥ
<12.5.7>   "pavitraṃ te vitataṃ brahmaṇaspata"iti
<12.5.8>     vitatamiva vā idamantarikṣamantareme antarikṣadevatyametadaharyat tṛtīyaṃ tadeva tadabhivadati
<12.5.9>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<12.5.10>     pāṣṭhauhaṃ bhavati
<12.5.11>     paṣṭhavāḍvā etenāṅgirasaścaturthasyāhno vācaṃ vadantīmupāśṛṇot sa ho vāgiti nidhanamupait tadasyābhyuditaṃ tadaharavasat
<12.5.12>     vācaḥ sāma bhavati
<12.5.13>     vāgvai dvādaśāho vācyeva tadvācā stuvate yajñasya prabhūtyai
<12.5.14>     niṣkirīyāḥ sattramāsata te tṛtīyamaharna prājānaṃstānetat sāma gāyamānān vāgupāplavat tena tṛtīyamahaḥ prājānaṃste'bruvanniyaṃ vāva nastṛtīyamaharadīdṛśaditi tṛtīyasyaivaiṣāhno dṛṣṭiḥ
<12.5.15>     śauktaṃ bhavati
<12.5.16>     śuktirvā etenāṅgiraso'ñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<12.5.17>     gaurīvitaṃ bhavati yadeva gaurīvitasya brāhmaṇam
<12.5.18>     tvāṣṭrīsāma bhavati
<12.5.19>     indraṃ vā akṣyāmayiṇaṃ bhūtāni nāsvāpayaṃstametena tvāṣṭrayo'svāpayaṃstadvāva tāstarhyakāmayanta
<12.5.20>     kāmasani sāma tvāṣṭrīsāma kāmamevaitenāvarundhe
<12.5.21>     indro vṛtrādvibhyadgāṃ prāviśat taṃ tvāṣṭrayo'bruvaṃ janayāmeti tametaiḥ sāmabhirajanayaṃ jāyāmahā iti vai sattramāsate jāyanta eva
<12.5.22>     ariṣṭaṃ bhavati
<12.5.23>     devāśca vā asurāścāspardhanta yaṃ devānāmaghnanna sa samabhavadyamasurāṇāṃ saṃ so'bhavat te devāstapo'tapyanta ta etadariṣṭamapaśyaṃstato'yaṃ devānāmaghnat saṃ so'bhavadyamasurāṇāṃ na sa samabhavadanenāriṣāmeti tadariṣṭasyāriṣṭatvamariṣṭayā evāriṣṭamantataḥ kriyate
<12.5.24>     trīḍaṃ bhavati trirātrasya dhṛtyai
<12.5.25>     dravantīmiḍāmuttamāmupayanti caturthasyāhnaḥ santatyai stomaḥ

<12.6.1>     pramaṃhiṣṭhāya gāyateti
<12.6.2>     yadgāyateti mahasa eva tadrūpaṃ kriyate
<12.6.3>   "taṃ te madaṅgaṇīmasīti madavadvai rasavat tṛtīyasavanaṃ madameva tadrasaṃ dadhāti
<12.6.4>   "śrudhī havaṃ tiraścyā"iti śrutyā eva
<12.6.5>     pramaṃhiṣṭhīya bhavati
<12.6.6>     pramaṃhiṣṭhīyena vā indro vṛtrāya vajraṃ prāvartayat tamastṛṇuta bhrātṛvyavān pramaṃhiṣṭhīyenokthāni praṇayeta stṛṇute bhrātṛvyaṃ vasīyāṃ ātmanā bhavati
<12.6.7>     hārivarṇaṃ bhavati
<12.6.8>     indraśca vai namuciścāsuraḥ samadadhātāṃ na no naktanna divāhanannārdreṇa na śuṣkeṇeti tasya vyuṣṭāyāmanudita āditye'pāṃ phenena śiro'chinadetadvai na naktaṃ na divā yat vyuṣṭāyāmanudita āditya etannārdranna śuṣkaṃ yadapāṃ phenastadenaṃ pāpīyāṃ vācaṃ vadadanvavartata vīrahanna druho druha iti tannarcā na sāmnāpahantumaśaknot
<12.6.9>     hārivarṇasyaiva nidhanenāpāhata
<12.6.10>     apaśucaṃhate hārivarṇasya nidhanena śriyaṃ ca haraścopaiti tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<12.6.11>     tairaścayaṃ bhavati
<12.6.12>     aṅgirasaḥ svargaṃ lokaṃ yanto rakṣāṃsyanvasacanta tānyetena tiraścāṅgirasastiryaṅparyavaidyat tiryaṅaparyavait tasmāt tairaścyaṃ pāpmā vāva sa tānasacata taṃ tairaścyenāpāghnatāpa pāpmānaṃ hate tairaścyena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<12.6.13>     saptadaśa eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai prajātyai

<12.7.1>   "pra ta āśvinīḥ pavamānadhenava"iti caturthasyāhnaḥ pratipadbhavati
<12.7.2>     āpte trirātre rūpeṇa gāyatryo dvitīyaṃ prayanti prati vai gāyatryā rūpam
<12.7.3>     jagatī pratipadbhavati jāgatametadaharyat tṛtīyaṃ jagatyā eva tajjagatīmabhisaṃkrāmanti
<12.7.4>     yadato'nyā pratipatsyāt pratikūlaṃ vānukūlaṃ vā syāt
<12.7.5>     pavamāno ajījanadityanurūpo bhavati
<12.7.6>     janadvadvā etadaharyaccaturthamannādyaṃ janayati virājaṃ janayatyekaviṃśaṃ stomaṃ janayati
<12.7.7>     pūrvamu caiva tadrūpamapareṇa rūpeṇānuvadati tadanurūpasyānurūpatvam
<12.7.8>     anurūpa enaṃ putro jāyate ya evaṃ veda
<12.7.9>     stotrīyānurūpau tṛcau bhavataḥ prāṇāpānānāmavarudhyai
<12.7.10>     ṣaḍṛcau bhavata ṛtūnāṃ dhṛtyai
<12.7.11>     tṛca uttamo bhavati yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyanti
<12.7.12>     ekaviṃśa eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai pratitiṣṭhati

<12.8.1>   "janasya gopā ajaniṣṭa jāgṛviḥ"ityāgneyamājyaṃ bhavati
<12.8.2>     janadvadvā etadaharyaccaturthamannādyaṃ janayati virājaṃ janayatyekaviṃśaṃ stomaṃ janayati
<12.8.3>   "yaṃ vāṃ mitrāvarūṇā"iti bārhataṃ maitrāvarūṇam
<12.8.4>     bṛhadetat parokṣaṃ yadvairājaṃ bārhatameva tadrūpaṃ nirdyotayati
<12.8.5>   "indro dadhīco asthabhir"iti dādhīcastṛco bhavati
<12.8.6>     dadhyaṅvā āṅgiraso devānāṃ purodhānīya āsīdannaṃ vai brahmaṇaḥ purodhā annādyasyāvarudhyai
<12.8.7>   "iyaṃ vāmasya manmana"ityaindrāgnam
<12.8.8>   "indrāgnī pūrvyastutirabhrādvṛṣṭirivājani"ityānuṣṭubhī vai vṛṣṭirānuṣṭubhametadaharyaccaturthaṃ samīcyau virājau dadhātyannādyāya stomaḥ

<12.9.1>   "payasva dakṣasādhana"iti gāyatrī bhavati sidhyai
<12.9.2>     yat pavasveti tadbṛhato rūpaṃ bārhataṃ hyetadahaḥ
<12.9.3>   "tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇa sakhya indo dive div purūṇi babhro nicaranti māmava paridhīṃratitāṃ ihi"iti
<12.9.4>     ati hyāyañchakunā iva paptimetyati hyapatat
<12.9.5>     punāno akramīdabhīti
<12.9.6>     gāyatryaḥ satyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa tasmāt triṣṭubhāṃ loke kriyante
<12.9.7>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
