<12.9.8>     caturṇidhanamātharvaṇaṃ bhavati catūrātrasya dhṛtyai
<12.9.9>     catuṣpadānuṣṭubhānuṣṭubhametadaharyaccaturtham
<12.9.10>     bheṣajaṃ vātharvaṇāni bheṣajameva tat karoti
<12.9.11>     nidhanakāmaṃ bhavati
<12.9.12>     ekaṃ vā anyena niruktena nidhanena kāmaṃ sanotyathaitannidhanakāmaṃ sarveṣāṃ kāmānāmavarudhyai
<12.9.13>     āṣṭādaṃṣṭraṃ bhavati
<12.9.14>     yadevāṣṭādaṃṣṭrasya brāhmaṇam
<12.9.15>     ābhīśavaṃ bhavatyahno dhṛtyai
<12.9.16>     yadvā adhṛtamabhīśunā taddādhāra
<12.9.17>     anutunnaṃ gāyati tathā hyetasyāhno rūpam
<12.9.18>     caturṇidhanamāṅgirasaṃ bhavati catūrātrasya dhṛtyai
<12.9.19>     svaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ tathā hyetasyāhno rūpam
<12.9.20>     sattrāsāhīyaṃ bhavati
<12.9.21>     yadvā asurāṇāmasoḍhamāsīt taddevāḥ sattrāsāhīyenāsahanta sattrainānasakṣmahīti tat sattrāsāhīyasya sattrāsāhīyatvam
<12.9.22>     sattrā bhrātṛvyaṃ sahate sattrāsāhīyena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<12.9.23>     gāyatrīṣu stuvanti pratiṣṭhāyai brahmavarcasāya yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyanti
<12.9.24>     vṛṣaṇvatyo gāyatrayo bhavanti tadu traiṣṭubhādrūpānnayanti stomaḥ

<12.10.1>   "pibā somamindra mandatu tvā yante suṣāva haryaśvādriḥ| soturbāhubhyāṃ suyato nārvā"ityayatamiva vai caturthamahastasyaiva yatyai
<12.10.2>     viśvāḥ pṛtanā abhibhṛtarannara ityatijagatī varṣīyayaśchanda ākramate'napabhraṃśāya
<12.10.3>     apabhraṃśa iva vā eṣa yajjayāyasaśchandasaḥ kanīyaśchanda upaiti yadeṣā caturthe'hanyatijagatī kriyate'napabhraṃśāya
<12.10.4>     yo rājā carṣaṇīnāmiti
<12.10.5>     rājye hyetarhi vāco'gacchan rājyamevaitayā yajamānaṃ gamayanti
<12.10.6>     chandobhirvai devā ādityaṃ svargaṃ lokamaharan sa nādhriyata taṃ vairājasya nidhanenādṛṃhaṃstasmāt parāṅcārvāṅcādityastapati parāṅcārvāṅcekāraḥ
<12.10.7>     prastāvaṃ prastutya viṣṭambhān viṣṭabhnoti mukhata eva tadannādyaṃ dhatte mukhaṃ hi sāmnaḥ prastāvaḥ
<12.10.8>     daśakṛtvo viṣṭabhnoti daśākṣarā virāṅvairājamannamannādyasyāvarudhyai
<12.10.9>     triṃśatkṛtvo viṣṭabhnoti bhūyaso'nnādyasyāvarudhyai
<12.10.10>     vairājaṃ sāma bhavati virāṭsu stuvanti vairājā viṣṭambhāḥ samīcīrvirājo dadhātyannādyāya
<12.10.11>     anutunnaṃ gāyati retodheyāyānutunnāddhi reto dhīyate
<12.10.12>     dakṣiṇa ūrāvudgaturagniṃ manthanti dakṣiṇato hi retaḥ sicyate
<12.10.13>     upākṛte'hiṅkṛte manthanti jātamabhihiṅkaroti
<12.10.14>     tasmājjātaṃ putraṃ paśavo'bhihiṅkurvanti
<12.10.15>     tasmai jātāyāmīmāṃsanta gārhapatye praharāmā3 āgnīdhrā3 āhavanīyā3 iti
<12.10.16>     āhavanīye praharantyetadāyatano vai yajamāno yadā havanīye svameva tadāyatanaṃ jyotiṣmat karoti
<12.10.17>     jyotiṣmān brahmavarcasī bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<12.10.18>     abhijuhoti śāntyai
<12.10.19>     ājyenābhijuhoti tejo vā ājyaṃ teja eva tadātman dhatte
<12.10.20>   "preddho agne dīdihi puro na"iti virājābhijuhotyannaṃ virāḍannādyasyāvarudhyai
<12.10.21>     traiśokaṃ brahmasāma bhavati
<12.10.22>     atijagatīṣu stuvantyahna utkrāntyā udvā etenāhnā krāmanti
<12.10.23>     diveti nidhanamupayanti pāpmano'pahatyā apapāpmānaṃ hate traiśokena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<12.10.24>     bhāradvājasya pṛśnyachāvākasāma bhavati
<12.10.25>     annaṃ vai devāḥ pṛśnīti vadantyannādyasyāvarudhyai
<12.10.26>     iḍābhiraiḍaṃ tathā hyetasyāhno rūpaṃ stomaḥ

<12.11.1>   "paripriyā divaḥ kavir"iti parivatyo bhavantyanto vai caturthamahastasyaitāḥ paryāptyai
<12.11.2>     tvaṃ hyaṅga daivyeti tvamiti bṛhato rūpaṃ bārhataṃ  hyetadahaḥ
<12.11.3>     somaḥ punāna ūrmiṇā vyaṃvāraṃ vidhāvati| agre vācaḥ pavamānaḥ kanikradaditi
<12.11.4>     agraṃ hyetarhi vāco'gacchannagramevaitayā yajamānaṃ gamayanti
<12.11.5>   "puro jitīvo andhasā"iti virājau vairājaṃ hyetadahaḥ
<12.11.6>   "somaḥ pavate janitā matīnām"iti prātassavane ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhītaṃ taṃ tṛtīyasavane prajanayanti
<12.11.7>     triṣṭubhaḥ satyo jagatyo rūpeṇa tasmājjagatīnāṃ loke kriyante
<12.11.8>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<12.11.9>     aurṇāyavaṃ bhavati
<12.11.10>     aṅgiraso vai sattramāsata teṣāmāptaḥ spṛtaḥ svargo loka āsīt panthānaṃ tu devayānaṃ na prājānaṃsteṣāṃ kalyāṇa āṅgiraso dhyāyamudavrajat sa ūrṇāyuṃ gandharvamapsarasāṃ madhye preṅkhayamāṇamupait sa iyāmiti yāṃ yāmabhyadiśat sainamakāmayata tamabhyavadat kālyāṇā3 ityāso vai vaḥ spṛtaḥ svargo lokaḥ panthānaṃ tu devayānaṃ na prajānīthedaṃ sāma svargyaṃ lokameṣyatha mā tu vocohamadarśamiti
<12.11.11>     sa ait kalyāṇaḥ so'bravīdāpto vai naḥ spṛtaḥ svargo lokaḥ panthānaṃ tu devayānaṃ prajānīma idaṃ sāma svargyaṃ tena stutvā svargaṃ lokameṣyāma iti kaste'vocadityahamevādarśamiti tena stutvā svargaṃ lokamāyannahīyata kalyāṇo'nṛtaṃ hi so'vadat sa eṣaḥ śvitraḥ
<12.11.12>     svargyaṃ vā etat sāma svargalokaḥ puṇyaloko bhavatyaurṇāyavena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<12.11.13>     bṛhatkaṃ bhavati
<12.11.14>     sāmārṣeyeṇa praśastaṃ tvaṃ hītyannādyasyāvarudhyai hīti vā annaṃ pradīyate ṣoḍaśinamu caivaitenodyacchati
<12.11.15>     ātīṣādīyaṃ bhavati
<12.11.16>     āyurvā ātīṣādīyamāyuṣo'ṣarudhyai
<12.11.17>     ātamitornidhanamupayantyāyureva sarvamāpnuvanti
<12.11.18>     nānadaṃ bhavati
<12.11.19>     jyāyo'bhyārambhamatihāya pañcamahaḥ ṣaṣṭhasyāhna ārambhastena ṣaṣṭhamaharārabhante santatyai
<12.11.20>     ṣoḍaśākṣareṇa prastauti ṣoḍaśinamu caivaitenodyacchati
<12.11.21>     āndhīgavaṃ bhavati
<12.11.22>     kayorannādyamavarūndha āndhīgavena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<12.11.23>     vātsapraṃ bhavati
<12.11.24>     etasmin vai vairājaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ pratitiṣṭhati vātsapreṇa tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<12.11.25>     vatsaprīrbhālandanaḥ śraddhāṃ nāvindata sa tapo'tapyata sa etadvātsapramapaśyat sa śraddhāmavindata śraddhā vindāmahā iti vai sattramāsate vindate śraddhām
<12.11.26>     īnidhanaṃ tathā hyetasyāhno rūpaṃ nidhanāntāḥ pavamānā bhavantyahno dhṛtyai stomaḥ

<12.12.1>   "gniṃ vo vṛdhantam"iti
<12.12.2>     avardhanta hyetarhi yajamānameva tathā vardhayanti
<12.12.3>   "vayamu tvāmapūrvya"ityapūrvāṃ hyetarhi prajāpatestanūmagacchannapūrvamevaitayā yajamānaṃ gamayanti
<12.12.4>   "imamindra sutaṃ piba jyeṣṭhamamartyaṃ madam"iti jyaiṣṭhaṃ hyetarhi vāco'gacchan jyaiṣṭhyamevaitayā yajamānaṃ gamayanti
<12.12.5>     saindhukṣitaṃ bhavati
<12.12.6>     sindhukṣidvai rājanyarṣirjyogaparuddhaścaran sa etat saindhukṣitamapaśyat so'vāgacchat pratyatiṣṭhadavagacchati pratitiṣṭhati saindhukṣitena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<12.12.7>     saubharaṃ bhavati bṛhatastejaḥ
<12.12.8>     pannamiva vai caturthamahastadetena bṛhatastejasottabhnoti saubhareṇa
<12.12.9>     vasiṣṭhasya priyaṃ bhavati
<12.12.10>     etena vai vasiṣṭha indrasya premāṇamagacchat premāṇaṃ devatānāṃ gacchati vāsiṣṭhena tuṣṭuvānaḥ stomaḥ

<12.13.1>     indraśca bṛhacca samabhavatāṃ tamindraṃ bṛhadekayā tanvātyaricyata tasyā abibhedanayā mābhibhaviṣyatīti so'bravīt ṣoḍaśī te'yaṃ yajñakraturastviti sa ṣoḍaśyabhavat tadasya janma
<12.13.2>     atiśriyā bhrātṛvyaṃ ricyate yo gāyatrīṣu dvipadāsu bṛhatā ṣoḍaśinā stute
<12.13.3>   "upa no haribhiḥ stutam"ityetā vai gāyatryo dvipadā etāsu stotavyam
<12.13.4>     indraḥ prajāpatimupādhāvadvṛtraṃ hanānīti tasmā etāmanuṣṭubhamapaharasaṃ prāyacchat tayā nāstṛṇuta yadastṛtā vyanadat tannānadasya nānadatvam
<12.13.5>     taṃ punarupādhāvat tasmai saptānāṃ hotrāṇāṃ haro nirmāya prāyacchat tamastṛṇuta
<12.13.6>     stṛṇute taṃ yaṃ tustūrṣate ya evaṃ veda
<12.13.7>     tasmāddharivatīṣu stuvanti harivatīḥ śaṃsanti harivatīṣu graho gṛhyate haro hyasmai nirmāya prāyacchat
<12.13.8>     ekaviṃśāyatano vā eṣa yat ṣoḍaśī sapta hi prātassavane hotrā vaṣaṭkurvanti sapta mādhyandine savane sapta tṛtīyasavane
<12.13.9>     gaurīvitaṃ bhavati
<12.13.10>     gaurīvitirvā etacchāktyo brahmaṇo'tiriktamapaśyat tadgaurīvitamabhavadatiriktaṃ etadatiriktena stuvanti yadgaurīvitena ṣoḍaśinaṃ śvastanavān bhavatyapi prajāyā upakḷptaḥ
<12.13.11>     viśālaṃ libjayā śrūtyābhyadhāditi hovācopoditirgopāleyo'nuṣṭubhi nānadamakargaurīvitena ṣoḍaśinamastoṣṭāñjasā śriyamupāgānna śriyā avapadyata iti
<12.13.12>     eṣa vai viśālaṃ libjayā bhūtyābhidadhāti yo'nuṣṭubhi nānadaṃ kṛtvā gaurīvitena ṣoḍaśinā stute'ñjasā śriyamupaiti na śriyā avapadyate
<12.13.13>     śakvarīṣu ṣoḍaśinā stuvīta yaḥ kāmayeta vajrī syāmiti
<12.13.14>     vajro vai ṣoḍaśī vajraḥ śakvaryo vajreṇaivāsmai vajraṃ spṛṇoti vajrī bhavati
<12.13.15>     anuṣṭupsu ṣoḍaśinā stuvīta yaḥ kāmayeta na mā vāgativadet
<12.13.16>     vajro vai ṣoḍaśī vāganuṣṭubvajreṇaivāsmai vācaṃ spṛṇoti nainaṃ vāgativadati
<12.13.17>   "sāvi soma indra ta"ityetāsu stotavyam
<12.13.18>     virāṭsvannādyakāmaḥ ṣoḍaśinā stuvīta vajro vai ṣoḍaśī vairājamannaṃ vajreṇaivāsmā annaṃ spṛṇotyannādo bhavati
<12.13.19>   "pra vo mahe vṛdhe bharadhvam"ityetāsu stotavyam
<12.13.20>     trayastriṃśadakṣarā vā etā virājo yadekaviṃśatiḥ pratiṣṭhā sā yaddvādaśa prajātiḥ sā
<12.13.21>     pratiṣṭhāya prajāyate no cāntasthāyāṃ jīryate ya evaṃ veda
<12.13.22>     atha vā etā ekapadāstryakṣarā viṣṇośchando bhurijaḥ śakvaryaḥ
<12.13.23>     etābhirvā indro vṛtramahan kṣipraṃ vā etābhiḥ pāpmānaṃ hanti kṣipraṃ vasīyān bhavati
<12.13.24>     catustriṃśadakṣarāḥ saṃstuto bhavati trayastriṃśaddevattāḥ prajāpatiścatustriṃśo devatānāṃ prajāpatimevopayantyariṣṭyai
<12.13.25>     hiraṇyaṃ sampradāyaṃ ṣoḍaśinā stuvate jyotiṣmānasya ṣoḍaśī bhavati
<12.13.26>     aśvaḥ kṛṣṇa upatiṣṭhati sāmyekṣayāya bhrātṛvyalokameva sa vidhamaṃstiṣṭhati
<12.13.27>     ekākṣaraṃ vai devānāmavamaṃ chanda āsīt saptākṣaraṃ paramaṃ navākṣaramasurāṇāmavamaṃ chanda āsīt pañcadaśākṣaraṃ paramaṃ devāśca vā asurāścāspardhanta tān prajāpatirānuṣṭubho bhūtvāntarātiṣṭhat taṃ devāsurā vyahvayanta sa devānupāvartata tato devā abhavan parāsurāḥ
<12.13.28>     bhavatyātmanā parāsya bhrātṛvyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<12.13.29>     te devā asurāṇāmekākṣareṇaiva pañcadaśākṣaramavṛñjata dvyakṣareṇa caturdaśākṣaraṃ tryakṣareṇa trayodaśākṣaraṃ caturakṣareṇa dvādaśākṣaraṃ pañcākṣareṇaikādaśākṣaraṃ ṣaḍakṣareṇa daśākṣaraṃ saptākṣareṇa navākṣaramaṣṭābhirevāṣṭāvavṛñjata
<12.13.30>     evameva bhrātṛvyādbhūtiṃ vṛṅkte ya evaṃ veda
<12.13.31>     aparuddhayajña iva vā eṣa yat ṣoḍaśī kanīyasvina iva vai tarhi devā āsan bhūyasvino'surāḥ kanīyasvina bhūyasvinaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ vṛṅkte ya evaṃ veda
<12.13.32>   "yasmādanyo na paro'sti jāto ya ābabhūva bhuvanāni viśvā| prajāpatiḥ prajayā samvidānastrīṇi jyotīṃ ṣi sacate sa ṣoḍaśī"ityudgātā grahamavekṣate
<12.13.33>     jyotiṣmānasya ṣoḍaśī bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<12.13.34>     ekaviṃśa eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai pratitiṣṭhati

<13.1.1>   "govit pavasva vasuviddhiraṇyavid"iti pañcamasyāhnaḥ pratipadbhavati
<13.1.2>     govidvā etadvasuviddhiraṇyavidyacchakvaryaḥ
<13.1.3>     paśavaśśakvaryaḥ sarvaṃ paśubhirvindate
<13.1.4>   "tvaṃ suvīro asi somaviśvavid"ityeṣa yāva suvīro yasya paśavastadeva tadabhivadati
<13.1.5>     tāste kṣarantu madhumadghṛtaṃ paya"iti madhumadvai ghṛtaṃ payaḥ paśavaḥ kṣaranti tadeva tadabhivadati
<13.1.6>   "pavamānasya viśvavid"ityanurūpo bhavati
<13.1.7>     viśvameva tadvit tamabhivadati viśvaṃ hi paśubhirvindate
<13.1.8>   "pra te sargā asṛkṣata"iti sṛṣṭānīva hyetarhyahāni
<13.1.9>     pūrvamu caiva tadrūpamapareṇa rūpeṇānuvadati yat pūrvaṃ rūpeṇānuvadati tadanurūpasyānurūpatvam, anurūpa enaṃ putro jāyate ya evaṃ veda stotrīyānurupau tṛcau bhavataḥ prāṇāpānānāmavarudhyai
<13.1.10>     saptarcau bhavataḥ chandasāṃ dhṛtyai
<13.1.11>     caturṛco   bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai
<13.1.12>     tṛca uttamo bhavati yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyanti
<13.1.13>     triṇava eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai puṣṭyai trivṛdvā eṣa puṣṭaḥ

<13.2.1>   "tava śriyo varṣasyeva vidyuta"ityāgneyamājyaṃ bhavati
<13.2.2>     śrīrvai paśavaḥ śrīḥ śakvaryaḥ tadeva tadabhivadati
<13.2.3>   "gneścikitra uṣasāmivetaya"itītānīva hyetarhyahānīti
<13.2.4>   "ā te yatente rathyo yathā pṛthag"ityeva hyetarhyahāni yatante
<13.2.5>   "purūruṇā ciddhyastyavo nūnaṃ vāṃ varuṇa"iti maitrāvaruṇam
<13.2.6>     yadvai yajñasya duriṣṭaṃ tadvaruṇo gṛhṇāti tadeva tadavayajati
<13.2.7>   "uttiṣṭhannojasā saha"ityaindram
<13.2.8>     pañca vā ṛtava utthānasya rūpamojasā sahetyojasaiva vīryeṇa sahottiṣṭhanti
<13.2.9>   "indrāgnī yuvāmima"iti rāthantaramaindrāgnam
<13.2.10>     rathantarametat parokṣaṃ yacchakvaryo rāthantarameva tadrūpaṃ nirdyotayati stomaḥ

<13.3.1>   "rṣā soma dyumattama"iti viṣṇumatyo gāyatryo bhavanti
<13.3.2>     brahma vai gāyatrī yajño viṣṇurbrahmaṇyeva tadyajñaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<13.3.3>   "soma uṣvāṇassotṛbhir"iti simānāṃ rūpaṃ svenaivaitāstadrūpeṇa samardhayati
<13.3.4>   "yat soma citramukthyam"iti gāyatryaḥ satyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa tasmāt triṣṭubhāṃ loke kriyante
<13.3.5>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<13.3.6>     yaṇvaṃ bhavati paśavo vai yaṇvaṃ paśūnāmavarudhyai
<13.3.7>     santataṃ gāyati yajñasya santatye
<13.3.8>     adhyardheḍaṃ tathā hyetasyāhno rūpam
<13.3.9>     śākalaṃ bhavati
<13.3.10>     etena vai śakalaḥ pañcame'hani pratyatiṣṭhat pratitiṣṭhati śākalena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.3.11>     vārśaṃ bhavati
<13.3.12>     vṛśo vaijānastryaruṇasya traidhātavasyaikṣvākasya purohita āsīt sa aikṣvāko'dhāvayat brāhmaṇakumāraṃ rathena vyacchinat sa purohitamabravīt tava mā purodhāyāmidamīdṛgupāgāditi tametena sāmnā samairayat tadvāva sa tarhyakāmayata kāmasani sāma vārśaṃ kāmamevaitenāvarundhe
<13.3.13>     adhyardheḍaṃ tathā hyetasyāhno rūpam
<13.3.14>     mānavaṃ bhavati
<13.3.15>     etena vai manuḥ prajāpatiṃ bhūmānamagacchat prajāyate bahurbhavati mānavena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.3.16>     ānūpaṃ bhavati
<13.3.17>     etena vai vadhryaśva ānūpaḥ paśūnāṃ bhūmānamāśnuta paśūnāṃ bhūmānamaśnuta ānūpena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.3.18>     vāmraṃ bhavati
<13.3.19>     māmārṣeyeṇa praśastaṃ yaṃ vai gāmaśvaṃ puruṣaṃ praśaṃsanti vāma iti taṃ praśaṃsantyaharevaitena praśaṃsanti
<13.3.20>     adhyardheḍaṃ tathā hyetasyāhno rūpam
<13.3.21>     triṇidhanamāgneyaṃ bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai
<13.3.22>     agriḥ sṛṣṭo nodadīpyata taṃ prajāpatiretena sāmnopādhamat sa udadīpyata dīptiśca vā etat sāma brahmavarcasaṃ ca dīptiścaivaitena brahmavarcasaṃ cāvarundhe
<13.3.23>     śaiśavaṃ bhavati
<13.3.24>     śiśurvā āṅgiraso mantrakṛtāṃ mantrakṛdāsīt sa pit.ṛn putrakā ityāmantrayata, taṃ pitaro'bruvanna dharmaṃ karoṣi yo naḥ pit.ṛn sataḥ putrakā ityāmantrayama iti, so'bravīdahaṃ vāva vaḥ pitāsmi yo mantrakṛdasmīti, taṃ deveṣvapṛcchanta te devā abuvanneṣa vāva pitā yo mantrakṛditi tadvai sa udajayadujjayati śaiśavena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.3.25>     gāyatrīṣu stuvanti pratiṣṭhāyai brahmavarcasāya yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyanti
<13.3.26>     viṣṇumatyo gāyatryo bhavanti tadu traiṣṭubhādrūpānna yanti stomaḥ

<13.4.1>     indraḥ prajāpatimupādhāvadvṛtraṃ hanānīti tasmā etacchandobhya indriyaṃ vīryaṃ nirmāya prāyacchadetena śaknuhīti tacchakvarīṇāṃ śakvarītvaṃ sīmānamabhinat tat simā mahnyamakarot tanmahnyā mahān ghoṣa āsīt tanmahānāmnyaḥ
<13.4.2>     diśaḥ pañcapadā dādhārartūn ṣaṭpadā chandāṃsi saptapadā puruṣaṃ dvipadā
<13.4.3>     dvyopaśāḥ saṃstutā bhavanti tasmāddvyopaśāḥ paśavaḥ
<13.4.4>     iḍe abhito'thakāraṃ tasmācchṛṅge tīkṣṇīyasī stūpāt
<13.4.5>     upakṣudrā gāyati tasmādupakṣudrāḥ paśavaḥ
<13.4.6>     aṃsaśliṣṭā gāyati tasmādasaṃśliṣṭāḥ paśavaḥ
<13.4.7>     nānārūpā gāyati tasmānnānārūpāḥ paśavaḥ
<13.4.8>     āpo vai kṣīrarasā āsaṃste devāḥ pāpavasīyasādabibhayuryadapa upanidhāya stuvate pāpavasīyaso vidhṛtyai
<13.4.9>     vidhṛtiḥ pāpavasīyaso bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<13.4.10>     gāyatramayanaṃ bhavati brahmavarcasakāmasya svarṇidhanaṃ madhunāmuṣmiṃlloka upatiṣṭhate, traiṣṭubhamayanaṃ bhavatyojaskāmasyāthakāraṇidhana mājyenāmuṣmiṃlloka upatiṣṭhate, jāgatamayanaṃ bhavati paśukāmasyeḍānidhanaṃ payasāmuṣmiṃlloka upatiṣṭhate
<13.4.11>     añjasāryo mālyaḥ śakvarīḥ prārautsīditi hovācālammaṃ pārijānataṃ rajanaḥ kauṇayo yadyenāḥ pratiṣṭhāpaṃ śakṣyatītyetadvā etāsāmañjayati pratiṣṭhitā ya ābhiḥ kṣipraṃ prastutya kṣipramudgāyati
<13.4.12>     śakvarībhiḥ stutvā purīṣeṇa stuvate
<13.4.13>     paśavo vai śakvaryo gauṣṭhaḥ purīṣaṃ goṣṭhameva tat paśubhyaḥ paryasyanti tamevainān pravartyantyavisraṃsāya
<13.4.14>   "indro madāya vāvṛdha"ityavardhanta hyetarhi
<13.4.15>     tāsu bārhadgiram
<13.4.16>   "svādoritthā viṣuvata"iti viṣuvān vai pañcamamahastāsu rāyovājīyam
<13.4.7>     indro yatīn sālāvṛkebhyaḥ prāyacchat teṣāṃ traya udaśiṣyanta pṛthuraśmirbṛhadgirī rāyovājaste'bruvan ko na imān putrān bhariṣyatītyahamitīndro'bravīt tānādhinidhāya paricāryaṃ carad(?) vardhayaṃstān vardhayitvābravīt kumārakā varān vṛṇīdhvamiti, kṣatraṃ mahyamityabravīt pṛthuraśmistasmā etena pārthuraśmena kṣatraṃ prāyacchat kṣatrakāma etena stuvīta kṣatrasyaivāsya prakāśo bhavati, brahmavarcasaṃ mahyamityabravīt bṛhādgiristasmā etena bārhadgireṇa prāyacchat brahmavarcasakāma etena stuvīta brahmavarcasī bhavati, paśūnmahyamityabravīdrāyovājastasmā etena rāyovājīyena paśūn prāyacchat paśukāma etena stuvīta paśumān bhavati
<13.4.18>     pārthuraśmaṃ rājanyāya brahmasāma kuryāt bārhadgiraṃ brāhmaṇāya rāyovājīyaṃ vaiśyāya svenaivenāṃstadrūpeṇa samardhayati stomaḥ

<13.5.1>   "sāvyaṃśurmadāya"iti gāyatrī bhavati madavadvai rasavat tṛtīyasavanaṃ madameva tadrasaṃ dadhāti
<13.5.2>   "bhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśa"ityabhīti rathantarasya rūpaṃ bṛhaditi bṛhata ubhayossaha rūpamupaityubhau hi varṇāvetadahaḥ
<13.5.3>   "prāṇā śiśurmahīnām"iti simānāṃ rūpaṃ mahyo hi simāḥ svenaivaināstadrūpeṇa samardhayati
<13.5.4>   "pavasva vājasātaya"iti vaiṣṇavyo'nuṣṭubho bhavanti
<13.5.5>     yajño vai viṣṇuryadatra nāpi kriyate tadviṣṇunā yajñenāpi karoti
<13.5.6>   "indurvājī pavate gonyoghā"iti simānāṃ rūpaṃ svenaivaināstadrūpeṇa samardhayati
<13.5.7>     triṣṭubhaḥ satyo jagatyo rūpeṇa tasmājjagatīnāṃ loke kriyante
<13.5.7>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<13.5.9>     santani bhavati pañcamasyāhnassantatyai
<13.5.90>     vāgvā eṣā pratatā yaddvādaśāhastasyā eṣa viṣuvān yat pañcamamahastāmevaitena santanoti
<13.5.11>     cyāvanaṃ bhavati
<13.5.12>     prajātirvai cyāvanaṃ prajāyate bahurbhavati cyāvanena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.5.13>     ebhyo vai lokebhyo vṛṣṭirapākrāmat tāṃ prajāpatiścyāvanenācyāvayadyadacyāvayat taccyāvanasya cyāvanatvaṃ cyāvayati vṛṣṭiṃ cyāvanena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.5.14>     kroṣa bhavati
<13.5.15>     etena vā indra indrakrośe viśvāmitrajamadagnī imā gāva ityākrośat paśunāmavarudhyai krośaṃ kriyate
<13.5.16>     gaurīvitaṃ bhavati yadeva gaurīvitasya brāhmaṇam
<13.5.17>     ṛṣabhaḥ śākvaro bhavati
<13.5.18>     paśavo vai śakvaryaḥ paśuṣveva tanmithunamapyarjati prajātyai na ha vā anṛṣabhāḥ paśavaḥ prajāyante
<13.5.19>     pārthaṃ bhavati
<13.5.20>     etena vai pṛthurvainya ubhayeṣāṃ paśūnāmādhipatyamāśnutobhayeṣāṃ paśunāmādhipatyamaśnute pārthena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.5.21>     aṣṭeḍaḥ padastobho bhavati
<13.5.22>     indro vṛtrāya vajramudayacchat taṃ ṣoḍaśabhirbhogaiḥ paryabhujat sa etaṃ padastobhamapaśyat tenāpāveṣṭayadapāveṣṭayanniva gāyet pāpmano'pahatyai
<13.5.23>     pāpmā vāva sa tamagṛhṇāt taṃ padastobhenāpāhatāpa pāpmānaṃ hate padastobhena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.5.24>     padoruttamamapaśyat tat padastobhasya padastobhatvam
<13.5.25>     dvādaśanidhano bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai
<13.5.26>     dāśaspatyaṃ bhavati
<13.5.27>     yāṃ vai gāṃ praśaṃsanti dāśaspatyeti tāṃ praśaṃsantyaharevaitena praśaṃsanti
<13.5.28>     nidhanāntāḥ pavamānā bhavantyahno dhṛtyai stomaḥ

<13.6.1>   "āte agna idhīmahi"iti
<13.6.2>     apacchidiva vā etadyajñakāṇḍaṃ yadukthāni yadeti salomatvāyaiva
<13.6.3>   "indrāya sāma gāyata"iti pūrṇāḥ kakubhastenānaśanāyuko bhavati
<13.6.4>     puruṣo vai kakuppuruṣameva tanmadhyataḥ prīṇāti
<13.6.5>   "sāvi soma indra ta"iti simānāṃ rūpaṃ svenaivaināṃstadrūpeṇa samardhayati
<13.6.6>     sañjayaṃ bhavati
<13.6.7>     devāśca vā asurāśca samadadhata yatare naḥ sañjayāṃsteṣāṃ naḥ paśavo'sāniti te devā asurān sañjayena samajayan yat samajayaṃstasmāt sañjayaṃ paśūnāmavarudhyai sañjayaṃ kriyate
<13.6.8>     saumitraṃ bhavati
<13.6.9>     dīrghajihvī vā idaṃ rakṣo yajñahā yajñiyānavalihatyacarat tāmindraḥ kayācana māyayā hantuṃ nāśaṃsatātha ha sumitraḥ kutsaḥ kalyāṇa āsa tamabravīdimāmacchā brūṣveti tāmacchā brūta sainamabravīnnāhaitanna śuśruva priyamiva tu me hṛdayasyeti tāmajñapayat tāṃ saṃskṛte'hatāṃ tadvāva tau tarhyakāmayetāṃ kāmasani sāma saumitraṃ kāmamevaitenāvarundhe
<13.6.10>     sumitraḥ san krūramakarityenaṃ vāgabhyavadat taṃ śugārcchat sa tapo'tapyata sa etat saumitramapaśyat tena śucamapāhatāpaśucaṃ hate saumitreṇa tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.6.11>     mahāvaiśvāmitraṃ bhavati
<13.6.12>     pāpmānaṃ hatvā yadamahīyanta tanmahāvaiśvāmitrasya mahāvaiśvāmitratvam
<13.6.13>     hāyā ihayā ohā oheti paśūnevaitena nyauhanta
<13.6.14>     trīḍaṃ bhavati trirātrasya dhṛtyai
<13.6.15>     dravantīmiḍāmuttamāmupayanti ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ santatyai
<13.6.16>     triṇava eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai puṣṭyai trivṛdvā eṣa puṣṭhaḥ

<13.7.1>   "jyotiryajñasya pavate madhupriyam"iti ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ pratipadbhavati
<13.7.2>     jyotirvai gāyatrī chandamāṃ jyotiḥ revatī sāmnāṃ jyotistrayastriṃśaḥ stomānāṃ jyotireva tat samyaksaṃdadhāsyapi ha putrasya putro jyotiṣmān bhavati
<13.7.3>   "madhu priyam"iti paśavo vai revatyo madhupriyaṃ tadeva tadabhivadati
<13.7.4>   "madintamo matsara indriyo rasa"itīndriyaṃ vai vīryaṃ rasaḥ paśavastadeva tadabhivadati
<13.7.5>   "mṛkṣata pravājina"ityanurūpo bhavati
<13.7.6>     sṛṣṭānīva hyetarhyahāni
<13.7.7>     pūrvamu caiva tadrūpamapareṇa rūpeṇānuvadati yat pūrvaṃ rūpamapareṇa rūpeṇānuvadati tadanurūpasyānurūpatvamanurūpa enaṃ putro jāyate ya evaṃ veda stotrīyānurūpau tṛcau bhavataḥ prāṇāpānānāmavarudhyai
<13.7.8>     daśarcau bhavato daśākṣarā virāṭvairājamannamannādyasyāvarudhyai
<13.7.9>     uttaro daśarco bhavati sodarka indriyasya vīryasya rasasyānatikṣārāya
<13.7.10>     yatra ve devā indriyaṃ vīryaṃ rasamapaśyaṃstadanunyatudan
<13.7.11>     caturṛco bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai
<13.7.12>     dhvasre vai puruṣantī tarantapurumīḍhābhyāṃ vaitadaśvibhyāṃ sahasrāṇyaditsatāṃ tāvaikṣetāṃ kathaṃ nāvidamāttamapratigṛhītaṃ syāditi tau pratyetāṃ dhvasrayoḥ puruṣantyorā sahasrāṇi dadmahe (daprahe) tarat sa mandī dhāvatīti tato vai tat tayorāttamapratigṛhītamabhavat
<13.7.13>     āttamasyāpratigṛhītaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<13.7.14>     vinārāśaṃso bhavatyubhayasyānnādyasyāvarudhyai mānuṣasya ca daivasya ca
<13.7.15>     tṛca uttamo bhavati yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyanti
<13.7.16>     trayastriṃśa eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai devatāsu vā eṣa pratiṣṭhitaḥ
<13.8.1>   "imaṃ stomamarhate jātavedasa"ityāgneyamājyaṃ bhavati sodarkamindriyasya vīryasya rasasyānatikṣārāya yatra vai devā indriyaṃ vīryaṃ rasamapaśyaṃstadanunyatudan
<13.8.2>   "prativāṃ sūra udita"iti sūravanmaitrāvaruṇam
<13.8.3>     anto vai sūro'nta etat ṣaṣṭhamaharahnāmanta eva tadante stuvate pratiṣṭhāyai
<13.8.4>   "bhindhi viśvā apadviṣa"ityaindraṃ sodarkamindriyasya vīryasya rasasyānatikṣārāya yatra vai devā indriyaṃ vīryaṃ rasamapaśyaṃstadanunyatudan
<13.8.5>   "yajñasya hi stha ṛtvija"ityaindrāgnaṃ sodarkamindriyasya vīryasya rasasyānatikṣārāya yatra vai devā indriyaṃ vīryaṃ rasamapaśyaṃstadanunyatudan stomaḥ

<13.9.1>   "indrāyendo marutvata"iti marutvatyo gāyatryo bhavanti
<13.9.2>     marutvaddhi mādhyandinaṃ savanam
<13.9.3>   "mṛjyamānaḥ suhastya"iti simānāṃ rūpam
<13.9.4>     samānaṃ vai simānāṃ rūpaṃ revatīnāṃ ca simābhyo hyadhirevatyaḥ prajāyante
<13.9.5>   "etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipa"ityādityā ādityā vā imāḥ prajāstāsāmeva madhyataḥ pratitiṣṭhati
<13.9.6>     gāyatryaḥ satyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa tasmāt triṣṭubhāṃ loke kriyante
<13.9.7>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<13.9.8>     iṣovṛdhīyaṃ bhavati
<13.9.9>     paśavo vā iṣovṛdhīyaṃ paśūnāmavarudhyā iṣe vai pañcamamaharvṛdhe ṣaṣṭhamavardhanta hyetarhi yajamānamevaitena vardhayanti
<13.9.10>     krauñcaṃ bhavati
<13.9.11>     kruṅṅeṣyamaharavindadeṣyamiva vai ṣaṣṭhamaharevaitena vindanti
<13.9.12>     vājadāvaryo bhavanti
<13.9.13>     annaṃ vai vājo'nnādyasyāvarudhyai yadā hi vā annamatha gaurathāśvo'tha puruṣo vājī
<13.9.14>     revatyo bhavanti pratiṣṭhāyai
<13.9.15>     ṣaṇṇidhanī ṣaḍrātrasya dhṛtyai
<13.9.16>     āpo vai revatyastā yat pṛṣṭhaṃ kuryurapaśuryajamānaḥ syāt paśūnasya nirdaheyuryatra vā āpo vivartante tadoṣadhayo jāyante'tha yatrāvatiṣṭhante nirmṛtukāstatra bhavanti tasmāt pavamāne kurvanti parācā hi pavamānena stuvate
<13.9.17>     methī vā iṣovṛdhīyaṃ rajjuḥ krauñcaṃ vatso vājadāvaryo revatyo mātaro yadetānyevaṃ sāmāni kriyanta evameva prattāṃ dugdhe
<13.9.18>     aukṣṇorandhre bhavataḥ
<13.9.19>     ukṣṇorandhro vā etābhyāṃ kāvyo'ñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.9.20>     vājajidbhavati sarvasyāptyai sarvasya jityai sarvaṃ vā ete vājaṃ jayanti ye ṣaṣṭhamaharāgacchanti
<13.9.21>     annaṃ vai vājo'nnādyasyāvarudhyai
<13.9.22>     varuṇasāma bhavati
<13.9.23>     etena vai varuṇo rājyamādhipatyamagacchadyaśo'bhavadrājyamādhipatyaṃ gacchati yaśo bhavati varuṇasāmnā tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.9.24>     aṅgirasāṃ goṣṭho bhavati
<13.9.25>     paśavo vai revatyo gauṣṭhameva tat paśubhyaḥ paryasyanti tamevainān pravartayantyavisraṃsāya
<13.9.26>     ihavadvāmadevyaṃ bhavati
<13.9.27>     etena vai vāmadevo'nnasya purodhāmagacchadannaṃ vai brahmaṇaḥ purodhā annasyāvarudhyai
<13.9.28>     gāyatrīṣu stuvanti pratiṣṭhāyai brahmavarñcasāya yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyanti
<13.9.29>     marutvatyo gāyatryo bhavanti tadu traiṣṭubhādrūpānna yanti stomaḥ

<13.10.1>     yātayāmānyanyāni chandāṃsyayātayāmā gāyatrī tasmādgāyatrīṣu stuvanti
<13.10.2>   "surūpa kṛtnumūtaya"ityabhyārambheṇa ṣaṭpadāḥ ṣaṣṭhasyāhno rūpaṃ tena ṣaṣṭhamaharārabhante santatyai
<13.10.3>   "ubhe yadindra rodasī"iti ṣaṭpadāḥ ṣaṣṭhasyāhno rūpaṃ tena ṣaṣṭhamaharārabhante santatyai
<13.10.4>     revatīṣu vāravantīyaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavati
<13.10.5>     apāṃ vā eṣa raso yadrevatyo revatīnāṃ raso yadvāravantīyaṃ sarasā eva tadrevatīḥ prayuṅkte yadvāravantīyena pṛṣṭhena stuvate
<13.10.6>     revadvā etadraivatyaṃ yadvāravantīyamasya revān revātyā jāyate
<13.10.7>     revān bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<13.10.8>     keśine vā etaddālbhyāya sāmāvirabhavat tadenamabravīdagātāro mā gāyanti mā mayodgāsiṣuriti kathaṃ ta āgā bhagava ityabravīdāgeyamevāsmyāgāyanniva gāyet pratiṣṭhāyai tadalammaṃ pārijānataṃ paścādakṣaṃ śayānametāmāgāṃ gāyantamajānāt tamabravīt purastvā dadhā iti tamabruvan ko nvayaṃ kasmā alamityalaṃ nu vai mahyamiti tadalammasyālammatvam
<13.10.9>     iheheti gāyet pratiṣṭhāyai
<13.10.11>     ṛṣabho raivato bhavati
<13.10.11>     paśavo vai raivatyaḥ paśuṣveva tanmithunamapyarjati prajātyai na ha vā anṛṣabhāḥ paśavaḥ prajāyante
<13.10.12>     śyeno bhavati
<13.10.13>     śyeno ha vai pūrvapretāni vayāṃsyāpnoti pūrvapretānīva vai pūrvāṇyahāni teṣāmāptyai śyenaḥ kriyate
<13.10.14>     śyeno vā etadahaḥ sampārayitumarhati sa hi vayasāmāśiṣṭhastasyānapahananāya sampāraṇāyaitat kriyate'nto hi ṣaṣṭhaṃ cāhaḥ saptamaṃ ca
<13.10.15>     brahmavādino vadanti yadbṛhadāyatanāni pṛṣṭhānyathaite dve gāyatryau dve jagatyau kva tarhi bṛhatyo bhavantīti
<13.10.16>     ye dve jagatyoḥ pade te gāyatryā upasampadyete tat sarvā bṛhatyo bhavantyāyatane pṛṣṭhāni yātayatyāyatanavān bhavati
<13.10.17>     ṣaṭpadāsu stuvanti ṣaḍrātrasya dhṛtyai
<13.10.18>     saptapadayā yajati saptamasyāhnaḥ santatyai stomaḥ

<13.11.1>   "parisvāno giriṣṭhā"iti parivatyo gāyatryo bhavanti sarvasya paryāptyai
<13.11.2>   "sasunveyo vasūnām"iti paśavo vai vasu paśūnāmavarudhyai
<13.11.3>   "taṃ vassakhāyo madāya"iti vālakhilyāḥ
<13.11.4>     vālakhilyāvetau tṛcau ṣaṣṭhe cāhani saptame ca
<13.11.5>     yadetau vālakhilyau tṛcau bhavato'hnoreva vyatiṣaṅgayāvyatisraṃsāya santatyai
<13.11.6>   "somāḥ pavanta indava"ityanuṣṭubho nibhasado bhavanti pratiṣṭhāyai
<13.11.7>   "yā pavā pavasvainā vasūni"iti triṣṭubhassatyo jagatyo rūpeṇa tasmājjagatīnāṃ loke kriyante
<13.11.8>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brahmāṇam
<13.11.9>     vaidanvātāni bhavanti
<13.11.10>     vidanvān vai bhārgava indrasya pratyahaṃstaṃ śugārcchat sa tapo'tapyata sa etāni vaidanvatānyapaśyat taiḥ śucamapāhatāpaśucaṃ hate vaidanvataistuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.11.11>     bharadvājasya lomata bhavati
<13.11.12>     paśavo vai loma paśūnāmavarudhyai
<13.11.13>     tadu dīrghamityāhurāyurvai dīrghamāyuṣo'varudhyai
<13.11.14>     kārṇaśravasaṃ bhavati śṛṇvanti tuṣṭuvānam
<13.11.15>     karṇaśravā vā etadāṅigarasaḥ paśukāmaḥ sāmāpaśyat tena sahasraṃ paśūnasṛjata yadetat sāma bhavati paśūnāṃ puṣṭyai
<13.11.16>     gaurīvitaṃ bhavati gaurivitasya brāhmaṇam
<13.11.17>     madhuścyunnidhanaṃ bhavati
<13.11.18>     paramasyānnādyasyāvarudhyai paramaṃ vā etadannādya yanmadhu
<13.11.19>     prajāpatervā etau stanau yadghṛtaścyunnidhanaṃ ca madhuścyunnidhanaṃ ca yajño vai prajāpatistametābhyāṃ dugdhe yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate taṃ dugdhe
<13.11.20>     krauñce bhavataḥ
<13.11.22>     śnauṣṭhaṃ bhavati
<13.11.23>     śnuṣṭirvā etenāṅgiraso'ñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāt lokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<13.11.24>     agnervā etadvaiśvānaraṃ sāma dīdihīti nidhanamupayanti dīdā eva hyagnirvaiśvānaraḥ
<13.11.25>     nidhanāntāḥ pavamānā bhavantyahno dhṛtyai stomaḥ

<13.12.1>     brahmavādino vadanti prāyaṇato dvipadāḥ kāryā udayanatā3 iti
<13.12.2>     udayanata eva kāryāḥ puruṣo vai dvipadāḥ pratiṣṭhāyai
<13.12.3>     vācā vai sarvaṃ yajñaṃ tanvate tasmāt sarvāṃ vācaṃ puruṣo vadati sarvā hyasmin saṃstutā pratitiṣṭhati
<13.12.4>     gūrdo bhavati
<13.12.5>     gopāyanānāṃ vai sattramāsīnānāṃ kirātakulyāvasuramāye antaḥparidhyasūn prākiratāṃ te''gre tvanno antama"ityagnimupāsīdaṃstenāsūnaspṛṇvaṃstadvāva te tarhyakāmayanta kāmasani sāma gūrdaḥ kāmamevaitenāvarundhe
<13.12.6>     gotamasya bhandra bhavati
<13.12.7>     āśiṣamevāsmā etenāśāste sāma hi satyāśīḥ
<13.12.8>     etena vai gotamo jemānaṃ mahimānamagacchat tasmādye ca parāñco gotamādye cārvāñcasta ubhaye gotamā ṛṣayo bruvate
<13.12.9>     udvaṃśaputrā bhavati
<13.12.10>     yadvā udvaṃśīyaṃ tadudvaṃśaputraḥ
<13.12.11>     ardheḍāmatisvarati
<13.12.12>     tasmādūdhardhārā ati carantīḍāyāmantataḥ paśuṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<13.12.13>     kḷpta upariṣṭādṛksāma ca vimucyete abhyu svareṇa saptamamahaḥ svarati santatyai
<13.12.14>     ūhuṣīva vā etarhi vāgyadā ṣaḍahaḥ santiṣṭhate na bahu vadennānyaṃ pṛcchennānyasmai prabrūyāt
<13.12.15>     madhu vāśayedghṛtaṃ vā yathehuṣo bahaṃ pratyanakti tathā tat
<13.12.16>     trayastriṃśa eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai devatāsu vā eṣa pratiṣṭhitaḥ

<14.1.1>     āpyante vā etat stomāśchandāṃsi yat ṣaḍaha āpyate
<14.1.2>     āpte ṣaḍahe chandāṃsi stomān kṛtvā prayanti
<14.1.3>   "pra kāvyamuśanevara bruvāṇa"iti gāyatryā rūpeṇa prayanti
<14.1.4>     iyaṃ vai gāyatryasyāmeva pratiṣṭhāya prayanti
<14.1.5>     triṣṭuppratipadbhavati
<14.1.6>     ojo vīryaṃ triṣṭubojasyeva vīrye parākramya prayanti
<14.1.7>     stotrīyastṛco bhavati prāṇāpānānāmavarudhyai
<14.1.8>     harivatyo bhavanti chandomānāmayātayāmatāyai
<14.1.9>     dvādaśarcau bhavataḥ
<14.1.10>     dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsarameva tatpūrvasmai ṣaḍahāya pratyudyacchati savīvadhatāyai
<14.1.11>     caturviṃśatirbhavanti caturviṃśatirardhamāsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsarameva tatpūrvasmai ṣaḍahāya pratyudyacchati savīvadhatāyai
<14.1.12>     dṛta aindrota iti hovācābhipratārī kākṣasenirye mahāvṛkṣasyāgraṃ gacchanti ka te tato bhavanti prarājan pakṣiṇaḥ patantyavāpakṣāḥ padyante
<14.1.13>     ye vai vidvāṃsaste pakṣiṇo ye'vidvāṃsaste'pakṣāstrivṛt pañcadaśāveva stomau pakṣau kṛtvā svarga lokaṃ prayanti
<14.1.14>     caturviṃśa eva stomo bhavati teja se brahmavarcasāya

<14.2.1>   "mūrdhānaṃ divo aratiṃ pṛthivyā"ityāgreyamājyaṃ bhavati
<14.2.2>     mūrdhā vā eṣa divo yastṛtīyastrirātraḥ
<14.2.3>   "vaiśvānaramṛta ājātamagnim"iti vaiśvānara iti vā agneḥ priyaṃ dhāma priyeṇaivainaṃ taddhāmnā parokṣamupaśikṣati
<14.2.4>   "pra vo mitrāya gāyata"iti dyāvāpṛthivīyaṃ maitrāvaruṇaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī vai mitrāvaruṇayoḥ priyaṃ dhāma priyeṇaivainau taddhāmnā parokṣamupaśikṣati
<14.2.5>   "indrāyāhi citrabhānav"ityārbhavamaindramṛbhavo vā indrasya priyaṃ dhāma priyeṇaivainaṃ taddhāmnā parokṣamupaśikṣati
<14.2.6>   "tamīḍiṣva yo arciṣā"ityaniruktamaindrāgnaṃ devatānāmanabhidharṣāyottamārdhe'nirāha devatānāmapraṇāśāya stomaḥ

<14.3.1>   "vṛṣā pavasva dhāraya"iti gāyatrī bhavatyahno dhṛtyai
<14.3.2>     vṛṣaṇvatyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa traiṣṭubhaṃ hyetadahaḥ
<14.3.3>   "punānaḥ soma dhāraya"iti dhṛtyai
<14.3.4>   "pro ayāsīdindurindrasya niṣkṛtam"iti pravatyo bhavanti praṇinīṣeṇyamiva hyetadahaḥ
<14.3.5>     jagatyaḥ satyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa tasmāt triṣṭubhāṃ loke kriyante
<14.3.7>     santani bhavati saptamasyāhnaḥ santatyai
<14.3.8>     yathā vai vyokasau vipradravata evamete ṣaṣṭhaṃ cāhaḥ saptamaṃ ca vipradravatastau yathā samānīya saṃyujyādevamevaite etena sāmnā saṃyunakti
<14.3.10>     yajño vai devebhyo'pākrāmat sa suparṇarūpaṃ kṛtvācarat taṃ devā etaiḥ sāmabhirārabhanta yajña iva vā eṣa yacchandomā yajñasyaivaiṣa ārambhaḥ
<14.3.11>     rohitakūlīyaṃ bhavatyājijityāyai
<14.3.12>     etena vai viśvāmitro rohitābhyāṃ rohitakūla ājimajayat
<14.3.13>     viśvāmitro bharatānāṃ manassatyāyāt so'dantibhirnāma janatayāṃśaṃ prāsyate māṃ māṃ yūyaṃ astikāṃ jayāthemāni mahyaṃ yūyaṃ pūrayātha yadīmāvidaṃ rohitāvaśmācitaṃ kūlamudvahāta iti sa ete sāmanī apaśyat tābhyāṃ yuktvā prāsedhat sa udjayat
<14.3.14>     ājirvā eṣa pratato yaddvādaśāhastasyaite ujjityai
<14.3.15>     kaṇvarathantaraṃ bhavati tejo vā etadrathantarasya yat kaṇvarathantaram
<14.3.16>     sarasameva tadrathantaraṃ prayuṅkte yat kaṇvarathantareṇa saptame'hani stuvate
<14.3.17>     jāmi dvādaśāhasyāstīti ha smāhogradevo rājanirbārhataṃ ṣaṣṭhamaharbārhataṃ saptamaṃ yat kaṇvarathantaraṃ bhavati tenājāmi
<14.3.18>     gauṅgavaṃ bhavati
<14.3.19>     agnirakāmayatānnādaḥ syāmiti sa tapo'tapyata sa etadgauṅgavamapaśyat tenānnādo'bhavadyadannaṃ vitvā gardadyadagaṅgūyat tadgauṅgavasya gauṅgavatvamannādyasyāvarudhyai gauṅgavaṃ kiyate
<14.3.20>     yat sāma devatā praśaṃyati tena yajamānāḥ satyāśiṣaḥ satyāśiṣo'sāmeti vai sattramāsate satyāśiṣa eva bhavanti
<14.3.21>     āyāsyaṃ bhavati tiraścīnanidhanaṃ pratiṣṭhāyai
<14.3.22>     ayāsyo vā āṅgirasa ādityānāṃ dīkṣitānāmannamāśnāt sa vyabhraṃśata sa etānyāyāsyānyapaśyat tairātmānaṃ samaśrīṇādvibhraṣṭamiva vai saptamamaharyadetat sāma bhavatyahareva tena saṃśrīṇāti
<14.3.23>     pravadbhargavaṃ bhavati

<14.4.1>   "vayaṃ ghatvā sutāvanta"iti bṛhatyo varṣīyaśchanda ākramate'napabhraṃśāya
<14.4.2>   "nakiṣṭaṃ karmaṇā naśad"iti bṛhatyaḥ satyo'bhyārambheṇa jagatyaḥ
<14.4.3>     apabhraṃśa iva vā eṣa yajjyāyasaḥ stomāt kanīyāṃsaṃ stomamupayanti yadetā abhyārambheṇa jagatyo bhavantyahna eva pratyuttambhāya
<14.4.4>     abhinidhanaṃ kāṇvaṃ bhavita
<14.4.5>     abhinidhanena vā indro vṛtrāya vajraṃ prāharat tamastṛṇuta stṛṇute bhrātṛvyamabhinidhanena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.4.7>     vaikhānasā vā ṛṣaya indrasya priyā āsaṃstān rahasyurdevamalimluḍmunimaraṇe'mārayat taṃ devā abruvan kva tarṣayo (?)'bhūvānniti tān praiṣamaicchat tānnāvindat sa imān lokānekadhāreṇāpunāt tānmunimaraṇe'vindat tānetena sāmnā samairayat tadvāva sa tarhyakāmayata kāmasani sāma vaikhānasaṃ kāmamevaitenāvarundhe stomaḥ

<14.5.1>   "yaste mado vareṇya"iti gāyatrī bhavati madavadvai rasavat tṛtīyasavanaṃ madameva tadrasaṃ dadhāti
<14.5.2>   "eṣasya dhārayā suta"iti kakubhaḥ satyo'bhyārambheṇa triṣṭubhaḥ
<14.5.3>     apabhraṃśa iva vā eṣa yat jyāyasaḥ stomāt kanīyāṃsaṃ stomamupayanti yadetā abhyārambheṇa triṣṭubho bhavantyahna eva pratyuttambhāya
<14.5.4>   "sakhāya āniṣīdata"iti vālakhilyā vālakhilyāvetau tṛcau ṣaṣṭhe cāhani saptame ca yadetau vākhilyau tṛcau bhavato'hnoreva vyatiṣaṅgāyāvyavasraṃsāya santatyai
<14.5.5>   "purojitī vo andhasa"iti virāḍannaṃ virāḍannādyasyāvarudhyai
<14.5.6>   "pra vājyakṣār"ityakṣarapaṅktiḥ stomānāṃ prabhūtiḥ
<14.5.7>     atho etad(?) dhyevaitarhi chando'yātayāma yadakṣarapaṅktistena chandomā ayātayāmānaḥ kriyante
<14.5.8>     brahmavādino vadanti yat ṣaḍahe stomāśchandāṃsyāpyante kiṃ chandasaśchandomā ityetacchandaso yadetā akṣarapaṅktaya iti brūyāt
<14.5.9>   "ye somāsaḥ parāvati"iti parāvatamiva vā etarhi yajño gatastamevaitenānvicchanti
<14.5.12>     dakṣaṇidhanaṃ bhavati
<14.5.13>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata tā asmāt sṛṣṭā abalā ivācchadayaṃstāsvetena sāmnā dakṣāyetyojo vīryamadadhādyadetat sāma bhavatyoja eva vīryamātman dhatte
<14.5.14>     śārkaraṃ bhavati
<14.5.15>     indraṃ sarvāṇi bhūtānyastuvan sa śarkaraṃ śiśumārarṣimupetyābravīt stuhi meti so'paḥ praskandannabravīdetāvato'haṃ tvāṃ stuyāmiti tasmādapāṃ vegamavejayat sa hīna ivāmanyata sa etat sāmāpaśyat tenāpo'nusamāśnuta tadvāva sa tarhyakāmayata kāmasani sāma śārkaraṃ kāmamevaitenāvarundhe
<14.5.13>     plavo bhavati
<14.5.17>     samudraṃ vā ete prasnāntītyāhurye dvādaśāhamupayantīti yo vā aplavaḥ samudraṃ prarunāti na sa tata udeti yata plavo bhavati svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
<14.5.18>     ativiśvāni duritā taremeti yadevaiṣāṃ duṣṭutaṃ duśśastaṃ tadetena taranti
<14.5.19>     ekādaśākṣaraṇidhano bhavatyekādaśākṣarā triṣṭubojīvīryaṃ triṣṭubojasyeva vīrye pratitiṣṭhati
<14.5.20>     gaurīvitaṃ bhavati yadeva gaurīvitasya brahmaṇam
<14.5.21>     kārtayaśaṃ bhavati
<14.5.22>     hīti nidhanamupayanti pāpmano'pahatyai
<14.5.23>     apa pāpmānaṃ hate kārtayaśena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.5.24>     sāhaiviṣaṃ bhavati
<14.5.25>     suhavirvā etenāṅgiraso'ñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.5.26>     brahmāvādino vadanti yat ṣaḍahe stomāśchandāṃsyāpyante kiṃ chandasaśchandomā iti puruṣaśchandasa iti brūyāt puruṣo vai pāṅktaḥ puruṣo dvipadā chandomānāmayātayāmatāyai
<14.5.27>     jarābodhīyaṃ bhavatyannādyasyāvarudhyai
<14.5.28>     annaṃ vai jarābodhīyaṃ mukhaṃ gāyatrī mukha eva tadannaṃ dhatte'nnamatti
<14.5.29>     annādo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<14.5.30>     gāyatrīṣu stuvanti pratiṣṭhāyai brahmavarcasāya yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyanti
<14.5.31>     iḍāntāḥ pāvamānā bhavanti paśavo vā iḍāḥ paśavaśchandomāḥ paśuṣveva tat paśūn dadhāti stomaḥ

<14.6.1>   "ā te vatso mano yamad"ityapacchidiva vā etadyajñakāṇḍaṃ yadukthāni yadeti salomatvāyaiva
<14.6.2>   "tvaṃ na indrābhara"iti pūrṇāḥ kakubhaḥ
<14.6.3>     apabhraṃśa iva vā eṣa yajjayāyasaḥ stomāt kanīyāṃsaṃ stomamupayanti yadetāḥ pūrṇāḥ kakubho bhavantyanapabhraṃśāya
<14.6.6>     vatsaśca vai medhātithiśca kāṇvāvāstāṃ taṃ vatsaṃ medhātithirākrośadabrāhmaṇo'si śūdrāputra iti so'bravīdṛtenāgniṃ vyayāva yataro nau brahmīyāniti vātsena vatso vyainmaidhātithena medhātithistasya na loma ca nauṣat tadvāva sa tarhyakāmayata kāmasani sāma vātsaṃ kāmamevaitenāvarundhe
<14.6.7>     sauśravasaṃ bhavati
<14.6.8>     upagurvai sauśravasaḥ kutsasyauravasya purohita āsīt sa kutsaḥ paryaśapadya indraṃ yajātā iti sa indraḥ suśravasamupetyābravīdyajasva māśanāyāmivā iti tamayajata sa indraḥ puroḍāśastaḥ kutsamupetyābravīdayakṣata mā kva te pariśaptamabhūditi kastvā yaṣṭeti suśravā iti sa kutsa aurava upāgāḥ sauśravasasyodgāyata audumbaryā śiro'cchinat sa śuśravā indramabravīt tvattanādvai medamīdṛgupāgāditi tametena sāmnā samairayat tadvāva sa tarhyakāmayata kāmasani sāma sauśravasaṃ kāmamevaitenāvarundhe
<14.6.9>     vīṅkaṃ bhavati
<14.6.10>     cyavano vai dādhīco'śvinoḥ priya āsīt so'jīryat tametena sāmnāpsu vyaiṅkayatāṃ taṃ punaryuvānamakurutāṃ tadvāva tau tarhyakāmayetāṃ kāmasani sāma vīṅkaṃ kāmamevaitenāvarundhe
<14.6.11>     caturviṃśa eva stomo bhavati tejase brahmavarcasāya

<14.7.1>   "śiśuṃ jajñānaṃ haryataṃ mṛjanti"ityaṣṭamasyāhnaḥ pratidbhavati
<14.7.2>     śiśuriva vā eṣa saptamenāhnā jāyate tamaṣṭamenāhnā mṛjanti
<14.7.3>     stotrīyastṛco bhavati prāṇāpānānāmavarudhyai
<14.7.4>     harivatyo bhavanti chandomānāmayātayāmatāyai
<14.7.5>     navarcā bhavanti
<14.7.7>     pañcarco bhavati pañcapadā paṅktiḥ pāṅktamannamannādyasyāvarudhyai
<14.7.8>     bāṇavān bhavatyanto vai bāṇo'nta etadaṣṭamamahnāmanta eva tadantena stuvate pratiṣṭhāyai
<14.7.9>     trayastṛcā bhavanti prāṇāpānānāṃ santatyai

<14.8.1>   "gniṃ vo devamagnibhiḥ sajoṣā"ityāgneyamājyaṃ bhavati
<14.8.2>     agnibhirityeva pūrvāṇyahāni abhi samiddhānyaṣṭamamaharabhisamindhe
<14.8.3>   "mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmaha"iti bārhataṃ maitrāvaruṇam
<14.8.4>     ugragādhamiva vā etacchandomāstadyathāta ugragādhe vyātiṣajya gāhanta evamevaitadrūpe vyatiṣajati chandomānāmasaṃvyāthāya
<14.8.5>   "tamindraṃ vājayāmasi"ityaindram
<14.8.6>     aṣṭamena vai devā ahnendramavājayannavamena pāpmānamaghnannaharevaitena vājayanti
<14.8.7>   "indre agnā namo bṛhad"iti bārhatamaindrāgnam
<14.8.8>     ugragādhamiva vā etadyacchandomāstadyathāta ugragādhe vyatiṣajya gāhanta evamevaitadrape vyatiṣajati chandomānāmasaṃvyāthāya stomaḥ

<14.9.1>   "dhvaryo adribhiḥ sutam"iti gāyatrī bhavatyahno dhṛtyai
<14.9.2>     gāyatryaḥ satyastriṣṭubhorūpeṇa traiṣṭubhaṃ hyetadahaḥ
<14.9.3>   "bhi somāsa āyava"ityabhīti rathantarasya rūpaṃ bṛhaditi bṛhata ubhayoḥ saha rūpamupaityubhau hi varṇāvetadahaḥ
<14.9.4>   "dhartā divaḥ pavate kṛtvyo rasa"ityadhṛta iva vā eṣastryaho yaddharteti dhṛtyā eva
<14.9.5>     jagatyassatyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa tasmāt triṣṭubho loke kriyante
<14.9.6>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<14.9.7>     vairūpaṃ bhavati
<14.9.8>     paśavo vai vairūpaṃ paśūnāmavarudhyai virūpaḥ saṃvatsaro virūpamannamannādyasyāvarudhyai
<14.9.9>     āśu bhārgavaṃ bhavati
<14.9.10>     aharvā etadavlīyata taddevā āśunābhyadhinvaṃstadāśorāśutvam
<14.9.11>     mārgīyavaṃ bhavati
<14.9.12>     devaṃ vā etaṃ mṛgayuriti vadantyetena vai sa ubhayeṣāṃ paśūnāmādhipatyamāśnutobhayeṣāṃ paśūnāmādhipatyamaśnute mārgīyaveṇa tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.9.13>     saumitraṃ bhavati
<14.9.14>     yadeva saumitrasya brāhmaṇam
<14.9.15>     aiṭataṃ bhavati
<14.9.16>     iṭan vā etena kāvyo'ñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.9.17>     sākamaśvaṃ bhavati yadeva  sākamaśvasya brāhmaṇam
<14.9.18>     tadu dhurāṃ sāmetyāhuḥ prāṇā vai dhuraḥ prāṇānāmavarudhyai
<14.9.19>     vilambasauparṇaṃ bhavati
<14.9.20>     ātmā vā eṣa sauparṇānāṃ yadaṣṭame'hani pakṣāvetāvabhito bhavato ye saptamanavamayorvīva vā antarātmā pakṣau lambate yadantarātmā pakṣau vilambate tasmādvilambasauparṇam
<14.9.22>     svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai sauparṇaṃ kriyate
<14.9.23>     dvitīyaṃ hyetadrūpaṃ yacchandomāḥ
<14.9.24>     paśavo vai vāmadevyaṃ paśavaśchandomāḥ paśuṣveva tat paśūn saṃdadhāti
<14.9.25>     gāyatrapārśvaṃ bhavati
<14.9.26>     aharvā etadavlīyata taddevā gāyatrapārśvena samatanvaṃstasmādgāyatrapārśvam
<14.9.27>     trirātro yadvyaśīryata tametaiḥ sāmabhirabhiṣajyan gāyatrapārśvenopāyacchan santaninā samatanvan saṃkṛtinā samaskurvan pratiṣṭhitau pūrvau trirātrāvapratiṣṭhita eṣa yadetānyeva sāmāni kriyanta etasyaiva pratiṣṭhityai
<14.9.28>     pauruhanmanaṃ bhavati
<14.9.29>     puruhanmā vā etena vaikhānaso'ñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.9.30>     abhyāghātyaṃ bhavatyabhyāghātyasāmāno hi chandomāḥ
<14.9.31>     dvaigataṃ bhavati
<14.9.32>     dvigadvā etena bhārgavo dviḥ svargaṃ lokamagacchadāgatya punaragacchat dvayoḥ kāmayoravarudhyai dvaigataṃ kriyate
<14.9.33>     hārāyaṇaṃ bhavati
<14.9.34>     indrastejaskāmo haraskāmastapo'tapyata, sa etaddhārāyaṇamapaśyat tena tejo haro'vārundha tejasvī harasvī bhavati hārāyaṇena tuṣtuvānaḥ
<14.9.35>     achidraṃ bhavati
<14.9.66>     yadvā etasyāhnaśchidramāsīt taddevā acchidreṇāpyauhaṃstadacchidrasyācchidratvam
<14.9.37>     bārhadukthaṃ bhavati
<14.9.38>     bṛhaduktho vā etena vāmneyo'nnasya purodhāmagacchadannaṃ vai brahmaṇaḥ purodhāmannādyasyāvarudhyai
<14.9.39>     udvadbhārgavaṃ bhavati
<14.9.40>     pravatā vai devāḥ svargaṃ lokaṃ prāyannudvatodāyan
<14.9.41>     nidhanāntāḥ pavamānā bhavantyahno dhṛtyai stomaḥ

<14.10.1>   "ka īṃ veda sute sacā"iti satobṛhatyaḥ
<14.10.2>     apabhraṃśa iva vā eṣa yassaptame'hani satobṛhatyo bhavanti nāṣṭame tasmādaṣṭame kāryā anapabhraṃśāya
<14.10.3>     tadāhuḥ śithilamiva vā etacchando yat satobṛhātītyeṣā vai pratiṣṭhitā bṛhatī yā punaḥpadā yadindra prāgapāgudagiti diśāṃ vimarśaḥ pratiṣṭityai
<14.10.4>     tāsu naipātithaṃ brahmasāma
<14.10.5>     sāmārṣeyavat svargāya yujyate svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.10.6>   "ubhayaṃ śṛṇavacca na"iti
<14.10.7>     yacca pṛṣṭhāni yāni caitānyahāni teṣāmubhayeṣāṃ santatyai
<14.10.8>     tāsu vaiyaśvam
<14.10.9>     vyaśvo vā etenāṅgiraso'ñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyā etat pṛṣṭhānāmantataḥ kriyate stomaḥ

<14.11.1>   "pavasva deva āyuṣagindraṃ gacchatu te mada"iti gāyatrī bhavati madavadvai rasavat tṛtīyasavanaṃ madameva tadrasaṃ dadhāti
<14.11.3>   "prāṇā śiśurmahīnām"iti prāṇavatyo bhavanti prāṇāneva tadyajamāne dadhāti
<14.11.4>   "bhī no vājasātamam"ityabhīti rathantarasya rūpaṃ rāthantaraṃ hyetadahaḥ
<14.11.5>   "pavasva soma mahān samudra"ityakṣarapaṅktiḥ stomānāṃ prabhūtiratho etaddhyevaitarhi chando'yātayāma yadakṣarapaṅktistena chandomā ayātayāmānaḥ kriyante brahmavādino vadanti yat ṣaḍahe stomāśchandāṃsyāpyante kiṃ chandasaśchandomā ityetacchandaso yadetā akṣarapaṅktaya iti brūyāt
<14.11.6>   "hinvanti sūramusraya"iti gāyatryaḥ satyo jagatyo rūpeṇa tasmājjagatīnāṃ loke kriyante
<14.11.7>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<14.11.8>     svāśirāmarko bhavati
<14.11.9>     annaṃ vā arko'nnādyasyāvarudhyai prāṇā vai svāśiraḥ prāṇānāmarudhyai
<14.11.90>     surūpaṃ bhavati
<14.11.12>     paśavo vai surūpaṃ paśūnāmavarudhyai
<14.11.12>     bhāsaṃ bhavati bhāti tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.11.13>     padanidhanaṃ rāthantaraṃ hyetadahaḥ
<14.11.14>     svarbhānurvā āsura ādityaṃ tamasāvidhyat sa na vyarocata tasyātrirbhāsena tamo'pāhan sa vyarocata yadvai tadbhā abhavat tadbhāsasya bhāsatvam
<14.11.15>     tama iva vā etānyahāni yacchandomāstebhya etena sāmnā vivāsayati
<14.11.16>     kākṣīvataṃ bhavati
<14.11.17>     kākṣīvān vā etenauśijaḥ prajātiṃ bhūmānamagacchat prajāyate bahurbhavati kākṣīvatena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.11.18>     āsitaṃ bhavati
<14.11.19>     asito vā etena daivalastrayāṇāṃ lokānāṃ dṛṣṭimapaśyat trayāṇāṃ kāmānāmavarudhyā āsitaṃ kriyate
<14.11.20>     aiṣiraṃ bhavati
<14.11.22>     traitaṃ bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai
<14.11.23>     padanidhanaṃ rāthantaraṃ rāthantaraṃ hyetadahaḥ
<14.11.24>     nāthavindu sāma vindate nāthaṃ nāthavindūnyetānyahāni yat chandomā nāthamevaitairvindate
<14.11.25>     gaurīvitaṃ bhavati yadeva gaurīvitasya brāhmaṇam
<14.11.26>     kautsaṃ bhavati
<14.11.27>     etena vai kutso'ndhaso vipānamapaśyat sa ha sma vai surādṛtinopavasathaṃ dhārayatyubhayasyānnādyasyāvarudhyai kautsaṃ kriyate
<14.11.28>     śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ bhavati
<14.11.29>     indro yatīn sālāvṛkebhyaḥ prāyacchat tamaślīlā vāgabhyavadat so'śuddho'manyata sa etacchuddhāśuddhīyamapaśyat tenāśudhyacchudhyati śuddhāśuddhīyena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.11.30>     krauñcaṃ bhavati yadeva krauñcasya brāhmaṇaṃ yat dvitīye'hani
<14.11.31>     rayiṣṭhaṃ bhavati
<14.11.32>     paśavo vai rayiṣṭhaṃ paśūnāmavarudhyai
<14.11.33>     audalaṃ bhavati
<14.11.34>     udalo vā etena vaiśvāmitraḥ prajātiṃ bhūmānamagacchat prajāyate bahurbhavatyaudalena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.11.36>     brahmavādino vadanti yat ṣaḍahe stomāśchandāṃsyāpyante kiṃ chandasaśchandomā iti puruṣachandasa iti brūyāt puruṣo vai pāṅktaḥ puruṣo dvipadāśchandomānāmayātayāmatayau
<14.11.37>     viśoviśīyaṃ bhavati
<14.11.38>     agnirakāmayata viśo viśo'tithissyāṃ viśo viśa ātithyamaśnuvīyeti sa tapo'tapyata sa etadviśoviśīyamapaśyat tena viśo viśo'tithirabhavat viśo viśa ātithyamaśnuta viśo viśo'tithirbhavati viśo viśa ātithyamaśnute viśoviśīyena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.11.39>     gāyatrīṣu stuvanti pratiṣṭāyai brahmavarcasāya yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyanti
<14.11.40>     iḍāntāḥ pavamānā bhavanti paśavo vā iḍā paśavaśchandomāḥ paśuṣveva tat paśūn dadhāti stomaḥ

<14.12.1>   "preṣṭhaṃ vo atithim"ityātithyasyaiva tadrūpaṃ kriyate
<14.12.2>   "aindra no gadhi priya"itīndriyasya vīryasyāvarudhyai
<14.12.3>   "purāṃ bhinduryuvā kaviramitaujā ajāyat  indro viśvasya karmaṇo dhartā vajrī puruṣṭuta"iti dhṛtyā eva
<14.12.4>     auśanaṃ bhavati
<14.12.5>     uśanā vai kāvyo'kāmayata yāvānitareṣāṃ kāvyānāṃ lokastāvantaṃ spṛṇuyāmiti sa tapo'tapyata sa etadauśanamapaśyat tena tāvantaṃ lokamaspṛṇodyāvānitareṣāṃ kāvyānāmāsīt tadvāva sa tarhyakāmayata kāmasani sāmauśanaṃ kāmamevaitenāvarundhe
<14.12.6>     sāṃvartaṃ bhavati
<14.12.7>     devānāṃ vai yajñaṃ rakṣāṃsyajighāṃsaṃstānyetena indraḥ saṃvartamapāvapadyat saṃvartamapāvapat tasmāt sāṃvartaṃ pāpmā vāva sa tānasacata taṃ sāṃvartenāpāghnatāpa pāpmānaṃ hate sāṃvartena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<14.12.8>     mārutaṃ bhavati
<14.12.9>     māsā vai raśmayo maruto raśmayo maruto vai devānāṃ bhūyiṣṭhā bhūyiṣṭhā asāmeti vai sattramāsate bhūyiṣṭhā eva bhavantyṛtumanti pūrvāṇyahānyanṛtavaḥ chandomā yadetat sāma bhavati tenaitānyahānyahānyṛtumanti bhavanti
<14.12.10>     catuścatvāriṃśa eva stomo bhavatyojasyeva tadvīrye pratitiṣṭhatyojo vīrye triṣṭup
<15.1.1>   "krān samudraḥ parame vidharmann"iti navamasyāhnaḥ pratipadbhavati
<15.1.2>     paramaṃ vā etadaharvidharma vidharma vā etadanyairaharbhiraharyannavamaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ hi variṣṭham
<15.1.3>   "matsi vāyumiṣṭaye rādhase na"iti vāruṇyeṣā bhavati yadvai yajñasya duriṣṭaṃ tadvaruṇo gṛhṇati tadeva tadavayajati
<15.1.4>     stotrīyastṛco bhavati prāṇāpānānāmavarudhyai
<15.1.5>     daśarco bhavati daśākṣarā virāṭa vairājamannamannādyasyāvarudhyai
<15.1.6>     saprabhṛtayo bhavāntīndriyasya vīryasya rasasyānaticārāya yatra vai devā indriyaṃ vīryaṃ  rasamapaśyaṃstadanunyatudan
<15.1.7>     aṣṭarco bhavati
<15.1.8>     (aṣṭāśaphāḥ paśavaḥ śaphaśastat paśūnāpnoti) aṣṭākṣarā gāyatrī tejo brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī teja eva brahmavarcasamavarundhe
<15.1.9>     ṣaḍṛcā bhavantyṛtūnāṃ dhṛtyai
<15.1.10>     catvāraḥ ṣaḍṛcā bhavanti caturviṃśatirardhamāsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsara eva pratitiṣṭhati
<15.1.11>     savānuttamaḥ ṣaḍṛco bhavatyubhayasya parokṣapratyakṣasyāvarudhyai
<15.1.12>     tṛca uttamo bhavati yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyanti
<15.1.13>     aṣṭācatvāriṃśa eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai prajātyai

<15.2.1>   "ganma mahā namasā yaviṣṭham"ityāgneyamājyaṃ bhavati
<15.2.2>     gacchantīva vā ete ye navamamahargacchanti
<15.2.3>   "yo dīdāya samiddhasve duroṇa"iti dīdāyeva hyeṣa yo navabhiraharbhistuṣṭuvānaḥ''svāhutam"iti svāhuto hyeṣa yo navabhiraharbhirāhuto''viśvataḥ pratyañcam"iti viśvato hyeṣa pratyaṅ
<15.2.4>   "tvaṃ varuṇa uta mitro agna"iti vāruṇyeṣā bhavati yadvai yajñasya duriṣṭaṃ tadvaruṇo gṛhṇati tadeva tadavayajati
<15.2.5>   "mitraṃ huve pūtadakṣam"iti rāthantaraṃ maitrāvaruṇam
<15.2.6>     ugragādhamiva vā etadyacchandomāstadyathāta ugragādhe vyatiṣajya gāhanta evamevaitadrūpe vyatiṣajati chandomānāmasaṃvyāthāya
<15.2.7>   "mahāṃ indro ya ojasā"ityaindramaṣṭamena vai devā ahnendramavājayannavamena pāpmānamaghnannaharevaitena mahayanti
<15.2.8>   "tā huve yayoridam"iti rāthantaramaindrāgnam
<15.2.9>     ugragādhamiva vā etadyacchandomāstadyathāta ugragādhe vyatiṣajya gāhanta evamevaitadrūpe vyatiṣajati chandomānāmasaṃvyāthāya stomaḥ

<15.3.1>   "pavamānasya jighnato hareścandrā asṛkṣata"iti harivatyo gāyatryo bhavanti chandomānāmayātayāmatāyai
<15.3.2>   "pavamānasya jighnata"iti vai bṛhato rūpaṃ''hareścandrā asṛkṣata"iti jagatyā ubhayoḥ saharūpamupaiti sāmnaśca chandasaśca
<15.3.3>   "parīto ṣiñcatā sutam"iti parivatyo bhavantyanto vai navamamahastasyaitāḥ paryāptyai
<15.3.4>   "sāvi somo aruṣo vṛṣā harir"iti jagatyassatyastriṣṭubho rūpeṇa tasmāt triṣṭubhāṃ loke kriyante
<15.3.5>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<15.3.6>     bharadvājasyādārasṛdbhavati
<15.3.7>     divodāsaṃ vai bharadvājapurohitaṃ nanājanāḥ paryayanta sa upāsīdadṛṣe gātuṃ me vindeti tasmā etena sāmnā gātumavindadgātuvidvā etatsāmānena dāre nāsṛnmeti tadadārasṛto'dārasṛttvaṃ vindate gātuṃ na dāre dhāvatyadārasṛtā tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.3.8>     surūpaṃ bhavati yadeva surūpasya brāhmaṇam
<15.3.9>     hariśrīnidhanaṃ bhavati
<15.3.10>     paśavo vai hariśriyaḥ paśūnāmavarudhyai śriyaṃ ca haraścopaiti tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.3.11>     saindhukṣitaṃ bhavati yadeva saindhukṣitasya brāhmaṇam
<15.3.12>     gatanidhanaṃ vābhravaṃ bhavati gatyai
<15.3.13>     vabhrurvā etena kaumbhyo'ñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.3.14>     iḍānāṃ saṃkṣāro bhavati
<15.3.15>     paśavo vā iḍā paśavaśchandomāḥ paśuṣveva tat paśūn dadhāti
<15.3.16>     ṛṣabhaḥ pāvamāno bhavati
<15.3.17>     paśavo vai chandomāḥ paśuṣveva tanmithunamapyarjati prajātyai na ha vā anṛṣabhāḥ paśavaḥ prajāyante
<15.3.18>     pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavati
<15.3.19>     pṛṣṭhaṃ vā etadahnāṃ yannavamaṃ pṛṣṭha eva tat pṛṣṭhena stuvate pratiṣṭhāyai
<15.3.20>     kaulmalabarhiṣaṃ bhavati
<15.3.21>     kulmalabarhirvā etena svargaṃ lokamapaśyat prajātiṃ bhūmānamagacchat prajāyate bahurbhavati kaulmalabarhiṣeṇa tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.3.22>     arkapuṣpaṃ bhavati
<15.3.23>     annaṃ vai devā arka iti vadanti rasamasya puṣpāmiti sarasamevānnādyamavarundhe'rkapuṣpeṇa tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.3.24>     dairghyaśravasaṃ bhavati
<15.3.25>     dīrghaśravā vai rājanya ṛṣirjyogaparuddho'śanāyaṃścaran sa etaddairghaśravasamapaśyat tena sarvābhyo digbhyo'nnādyamavārundha sarvābhyo adigbhyo'nnādyamavārundhe dairghaśravasena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.3.26>     vaiyaśvaṃ bhavati yadeva vaiyaśvasya brāhmaṇam
<15.3.27>     abhīśavaṃ yadābhīśavasya
<15.3.28>     devasthānaṃ bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai saṃskṛti bhavati saṃṃskṛtyai
<15.3.29>     aharvā etadavlīyata taddevā devasthāne tiṣṭhantaḥ saṃkṛtinā samaskurvaṃstat saṃkṛteḥ saṃkṛtitvaṃ devasthānena vai devāḥ svarge loke pratyatiṣṭhan svarge loke pratitiṣṭāmetyetat
<15.3.30>     varuṇāya devatā rājyāya nātiṣṭhanta sa etaddevasthānamapaśyat tato vai tāstasmai rājyāyātiṣṭhanta tiṣṭhante'smai samānāḥ śreṣṭhyāya
<15.3.31>     kṣatrasyevāsya prakāśo bhavati pratitiṣṭhati ya evaṃ veda
<15.3.32>     bhargo bhargeṇa tuṣṭuvāno bhavati yaśo yaśasā
<15.3.33>     vāsiṣṭhaṃ bhavati yadeva vāsiṣṭhasya brāhmaṇam
<15.3.34>     dīrghatamaso'rko bhavatyannaṃ vā arko'nnādyasyāvarudhyai
<15.3.35>     sāmarājaṃ bhavati sāmrājyamādhipatyaṃ gacchati sāmarājñā tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.3.36>     tadu saṃvadityāhuḥ saṃvatā vai devāḥ svargaṃ lokaṃ prāyannudvatodāyan
<15.3.37>     nidhanāntāḥ pavamānā bhavantyahno dhṛtyai stomaḥ

<15.4.1>   "śrāyanta iva sūryam"iti sūryavatyo bhavanti
<15.4.2>     ādityadevatyaṃ hyetadaharanto vai sūro'nta etannavamamahnāmanta eva tadantena stuvate pratiṣṭhāyai
<15.4.3>   "yata indra bhayāvahe tato no abhayaṃ kṛdhi| maghavañchagdhi tava tanna ūtaye vidviṣo vimṛdho jahi"iti dviṣaścaiva mṛdhaśca navamenāhnā vihatya daśamenāhnottiṣṭhanti
<15.4.5>     śrīrvai śrāyantīyaṃ śrīrnavamamahaḥ śriyameva tacchriyāṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<15.4.6>     samantaṃ bhavati
<15.4.7>     samantena paśukāmaḥ stuvīta purodhākāmaḥ samantena stuvīta
<15.4.8>     āgneyī pṛthivyāgneyo brāhmaṇa aindrī dyauraindro rājanyo'ntarikṣeṇa dyāvāpṛthivī samante antarikṣeṇaivainaṃ samantaṃ karoti vindate paśūn pra purodhāmāpnoti ya evaṃ vidvān samantena stuvate stomaḥ

<15.5.1>   "tvaṃ somāsi dhārayur"iti gāyatrī bhavatyahno dhṛtyai tvamiti bṛhato rūpaṃ bārhataṃ hyetadahaḥ
<15.5.2>   "tvaṃ hyaṅga daivyā"iti tvamiti bṛhato rūpaṃ bārhataṃ hyetadahaḥ
<15.5.3>   "pavasva deva vītaya"iti bṛhato rūpaṃ bārhataṃ hyetadahaḥ
<15.5.4>   "parityaṃ haryataṃ harim"iti parivatyo bhavantyanto vai navamamahasyaitāḥ paryāptyai
<15.5.5>   "pavasva soma mahe dakṣāya"ityakṣarapaṅktiḥ stomānāṃ prabhūtiratho etaddhyevaitarhi chando'yātayāma yadakṣarapaṅktistena chandomā ayātayāmānaḥ kriyante brahmavādino vadanti yat ṣaḍahe stomāśchandāṃsyāpyante kiṃ chandasaśchandomā ityetacchandaso yadetā akṣarapaṅktaya iti brūyāt
<15.5.6>   "upoṣu jātamapturam"iti gāyatryassatyo jagatyo rūpeṇa tasmāt jagatīnāṃ loke kriyante
<15.5.7>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brahmaṇam
<15.5.8>     āśvasūktaṃ bhavati
<15.5.9>     agniṃ vai pūrvairaharbhirājuhotyathaitadādityadaivatyamahaḥ śukra āhuta ityasau vā ādityaḥ śukrastamevaitenājuhoti
<15.5.10>     śāmmadaṃ bhavati
<15.5.11>     śammadvā etenāṅgiraso'ñjasā svargaṃ lokamapaśyat svargasya lokasyānukhyātyai svargāllokānna cyavate tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.5.13>     āśiṣamevāsmā etenāśāste sāma hi satyāśīḥ
<15.5.14>     dāvasurvā etadāṅgirasaḥ paśukāmaḥ sāmāpaśyat tena sahasraṃ paśūnasṛjat yadetat sāma bhavati paśūnāṃ puṣṭyai
<15.5.15>     pratīcīneḍaṃ kāśītaṃ bhavati
<15.5.16>     parācībhirvā anyābhiriḍābhī reto dadhadathaitat pratīcīneḍaṃ kāśītaṃ prajātyai tasmāt parāñco garbhāḥ sambhavanti pratyañcaḥ prajāyante tasmādute'vācīnabilebhyo nāvapadyanta etena hyeva te dhṛtāḥ
<15.5.17>     hāviṣkṛtaṃ bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai kṛtānuvāda eva saḥ
<15.5.18>     sauparṇaṃ bhavati yadeva sauparṇasya brahmaṇam
<15.5.19>     vaiśvamanasaṃ bhavati
<15.5.20>     viśvamanasaṃ vā ṛṣimadhyāyamudvrajitaṃ rakṣo'gṛhṇāt tamindro'cāyadṛṣiṃ vai rakṣo'grahīditi tamabhyavadadṛṣe kastvaiṣa iti, sthāṇuriti brūhīti rakṣo'bravīt, sa sthāṇurityabravīt tasmai vā etena praharetyasmā iṣīkāṃ vajraṃ prayacchannabravīt tenāsya sīmānamabhinat saiṣendreṇateṣīkā pāpmā vāva sa tamagṛhṇāt taṃ vaiśvamanasenāpāhatāpapāpmānaṃ hate vaiśvamanasena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.5.21>     gaurīvitaṃ bhavati yadeva gaurīvitasya brāhmaṇam
<15.5.22>     nihavo bhavatyannādyasyāvarudhyai
<15.5.23>     hīti vā annaṃ pradīyata ītyāgrirannamatti
<15.5.24>     ṛṣayo vā indraṃ pratyakṣa nāpaśyan sa vasiṣṭho'kāmayata kathamindraṃ pratyakṣaṃ paśyeyamiti sa etannihavamapaśyat tato vai sa indraṃ pratyakṣamapaśyat, sa enamabravīdbrāhmaṇaṃ te vakṣyāmi yathā tvatpurohitā bharatāḥ prajaniṣyante'tha mānyebhya ṛṣibhyo mā pravoca iti tasmā etān stomabhāgānabravīt tato vai vasiṣṭhapurohitā bharatāḥ prājāyanta sendraṃ vā etat sāma yadetat sāma bhavati sendratvāya
<15.5.25>     yadvāhiṣṭhīyaṃ bhavati
<15.5.26>     brahmayaśasaṃ vā etāni sāmānyṛcā śrotrīyāṇi brahmāyaśasī bhavati yadvāhiṣṭhīyena tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.5.27>     āsitaṃ bhavati yadevāsitasya brāhmaṇam
<15.5.28>     sādhraṃ bhavati siddhyai
<15.5.29>     ākūpāraṃ bhavati
<15.5.30>     akūpāro vā etena kaśyapo jemānaṃ mahimānamagacchajjemānaṃ mahimānaṃ gacchatyākūpāreṇa tuṣṭuvānaḥ
<15.5.31>     vidharma bhavati dharmasya vidhṛtyai
<15.5.32>     brahmavādino vadanti yat ṣaḍahe stomāśchandāṃsyāpyante kiṃ chandasaśchandomā iti puruṣacchandasa iti brūyāt puruṣo vai pāṅktaḥ puruṣo dvipadā chandomānāmayātayāmatāyai
<15.5.33>     śrudhyaṃ bhavati
<15.5.34>     paśavo vai śrudhyaṃ paśūnāmavarudhyai
<15.5.35>     prajāpatiḥ paśūnasṛjata te'smāt sṛṣṭā apākrāmaṃstānetena sāmnā śrūdhiyā ehiyetyanvahvayat ta enamupāvartanta yadetat sāma bhavati paśūnāmupāvṛtyai
<15.5.36>     upainaṃ paśava āvartante ya evaṃ veda
<15.5.37>     gāyatrīṣu stuvanti pratiṣṭhāyai brahmavarcasāya yenaiva prāṇena prayanti tamabhyudyantīḍāntāḥ pavamānā bhavanti paśavo vā iḍā paśavaśchandomāḥ paśuṣveva tat paśūn dadhāti stomaḥ

<15.6.1>     āgreyīṣu pūrveṣāmahnāmukthāni praṇayantyathaitasyāhna āgneyyaindrayāṃ praṇayantyubhayoreva rūpayoḥ pratitiṣṭhati
<15.6.2>     aidhmavāhaṃ bhavati
<15.6.3>     āgneyyaindrīṣu stuvanti brahma caiva tat kṣatraṃ ca sayujīkaroti brahmaiva kṣatrasya purastānnidadhāti brāhmaṇe kṣatraṃ ca viśaṃ cānuge karoti
<15.6.4>     traikakubhaṃ bhavati
<15.6.5>     ojasyeva tadvīrye pratitiṣṭhatyojo vīryaṃ traikakubham
<15.6.6>     udvaṃśīyaṃ bhavati yadevodvaṃśīyasya brāhmaṇam
<15.6.7>     aṣṭācatvāriṃśaṃ eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai prajātyai
<15.7.1>     gāyatraṃ vai saptamamahastraiṣṭubhamaṣṭamaṃ jāgataṃ navamamathaitadānuṣṭubhamaharyaddaśamam
<15.7.2>     tadāhuryadānuṣṭubhaṃ stomyāṃ pratyakṣamupeyuḥ parāṃ parāvataṃ yajamāno gacchenna pratitiṣṭhediti yā vai caturviṃśatirgāyatryastā aṣṭādaśānuṣṭubho'nuṣṭubhameva tat stomyāṃ parokṣamupayanti pratiṣṭhāyai pratitiṣṭhati
<15.7.3>     prajāpatiṃ vā etenāhnā pariveviṣati tatra vyavavadyaṃ yadvai śreṣṭhe pariviṣyamāṇo vadatyannādyasya so'vagrahastasmānna vyavavadyamannādyasyānavagrāhāya
<15.7.4>     tadu vyavavadyaṃ yathā śreṣṭhāya baliṃ hriyamāṇaṃ panthānaṃ paryanuvedayati gatyai tathā tat
<15.7.5>     yāvatyanuṣṭuptāvatīṃ vācaṃ sampādya vibrūyustadvanatiriktaṃ svasyo caiva yajñasyāriṣṭyai
<15.7.6>     abhi vā ete devānārohantītyāhurye daśabhiraharbhiḥ stuvata iti pañcānāmahnāmanurūpaiḥ pratyavayanti yathābhyāruhya pratyavarohet tathā tannavarco bhavati yā evāmūḥ prayacchan yā avadadhāti tā etā udasyati
<15.7.7>     vāruṇyeṣā bhavati yadvai yajñasya duriṣṭaṃ tadvaruṇo gṛhṇāti tadeva tadavayajatyādityaiṣā bhavatīyaṃ vā aditirasyāmeva pratitiṣṭhati
<15.7.8>     caturviṃśa eva stomo bhavati tejase brahmarcasāya

<15.8.1>   "suṣamiddho na āvaha"ityāpriya ājyāni bhavanti
<15.8.2>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata sa dugdho riricānāmanyata sa etānyāpriya ājyānyapaśyat tairātmānamāprīṇāt dugdha iva vā eṣa riricāno yo daśabhiraharbhistuṣṭuvāno yadetānyāpriya ājyāni bhavantyātmānamevaitairāprīṇāti
<15.8.3>   "yadadya sūra udita"iti sūravanmaitrāvaruṇam
<15.8.4>     anto vai sūro'nta etaddaśamamahnāmanta eva tadantena stuvate pratiṣṭhāyai
<15.8.5>   "ut tvā madantu somā"ityudvadaindramutthānasya rūpam
<15.8.6>   "indrāgnī āgataṃ sutam"iti yenaiva rūpeṇa prayanti tadabhyudyanti stomaḥ

<15.9.1>   "uccā te jātamandhasa"ityudvatyo gāyatryo bhavantyutthānasya rūpam
<15.9.2>   "punānassoma dhāraya"iti panthānameva tat paryavayanti
<15.9.3>   "ā jāgṛvirvipra ṛtaṃ matīnām"iti yadāpte pravatīḥ kuryuratipadyeranyadāvatyo bhavantyanatipādāya
<15.9.4>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<15.9.5>     āmahīyavaṃ bhavati kḷptiścānnādyaṃ ca kḷptiṃ caivaitenānnādyaṃ cābhyuttiṣṭhanti
<15.9.6>     ājigaṃ bhavatyājijityāyai
<15.9.7>     ājirvā eṣa pratato yat dvādaśāhastasyaitadujjityai
<15.9.8>     ābhīkaṃ bhavatyabhikrāntyai
<15.9.9>     āṅgirasastapastepānāḥ śucamaśocaṃsta etat sāmāpaśyaṃstānabhīke'bhyavarṣat tena śucamaśamayanta yadabhīke'bhyavarṣat tasmādābhīkaṃ yāmeva pūrvairaharbhiḥ śucaṃ śocanti tāmetenānna śamayitvottiṣṭhanti
<15.9.10>     utsedho bhavati
<15.9.11>     utsedhena vai devāḥ paśūnudasedhanniṣedhena paryagṛhṇan
<15.9.12>     antarotsedhaniṣedhau yajñāyajñīyam
<15.9.13>     paśavo'nnādyaṃ yajñāyajñīyaṃ paśūneva tadannādyamutsedhaniṣedhābhyāṃ parigṛhṇāti
<15.9.14>     mādhyandine vai pavamāne devā yajñāyajñīyena yajñaṃ saṃsthāpya svargaṃ lokamārohaṃstadya evaṃ veda mādhyandina evaitat pavamāne yajñāyajñīyena yajñaṃ saṃsthāpya svargaṃ lokamārohati
<15.9.15>     atho parokṣamanuṣṭubhaṃ sampadyate'hareṣā vai pratyakṣamanuṣṭupyadyajñāyajñīyaṃ tadyat tṛtīyasavane kuryuḥ pratyakṣamanuṣṭubhamṛccheyustasmānmādhyandine kurvanti tena parokṣamanuṣṭubhamupayanti
<15.9.16>     gaurīvitaṃ bhavati
<15.9.17>     etadvai yajñasya śvastanaṃ yadgaurīvitametadāyatano yajamāno yanmadhyandino yadgaurīvitaṃ madhyandine bhavati śvastanameva tadyajamāna ātman dhatte stomaḥ

<15.10.1>   "kayā naścitra ābhuvad"iti kavatyastena prājāpatyāḥ ko hi prajāpatiḥ prajāpaterāptyai
<15.10.2>   "mā cidanyadviśaṃsata"ityutthānameva tadāśiṣo hyetarhi
<15.10.3>   "udu tye madhumattamā"ityudvatya udayanīye'hanyetadāśiṣo hyevaitarhi
<15.10.4>   "tarobhirvo vidadvasum"iti stomo vai taro yajño vidadvasuḥ stomena vai yajño yujyate yat tarobhirvo vidadvasumityāha yajñameva tadyunakti
<15.10.5>     vāmadevyasyarkṣu rathantaraṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavati
<15.10.6>     gāyatrī vai rathantarasya yoniḥ svāyāmeva tadyonau rathantaraṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<15.10.7>     tejo vai gāyatrī chandasā tejo rathantaraṃ sāmnā tejaścaturviṃśastomānāṃ teja eva tat samyaksaṃdadhātyapi ha putrasya putrastejasvī bhavati
<15.10.8>     aṣṭākṣareṇa prathamāyā ṛcaḥ prastautyaṣṭāśaphāṃstatpaśūnavarundhe
<15.10.9>     dyvakṣareṇottarayorṛcoḥ prastauti dvipādyajamāno yajamānameva yajñe paśuṣu pratiṣṭhāpayati
<15.10.10>     gāyatraṃ vai rathantaraṃ gāyatracchando yadgāyatrīṣu rathantaraṃ bhavati tena svāyāṃ janatāyāmṛdhnotīme vai lokā gāyatrī yadgāyatrīṣu rathantaraṃ bhavatīmāneva tallokān samāpyottiṣṭhanti
<15.10.11>     maidhātithaṃ bhavati
<15.10.12>     etena vai medhātithiḥ kāṇvo vibhindukādvyūdhnīrgā udasṛjata paśūnāmavarudhyai maidhātithaṃ kriyate
<15.10.13>     abhīvarto brahmasāma bhavatyekākṣaranidhanaḥ pratiṣṭhāyai
<15.10.14>     ekākṣarā vai vāgvācyeva pratiṣṭhāyottiṣṭhanti
<15.10.15>     kāleyamacchāvākasāma bhavati
<15.10.16>     samānaloke vai kāleyaṃ ca rathantaraṃ ceyaṃ vai rathantaraṃ paśavaḥ kāleyamasyāṃ caiva paśuṣu ca pratiṣṭhāyottiṣṭhanti stomaḥ

<15.11.1>   "svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā"iti gāyatrī bhavati madavadvai rasavat tṛtīyasavanaṃ madameva tadrasaṃ dadhāti
<15.11.2>     gāyatraṃ bhavati yadeva gāyatrasya brāhmaṇam
<15.11.3>     saṃhitaṃ bhavati dyvakṣaraṇidhanaṃ pratiṣṭhāyai pratiṣṭhāyaivottiṣṭhanti
<15.11.4>     saphaṃ bhavati
<15.11.5>     saphena vai devā imān lokān samāpnuvant samāpnuvaṃstat saphasya saphatvamimānevaitena lokān samāpyottiṣṭhanti
<15.11.6>     rohitakūlīyaṃ bhavati yadeva rohitakūlīyasya brāhmaṇam
<15.11.7>     śyāvāśvāndhīgave bhavataḥ samīcyau virājau dadhātyannādyāya
<15.11.8>     pipīlikāmadhyāsu stuvanti
<15.11.9>     indro vṛtraṃ hatvā nāstṛṣīti manyamānaḥ parāṃ parāvatamagacchat sa etāmanuṣṭubhaṃ vyauhat tāṃ madhye vyavāsarpadindragṛhe vā eṣo'bhaye yajate abhaya uttiṣṭhati ya evaṃ vidvānetāsu stuvate
<15.11.10>     yajñāyajñīyanidhanaṃ sauhaviṣaṃ bhavati yajñāyajñīyādeva tat tṛtīyasavene na yanti
<15.11.11>     vājajidbhavati
<15.11.12>     sarvasyāptyai sarvasya jityai sarvaṃ vā ete vājaṃ jayanti ye daśamamaharāgacchantyannaṃ vai vājo'nnādyasyāvarudhyai
<15.11.13>     daśākṣaraṃ nidhanamupayanti daśarātrasya dhṛtyai daśākṣarā virāḍvairājamannamannādyasyāvarudhyai
<15.11.14>     sūryavatīṣu stuvantyanto vai sūro'nta etaddaśamamahnāmanta eva tadantena stuvate pratiṣṭhāyai
<15.11.15>     upavatyo bhavanti pratiṣṭhāyai parivatyo bhavanti sarvasya paryāptyai
<15.11.16>     caturviṃśa eva stomo bhavati tejase brahmavarcasāya

<15.12.1>     virāṭsu vāmadevyamagniṣṭomasāma bhavati śāntyai kḷptyai
<15.12.2>     sadvai vāmadevyaṃ sāmnāṃ sadvirāṭchandasāṃ sat trayastriṃśaḥ stomānāṃ satāmantān saṃdhāyottiṣṭhantyapi ha putrasya sattvamaśnute
<15.12.3>     brahmavādino vadanti yatassattrādudasthātā3 sthitā3 iti yadyata iti brūyurapratiṣṭhānā aprajaso bhaviṣyantītyenān brūyādyat sthitāditi brūyuḥ sthāyukaiṣāṃ śrīrbhaviṣyati na vasīyāṃso bhaviṣyantītyenān brūyāt pūrṇādeva pūrṇamabhyudasthāmeti brūyuḥ
<15.12.4>     ete vai pūrṇāt pūrṇamabhyuttiṣṭhanti ye vāmadevyena stutvottiṣṭhanti
<15.12.5>     antarikṣaṃ vai vāmadevyamantarikṣeṇedaṃ sarvaṃ pūrṇam
<15.12.6>     eṣa vai samṛddhaḥ stomo yat trayastriṃśastrayastriṃśadakṣarāsu samṛddhāveva pratitiṣṭhanti
<15.12.7>     sarveṣāṃ vā etāśchandasāṃ rūpaṃ yāt tripadāstena gāyatryo yadekādaśākṣarāṇi padāni tena triṣṭubho yat dvādaśākṣaraṃ padaṃ tena jagatyo yat trayastriṃśadakṣarāstena virājastenaiva cānuṣṭubho na hyekasmādakṣarādvirādhayanti
<15.12.8>     trayastriṃśa eva stomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyai devatāsu vā eṣa pratiṣṭhitaḥ
<16.1.1>     prajāpatirvā idameka āsīnnāharāsīnna rātrirāsīt so'sminnandhe tamasi prāsarpat sa aicchat sa etamabhyapadyata tato vai tasmai vyaucchadvyuṣṭirvā eṣa āhriyate yadvai tajjyotirabhavat tat jyotiṣo jyotiṣṭvam
<16.1.2>     eṣa vāva prathamo yajñānāṃ ya etenāniṣṭvāthānyena yajate gartapatyameva tajjīyate pra vā mīyate
<16.1.3>     yathā vā idamagnerjātādagnayo vihriyanta evametasmādadhyanye yajñā vihriyante
<16.1.4>     yo hi trivṛdanyaṃ yajñakratumāpadyate sa taṃ dīpayati yaḥ pañcadaśaḥ sa taṃ yaḥ saptadaśaḥ sa taṃ ya ekaviṃśaḥ sa tam
<16.1.5>     etat tadyadāhureko yajña ityetaddhi sarve jyotiṣṭomā bhavanti
<16.1.6>     asthūrirvā eṣa santato yajño dvau dvau hi stomau savanaṃ vahatastrivṛt pañcadaśau prātaḥsavanaṃ pañcadaśasaptadaśau mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ saptadaśaikaviṃśau tṛtīyasavanam
<16.1.7>     yā mitadakṣiṇaiva syādeṣa eva kārya iyaṃ vai jyotiriyamamitasya yantrikaiṣā vā etaṃ yantumarhati
<16.1.8>     tasya navatiśataṃ stotrīyāstāsāṃ yā aśītiśataṃ tāḥ ṣaṭtriṃśinyo virājaḥ ṣaḍṛtava ṛtuṣveva virājā pratitiṣṭhati
<16.1.9>     atha yā daśaiṣā vā ātmanyā virāḍetasyāṃ vā idaṃ puruṣaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ
<16.1.5>     gauścāśvaścāśvataraśca gardabhaścājāścāvayaśca vrīhayaśca yavāśca tilāśca māṣāścaitasyāmeva virāji pratitiṣṭhati
<16.1.11>     tasya dvādaśaṃ śataṃ dakṣiṇāḥ
<16.1.12>     vīrahā vā eṣa devānāṃ yaḥ somamabhiṣuṇoti yāḥ śataṃ vairaṃ taddevānavadayate'tha yā daśa daśa prāṇāḥ prāṇāṃstābhiḥ spṛṇoti yaikādaśyātmānaṃ tayā yā dvādaśī saiva dakṣiṇā
<16.1.13>     śleṣma vā etadyajñasya yaddakṣiṇā na vā aśleṣmā ratho vahatyatha śleṣmavatā yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate tamabhyaśnuta evametena dakṣiṇāvatā yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate tamabhyaśnute
<16.1.14>     śubho vā etā yajñasya yaddakṣiṇā yaddakṣiṇāvatā yajate śubhamevāsmin dadhāti
<16.1.1>     athaiṣa gauḥ
<16.1.2>     gavā vai devā asurānebhyo lokebhyo'nudantaibhyo lokebhyo bhrātṛvyaṃ nudate ya evaṃ veda
<16.1.3>     yadvai taddevā asurānebhyo lokebhyo govayaṃstadgorgotvam
<16.1.4>     govayati pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ ya evaṃ veda
<16.1.5>     tasya pañcadaśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ vajro vai pañcadaśo vajrameva tat purastānnidadhāti tena vijayate
<16.1.6>     paśustomo vā eṣa evamiva vai paśuḥ samāhitaḥ śiraḥ sthavīyo'ṇīyasyo grīvā pārśvābhyāṃ varīyāṃ sakthibhyāṃ variṣṭhaḥ
<16.1.7>     yat pañcadaśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ bhavati trivṛt tyājyāni saptadaśa mādhyandinaṃ savanamekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ rūpeṇaivainaṃ tat samardhayati
<16.1.8>     ekā saṃstutānāṃ virājamatiricyate tasmāt paśoḥ paścādatiriktam

<16.2.1>     athaiṣa gauḥ
<16.2.2>     gavā vai devā asurānebhyo lokebhyo'nudantaibhyo lokebhyo bhrātṛvyaṃ nudate ya evaṃ veda
<16.2.3>     yadvai taddevā asurānebhyo lokebhyo govayaṃstadgorgotvam
<16.2.4>     govayati pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ ya evaṃ veda
<16.2.5>     tasya pañcadaśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ vajro vai pañcadaśo vajrameva tat purastānnidadhāti tena vijayate
<16.2.6>     paśustomo vā eṣa evamiva vai paśuḥ samāhitaḥ śiraḥ sthavīyo'ṇīyasyo grīvā pārśvābhyāṃ varīyāṃsakthibhyāṃ variṣṭhaḥ
<16.2.7>     yat pañcadaśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ bhavati trivṛntyājyāni saptadaśa mādhyandinaṃ savanamekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ rūpeṇaivainaṃ tat samardhayati
<16.2.8>     ekā saṃstutānāṃ virājamatiricyate tasmāt paśoḥ paścādatiriktam

<16.3.1>     athaiṣa āyuḥ
<16.3.2>     āyuṣā vai devā asurānāyuvatāyute bhrātṛvyaṃ ya evaṃ veda
<16.3.3>     svargakāmo yajeta
<16.3.4>     ūrdhvāḥ stomā yantyanapabhraṃśāya
<16.3.5>     etenaivāmayāvinaṃ yājayedatirātraḥ kāryaḥ
<16.3.6>     sa gāyatrīṃ saṃpadyate prāṇo gāyatryāyureṣa āyuścaivāsmin prāṇaṃ cobhe samīcī dadhāti
<16.3.7>     svargyā vā ete stomā yat jyotirbhavati jyotirevāsmai sa purastāddharatyathaiṣa gaurekayā virājamatirikta ārambhaṇameva tadathaiṣa āyurekasyā virāja ūna āsāda eva so'tho ūnātiriktau stomau mithunau prajātyai
<16.3.8>     ete vai trikadrukāḥ stomā etairvā indraḥ sarvāṃ tṛptimatṛpyat
<16.3.9>     tṛpyati prajayā paśubhirya evaṃ veda

<16.4.1>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata tā asmai śraiṣṭhyāya nātiṣṭhanta sa āsāṃ diśāṃ prajānāṃ ca rasaṃ pravṛhya srajaṃ kṛtvā pratyamuñcata tato'smai prajāḥ śraiṣṭhyāyātiṣṭhanta
<16.4.2>     tiṣṭhante'smai samānāḥ śraiṣṭhyāya ya evaṃ veda
<16.4.3>     so'kāmayatendro me prajāyāṃ śreṣṭhaḥ syāditi tāmasmai srajaṃ pratyamuñcat tato vā indrāya prajāḥ śraiṣṭhyāyātiṣṭhanta tacchilpaṃ paśyantyo yat pitaryapaśyan
<16.4.4>     tasmādyaḥ putrāṇāṃ dāyaṃ dhanatamamivopaiti taṃ manyante'yamevedaṃ bhaviṣyatīti
<16.4.5>     tato vā idamindro viśvamajayadyadviśvamajayat tasmādviśvajit
<16.4.6>     so'kāmayata yanme'nabhijitaṃ tadabhijayeyamiti sa etamabhijitamapaśyat tenānabhijitamabhyajayat
<16.4.7>     yadabhijidbhavatyanābhijitasyābhijityai
<16.4.8>     tau vā etāvindrastomau vīryavantau śilpaṃ vā etau nāma stomāvāstām
<16.4.9>     paśyate gṛhe śilpaṃ ya evaṃ veda
<16.4.10>     na vai yamau nāma stomau sto yo yamābhyāṃ yajetaitābhyāṃ yajeta samṛdhyai
<16.4.11>     punarabhyāvartaṃ stomā bhavanti punarabhyāvartaṃ hyetābhyāmindro'jitamajayat
<16.4.12>     trīṃstrivṛdabhijitaḥ praṇayati trīn pañcadaśaḥ trīn saptadaśaḥ trīnekaviṃśaste dvādaśa saṃpadyante dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ prajāpatiḥ prajāpatimevāpnoti
<16.4.13>     caturastrivṛdviśvajitaḥ praṇayati caturaḥ pañcadaśaścaturaḥ saptadaśaste dvādaśa saṃpadyante dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsaraḥ prajāpatiḥ prajāpatimevāpnoti

<16.5.1>   "upa tvā jāmayo gira"ityupavatī pratipadbhavati stomasya rūpam
<16.5.2>     prāṇairvā eṣa vyṛdhyata ityāhuryaḥ sarvaṃ dadāti sarvān stomān sarvāṇi pṛṣṭhānyupaitīti yadvāyavyā bhavati prāṇānāṃ samṛdhyai
<16.5.3>     sarasvataśca sarasvatyāścottare bhavataḥ
<16.5.4>     mithunaṃ vā etadyat sarasvāṃśca sarasvatī ca mithunamevāsya yajñamukhe dadhāti prajananāya
<16.5.5>     sāvitrī caturthī bhavati
<16.5.6>     duṣkaraṃ vā eṣa karoti yaḥ sarvaṃ dadāti yat sāvitrī bhavati savitṛprasṛtaṃ me karmāsaditi savitṛprasūtamevāsya karma bhavati
<16.5.7>     brāhmaṇaspatyā pañcamī bhavati
<16.5.8>     brahma vai brahmaṇaspatirbrahmaivāsya yajñamukhe dadhāti
<16.5.9>     āgnipāvamānī ṣaṣṭhī bhavati
<16.5.10>     agnirevainaṃ niṣṭapati pavamānaḥ punāti pūtamevainaṃ yajñiyaṃ pṛṣṭhānyupanayati
<16.5.11>     yanti vā ete patha ityāhurye saṃbhāryāḥ kurvate
<16.5.12>     pāvamāna uttamastṛco bhavati tena patho nayanti
<16.5.13>     sa tu vai pṛṣṭhaiḥ stuvītetyāhurya etāni bahiṣpavamāne yuñjyāditi
<16.5.14>     upavatī pratipadbhavatyupa vai rathantaraṃ rathantaramevāsmai tayā yunakti
<16.5.15>     sarasvatī dvitīyā bhavati svargo lokaḥ sarasvān svargo loko bṛhadbṛhadevāsmai tayā yunakti
<16.5.16>     sarasvatyāstṛtīyā bhavati vāgvai sarasvatī vāgvairūpaṃ vairūpamevāsmai tayā yunakti
<16.5.17>     sāvitrī caturthī bhavati prajāpatirvai savitā prajāpatirvairājaṃ vairājamevāsmai tayā yunakti
<16.5.18>     brāhmaṇaspatyā pañcamī bhavati brahma vai brahmaṇaspatirbrahma śakvaryaḥ śakvarīrevāsmai tayā yunakti
<16.5.19>     āgnipāvamānī ṣaṣṭhī bhavati gāyatrī vai revatī gāyatracchandā agnī revatīrevāsmai tayā yunakti
<16.5.20>     na catvāri ṣaḍbhyo vibhavanti yadaniruktāni tena vibhavanti
<16.5.21>     sarvāṇi svārāṇyājyāni tajjāmi nānādevatyaiḥ stuvantyajāmitāyai
<16.5.22>   "suṣamiddho na āvaha"ityāpriya ājyāni bhavanti
<16.5.23>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata sa dugdho riricāno'manyata sa etānyāpriya ājyānyapaśyat tairātmānam [thusBī̭ Kṣṣātmanam] āprīṇāddugdha iva vā eṣa riricāno yaḥ sarvaṃ dadāti yadāpriya ājyāni bhavantyātmānamevaitairāprīṇāti
<16.5.24>     etarhi tu vai pṛṣṭhāni yathāyatanaṃ kalpanta ityāhuryadrathantaraṃ prathamaṃ bṛhaduttamaṃ madhya itarāṇīti
<16.5.24>     jāmi vā etadyajñe kriyata ityāhuryat sarvāṇi nidhanavanti saha kriyanta iti yadantarā somā yantyantarokthāni śasyante'ntarā vaṣaṭkurvanti tenājāmi
<16.5.26>     vyatyāsamiḍāśca nidhanāni cāhustenājāmi
<16.5.27>     cyavante vā etadrevatyaḥ svādāyatanādityāhuryat trayastriṃśāt stomādyantīti yadgāyatryo bhavanti tenāyatanānna cyavante yā hi kā ca gāyatrī sā revatī

<16.6.1>     paraśubhirvā eṣa vyṛdhyata ityāhuryaḥ sarvaṃ dadāti tacchavīṃ paridhatte paśubhireva samṛdhyate
<16.6.2>     rohiṇī chavī bhavatyetadvai paśunāṃ bhūyiṣṭhaṃ rūpaṃ yadrohitaṃ sākṣādevainānavarundhe
<16.6.3>     araṇye tisro vasatyāraṇyaṃ tābhirannādyamavarundhe
<16.6.4>     udumbare vasatyūrgudumbara ūrjamevāvarundhe
<16.6.5>     khanitreṇa jīvatyavṛttimapajayati
<16.6.6>     ubhayataḥkṣṇudabhrirbhavatyubhayata evāsmā annādyaṃ rajatyasmācca lokādamuṣmācca
<16.6.7>     niṣādeṣu tisro vasatyasyāṃ vā ete parīttā yadevāsyāmannādyaṃ tadavarundhe
<16.6.8>     jane tisro vasati janyaṃ tābhirannādyamavarundhe
<16.6.9>     samānajane tisraḥ samānajanyaṃ tābhiḥ
<16.6.10>     dvādaśaitā rātrayo bhavanti dvādaśamāsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsaramanvannādyaṃ prajāyate tadevāptvāvarundhe
<16.6.11>     saṃvatsaraṃ na yācedāmādyamiva vā etadyaḥ sadyodattaṃ pratyatti sadyo vai devānaṃ saṃvatsaraḥ
<16.6.12>     nodīyamānaṃ prati nudennādyasyāpratinodāya
<16.6.13>     uṣṇīṣaṃ bibharti śilpatvāya
<16.6.14>     na mṛnmayena pibedāhutirvā eṣā yadbrāhmaṇasya mukhaṃ na vai mṛnmayamāhutimānaśe'tha yadamṛnmayapo bhavati sva eva mukha āhutiṃ juhoti

<16.7.1>     pañcaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ
<16.7.2>     sarvajitā vai devāḥ sarvamajayan sarvasyāptyai sarvasya jityai sarvamevaitenāpnoti sarvaṃ jayati
<16.7.3>     tasya mahāvrataṃ pṛṣṭham
<16.7.4>     arkyaṃ śasyate
<16.7.5>     caturviṃśatiḥ saṃvatsarasyārdhamāsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ pañcaviṃśo'nnaṃ vrataṃ saṃvatsarādetenānnādyamavarundhe
<16.7.6>     annādo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<16.7.7>     etena vai gaurāṅgirasaḥ sarvaṃ pāpmānamatarat sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ taratyetena stomena tuṣṭuvānaḥ

<16.8.1>     athaiṣa jyotiḥ
<16.8.2>     parāṅvai trirātro'rvāṅagniṣṭomo yastrirātre vibhraṃśate na tasmin punarastyatha yo'gniṣṭome prāyaścittimat tadapi hyetenaikaviṃśatidakṣiṇena punaryajeta yasmin hyeva yajñakratau vibhraṃśate saiva tasya prāyaścittiḥ
<16.8.3>     upasadi sahasraṃ prātaranuvākamanvāha tadasau lokaḥ sahasraṃ dakṣiṇāstadantarikṣaṃ sahasrametānyakṣarāṇi tadayaṃ loka eṣu lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati ya evaṃ veda
<16.8.4>     ṛksthā vā anyat sahasramityāhurakṣaresthā anyaditi yat trirātre dīyate tadṛksthā| atha yadagniṣṭome tadakṣaresthā
<16.8.5>     yat sahasrākṣarāsu brahmasāma bhavati sahasrasyaiva sā pratiṣṭutiḥ
<16.8.6>     yāvadvai sahasraṃ gāva uttarādharā ityāhustāvadasmāt lokāt svargo loka iti tasmādāhuḥ sahasrayājī vā imān lokān prāpnoti
<16.8.7>     paśubhirvā eṣa vyṛdhyata ityāhuryaḥ sadyaḥ sahasraṃ dadātīti paṅktiṣu brahmasāma bhavati pāṅkto yajñaḥ pāṅktāḥ paśavo yajña eva paśuṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<16.8.8>     trivṛtaṃ stomaṃ saṃpadyate virājaṃ chandaḥ
<16.8.9>     prāṇo vai trivṛdannaṃ virāṭna vai prāṇa ṛte'nnāt pārayati nānnamṛte prāṇāt prāṇeṣu caivānnādye ca pratitiṣṭhati

<16.9.1>     athaiṣa sarvajyotiḥ sarvasyāptiḥ sarvasya jitiḥ sarvamevaitenāpnoti sarvaṃ jayati
<16.9.2>     paramo vā eṣa yajñaḥ paramaṃ sahasraṃ paramatāṃ gacchati ya evaṃ veda
<16.9.3>     tasya dviśatāḥ stotrīyā anto vai vāco dviśatamantaḥ sahasramanta eva tadantaṃ pratiṣṭāpayati
<16.9.4>     kṛtastomo vā eṣa sarvamevaitenāpnoti sarvaṃ jayati sarvaṃ hi kṛtena jayati
<16.9.5>     virājaṃ saṃpadyate'nnaṃ virāḍannādyamevāvarundhe
<16.9.6>     ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomo bhavati pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśo'ntata eva yajñasya pratitiṣṭhati

<16.10.1>     athaiṣa viśvajyotirukthyaḥ
<16.10.2>     paśavo vā ukthāni paśavo viśvaṃ jyotirviśva eva jyotau paśuṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<16.10.3>     aharbhirvai trirātra imān lokānāpnoti savanaireṣa uttamamuttaraṃ trirātrasyāharvarṣīya uttaramuttarametasya savanaṃ varṣīyastena trirātramāpnotyuttara uttara eṣāṃ lokānāṃ jyāyāṃstenemān lokānāpnoti
<16.10.4>     asthūrirvā eṣa santato yajño dvau dvau hi stomau savanaṃ vahataḥ
<16.10.5>     trivṛtpañcadaśau prātaḥsavanaṃ saptadaśapañcaviṃśau mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ caturviṃśaikaviṃśau tṛtīyasavanam
<16.10.6>     yadvai yukte santata ādhīyate vahati tadyathā yukte santata ādadhyādevametasmin sahasramādhīyate
<16.10.7>     ubhe bṛhadrathantare bhavataḥ
<16.10.8>     iyaṃ vai rathantaraṃ dyaurbṛhadevāsmāllokādgāyatyevāmuṣmādubhayoranayorlokayoḥ pratitiṣṭhati
<16.10.9>     anuṣṭubhyātharvaṇaṃ bhavati
<16.10.10>     bheṣajaṃ vai devānāmatharvāṇo bheṣajāyaivāriṣṭyai
<16.10.11>     udvaṃśīyamukthānāmantato bhavati sarveṣāṃ vā etat pṛṣṭhānāṃ rūpaṃ sarveṣveva rūpeṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<16.10.12>     uktho bhavati paśavo vā ukthāni paśavaḥ sahasraṃ paśuṣveva tat paśūn dadhāti

<16.11.1>     yo vā agniṣṭome trirātraṃ protaṃ vidyāt so'gniṣṭome sahasraṃ dadyāt trirātrāyatanaṃ hi sahasram
<16.11.2>   "upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ''''upoṣu jātamapturaṃ''''pavasva vāco agriya"iti pratipado bhavantyetadvai trirātramakaḥ
<16.11.3>     pavamāne rathantaraṃ karoti pavamānasyāntyaṃ vāmadevyaṃ bṛhat pṛṣṭham
<16.11.4>     iyaṃ vai rathantaramantarikṣaṃ vāmadevyaṃ dyaurbṛhadime lokāstrirātro yadetāni sāmāni sadhryañci karotīmāneva tallokān saṃdadhāti tena trirātramāpnoti
<16.11.5>     kakubhaṃ prācīmudūhati
<16.11.6>     puro hyetayā satyā apaśuvīryaṃ karoti
<16.11.7>     tasyāmiḍānāṃ saṃkṣāraḥ puruṣo vai kakuppaśava iḍānāṃ saṃkṣāra ātmanyeva tat paśūn pratiṣṭhāpayati
<16.11.8>   "pratnaṃ pīyūṣaṃ pūrvyaṃ yaduktham"iti satobṛhatyo bhavanti
<16.11.9>     satobṛhatyā vai devā imān lokān vyāpnuvannimānevaitābhirlokān vyāpnoti
<16.11.10>     tā vā etā gāyatryo yat tripadāstena gāyatryastā vā etā jagatyo yaddvādaśākṣarāṇi padāni tena jagatyastā vā etā bṛhatyo yat ṣaṭtriṃśadakṣarāstena bṛhatyaḥ sarveṣāṃ vā etāśchandasāṃ rūpaṃ sarvāṇi rūpāṇi paśūnāmavarundhe
<16.11.11>     etadvai pratyakṣaṃ mahāvrataṃ tasya gāyatraṃ śiro bṛhadrathantare pakṣau vāmadevyamātmā yajñāyajñīyaṃ pucchaṃ dakṣiṇā evārkyameṣa vāva pratyakṣaṃ mahāvratena stuvate ya etena yajate
<16.11.12>     tasya bṛhat pṛṣṭhaṃ  paṅktiṣu brahmasāma tadāhuśchando vyādhīyate yadbṛhat pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavati paṅktiṣu brahmasāmeti
<16.11.13>     śrāyantīyameva kāryaṃ na chando vyādhīyate
<16.11.14>     eṣo sahasrasya pratiṣṭutiḥ
<16.11.15>     sahasramanyamabhitiṣṭhatītyāhuḥ sahasramanyo'nvātiṣṭhatīti
<16.11.16>     kakubhaṃ prācīmudūhatyatha yadeṣā dvipadā kakubho loke kriyate sahasrasyaiva so'nvāsthāyaḥ
<16.11.17>     anuṣṭubhaṃ saṃpadyate vāganuṣṭubvāktrirātrastena trirātramāpnoti

<16.12.1>     ādityāścāṅgirasaścādīkṣanta te svarge loke'spardhanta te'ṅgirasa ādityebhyaḥ śvaḥ sutyāṃ prābruvaṃsta ādityā etamapaśyaṃstaṃ sadyaḥ parikrīyāyāsyamudgātāraṃ vṛtvā tena stutvā svargaṃ lokamāyannahīyantāṅgirasaḥ
<16.12.2>     bhrātṛvyavān yajeta
