<16.12.3>     bhavatyātmanā parāsya bhrātṛvyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<16.12.4>     tasmā amumādityamaśvaṃ śvetaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇāmānayaṃste pratigṛhya vyabhraṃśata sa etānyāyāsyānyapaśyat tairātmānaṃ samaśrīṇāt
<16.12.5>     tadāyāsyāni bhavanti bheṣajāyaiva śāntyai
<16.12.6>     svargakāmo yajeta
<16.12.7>     bṛhatīṃ saṃpadyate bṛhatyā vai devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāyan svargamevaitena lokamāpnoti
<16.12.8>     paśukāmo yajeta
<16.12.9>     paśavo vai bṛhatī paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhati

<16.13.1>     etasyaivaikaviṃśamagniṣṭomasāma kṛtvāmayāvinaṃ yājayet
<16.13.2>     prāṇo vai trivṛt prāṇa ādityaḥ prāṇaireṣa vyṛdhyate ya āmayāvī prāṇairevainaṃ samardhayati [thusBī̭ Kṣṣseemsto readsamarṅghayati]
<16.13.3>     virājaṃ saṃpadyate'pa vā etasmādannādyaṃ krāmati ya āmayāvyannaṃ virāḍannadyamevāsmin dadhāti
<16.13.4>     ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomo bhavatyapratiṣṭhito vā eṣa ya āmayāvī pratiṣṭhaikaviṃśaḥ pratyeva tiṣṭhati
<16.13.5>     etenaivānnādyakāmo vā pratiṣṭhākāmo vā yajetānnaṃ virāṭpratiṣṭhaikaviṃśo'ttyannaṃ pratitiṣṭhati
<16.13.6>     urvarā vedirbhavatyetadvā asyā vīryavattamaṃ vīryeṇaiva yajñaṃ samardhayati
<16.13.7>     khala uttaravediratra hi sa rasaḥ samavaiti sarasameva yajñaṃ karoti
<16.13.8>     khalevālī yūpo bhavatyetayā hi taṃ rasamutkṛṣanti
<16.13.9>     trivatsaḥ sāṇḍaḥ somakrayaṇaḥ sendratvāya
<16.13.10>     sarvā diśo'śvarathāḥ somapravākā vidhāvanti
<16.13.11>     sarvābhya evāsmai digbhyo'nnādyamavarundhe
<16.13.12>     yojane caturvāhiṇā prācyāṃ diśi prāhaivamiva vā adhvāno vimitā yaivādhvano mātrātāṃ dhāvayanti traipade praṣṭhivāhinodīcyāṃ gavyūtau dvyogena praticyāṃ krośe sthūriṇā dakṣiṇaitadvai diśāṃ rūpaṃ yadaiva diśāṃ rūpaṃ tena yajñaṃ samardhayanti
<16.13.13>     sakṣīradṛtayo rathā bhavanti tato yannavanītamudiyāt tadājye'pi kāryaṃ sadyastvāya

<16.14.1>     athaiṣo'ṅgirasāmanukrīḥ
<16.14.2>     etena vā aṅgirasa ādityānāpnuvan yo hīna ānujāvara iva syāt sa etena yajetāpnoti pūrveṣāṃ prahāmāpnuvan hyetenāṅgirasa ādityān
<16.14.3>     tasya caturviṃśau pavamānau
<16.14.4>     caturviṃśatyakṣarā gāyatrī gāyatryā vai devā imān lokān vyāpnuvannimānevaitena lokān vyāpnoti
<16.14.5>     tejo brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī teja eva brahmavarcasamavarundhe prāṇo gāyatrī prajananaṃ prāṇādeva gāyatryāḥ prajāyate
<16.14.6>     ubhaye stomā yugmantaścāyujaś[thusBī̭ Kṣṣcāryujaś] ca tanmithunaṃ tasmānmithunāt prajāyate
<16.14.7>     virājaṃ saṃpadyate'nnaṃ virāḍannādyamevāvarundhe
<16.14.8>     ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomo bhavati pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśo'ntata eva yajñasya pratitiṣṭhati

<16.15.1>     athaiṣa viśvajicchilpaḥ
<16.15.2>     śilpaṃ vā eṣa stomānāṃ paśyati gṛhe śilpaṃ ya evaṃ veda
<16.15.3>     tasyāṣṭādaśau pavamānau
<16.15.4>     cakrīvān vā eṣa yajñaḥ kāmāya yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate tametenābhyaśnute yatra hi cakrīvatā kāmayate tadabhyaśnute
<16.15.5>     svargakāmo yajeta
<16.15.6>     svargo lokaḥ pṛṣṭhāni svargamevaitena lokamāpnoti
<16.15.7>     tejo brahmavarcasaṃ pṛṣṭhāni yadekadhā pṛṣṭhāni bhavantyekadhaivāsmiṃstejo brahmavarcasaṃ dadhāti
<16.15.8>     annaṃ paśavaḥ pṛṣṭhāni yadekadhā pṛṣṭhāni bhavantyekadhaivāsminnannādyaṃ paśūn dadhāti
<16.15.9>     tadāhurnānālokāni pṛṣṭhāni yadekasmin yajñakratau samavarudhyanta īśvaro yajamāno'pratiṣṭhātoriti
<16.15.10>     ekaviṃśaṃ hotuḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavati pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśo madhya eva yajñasya pratitiṣṭhatyekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomo bhavati pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśo'ntata eva yajñasya pratitiṣṭhati
<16.15.11>     dvāvetāvekaviṃśau bhavato dvipādyajamāno yajamānameva yajñe paśuṣu ca pratiṣṭhāpayati

<16.16.1>     athaiṣa ekatrikaḥ prajāpaterudbhit
<16.16.2>     etena vai prajāpatireṣāṃ lokānāmudabhinat
<16.16.3>     kṛtastomo vā eṣa udbhinnaṃ hyeva kṛtasya
<16.16.4>     yadekayā stuvantyeko vai prajāpatiḥ prajāpatimevāpnotyatha yat tisṛbhistraya ime lokā eṣveva lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<16.16.5>     tā u catasrassaṃpadyante catuṣpādāḥ paśavaḥ paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhati
<16.16.6>     gāyatrīṃ saṃpadyate tejo brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī teja eva brahmavarcasamavarundhe
<16.16.7>     prāṇo gāyatrī prajananaṃ prāṇādeva gāyatryāḥ prajāyate
<16.16.8>   "yā rucā hariṇyā punāna"ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ
<16.16.9>     sarveṣāṃ vā eṣā chandasāṃ rūpaṃ sarveṣveva chandaḥsvārbhavaṃ pavamānaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<16.16.10>     tasyāṃ gāyatrapārśvaṃ na gāyatrādeti na sāmno na nidhanāt

<17.1.1>     devā vai svargaṃ lokamāyaṃsteṣāṃ daivā ahīyanta vrātyāṃ pravasantasta āgacchan yato devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāyaṃstena taṃ stomaṃ na chando'vindan yena tānāpsyaṃste devā maruto'bruvannetebhyastaṃ stomaṃ tacchandaḥ prayacchata yenāsmānāpnavāniti tebhya etaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ stomaṃ prāyacchan parokṣamanuṣṭubhaṃ tato vai te tānāpnuvan
<17.1.2>     hīnā vā ete hīyante ye vrātyāṃ pravasanti na hi brahmacaryaṃ caranti na kṛṣiṃ vaṇijyāṃ ṣoḍaśo vā etat stomaḥ samāptumarhati
<17.1.3>     marutstomo vā eṣa yāni kṣudrāṇi chandāṃsi tāni marutām
<17.1.4>     kakubhaṃ prācīmudūhatyatha yadeṣā dvipadā kakubho loke kriyate rūpeṇaivenāṃstat samardhayati
<17.1.2>   "dhāhīndra girvaṇa"iti viṣamaṃ chando viṣama iva vai vrātaḥ sarvānevaitān samān karoti
<17.1.6>     tāsu dyautānam
<17.1.7>     dyutāno mārutasteṣāṃ gṛhapatirāsīt ta etena stomenāyajanta te sarva ārdhnuvan yadetat sāma bhavatyṛdhyā eva
<17.1.8>     yanniruktaṃ nidhanamupeyurgṛhapatirevardhnuyādapetara ārdhnītātha yadaniruktamupayanti sarvānevaitānṛddhau bhūtau pratiṣṭhāpayati
<17.1.9>     garagiro vā ete ye brahmādyaṃ janyamannamadantyaduruktavākyaṃ duruktamāhuradaṇḍyaṃ daṇḍena ghnantaścarantyadīkṣitā dīkṣitavācaṃ vadanti ṣoḍaśo vā eteṣāṃ stomaḥ pāpmānaṃ nirhantumarhati yadete catvāraḥ ṣoḍaśā bhavanti tena pāpmano'dhi nirmucyante
<17.1.10>   "devo vo draviṇodā"ityagniṣṭomasāma kāryaṃ devatāsvevainān pratiṣṭhāpayati
<17.1.11>     atho khalvāhuḥ''darśi gātuvittama"ityeva satobṛhatīṣu kāryaṃ viṣama iva vai vrātaḥ sarvānevainān satobṛhataḥ karoti
<17.1.12>     tadāhuḥ śithilamiva vā etat chandaścarācaraṃ yat satobṛhatī''devo vo draviṇodā"ityeva kāryam
<17.1.13>     eṣā vai pratiṣṭhitā bṛhatī yā punaḥpadā tadyat padaṃ punarārabhate tasmāt putro mātaramadhyeti
<17.1.14>     uṣṇīṣaṃ ca pratodaśca jyāhṇoḍaśca vipathaśca phalakāstīrṇaḥ kṛṣṇaśaṃ vāsaḥ kṛṣṇavalakṣe ajine rajato niṣkastadgṛhapateḥ
<17.1.15>     valūkāntāni dāmatūṣāṇītareṣāṃ dve dve dāmanī dve dve upānahau dviṣaṃhitānyajināni
<17.1.16>     etadvai vrātyadhanaṃ yasmā etaddadati tasminneva mṛjānā yānti
<17.1.17>     trayastriṃśatā trayastriṃśatā gṛhapatimabhisamāyanti trayastriṃśaddhi devā ārdhnuvanṛdhyā eva
<17.2.1>     athaiṣa ṣaṭṣoḍaśī ye nṛśaṃsā ninditāḥ santo vrātyāṃ pravaseyusta etena yajeran
<17.2.2>     abhipūrveṇa vā ete pāpmanā gṛhītā ye nṛśaṃsā ninditāḥ santo vrātyāṃ pravasanti yat ṣaṭṣoḍaśāni stotrāṇi bhavanti tena pāpmāno'dhi nirmucyante
<17.2.3>     yadekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomo bhavati pratiṣṭhāyā ekaviṃśo madhyata eva yajñasya pratitiṣṭhati
<17.2.4>     uktho bhavati paśavo vā ukthāni paśavo nṛśaṃsamagryaṃ pariṇayanti paśubhirevainānagryaṃ pariṇayati

<17.3.1>     athaiṣa dviṣoḍaśo ye kaniṣṭhāḥ santo vrātyāṃ pravaseyusta etena yajeran
<17.3.2>     hīnā vā ete ahīyante ye kaniṣṭhāḥ santo vrātyāṃ pravasanti yat trivṛtaḥ pavamānā bhavanti mukhaṃ vai trivṛt stomānāṃ mukhata evainān yajñasya pariṇayati
<17.3.3>     yadvai ṣoḍaśe stotre bhavatastena pāpmano'dhi nirmucyante
<17.3.4>     ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomo bhavati pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśo'ntata eva yajñasya pratitiṣṭhati

<17.4.1>     athaiṣa śamanīcāmeḍhrāṇāṃ stomo ye jyeṣṭhāḥ santo vrātyāṃ pravaseyusta etena yajeran
<17.4.2>     agrādagraṃ rohantyūrdhvāḥ stomā yantyanapabhraṃśāya
<17.4.3>     etena vai śamanīcāmeḍhrā ayajanta teṣāṃ kuṣītakaḥ sāmaśravaso gṛhapatirāsīt tān luśākapiḥ khārgaliranuvyāharadavākīrṣata kanīyāṃsau stomāvupāguriti tasmāt kauṣītakānāṃ na kaścanātīva jihīte yajñāva kīrṇā hi

<17.5.1>     indro vai triśirasaṃ tvāṣṭramahaṃstamaślīlā vāgabhyavadat so'gnimupādhāvat sa etadagnistotramapaśyat tadātmanyadhividhāya tenainamayājayat tenāsyāślīlāṃ vācamapāhan
<17.5.2>     apāślīlāṃ vācaṃ hate ya evaṃ veda
<17.5.3>     yo'pūta iva syādagniṣṭutā yajetāgninaivāsya pāpmānamapahatya trivṛtā tejo brahmavarcasaṃ dadhāti
<17.5.4>     tadāhuryat trivṛdbhavatyekasmādevāṅgāt pāpmānamapahanti mukhādeveti
<17.5.5>     jyotiṣṭoma eva kāryaḥ
<17.5.6>     yat trivṛdbhavati yadevāsya mukhato'pūtaṃ tat tenāpahanti yat pañcadaśo yadevāsyorasto bāhvorapūtaṃ tat tenāpahanti yat saptadaśo yadevāsya madhyato'pūtaṃ tat tenāpahanti yadekaviṃśo yadevāsya padoraṣṭhīvatorapūtaṃ tat tenāpahanti
<17.5.7>     vaiśvānaraṃ vā eṣa praviśatītyāhuryo'gniṣṭutā yajata iti vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma kāryaṃ tasya yadapūtaṃ tadagniḥ kṣāpayatyathetaraḥ śuciḥ pūta udeti

<17.6.1>     trivṛdagniṣṭudagniṣṭomastasya vāyavyāsvagniṣṭomasāma
<17.6.2>     brahmavarcasakāmo yajeta
<17.6.3>     tejo vai trivṛdbrahmavarcasaṃ yadvāyavyāsvagniṣṭomasāma bhavatyupaivainaṃ taddhamati
<17.6.4>     yathā hiraṇyaṃ niṣṭapedevamenamagniṣṭunniṣṭapati

<17.7.1>     etasyaiva revatīṣu vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma kṛtvā paśukāmo yajeta
<17.7.2>     jaratkakṣo vā eṣa yo'paśuryathā vai jaratkakṣe paśavo na ramanta evametasmin paśavo na ramante yo'paśuryadā vai jaratkakṣamagnirdahatyathainamabhivarṣatyathāsminnoṣadhayo jāyante'tha vai tasmin paśavo ramante
<17.7.3>     ramante'smin paśavo ya evaṃ veda
<17.7.4>     yadevāsyāpaśavyaṃ tadagniṣṭunnirdahati yadagniṣṭunnirdahati tadadbhī revatībhiḥ śamayati

<17.8.1>     jyotiṣṭomenāgniṣṭutā yajñavibhraṣṭo yajeta
<17.8.2>     yasmin vā yajñakratau vibhraṃśeta
<17.8.3>     agnirvā etasya havyamatti yo yajñe vibhraṃśate na devatā havyaṃ gamayatyagnimevaikadhardhnoti
<17.8.4>     yadvai saṃśīryate'thānyanniṣkurvanti tena tadyāti yadā vāva tanniṣkurvantyatha tadyāti yeṣveva stomeṣu vibhraṃśate yasmin yajñakratau taireva yajeta yeṣveva stomeṣu vibhraṃśate yasmin yajñakratau teṣveva pratitiṣṭhati

<17.9.1>     saptadaśenāgniṣṭutānnādyakāmo yajeta
<17.9.2>     annaṃ vai saptadaśo'gnirannādyasya pradātāgnirevāsmā annādyaṃ prayacchati
<17.9.3>     annādo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<17.9.4>     sarvaḥ saptadaśo bhavati prajāpatirvai saptadaśaḥ prajāpatimevāpnoti

<17.10.1>     trivṛdagniṣṭomastasyāniruktaṃ prātaḥsavanam
<17.10.2>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata tā asmāt sṛṣṭā apākrāman sa etadaniruktaṃ prātaḥsavanamapaśyat tenāsāṃ madhyaṃ vyavait tā enamupāvartanta paryenamāviśan
<17.10.3>     grāmakāmo yajeta yadetadaniktaṃ prātaḥsavanaṃ bhavati madhyamevāsāṃ vyavaityupainamāvartante paryenaṃ viśanti
<17.10.4>     sa eṣa prajāpaterapūrvo nāsmāt pūrvo bhavati ya evaṃ veda

<17.11.1>     trivṛdagniṣṭomaḥ
<17.11.2>     tasya prātaḥsavane sanneṣu nārāśaṃseṣvekādaśadakṣiṇā vyādiśatyaśvadvādaśā madhyandine tā ubhayīrapākarotyekādaśa tṛtīyasavane tā vaśāyāmapākaroti
<17.11.3>     trayastriṃśadetā dakṣiṇā bhavanti trayastriṃśaddevatā devatā evāpnotyaśvaścatustriṃśo dakṣiṇānāṃ prajāpatiścatustriṃśo devatānāṃ prajāpatimevāpnoti
<17.11.4>     sa eṣa bṛhaspatisavo bṛhaspatirakāmayata devānāṃ purodhāṃ gaccheyamiti sa etenāyajata sa devānāṃ purodhāmagacchat
<17.11.5>     gacchati purodhāṃ ya evaṃ veda
<17.11.6>     sa eṣa sthapatisavo yaṃ sthāpatyāyābhiṣiñceran sa etena yajeta
<17.11.7>     gacchati sthāpatyaṃ ya evaṃ veda
<17.11.8>     kṛṣṇājine'dhyabhiṣicyata etadvai pratyakṣaṃ brahmavarcasaṃ brahmavarcasa evādhyabhiṣicyate
<17.11.9>     ājyenābhiṣicyate teja ājyaṃ teja ātman dhatte

<17.12.1>     trivṛdagniṣṭomaḥ sa sarvasvāro yaḥ kāmayetānāmayatāmuṃ lokamiyāmiti sa etena yajeta
<17.12.2>     prāṇo vai trivṛt prāṇaḥ svaraḥ prāṇānevāsya bahirṇirādadhāti tājakpramīyate
<17.12.3>     trivṛdvai stomānāṃ kṣepiṣṭho yat trivṛdbhavatyāśīyaḥ saṃgacchātā ityananto vai svaro'nanto'sau loko'nantamevainaṃ svargaṃ lokaṃ gamayati
<17.12.4>     abhivatyaḥ pravatyo bhavantyasmādevainaṃ lokāt svargaṃ lokaṃ gamayanti
<17.12.5>     ārbhavapavamāne stūyamāna audumbaryā dakṣiṇā prāvṛto nipadyate tadeva saṃgacchate
<17.12.6>     sa eṣa śunaskarṇastoma etena vai śunaskarṇo bāṣkiho'yajata tasmācchunaskarṇastoma ityākhyāyate

<17.13.1>     trivṛdagniṣṭomo vaiśvadevasya lokaḥ
<17.13.2>     āgneyī pratipadvaiśvadevaḥ paśurbārhaspatyānubandhyā
<17.13.3>     na yūpaṃ minvanti nottaravediṃ nivapanti
<17.13.4>     paridhau paśuṃ niyuñjanti
<17.13.5>     pañcāśaddakṣiṇā
<17.13.6>     ahataṃ vasāno'vabhṛthādudeti caturo māso na māṃsamaśnāti na striyamupaiti
<17.13.7>     tataścaturṣu māseṣu varuṇapraghāsānāṃ loke dvidivaḥ
<17.13.8>     vāruṇī pratipanmārutaḥ paśuḥ
<17.13.1>     kavatī pratipadvāruṇaḥ paśuḥ
<17.13.10>     maitrāvaruṇyanūbandhyā minvanti yūpaṃ nyuttaravediṃ vapanti yūpe paśū niyuñjanti
<17.13.11>     śataṃ dakṣiṇā ahataṃ vasāno'vabhṛthādudeti caturo māso na māṃsamaśnāti na striyamupaiti
<17.13.12>     tataścaturṣu māseṣu sākamedhānāṃ loke trirātraḥ
<17.13.13>     anīkavatī pratipadāgneyaḥ paśurmārutī pratipadaindrāgnaḥ paśurvaiśvakarmaṇī pratipadekādaśinī paśavaḥ sauryānūbandhyā minvanti yūpaṃ nyuttaravediṃ vapanti yūpe paśūnniyuñjanti pañcāśacchataṃ dakṣiṇā
<17.13.14>     ahataṃ vasāno'vabhṛthādudeti caturo māso na māṃsamaśnāti na striyamupaiti
<17.13.15>     tataścaturṣu māseṣu śunāsīryasya loke jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomaḥ
<17.13.16>     upavatī pratipadvāyavyaḥ paśurāśvinyanūbandhyā minvanti yūpaṃ nyuttaravediṃ vapanti yūpe paśuṃ niyuñjanti dvādaśaṃ śataṃ dakṣiṇā
<17.13.17>     agniḥ saṃvatsaraḥ sūryaḥ parivatsaraścandramā idāvatsaro vāyuranuvatsaro'gniṃ saṃvatsaraṃ vaiśvadevenāpnoti sūryaṃ parivatsaraṃ varuṇapraghāsaiścandramasamidāvatsaraṃ sākamedhairvāyumanuvatsaraṃ śunāsīryeṇa
<17.13.18>     haviryajñairvai devā imaṃ lokamabhyajayannantarikṣaṃ paśumadbhiḥ somairamumimāneva lokānāpnotyeṣu lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati ya evaṃ veda
<17.14.1>     yadāgnihotraṃ juhotyatha daśa gṛhamedhina āpnotyekayā rātryā, yadā daśa saṃvatsarānagnihotraṃ juhotyatha darśapūrṇamāsayājinamāpnoti, yadā daśa saṃvatsarān darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajate'thāgniṣṭomayājinamāpnoti, yadā daśabhiragniṣṭomairyajate'tha sahasrayājinamāpnoti, yadā daśabhiḥ sahasrairyajate'thāyutayājinamāpnoti, yadā daśabhirayutairyajate'tha prayutayājinamāpnoti, yadā daśabhiḥ prayutairyajate'tha niyutayājinamāpnoti, yadā daśabhirniyutairyajate'thārbudayājinamāpnoti, yadā daśabhirarbudairyajate'tha nyarbudayājinamāpnoti, yadā daśabhirnyarbudairyajate'tha nikharvakayājinamāpnoti, yadā daśabhirnikharvakairyajate'tha badvayājinamāpnoti, yadā daśabhirbadvairyajate'thākṣitayājinamāpnoti yadā daśabhirakṣitairyajate'tha gaurbhavati, yadā gaurbhavatyathāgnirbhavati yadāgnirbhavatyatha saṃvatsarasya gṛhapatimāpnoti
<17.14.2>     yadā saṃvatsarasya gṛhapatirbhavatyatha vaiśvadevasya mātrāmāpnotyato vā itare parastarāṃ parastarāmeva sarve
<17.14.3>     etāneva lokānāpnotyetān lokān jayati ya evaṃ veda

<18.1.1>     saptadaśo'gniṣṭomaḥ
<18.1.2>     devāśca vā asurāśca prajāpaterdvayāḥ putrā āsaṃste'surā bhūyāṃso balīyāṃsa āsan kanīyāṃso devāste devāḥ prajāpatimupādhāvan sa etamupahavyamapaśyat
<18.1.3>     sa aikṣata yanniruktamāhariṣyāmyasurā me yajñaṃ haniṣyantīti so'niruktamāharat
<18.1.4>     sa uttame stotre''devo vo draviṇodā"iti devānabhiparyāvartata
<18.1.5>     tato devā abhavan parāsurāḥ
<18.1.6>     bhavatyātmanā parāsya bhrātṛvyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<18.1.7>     atho khalvāhuḥ''yajñāyajñā vo agnaya"ityeva kāryam
<18.1.8>     agnirvai sarvā devatāstena na devatānāṃ kāṃ canāntareti
<18.1.9>     indro yatīn sālāvṛkeyebhyaḥ prāyacchat tamaślīlā vāgabhyavadat sa prajāpatimupādhāvat tasmā etamupahavyaṃ prāyacchat taṃ viśve devā upāhvayanta tasmādupahavyaḥ
<18.1.10>     abhiśasyamānaṃ yājayet
<18.1.11>     devatā vā etaṃ parivrajanti yamanṛtamabhiśaṃsanti devatā evāsyānnamādayanti
<18.1.12>     tasya pūtasya svaditasya manuṣyā annamadanti
<18.1.13>     grāmakāmo yajeta
<18.1.14>     mārutī bhavati marutau vai devānāṃ viśo viśamevāsmā anuniyunaktyanapakrāmukāsmādviḍbhavati
<18.1.15>     paśukāmo yajeta pauṣī bhavati
<18.1.16>     paśavo vai pūṣā paśūnevāvarundhe
<18.1.17>     vaiśvadevī bhavati viśve hyenaṃ devā upāhvayanta
<18.1.18>     bṛhatsāmā bhavati
<18.1.19>     prajāpatirhyenamindrāya prāyacchat
<18.1.20>     aśvaḥ śyavo dakṣiṇā
<18.1.21>     sa hyaniruktaḥ
<18.1.22>     sa brahmaṇe deyaḥ
<18.1.23>     brahmā vā ṛtvijāmaniruktaḥ svenaivainaṃ tadrūpeṇa samardhayati
<18.1.24>     yāvaddha vai kumāre sadyo jāta eno nāsmiṃstāvacca naino bhavati ya evaṃ veda

<18.2.1>     saptadaśo'gniṣṭomaḥ
<18.2.2>     tasya dvādaśa dīkṣopasadaḥ
<18.2.3>     svargakāmo yajeta
<18.2.4>     dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsaraḥ svargo lokaḥ svargamevaitena lokamāpnoti
<18.2.5>     ghṛtavrato bhavati
<18.2.6>     devavrataṃ vai ghṛtaṃ devavratenaiva devatā apyeti
<18.2.7>     uttareṇottareṇa kāṇḍenopaityuttara uttara eṣāṃ lokānāṃ jyāyān svargasya samaṣṭyai
<18.2.8>     bṛhatsāma bhavati bṛhatā vai devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāyan svargamevetena lokamāpnoti
<18.2.9>     ṛtamuktvā prasarpantyṛtenaivainaṃ svargaṃ lokaṃ gamayanti
<18.2.10>     somacamaso dakṣiṇā devatayaiva devatā apyeti
<18.2.11>     audumbaro bhavatyūrgudumbara ūrjamevāvarundhe
<18.2.12>     sagotrāya brahmaṇe deyaḥ somapīthasyāvidohāya
<18.2.13>     sarvaḥ saptadaśo bhavati
<18.2.14>     dvādaśa māsāḥ pañcartavaḥ sa vai saṃvatsaraḥ  saṃvatsaraḥ svargo lokaḥ svargamevaitena lokamāpnoti

<18.3.1>     saptadaśo'gniṣṭomaḥ
<18.3.2>     tasya dīkṣaṇīyāyāmiṣṭau dvādaśamānaṃ hiraṇyaṃ dadāti caturviṃśatimānaṃ prāyaṇīyāyāṃ dve caturviṃśatimāne ātithyāyāṃ catvāri caturviṃśatimānāni prātaḥ prathamāyāmupasadyaṣṭau caturviṃśatimānānyaparāhṇe prathamāyāmupasadi ṣoḍaśacaturviṃśatimānāni prātarmadhyamāyāmupasadi dvātriṃśataṃ caturviṃśatimānānyaparāhṇe madhyamāyāmupasadi catuḥṣaṣṭiṃ caturviṃśatimānāni prātaruttamāyāmupasadyaṣṭāviṃśatiśataṃ caturviṃśatimānānyaparāhṇa uttamāyāmupasadi dve aṣṭāviṃśatiśatamāne agnīṣomīyasya paśorvapāyāṃ catvāryaṣṭāviṃśatiśatamānāni prātaḥ paśorvapāyāmaṣṭāvaṣṭāviṃśatiśatamānāni prātaḥsavane sanneṣu nārāśaṃseṣu ṣoḍaśāṣṭāviṃśatiśatamānāni mādhyandine savane'naḍucchataṃ ca rukmo hotuḥ sragudgāturdvātriṃśatamaṣṭāviṃśatiśatamānāni tṛtīyasavane sanneṣu nārāśaṃseṣu catuḥṣaṣṭimaṣṭāviṃśatiśatamānānyudayanīyāyāmiṣṭāvaṣṭāviṃśatiśatamaṣṭāviṃśatiśatamānāni vaśāyā vapāyām
<18.3.3>     eṣa vā anaḍuho lokamāpnoti ya evaṃ veda
<18.3.4>     eṣa vai jyotiṣmantaṃ puṇyaṃ lokaṃ jayati ya evaṃ vidvānetena yajate

<18.4.1>     saptadaśo'gniṣṭomaḥ
<18.4.2>     tasya prātaḥsavanīyān somān pratiduhā śrīṇāti śṛtena madhyandine dadhnā tṛtīyasavane
<18.4.3>     paśukāmo yajeta
<18.4.4>     yat sarvāṇi savanānyāśīrvanti bhavantyanusavanamevainaṃ paśubhiḥ sarmardhayati prajā tvasya mīliteva bhavati śukriye hi savane payasā śrīṇāti
<18.4.5>     vaiśyaṃ yājayet
<18.4.6>     etadvai vaiśyasya samṛddhaṃ yat paśavaḥ paśubhirevainaṃ samardhayati
<18.4.7>     tasya kaṇvarathantaraṃ pṛṣṭham
<18.4.8>     sadoviśīyaṃ brahmasāma
<18.4.9>     paśavo vai kaṇvarathantaraṃ paśavaḥ sadoviśīyamābhipūrvānevāsmin paśūn dadhāti
<18.4.10>     sarvaḥ saptadaśo bhavati
<18.4.11>     dvādaśa māsāḥ pañcartavaḥ sa vai saṃvatsaraḥ  saṃvatsaraṃ paśavo'nuprajāyante tānevāptvāvarundhe

<18.5.1>     saptadaśa ukthyaḥ
<18.5.2>     indro vṛtramahan sa viṣvaṅvīryeṇa vyārcchat tasmai devāḥ prāyaścittimaicchaṃstaṃ na kiṃ canādhinot taṃ tīvrasoma evādhinot
<18.5.3>     somātipavitaṃ yājayet
<18.5.4>     chidra iva vā eṣa yaṃ somo'tipavate yat tīvrasomena yajate pihityā evācchidratāyai
<18.5.5>     rājānamaparuddhaṃ yājayet
<18.5.6>     viḍvā etamatipavate yo rājāparudhyate yat tīvrasomena yajate pihityā evācchidratāyai
<18.5.7>     grāmakāmo yajeta
<18.5.8>     grāmo vā etamatipavate yo'laṃ grāmāya san grāmaṃ na vindate yat tīvrasomena yajate pihityā evācchidratāyai
<18.5.9>     prajākāmo yajeta prajā vā etamatipavate yo'laṃ prajāyāḥ san prajāṃ na vindate yat tīvrasomena yajate pihityā evācchidratāyai
<18.5.10>     paśukāmo yajeta paśavo vā etamatipavante yo'laṃ paśubhyaḥ san paśūnna vindate yat tīvrasomena yajate pihityā [thusBī̭'pihityā Kṣṣ] evācchidratāyai
<18.5.11>     āmayāvinaṃ yājayet prāṇā vā etamatipavante ya āmayāvī yat tīvrasomena yajate pihityā evācchidratāyai
<18.5.12>     śatamāśiraṃ duhanti tīvrayantyevainam
<18.5.13>     tat tā u eva dakṣiṇāḥ
<18.5.14>     abhyabhisomānunnayanti tīvra enaṃ dhinavadityubhāvadhvaryū sarve camasādhvaryavo'cchāvākāya pratigṛṇanti tīvrayantyevainam
<18.5.15>     tadabhakṣayanta ṛtvijaścamasānavajighranti tīvrayantyevainaṃ tat tānacchāvākasya stotre bhakṣayanti tīvrayantyevainam
<18.5.16>     tadyat savanāni vyavabhakṣayeyurapakrāmukā yajamānācchrīḥ syāt sakṛtsakṛt savanānāmantato bhakṣayanti savanānāmasaṃbhedāya
<18.5.17>     rathantaraṃ sāma bhavati
<18.5.18>     iyaṃ vai rathantaramasyāṃ vā eṣa na pratitiṣṭhati yo na pratitiṣṭhatyasyāmevainaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<18.5.19>     śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma bhavatyetadevāsmiñchrīṇāti
<18.5.21>     vāganuṣṭupvāco raso yajñāyajñīyaṃ vācyevāsya rasaṃ dadhāti
<18.5.22>     viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma bhavatyetadevāsmin sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<18.5.23>     udvaṃśīyamukthānāmantato bhavati sarveṣāṃ vā etat pṛṣṭhānāṃ rūpaṃ sarveṣveva rūpeṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<18.5.24>     ukthyo bhavati paśavo vā ukthāni paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhati

<18.6.1>     saptadaśa ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśimān saptadaśī
<18.6.2>     yāvān vai prajāpatirūrdhvastāvāṃstiryaṅ
<18.6.3>     yāvanta ime lokā ūrdhvāstāvantastiryañcaḥ
<18.6.4>     vājapeyayājī vāva prajāpatimāpnoti
<18.6.5>     yat saptadaśa stotrāṇi tenordhvamapnoti yat sarvaḥ saptadaśastena tiryañcam
<18.6.6>     tasya nānāvīryāṇi savanāni
<18.6.7>     aniruktaṃ prātaḥsavanaṃ vājavanmādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ citravat tṛtīyasavanam
<18.6.8>     yadaniruktaṃ prātaḥsavanaṃ bhavatyanirukto vai prajāpatiḥ prajāpatimevāpnoti yadvājavanmādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ annaṃ vai vājo'nnādyasyāvarudhyai yaccitravat tṛtīyasavanaṃ svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
<18.6.9>     viyonirvājapeya ityāhuḥ prājāpatyaḥ sanniruktasāmeti yadaniruktaṃ prātaḥsavanaṃ tena sayoniḥ
<18.6.10>     rathantaraṃ sāma bhavatyāśīya ujjhityai
<18.6.11>     iyaṃ vai rathantaramasyāmevādhyabhiṣicyate
<18.6.12>     tasmādvājapeyayājyapratyavarohī
<18.6.13>     asyāṃ hi so'dhyabhiṣicyate
<18.6.14>     abhīvarto brahmasāma bhavati brahmaṇo vā eṣa ṛṣabha ṛṣabhatāmevainaṃ gamayati
<18.6.15>     yajñāyajñīyamanuṣṭubhi bhavati vāganuṣṭupvāco raso yajñāyajñīyaṃ vācyevāsya rasaṃ dadhāti
<18.6.16>     vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma bhavatīndriyasya vīryasya parigṛhītyai
<18.6.17>     udvaṃśīyamukthānāmantato bhavati sarveṣāṃ vā etat pṛṣṭhānāṃ rūpaṃ sarveṣveva rūpeṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<18.6.18>     gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma bhavati
<18.6.19>     atiriktaṃ gaurīvitamatiriktaḥ ṣoḍaśyatirikta evātiriktaṃ dadhāti
<18.6.20>     tadāhurjāmi vā etadyajñe kriyate yadudvaṃśīyādgaurīvitena stuvate svārāt svāreṇeti
<18.6.21>     na jāmyasti savanaṃ saṃtiṣṭhate
<18.6.22>     ukthaṃ śasyate vaṣaṭkāro'ntarā tenājāmi
<18.6.23>     apacchidiva vā etadyajñakāṇḍaṃ yat ṣoḍaśī tenājāmi
<18.6.24>     yajñāraṇye saṃtiṣṭhata ityāhuratyukthānyetyati ṣoḍaśinaṃ na rātriṃ prāpnotīti
<18.6.25>     viṣṇoḥ śipiviṣṭavatīṣu bṛhaduttamaṃ bhavati
<18.6.26>     eṣā vai prajāpateḥ paśuṣṭhā tanūryacchipiviṣṭaḥ prāṇo vai bṛhat prāṇa eva paśuṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<18.6.27>     bṛhatā stuvanti bṛhadamuṃ lokamāptumarhati tamevāpnoti

<18.7.1>     prajāpatirakāmayata vājamāpnuyāṃ svargaṃ lokamiti sa etaṃ vājapeyamapaśyadvājapeyo vā eṣa vājamevaitena svargaṃ lokamāpnoti
<18.7.2>     śukravatyo jyotiṣmatyaḥ prātaḥsavane bhavanti tejo brahmavarcasaṃ tābhiravarundhe
<18.7.3>     vājavatyo mādhyandine bhavanti svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai
<18.7.4>     annavatyo gaṇavatyaḥ paśumatyastṛtīyasavane bhavanti bhūmānaṃ tābhiravarunadhe
<18.7.5>     sarvaḥ saptadaśo bhavati prajāpatirvai saptadaśaḥ prajāpatimevāpnoti
<18.7.6>     hiraṇyasraja ṛtvijo bhavanti mahasa eva tadrūpaṃ kriyate
<18.7.7>     eṣa me'muṣmin loke prakāśo'saditi
<18.7.8>     jyotirvai hiraṇyaṃ jyotirevāsmin dadhāti
<18.7.9>     ājiṃ dhāvanti yajamānamujjāpayanti svargamevainaṃ tallokamujjāpayanti
<18.7.10>     nākaṃ rohati svargameva tallokaṃ rohati
<18.7.11>     sarajase rohati manuṣyalokādevainamantardadhāti
<18.7.12>     vājināṃ sāma brahmā rathacakre'bhigāyati vājo vai svargo lokaḥ svargamevainaṃ tallokamujjāpayati
<18.7.13>     viṣṇoḥ śipiviṣṭavatīṣu bṛhaduttamaṃ bhavati svargameva tallokaṃ rūḍhvā bradhnasya viṣṭapamabhyatikrāmati

<18.8.1>     agniṣṭomaṃ prathamamāharati yajñamukhaṃ vā agniṣṭomo yajñamukhamevārabhya savamākramate
<18.8.2>     athaiṣo'bhiṣecanīyaḥ
<18.8.3>     tasya dvātriṃśāḥ pavamānā dvātriṃśadakṣarā'nuṣṭubvāganuṣṭubyāvatī vāktayaiva sūyate
<18.8.4>     saṃśara iva vā eṣa chandasāṃ yadviṣamā stomā ayathāpūrvamiti
<18.8.5>     yat samāḥ pavamānāstenāsaṃśarastena yathāpūrvam
<18.8.6>     ātmanā vā agniṣṭomenardhnotyātmanā puṇyo bhavatyatha yadukthāni paśavo vā ukthāni viḍukthāni yadukthāni bhavantyanusantatyā eva
<18.8.7>   "vāyo śukro ayāmita"iti vāyavyā pratipadbhavati vāgvai vāyurvācamevāsya yajñamukhe yunakti tayābhiṣicyate sarvasyā eva vācaḥ sūyate sarvā enaṃ vāco rājeti vadanti
<18.8.8>     saṃbhāryā bhavanti pṛṣṭhānyeva tābhiryunakti yannānādevatyāstena yunakti vīryaṃ vai pṛṣṭhāni vīrya evādhyabhiṣicyate
<18.8.9>     yanti vā ete yajñamukhādityāhurye saṃbhāryāḥ kurvata iti
<18.8.10>     yat''pavasya vāco agriya"iti tena yajñamukhānna yanti
<18.8.11>   "davidyutatyā rucā"iti chandasāṃ rūpaṃ chandāṃsyevāsya yajñamukhe yunakti tairabhiṣicyate
<18.8.12>   "etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipa"ityādityā ādityā vā imāḥ prajāstāsāmeva madhyataḥ sūyate
<18.8.13>   "pavasvendo vṛṣā suta"vṛṣaṇvatyo bhavanti triṣṭubho rūpaṃ vīryaṃ vai triṣṭupvīrya evādhyabhiṣicyate
<18.8.14>   "utte śuṣmāsa īrata"ityudvatyo bhavantyudvadvā anuṣṭubho rūpamānuṣṭubho rājanyastasmādudvatyo bhavanti
<18.8.15>   "pavamānasya te kava"iti prāṇānāṃ kḷptyai
<18.8.16>   "dha kṣapā pariṣkṛta"ityanuṣṭupprathamānuṣṭubuttamā vāgvā anuṣṭubvācaiva prayanti vācamabhyudyanti
<18.8.17>     chinnamiva vā etadyadekarcā yadetāvānuṣṭubhau tṛcāvabhito bhavato bahavaḥ puraetāro bhavanti bahavaḥ paścāpinaḥ
<18.8.18>     sauryānuṣṭubuttamā bhavati svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai

<18.9.1>     varuṇasya vai suṣuvāṇasya bhargo'pākrāmat sa tredhāpatadbhṛgustṛtīyamabhavacchrāyantīyaṃ tṛtīyamapastṛtīyaṃ prāviśat
<18.9.2>     yadbhārgavo hotā bhavati tenaiva tadindriyaṃ vīryamāptvāvarundhe yat śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma bhavati tenaiva tadindriyaṃ vīryamāptvārundhe yat puṣkarasrajaṃ pratimuñcate tenaiva tadindriyaṃ vīryamāptvāvarundhe
<18.9.3>     daśamī bhavati
<18.9.4>     daśa camasā daśa camasādhvaryavo daśa daśa camasamabhiyantyā daśamāt puruṣādanvākhyāya prasarpanti daśa samṛddho hyeṣa yajña enaṃ vāva te tadyajñamanvaicchan ya etena yajata etadevendriyaṃ vīryamāttvāsmin dadhāti
<18.9.5>     sarvaḥ saptadaśo bhavati dvādaśa māsāḥ pañcartavaḥ sa vai saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsarādevendriyaṃ vīryamāptvāvarundhe
<18.9.6>     indro vṛtramahaṃstasyeyaṃ citrāṇyupaidrūpāṇyasau nakṣatrāṇāmavakāśena puṇḍarīkaṃ jāyate yat puṣkarasrajaṃ pratimuñcate vṛtrasyaiva tadrūpaṃ kṣatraṃ pratimuñcate
<18.9.7>     dvādaśa puṣkarā bhavanti dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsare'ntarbhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca bhūtena caivainaṃ bhavyena ca samardhayati
<18.9.8>     sragudgātussaurya udgātā na vai tasmai vyaucchadatho vyevāsmai vāsayati
<18.9.9>     rukmo hoturāgneyo hotātho amumevāsmā ādityamunnayati
<18.9.10>     prākāśāvadhvaryoryāviva hyadhvaryū atho cakṣuṣī evāsmin dadhāti
<18.9.11>     aśvaḥ prastotuḥ prājāpatyo'śvaḥ prājāpatyaḥ prastotātho preva hyaśvaḥ prothati preva prastotā stauti
<18.9.12>     dhenuḥ pratihartuḥ paya evāsmin dadhāti
<18.9.13>     vaśā maitrāvaruṇasya vaśaṃ mā nayāditi
<18.9.14>     ṛṣabho brāhmaṇācchaṃsino vīryaṃ vā ṛṣabho vīryamevāsmin dadhāti
<18.9.15>     vāsaḥ potuḥ pavitratvāya
<18.9.16>     varāsī neṣṭuranulambeva hyeṣā hotrā
<18.9.17>     sthūri yavācitamacchāvākasya sthūririva hyeṣā hotrātho nirvaruṇatvāyaiva yavā na vai tarhi yadasyāṃ dakṣiṇā abhyabhavannatho asya ta eva tenābhīṣṭāḥ prītā bhavanti
<18.9.18>     anaḍvānagnīdho yuktyai
<18.9.19>     ajaḥ subrahmaṇyāyai
<18.9.20>     vatsataryunnetuḥ sāṇḍastrivatso grāvastuto mithunatvāya
<18.9.21>     dvādaśa paṣṭhauhyo garbhiṇyo brahmaṇo dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsara eva pratitiṣṭhatyatha yadgarbhiṇyo vāgvai dhenurmantro garbho vācyevāsya mantraṃ dadhātyāmantraṇīyo bhavatyatha yaddhenubhavyā dvādaśa payāṃsi tānyevāsmin dadhāti tasmādāhuḥ payasvī rājā puṇya iti

<18.10.1>     yo vai savādeti nainaṃ sava upanamatyatha yaḥ sāmabhya eti pāpīyān suṣuvāṇo bhavati
<18.10.2>     saṃbhāryā bhavanti pṛṣṭhānyeva tābhiryunakti
<18.10.3>     etāni vāva sāmāni yat pṛṣṭhāni
<18.10.4>     yat saṃbhāryā bhavanti tadeva sāmabhyo naiti
<18.10.5>     yāni devarājñāṃ sāmāni tairamuṣmin loka ṛdhnoti yāni manuṣyarājñāṃ tairasminnubhayoranayorlokayorṛdhnoti devaloke ca manuṣyaloke ca
<18.10.6>     sāmatriṣṭubhyadhyabhiṣicyate vīryaṃ vai sāma triṣṭupvīrya evādhyabhiṣicyate
<18.10.7>     ekādaśa rājasāmāni bhavantyekādaśākṣarā triṣṭubojo vīryaṃ triṣṭubojasyeva vīrye'dhyabhiṣicyate
<18.10.8>     yat trivṛtamabhiṣecanīye kuryurbrahma kṣatrāyāpidadhyuryat trivṛtamuddharanti brahma tat kṣatrāduddharanti tasmādbharatāṃ pratidaṇḍā brahmaṇā na hi te trivṛtamabhiṣecanīye kurvanti
<18.10.9>     ekaviṃśo'bhiṣecanīyasyottamaḥ saptadaśo daśapeya ekaviṃśaḥ keśavapanīyasya prathamaḥ kṣatraṃ vā ekaviṃśo viṭsaptadaśaḥ kṣatreṇaivāsmai viśamubhayataḥ parigṛhṇātyanapakrāmukāsmādviṭbhavati
<18.10.10>     yadvai rājasūyenābhiṣicyate tat svargaṃ lokamārohati sa yadimaṃ lokaṃ nopāvarohedati janaṃ vā gacchedudvā mādyedyadeṣo'rvācīnastomaḥ keśavapanīyo bhavatyasya lokasyānuddhānāya yathā śākhāyāḥ śākhāmālambhamupāvarohedevametenemaṃ lokamupāvarohati pratiṣṭhāyai

<18.11.1>     indro vṛtramahan sa viṣyaṅvīryeṇa vyabhraṃśata sa etacchrāyantīyamapaśyat tenātmānaṃ samaśrīṇādindriyeṇa vā eṣa vīryeṇa vyṛdhyate yo rājasūyenābhiṣicyate vṛtraṃ hi hanti yacchrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma bhavati punarevātmānaṃ saṃśrīṇāti
<18.11.2>     yajñāyajñīyamanuṣṭubhi bhavati vācā vā eṣa vyṛdhyate yo rājasūyenābhiṣicyate vṛtraṃ hi hanti vāganuṣṭubvāco raso yajñāyajñīyaṃ vācyevāsya rasaṃ dadhāti
<18.11.3>     vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma bhavatīndriyeṇa vā eṣa vīryeṇa vyṛdhyate yo rājasūyenābhiṣicyate vṛtraṃ hi hanti yadvāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma bhavatīndriyasya vīryasya parigṛhītyai
<18.11.4>     aśrayan vāva śrāyantīyenāvārayanta vāravantīyenendriyasya vā eṣā vīryasya parigṛhītiḥ
<18.11.5>     apratiṣṭhito vā eṣa yo rājasūyenābhiṣicyate yadā vā etena dvirātreṇa yajate'thaiva pratiṣṭhā
<18.11.6>     yāvanti saṃvatsarasyāhorātrāṇi tāvatya etāḥ stotrīyāḥ saṃvatsara eva pratitiṣṭhati
<18.11.7>     agniṣṭomaḥ pūrvamaharatirātra uttaraṃ nānaivāhorātrayoḥ pratitiṣṭhati
<18.11.8>     amāvāsyāyāṃ pūrvamaharudriṣṭa [em. Calanduddṛṣṭa] uttaraṃ nānaivārdhamāsayoḥ pratitiṣṭhati paurṇamāsyāṃ pūrvamaharvyaṣṭakāyāmuttaraṃ nānaiva māsoḥ pratitiṣṭhati tadāhurya eva samānapakṣe puṇyāhanī syātāṃ tayoreva kāryaṃ samṛdhyai
<18.11.9>     apaśavyo dvirātra ityāhurdve hyete chandasī gāyatraṃ ca traiṣṭubhaṃ ca jagatīmantaryantīti na tena jagatī kṛtā yat tṛtīyasavane kriyate
<18.11.10>     yadā vā eṣāhīnasyāharbhajate sāhvasya vā savanamathaiva jagatī kṛtā traiśokamuttarasyāhno brahmasāma bhavati vaikhānasamacchāvākasāma yacchukriye savane kriyete tenaiva jagatī kṛtā tena paśavyaḥ
<18.11.11>     vyuṣṭirvā eṣa dvirātro vyevāsmai vāsayati

<19.1.1>     athaiṣa rāṭ
<19.1.2>     yo rājya āśaṃsamāno rājyaṃ na prāpnuyāt sa etena yajeta rājaivainaṃ rājānaṃ karoti taṃ tu vairājeti vadeyuryaṃ rājā rājānaṃ kuryādrājaivainaṃ rājānaṃ karoti
<19.1.3>     chando'nye yajñāḥ saṃpadyante stomameṣa vīryaṃ vai stomo vīrya evādhyabhiṣicyate
<19.1.4>     aṣṭāvekaviṃśāḥ saṃstuto bhavatyaṣṭau vai vīrā rāṣṭraṃ samudyacchanti rājabhrātā ca rājaputraśca purohitaśca mahiṣī ca sūtaśca grāmaṇī ca kṣattā ca saṃgrahītā caite vai vīrā rāṣṭraṃ samudyacchantyeteṣvevādhyabhiṣicyate
<19.1.5>     kṣatraṃ vā ekaviṃśaḥ pratiṣṭhā kṣatrasyevāsya prakāśo bhavati pratitiṣṭhati ya evaṃ veda

<19.2.1>     athaiṣa virāḍannādyakāmo yajeta
<19.2.2>     parokṣamanye yajñā virājaṃ saṃpadyante pratyakṣameṣa virājaṃ saṃpannaḥ
<19.2.3>     pratyakṣametenānnādyamavarundhe'nnādo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<19.2.4>     sarvo daśadaśī bhavati daśākṣarā virāḍvairājamannamannādyasyāvarudhyai
<19.2.5>     tā u pañca pañca bhavanti pāṅkto yajñaḥ pāṅktā paśavo yajña eva paśuṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<19.2.6>     etenaiva pratiṣṭhākāmo yajeta daśabhirvā idaṃ puruṣaḥ pratiṣṭhito'syāmeva pratitiṣṭhati

<19.3.1>     athaiṣa aupaśadaḥ
<19.3.2>     gandharvāpsarasāṃ stomaḥ prajākāmo yajeta gandharvāpsaraso vai manuṣyasya prajāyā vāprajastāyā veśate teṣāmatra somapīthastān svena bhāgadheyena prīṇāti te'smai tṛptāḥ prītāḥ prajāṃ prayacchanti
<19.3.3>     ekaikā stotrīyopajāyate prajāmevāsmā upajanayati
<19.3.4>     kakubhaṃ prācīmudūhati puruṣo vai kakubgarbha eva sa madhyato dhīyate
<19.3.5>     atha yadeṣā dvipadā kakubho loke kriyate garbha eva taddhi taṃ prajanayati
<19.3.6>     cyāvanaṃ bhavati prajātirvai cyāvanam
<19.3.7>     prajāyate bahurbhavati ya evaṃ veda
<19.3.8>     vasiṣṭhasya janitre bhavato vasiṣṭho vā ete putrahataḥ sāmanī apaśyat sa prajayā paśubhiḥ prājāyata yadete sāmanī bhavataḥ prajātyai
<19.3.9>     dve saṃstutānāṃ virājamatiricyete dve striyā ūne prajananāya prajananameva tat kriyate prajātyai

<19.4.1>     athaiṣa punaḥstomaḥ
<19.4.2>     yo bahu pratigṛhya garagīriva manyeta sa etena yajeta
<19.4.3>     yaikādaśī yadeva pūrvavayase bahu pratigṛhṇāti yadgaraṃ girati yadanannamatti prātaḥsavanāya tanniharati
<19.4.4>     atha yā dvādaśī yadevottaravayase bahu pratigṛhṇati yadgaraṃ girati yadanannamatti tṛtīyasavanāya tanniharati
<19.4.5>     vairājo vai puruṣaḥ sa madhyato'śuddho madhyata evainaṃ pāpmano muñcati
<19.4.6>     śuddhāśuddhīye bhavataḥ
<19.4.7>     indro yatīn sālāvṛkeyebhyaḥ prāyacchat tamaślīlā vāgabhyavadat so'śuddho'manyata sa ete śuddhāśuddhīye apaśyat tābhyāmaśudhyat
<19.4.8>     yadeva bahu pratigṛhṇāti yadgaraṃ girati yadanannamatti yadaśuddho manyate tadetābhyāṃ śudhyati
<19.4.9>     gauṣūktaṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca bhavataḥ
<19.4.10>     gauṣūktiścāśvasūktiśca bahu pratigṛhya garagirāvamanyetāṃ tāvete sāmanī apaśyatāṃ tābhyāṃ garaṃ niraghnātām
<19.4.11>     yadeva bahu pratigṛhṇāti yadgaraṃ girati yadanannamatti tadetābhyāṃ nirhate
<19.4.12>     pañcadaśa stotrāṇi bhavantyojo vīryaṃ pañcadaśaḥ pāpmana evainaṃ muktvaujasā vīryeṇa samardhayati

<19.5.1>     athaiṣa catuṣṭomaḥ
<19.5.2>     paśukāmo yajeta
<19.5.3>     yaccatasṛbhirbahiṣpavamānaṃ bhavati catuṣpādāḥ paśavaḥ paśūnevāvarundhe
<19.5.4>     yadaṣṭābhirājyānyaṣṭāśaphāḥ paśavaḥ śaphaśastat paśūnāpnoti
<19.5.5>     yat dvādaśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamāno dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsaraṃ paśavo'nu prajāyante tānevāvarundhe
<19.5.6>     yat ṣoḍaśāni pṛṣṭhāni ṣoḍaśa kalāḥ paśavaḥ kalāśastat paśūnāpnoti
<19.5.7>     yadviṃśa ārbhavaḥ pāṅktatvameṣāṃ tadāpnoti
<19.5.8>     yaccaturviṃśo'gniṣṭomaścaturviṃśatyakṣarā gāyatrī tejo brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī teja eva brahmavarcasamavarundhe
<19.5.9>     prāṇo gāyatrī prajananaṃ prāṇādeva gāyatryāḥ prajāyate
<19.5.10>     ekaṃ sāma bahūni chandāṃsi tasmādeko bahūn poṣān puṣyati
<19.5.11>     ātmā vā agniṣṭomaḥ paśavaśchandāṃsyātmanyeva tat paśūn pratiṣṭhāpayati noktho nāgniṣṭomo na hi grāmyāḥ paśavo nāraṇyāḥ

<19.6.1>     atha yasya catvāri stotrāṇi catasṛbhiścatvāryaṣṭābhiścatvāri dvādaśabhiścatvāri ṣoḍaśabhiḥ sa gāṃ nātivadati
<19.6.2>     ṣoḍaśakalāḥ paśavaḥ kalāśastat paśūn [Kṣṣ| Bī praśūn] āpnoti
<19.6.3>     ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśimān bhavati paśavo vā ukthāni vajraḥ ṣoḍaśī vajreṇaivāsmai paśūn parigṛhṇātyanapakrāmukā asmāt paśavo bhavanti noktho nātirātro na hi grāmyāḥ paśavo nāraṇyāḥ

<19.7.1>     asurāṇāṃ vai balastamasā prāvṛto'śmāpidhānaścāsīt tasmin gavyaṃ vasvantarāsīt taṃ devā nāśaknuvan bhettuṃ te bṛhaspatimabruvannimānna utsṛjeti sa udbhidaiva balaṃ vyacyāvayadbalabhidābhinat tānutsedhenaivodasṛjanniṣedhena paryagṛhṇāt
<19.7.2>     paśukāmo yajeta
<19.7.3>     yadudbhidā yajeta balamevasmai vicyāvayati yadbalabhidā balamevāsmai bhinatti
<19.7.4>     utsedhaniṣedhau brahmasāmanī bhavata utsedhenaivāsmai paśūnutsidhya niṣedhena parigṛhṇāti
<19.7.5>   "yajña indramavardhayad"iti brahmaṇa ājyaṃ rūpeṇa samṛddham
<19.7.6>     saptisaptadaśau bhavato yat saptabhiḥ stuvanti sapta grāmyāḥ paśavaḥ paśūnevāvarūndhe saptapadā śakvarī paśavaḥ śakvarī paśūnevāvarūndhe'tha yat saptadaśabhiḥ prajāpatirvai saptadaśaḥ prajāpatimevāpnoti
<19.7.7>     gāyatrīṃ saṃpadyate tejo brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī teja eva brahmavarcasamavarundhe prāṇo gāyatrī prajananaṃ prāṇādeva gāyatryāḥ prajāyate

<19.8.1>     athaiṣo'pacitirapacitikāmo yajetāpacityaivāsmā apacitiṃ vindati
<19.8.2>     tasya caturviṃśau pavamānau caturviṃśatyakṣarā gāyatrī tejo brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī tejasaivāsmai brahmavarcasenāpacitiṃ vindati
<19.8.3>     ubhe bṛhadrathantare bhavata ubhābhyāmevāsmai bṛhadrathantarābhyāmapacitiṃ vindati
<19.8.4>     bhargayaśasī bhavato bhargeṇaivāsmai bhargo dadhāti yaśasā yaśaḥ
<19.8.5>     ubhaye stomā yugmantaścāyujaścobhayairevāsmai stomairapacitiṃ vindati
<19.8.6>     tadāhurvilomāna stomā īśvarā yajamānaṃ vikṣetorvi hyatiyantīti
<19.8.7>     ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomo bhavati pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśo'ntata eva yajñasya pratitiṣṭhati

<19.9.1>     athaiṣa sarvastomo'pacitirapacitikāmo yajeta sarvairevāsmai stomairapacitiṃ vindati
<19.9.2>     virājaṃ saṃpadyata eṣa vā apacito yo'nnādo'nnaṃ virāḍannādyamevāsmin dadhāti
<19.9.3>     ubhe bṛhadrathantare bhavato bhargayaśasī bhavata ubhaye stomāśchandomāśca pṛṣṭhyāścobhayairevāsmai stomairapacitiṃ vindati
<19.9.4>     tasya chandomāḥ pṛṣṭhāni paśavo vai chandomā annaṃ pṛṣṭhānyabhipūrvamevāsminnannādyaṃ paśūn dadhāti yacchandomavāṃstena dvādaśāhayājinamāpnoti
<19.9.5>     tadāhurnānālokāḥ stomāśchandomāśca pṛṣṭhyāśca yadekasmin yajñakratau samavarudhyanta īśvaro [corr. Calanḓ Bī | Kṣṣīśvarā] yajamāno'pratiṣṭhātoriti
<19.9.6>     ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomo bhavati pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśaḥ pratiṣṭhāmeva tadabhyāyanti

<19.10.1>     pakṣī vā eṣa stomaḥ
<19.10.2>     pakṣyeṣa nidhīyate
<19.10.3>     na vā apakṣaḥ pakṣiṇamāpnotyatha yadeṣa pakṣyapakṣiṇi nidhīyate tasmāt pakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaiḥ patanti
<19.10.4>     pakṣī jyotiṣmān puṇyān lokān saṃcarati ya evaṃ veda
<19.10.5>     trivṛtāvabhito bhavatastejo brahmavarcasaṃ trivṛt teja eva brahmavarcasamavarundhe
<19.10.6>     atha pañcadaśau vīryaṃ vai pañcadaśo vīryamevāvarundhe
<19.10.7>     atha saptadaśau paśavo vai saptadaśaḥ paśūnevāvarundhe
<19.10.8>     athaikaviṃśau pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśo madhyata eva yajñasya pratitiṣṭhati
<19.10.9>     atha triṇavāvime vai lokāstriṇava eṣveva lokeṣu pratitiṣṭhati
<19.10.10>     atha trayastriṃśo varṣma vai trayastriṃśaḥ
<19.10.11>     varṣma svānāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<19.10.12>     eṣa vāva bradhnasya viṣṭapo yadetau trayastriṃśau madhyataḥ saṃdhīyete tena bradhnasya viṣṭapamārohati
<19.10.13>     madhyato vā agnirvariṣṭhastasmādete stomā madhyato variṣṭhāḥ kriyante
<19.10.14>     trivṛtā praiti trivṛtodeti prāṇo vai trivṛt prāṇenaiva praiti prāṇamabhyudeti

<19.11.1>     athaiṣa jyotiḥ
<19.11.2>     tasya trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśānyājyāni caturviṃśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamānaḥ saptadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni
<19.11.3>     prāṇo vai trivṛdātmā pañcadaśaḥ
<19.11.4>     mukhaṃ gāyatryannaṃ vai saptadaśo mukhata eva tadannaṃ dhatte
<19.11.5>     annamattyannādo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<19.11.6>     mādhyandinena vai pavamānena devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāyan yadeṣa caturviṃśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamāno bhavati svargasya lokasyākrāntyai caturviṃśatyakṣarā gāyatrī tejo brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī teja eva brahmavarcasamavarundhe prāṇo gāyatrī prajananaṃ prāṇādeva gāyatryāḥ prajāyate
<19.11.7>     ubhaye stomā yugmantaścāyujaśca tanmithunaṃ tasmānmithunāt prajāyate
<19.11.8>     pakṣi vā etacchandaḥ pakṣī jyotiṣmān puṇyān lokān saṃcarati ya evaṃ veda
<19.11.9>     madhyato vā agnirvariṣṭhastasmādete stomā madhyato variṣṭhāḥ kriyante
<19.11.10>     ekā saṃstutānāṃ virājamatiricyata ekākinamevainamannādyasyādhyakṣaṃ karoti
<19.11.11>     jyotirvā eṣo'gniṣṭomo jyotiṣmantaṃ puṇyaṃ lokaṃ jayati ya evaṃ vidvānetena yajate

<19.12.1>     athaiṣa ṛṣabhaḥ
<19.12.2>     ṛṣabho vā eṣa stomānāmṛṣabhatāṃ gacchati ya evaṃ veda
<19.12.3>     rājanyaṃ yājayedṛṣabho vai paśūnāmadhipatī rājanyo manuṣyāṇām
<19.12.4>     yo vā asti so'dhipatiḥ
<19.12.5>     adhipatiḥ samānānāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<19.12.6>     tasya sadoviśīyaṃ mādhyandine pavamāne bhavati viśamevāsmai savanābhyāṃ parigṛhṇātyanapakrāmukāsmādviḍbhavati
<19.12.7>     samantaṃ bhavati samantāmevāsmai viśaṃ karotyanapakrāmukāsmādviḍbhavati
<19.12.8>     ubhe bṛhadrathantare bhavata iyaṃ vai rathantaraṃ dyaurbṛhadevāsmāllokādgāyatryevāmuṣmādubhayoranayorlokayoḥ pratitiṣṭhati
<19.12.9>     anuṣṭubhi bṛhadbhavatyanto vā anuṣṭupchandasāmanto bṛhat sāmnāmanto rājanyo manuṣyāṇāmanta eva tadantaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati tasmādyo rājanyānāṃ hīyate na sa punaragraṃ paryeti

<19.13.1>     yo vai vājapeyaḥ sa rājasūyo yo rājasūyaḥ sa varūṇasavo'thaiṣa gosavaḥ svārājyo vā eṣa yajñaḥ
<19.13.2>     svārājyaṃ gacchati ya evaṃ veda
<19.13.3>     prajāpatirhi svārājyaṃ parameṣṭhī svārājyam
<19.13.4>     parameṣṭhitāṃ gacchati ya evaṃ veda
<19.13.5>     ubhe bṛhadrathantare bhavatastaddhi svārājyaṃ svārājyaṃ gacchati ya evaṃ veda
<19.13.6>     ayutaṃ dakṣiṇāstaddhi svārājyaṃ svārājyaṃ gacchati ya evaṃ veda
<19.13.7>     pratiduhābhiṣicyate taddhi svārājyaṃ svārājyaṃ gacchati ya evaṃ veda
<19.13.8>     bṛhataḥ stotra pratyabhiṣicyate taddhi svārājyaṃ svārājyaṃ gacchati ya evaṃ veda
<19.13.9>     anuddhate dakṣiṇata āhavanīyasyābhiṣicyate'syāmevānantarhite'dhyabhiṣicyate
<19.13.10>     sarvaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśastena gosavaḥ

<19.14.1>     athaiṣa marutstoma etena vai maruto'parimitāṃ puṣṭimapuṣyannaparimitāṃ puṣṭiṃ puṣyati ya evaṃ veda
<19.14.2>     yadgaṇaśaḥ stomāstena marutstomo gaṇaśo hi marutaḥ
<19.14.3>     etenaiva trīn yājayet
<19.14.4>     yat trīṇi trivṛnti stotrāṇi bhavanti nānā brahmavarcase pratitiṣṭhanti
<19.14.5>     yat trīṇi pañcadaśāni nānā vīrye
<19.14.6>     yat trīṇi saptadaśāni nānā paśuṣu
<19.14.7>     yat trīṇyekaviṃśāni nānā pratitiṣṭhanti
<19.14.8>     pratitiṣṭhati ya evaṃ veda

<19.15.1>     athaiṣa indrāgnyoḥ kulāyaḥ prajākāmo vā paśukāmo vā yajeta prajā vai kulāyaṃ paśavaḥ kulāyaṃ gṛhāḥ kulāyaṃ kulāyameva bhavati
<19.15.2>     etenaiva dvau yājayet
<19.15.3>     yat ṣaṭtrivṛnti stotrāṇi bhavanti nānā brahmavarcase pratitiṣṭhato yat dve pañcadaśe nānā vīrye yat dve saptadaśe nānā paśuṣu yat dve ekaviṃśe nānā pratitiṣṭhataḥ pratitiṣṭhati ya evaṃ veda

<19.16.1>     athaiṣa pañcadaśa indrastoma ukthyaḥ
<19.16.2>     etena vā indro'tyanyā devatā abhavadatyanyāḥ prajā bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<19.16.3>     rājanyaṃ yājayet
<19.16.4>     sarvaḥ pañcadaśo bhavatyojo vīryaṃ pañcadaśa ojasaivainaṃ vīryeṇa samardhayati
<19.16.5>     aindrīṣu bhavantīndriyeṇaivainaṃ vīryeṇa samardhayati
<19.16.6>     ukthyo bhavati paśavo vā ukthāni viḍukthāni viśamevāsmai paśūnanuniyunaktyanapakrāmukāsmādviḍbhavati
<19.16.7>     pañcadaśa stotrāṇi pañcadaśāni bhavantyojo vīryaṃ pañcadaśo'bhipūrvamevāsminnojo vīryaṃ dadhāti

<19.17.1>     athaiṣa indrāgnyoḥ stoma etena vā indrāgnī atyanyā devatā abhavatāmatyanyāḥ prajā bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<19.17.2>     trivṛt pañcadaśo bhavati
<19.17.3>     brahma vai trivṛt kṣatraṃ pañcadaśo brahmaṇa iva cāsya kṣatrasyeva ca prakāśo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<19.17.4>     rājā ca purohitaśca yajeyātām
<19.17.5>     gāyatrīṃ ca jagatīṃ ca saṃpadyate
<19.17.6>     tejo brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatryā brāhmaṇo'varundhe viśaṃ rājā jagatyā praviśati
<19.17.7>     purodhākāmo yajeta
<19.17.8>     bṛhaspatirakāmayata devānāṃ purodhāṃ gaccheyamiti sa etenāyajata sa devānāṃ purodhāmagacchadgacchati purodhāṃ ya evaṃ veda

<19.18.1>     athaiṣa vighanaḥ
<19.18.2>     indro'kāmayata pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ vihanyāmiti sa etaṃ vighanamapaśyat tena pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ vyahan vi pāpmānaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ hate ya evaṃ veda
<19.18.3>     yat trivṛdbhavati prāṇāṃstenāvarundhe yat dvādaśaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ tena yat pañcadaśo vīryaṃ tena yat saptadaśo'nnādyaṃ tena yadekaviṃśaḥ pratiṣṭhā tena yannavadaśaḥ prajananaṃ tena yaccaturviṃśo brahmavarcasaṃ tena  yat triṇavo vajraṃ bhrātṛvyāya praharati
<19.18.4>     paśukāmo yajeta bṛhatīṃ saṃpadyate paśavo vai bṛhatī paśūnevāvarundhe
<19.18.5>     ṣaḍetā bṛhatyo bhavanti ṣaḍṛtavaḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsaraṃ paśavo'nu prajāyante tānevāptvāvarundhe

<19.19.1>     indramadevyo māyā ayacanta sa prajāpatimupādhāvavat tasmā etaṃ vighanaṃ prāyacchat tena sarvā mṛdho vyahata yadvyahata tadvighanasya vighanatvam
<19.19.2>     sarvā mṛdho vihate ya evaṃ vidvān vighanena yajate yamevaṃ vidvān vighanena yājayati
<19.19.3>     paśukāmo yajeta paśavo vai bṛhatī paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhati

<20.1.1>     trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśānyājyāni pañcadaśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamānaḥ saptadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni saptadaśa ārbhava ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ sokthyaḥ pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiḥ
<20.1.2>     jyotiṣṭomenātirātreṇarddhikāmo yajetābhikramo vā eṣa stomānāmabhikrāntyā abhikrāntena hi yajñasyardhnoti
<20.1.3>     eṣo'gniṣṭoma eṣa ukthya eṣo'tirātro'gniṣṭomena vai devā imaṃ lokamabhyajayannukthyairantarikṣaṃ rātryāmuṃ lokamajayannahorātrābhyāmabhyavartanta
<20.1.4>     parācyo vā anyā vyucchanti pratīcyo'nyā eṣā vāva pratīcī vyucchati yāśvinena vyucchati pratīcīrevāsmā uṣaso vivāsayati
<20.1.5>     dve saṃstutānāṃ virājamatiricyete eṣā vai stanavatī virāṭyaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate tametāṃ dugdhe
<20.1.6>     trivṛtā praiti trivṛtodeti prāṇo vai trivṛt prāṇenaiva praiti prāṇamabhyudeti

<20.2.1>     trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśānyājyāni saptadaśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamāna ekaviṃśāni pṛṣṭhāni triṇava ārbhavastrayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ pratyavarohīṇyukthāni triṇavaṃ prathamaṃ dve ekaviṃśe saṣoḍaśike pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiḥ
<20.2.2>     sarvastomenātirātreṇa bubhūṣan yajeta sarvasyāptyai sarvasya jityai sarvamevaitenāpnoti sarvaṃ jayati
<20.2.3>     yat trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ bhavati tat trivṛtaṃ stomamāpnoti gāyatrīṃ chando yat pañcadaśānyājyāni tat pañcadaśaṃ stomamāpnoti triṣṭubhaṃ chando yat saptadaśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamānastat saptadaśaṃ stomamāpnoti jagatīṃ chando yadekaviṃśāni pṛṣṭāni tadekaviṃśaṃ stomamāpnotyanuṣṭubhaṃ chando yat triṇava ārbhavastat triṇavaṃ stomamāpnoti paṅktiṃ chando yat trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomastat trayastriṃśaṃ stomamāpnoti virājaṃ chando yaduṣṇikkakubhau kriyete taduṣṇikkakubhāvāpnoti yadāśvinaṃ śasyate tat sarvamevaitenāpnoti sarvaṃ jayati
<20.2.4>     prāñcaṃ vai trayastriṃśo yajñaṃ prabhujati tamadhvaryurekādaśinyā purastāt pratyudyacchatyekādaśa raśanā ekādaśa paśava ekādaśa yūpā bhavanti tat trayastriṃśe trayastriṃśaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<20.2.5>     tayā samudyatayā rātryā yaṃ yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate taṃ tamabhyaśnute yaṃ yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate taṃ tamabhyaśnute ya evaṃ veda

<20.3.1>     trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśānyājyāni saptadaśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamāna ekaviṃśaṃ hotuḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ chandomā itarāṇi triṇava ārbhavastrayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ pratyavarohīṇyukthāni triṇavaṃ prathamamathaikaviṃśamatha saptadaśamekaviṃśaḥ ṣoḍaśī pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhistrivṛt prathamamatiriktastotramatha pañcadaśamatha saptadaśamathaikaviṃśam
<20.3.2>     prajāpatiḥ paśūnasṛjata te'smāt sṛṣṭā apākrāmaṃstānagniṣṭomena nāpnot tānukthairnāpnot tān ṣoḍaśinā nāpnot tān rātryā nāpnot tān sandhinā nāpnot tānāśvinena nāpnot tānagnimabravīdimānma īpseti tānagnistrivṛtā stomena jarābodhīyena sāmnā nāpnot tānindramabravīdimānma īpseti tānindraḥ pañcadaśena stomena sattrāsāhīyena sāmnā nāpnot tān viśvān devānabravīdimānma ipsateti tān viśve devāḥ saptadaśena stomena mārgīṃyaveṇa sāmnā nāpnuvaṃstān viṣṇumabravīdimānma īpseti tān viṣṇurekaviṃśena stomenāpnodvāravantīyenāvārayatedaṃ viṣṇurvicakrama iti vyakramata
<20.3.3>     yasmāt pra preva paśavo bhraṃśeran sa etena yajeta
<20.3.4>     etena vai devā jaitvāni jitvā yaṃ yaṃ kāmamakāmayanta taṃ tamāpnuvan yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate tametenāpnoti
<20.3.5>     tadaptoryāmno'ptoryāmatvam

<20.4.1>     trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśaṃ hoturājyaṃ navasaptadaśāni stotrāṇyekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ sokthaḥ pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiḥ
<20.4.2>     navasaptadaśenātirātreṇa prajākāmo yajeta nava vai prāṇāḥ prajāpatiḥ saptadaśaḥ prāṇebhya eva tadadhi prajāpateḥ prajāḥ prajāyante
<20.4.3>     kakubhaṃ prācīmudūhati puruṣo vai kakupgarbho vā eṣa madhyato dhīyate
<20.4.4>     tasyāṃ sākamaśvam
<20.4.5>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata tā na prājāyanta sa etat sāmāpaśyat tā aśvo bhūtvābhyajighrat tāḥ prājāyanta prajananaṃ vā etat sāma
<20.4.6>     prajāyate bahurbhavati ya evaṃ veda
<20.4.7>     dvipadāṃ kakubho loke karoti garbhameva taddhitaṃ madhyataḥ prajanayati
<20.4.8>     atirātro bhavatyahorātre vā anu prajāḥ prajāyante'horātre evānu prajayā paśubhiḥ prajāyate

<20.5.1>     trivṛt bahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśānyājyāni pañcadaśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamānaḥ saptadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni saptadaśa ārbhava ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ saptadaśānyukthāni pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiḥ
<20.5.2>     viṣuvatātirātreṇa jyeṣṭhaṃ jyaiṣṭhineyaṃ yājayedviṣuvān vā eṣa stomānāṃ viṣuvāneva bhavati yadeka ekaviṃśo bhavatyekaviṃśo vā ito'sāvāditya dvādaśa māsāḥ pañcartavastraya ime lokā
<20.5.3>     asāvāditya ekaviṃśa ādityasyaivainaṃ mātrāṃ gamayati
<20.5.4>     eṣa vā udeti na vā enamanyat jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ pratyudeti
<20.5.5>     nainamanyaḥ sveṣu pratyudeti ya evaṃ veda

<20.6.1>     pañcadaśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ trivṛntyājyāni saptadaśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanamekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ sokthaṃ pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhirgoṣṭomenātirātreṇa bhrātṛvyavān yajeta gavā vai devā asurānebhyo lokebhyo'nudanta rātryānapajayyamajayannebhyo lokebhyo bhrātṛvyaṃ praṇudya rātryānapajayyaṃ jayati

<20.7.1>     trivṛt bahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśānyājyāni saptadaśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanamekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ sokthaṃ pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhirāyuṣṭomenātirātreṇa svargakāmo yajetordhvāḥ stomā yantyanapabhraṃśāya yadatirātro bhavatyahorātrābhyāmeva svargaṃ lokameti

<20.8.1>     trivṛt bahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśaṃ hoturājyaṃ saptadaśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya pañcadaśaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ saptadaśamacchāvākasyaikaviṃśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamānaḥ saptadaśaṃ hotuḥ pṛṣṭhamekaviṃśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya triṇavaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsina ekaviṃśamacchāvakasya triṇava ārbhavastrayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ pratyavarohīṇyukthāni triṇavaṃ prathamaṃ dve ekaviṃśe saṣoḍaśike pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhirabhijitātirātreṇa bhrātṛvyavān yajetābhijitā vai devā asurānimān lokānabhyajayat rātryānapajayyamajayannabhijitaiva bhrātṛvyamimān lokānabhijitya rātryānapajayyaṃ jayati

<20.9.1>     trivṛt bahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśaṃ hoturājyaṃ saptadaśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasyaikaviṃśaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ pañcadaśamacchāvākasya saptadaśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamāna ekaviṃśaṃ hotuḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ triṇavaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya
<20.9.1>     saptadaśaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsina ekaviṃśamacchāvākasya triṇava ārbhavastrayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ pratyavarohīṇyukthāni triṇavaṃ prathamaṃ dve ekaviṃśe saṣoḍaśike pañcadaśī rātriḥ trivṛt sandhirviśvajitātirātreṇa paśukāmo yajeta
<20.9.2>     reto hi nābhānediṣṭhīyaṃ paśavo vālakhilyā yannābhānediṣṭhīyaṃ pūrvaṃ śasyata uttarā vālakhilyā retasastat paśavaḥ prajāyante rūpāṇi vikaroti yadvārṣākapamṛtuṣu pratitiṣṭhati yadevayāmarut

<20.10.1>     trivṛt ātirātreṇa brahmavarcasakāmo yajeta tejo vai trivṛt brahmavarcasaṃ teja eva brahmavarcasamavarundhe tejasi brahmavarcase pratitiṣṭhati pañcadaśenātirātreṇa vīryakāmo yajetaujo vīryaṃ pañcadaśa oja eva vīryamavarundha ojasi vīrye pratitiṣṭhati saptadaśenātirācreṇānnādyakāmo yajetānnaṃ vai saptadaśo'nnādyamevāvarundha ekaviṃśenātirātreṇa pratiṣṭhākāmo yajeta pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśo yadatirātro bhavatyahorātrayoreva pratitiṣṭhati

<20.11.1>     jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomaḥ pūrvamahaḥ sarvastomo'tirātra uttaram
<20.11.2>     tasya caturviṃśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ pañadaśānyājyāni saptadaśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamāna ekaviṃśāni pṛṣṭhāni triṇava ārbhavastrayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ pratyavarohīṇyukthāni triṇavaṃ prathamamathaikaviṃśamatha saptadaśamekaviṃśaḥ ṣoḍaśī pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiḥ
<20.11.3>     aṅgirasaḥ svargaṃ lokamāyaṃsteṣāṃ haviṣmāṃśca haviṣkṛccāṅgirasāvahīyetā tāvāgacchetāṃ yato'ṅgirasaḥ svargaṃ lokamāyaṃstāvatapyetāṃ tāvete sāmanī apaśyatāṃ tābhyāṃ dvirātramatanvātāṃ tena svargaṃ lokamaitām
<20.11.4>     yaḥ pauṇyo hīna iva syāt sa etena yajetāpnoti pūrveṣāṃ prahāmāpnutāṃ hi tāvaṅgirasaḥ
<20.11.5>     prajākāmo yajeta dvitīyaṃ hyetadyat prajāḥ
<20.11.6>     svargakāmo yajeta dvitīyāddhi lokāt paro loko'bhiprakramyo durādho dvirātra ityāhuryadagniṣṭomaḥ pūrvamaharbhavatyukthyamantaryanti yadyukthyo'gniṣṭomam
<20.11.7>     yajjyotirukthyaḥ pūrvamaharbhavati nāgniṣṭomamantaryanti nokthāni
<20.11.8>     tadāhureṣā vāva yajñasya mātrā yadagniṣṭomo yadagniṣṭomaḥ pūrvamaharbhavati yajñasya mātrāṃ nātikrāmatyathottarasyāhna ukthebhyo'dhi rātrimupayanti tenokthānyanantaritāni
<20.11.9>     caturviṃśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ bhavatyuttarasyāhnaścaturviṃśatyakṣarā gāyatrī prajananaṃ gāyatrī prajātyai
<20.11.10>     ubhaye stomā yugmantaścāyujaśca tanmithunaṃ tasmānmithunāt prajāyate
<20.11.11>     sarvastomo'tirātro bhavati sarvasyāptyai sarvasya jityai sarvamevaitenāpnoti sarvaṃ jayati

<20.12.1>     atha yasya jyotirukthyaḥ pūrvamaharbhavatyāyuratirātra uttaram
<20.12.2>     tisraḥ pūrvasyāhno virājamatiricyante dvābhyāmuttaramaharvirāja ūnamūnātiriktaṃ vā anuprajāḥ prajāyante
<20.12.3>     pra prajayā pra paśubhirjāyate ya evaṃ veda
<20.12.4>     ekā saṃstutānāṃ virājamatiricyate ekākinamevainamannādyasyādhyakṣaṃ karoti
<20.12.5>     etena vai citrarathaṃ kāpeyā ayājayaṃstamekākinamannādyasyādhyakṣamakurvaṃstasmāccaitrarathīnāmekaḥ kṣatrapatirjāyate'nulamba iva dvitīyaḥ

<20.13.1>     atha yasya trivṛt pañcadaśo'gniṣṭomaḥ pūrvamaharbhavatyāyuratirātra uttaram
<20.13.2>     mithunābhyāṃ stomābhyāmuttaramahaḥ prajanayanti tat prajātaṃ śva ārabhante cakre vā ete sākaṃvṛtī yat trivṛt pañcadaśau stomau yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate tametenābhyaśnute yatra yatra hi cakrīvatā kāmayate tat tadabhyaśnute
<20.13.3>     atha yadāyuratirātro bhavati pratiṣṭhityai
<20.13.4>     etena vai kapivano bhauvāyana iṣṭvārūkṣatāmagacchat
<20.13.5>     arūkṣo bhavati ya evaṃ vidvānetena yajate

<20.14.1>     trivṛt prātaḥsavanaṃ pañcadaśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ saptadaśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ pañcadaśaṃ prātaḥsavanaṃ saptadaśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanamekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ sokthamekaviṃśaṃ prātaḥsavanaṃ triṇavaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ trayastriṃśa ārbhavaścatustriṃśo'gniṣṭoma ekaviṃśānyukthāni saṣoḍaśikāni ṣoḍaśaṃ prathamaṃ rātriṣāma pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiḥ
<20.14.2>     prajāpatirvā idameka āsīt tasya vāgeva svamāsīdvāgdvitīyā sa aikṣatemāmeva vācaṃ visṛjā iyaṃ vā idaṃ sarvaṃ vibhavantyeṣyatīti sa vācaṃ vyasṛjata sedaṃ sarvaṃ vibhavantyait sordhvodātanodyathāpāṃ dhārā santataivaṃ tasyā eti tṛtīyamacchinat tadbhūmirabhavadabhūdiva vā idamiti tadbhūmerbhūmitvaṃ keti tṛtīyamacchinat tadantarikṣamabhavadantareva vā idamiti tadantarikṣasyāntarikṣatvaṃ ho iti tṛtīyamūrdhvamudāsyat tat dyaurabhavadadyutadiva vā iti taddivo divatvam
<20.14.3>     eṣā vāva pratyakṣaṃ vāgyajjihvāgreṇaitadvāco vadati yadeti madhyenaitadvāco vadati yat keti sarvayaitadvāco raso'dhyūrdhva udvadati yaddho iti
<20.14.4>     yadetāni rūpāṇyanvahaṃ vyajyante mukhata eva tadvācaṃ visṛjante mukhato yajñiyaṃ karma
<20.14.5>     prajāpatirvā idamekākṣarāṃ vācaṃ satīṃ tredhā vyakarot ta ime lokā abhavan rūkṣā anupajīvanāḥ sa aikṣata kathamime lokā loma gṛhṇīyuḥ kathamupajīvanīyāḥ syuriti sa etaṃ trirātramapaśyat tamāharat tenemān lokānanvātanot tato vā ime lokā lomāgṛhṇaṃstata upajīvanīyā abhavaṃstrirātrasya vā idaṃ puṣṭaṃ trirātrasyodaraṇaṃ yadidameṣu lokeṣvadhi
<20.14.6>     gacchati paśūnāṃ bhūmānaṃ dvipadāṃ catuṣpadāṃ ya evaṃ veda
<20.14.7>     prajāpatiryadvācaṃ vyasṛjata sākṣaradeveti prathamaṃ kṣeti dvitīyaṃ reti tṛtīyaṃ yena yena vai rūpeṇa prajāpatirvācaṃ vyasṛjata tena tena rūpeṇājyāni cārabhyante'hāni cāpyante
<20.13.8>     tadāhurbrahmavādino'kṣarestho vai trirātra ityekākṣarā vai vāktryakṣaramakṣaraṃ tryakṣaraḥ puruṣaḥ sa vā enaṃ vedetyāhurya enaṃ puruṣasammitaṃ vedeti

<20.15.1>     etena vai devā eṣu lokeṣvārdhnuvannetena svargaṃ lokamāyan
<20.15.2>     vāgvai trirātro vāco rūpeṇājyāni cāhāni ca vibhajyanta ekākṣarā vai vāktryakṣaramakṣaramakṣarasya rūpeṇa vibhajyante trayo gandharvāsteṣāmeṣā bhaktiragneḥ pṛthivī vāyorantarikṣamasāvādityasya dyaustrayo gharmāsa uṣasaṃ sacante
<20.15.3>     agniruṣasaṃ sacate vāyuruṣasaṃ sacate'sāvāditya uṣasaṃ sacate
<20.15.4>     trīṇi mithunāni tānyeṣaḥ
<20.15.5>     mithunaṃ dve sambhavato mithunādyat prajāyate tat tṛtīyam
<20.15.6>     indro vṛtrāya vajramudayacchat so'bravīnmā me praharṣīrasti vā idaṃ mayi vīryaṃ tat te pradāsyāmīti tadasmai prāyacchat tadviṣṇuḥ pratyagṛhṇāt sa dvitīyaṃ sa tṛtīyamudayacchat sa evābravīnmā me prahārṣīrasti vā idaṃ mayi vīryaṃ tat te pradāsyāmīti tadasmai prāyacchat tadviṣṇuḥ pratyagṛhṇādetadvāca tadabhyanūcyate
<20.15.7>     ubhau jigyathurna parājayethe na parājigye kataraśca nainoḥ indraśca viṣṇo yadapaspṛdhethāṃ tredhā sahasraṃ vitad(?) airayethāmiti
<20.15.8>     etadvā ābhyāṃ tat sahasraṃ prāyacchat
<20.15.9>     tasyaiṣā bhaktirya ārṣeyo vidvāṃstasmai prathame'hani deyaṃ yathā vā iyaṃ evaṃ sa pratiṣṭhiteyaṃ pratiṣṭhitaḥ saḥ
<20.15.10>     yo'nārṣeyo vidvāṃstasmai dvitīye'hani deyaṃ yathā vā antarikṣamevaṃ so'ntarikṣamityantarikṣaṃ vidurvedaṃ tasya viduḥ
<20.15.11>     ya ārṣeyo vidvāṃstasmai tṛtīye'hani deyaṃ yathā vai dyaurevaṃ sa dyauriti divaṃ vidurbandhu tasya viduḥ
<20.15.12>     śatānyanvahaṃ dīyante eṣā vāva yajñasya mātrā yacchataṃ saiva sāvicchinnā dīyate daśato'nvahaṃ dīyante daśākṣarā virāḍvairājo yajñaḥ saiva sā vicchinnā dīyate
<20.15.13>     trayastriṃśacca trīṇi ca śatāni prathame'hani deyāstathā dvitīye tathā tṛtīye
<20.15.14>     athaiṣā dvidevatyā trirūpā brahmaṇo dve tṛtīye tṛtīyamagnīdhaḥ
<20.15.15>     kāmyāsi priyāsi havyāsīḍe rante sarasvati mahi viśruta etāni te'ghnye nāmāni deveṣu naḥ sukṛto brūtāt

<20.16.1>     idaṃ vāva prathamenāhnā vyakarodyadidamasyāmadhyāyat ta mūlamidaṃ dvitīyena yadidaṃ prāṇādejatyadastṛtīyena yadvarṣati yannakṣatrāṇi yadamuṃ lokaṃ bheje
<20.16.2>     tadāhurbrahmavādino mahāvrataṃ vā etadyadeṣa trirātra iti tasyai tadeva mukhaṃ yadeteṣāmahvāṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ ye abhito'hanī tau pakṣau yanmadhyamamahaḥ sa ātmāgniṣṭomasāmāni puccham
<20.16.3>     yadevāsāvudeti tanmukhaṃ ye abhito'hanī tau pakṣau yanmadhyamamahaḥ sa ātmāgniḥ puccham
<20.16.4>     yadevāsāvudeti tanmukhaṃ ye abhito'hanī tau pakṣau yanmadhyamamahaḥ sa ātmā yadastameti tat puccham
<20.16.5>     etāvān vāva trirātro gāyatraḥ prāṇastraiṣṭubhaṃ cakṣurjāgataṃ śrotraṃ sarvamāyureti ya evaṃ veda
<20.16.6>     tadāhurbrahmavādinaḥ kiyāṃstrirātra itīyāniti brūyādiyaddhayetadabhyatho iyāniti brūyādiyaddhayevaitadabhiparā3ṅarvā3ṅityāhuḥ parāṅiti brūyāt parāṅhi vadati parāṅpaśyati parāṅprāṇityekā3 dvā3 u trayā3 ityāhureka iti brūyāt samāno hyeṣa yat prāṇo'pāno vyānastadyathā vā ado maṇau sūtramotamevameṣu lokeṣu trirātra otaḥ śobhate'sya mukhaṃ ya evaṃ veda
<20.16.7>     yadvai trirātrasya saloma tadasya viloma yadasya viloma tadasya saloma tadyadetat paraṃ sadaharavaraṃ kriyate yajamānāyaiva tat paśūn parigṛhṇāti prajananāya na hyamuṣmin loke paśavaḥ prajāyante
<20.16.8>     ete vāva chandasāṃ vīryavattame yadgāyatrī ca triṣṭupca yadete abhito bhavato madhye jagatī vīryavatībhyāmeva tacchandobhyāṃ paśūn parigṛhṇāti prajananāya na hyamuṣmin loke paśavaḥ prajāyante
<20.16.9>     asau vāva trirātro yathodetyevaṃ prathamamaharyathā madhyandina evaṃ dvitīyaṃ yathāstametyevaṃ tṛtīyaṃ gacchatyamuṣyasāyujyaṃ gacchati sāveśyaṃ ya evaṃ veda

<21.1.1>     indro marutaḥ sahasramajināt svāṃ viśaṃ somāya rājñe procya tasmādrājñe procya viṃśa jinanti tau yamo'śṛṇonmaruto ha sahasramajyāśiṣṭāmiti sa āgacchat so'bravīdupa māsmin sahasre hvayethāmiti tamupāhvayetāṃ sa yamo'paśyadekāṅgāṃ sahasre'pi sahasrasya payo bibhratīṃ so'bravīdiyameva mamāstu sahasraṃ yuvāṃ vikalpayethāmiti tāvabrūtāṃ yathā vāva tvametāṃ paśyasyevamāvametāṃ paśyāva iti
<21.1.2>     tayā vā idaṃ sahasraṃ vikalpayāmahā ityabruvaṃstāmudake prāveśayaṃste'bravanna śānāharāmahai yasmai na iyaṃ prathamāyādeṣyati iti te'ṃśānāharanta somasya prathama aidathendrasyātha yamasya
<21.1.3>     te'bruvan somāya rājña udehi tṛtīyena cātmanastṛtīyena ca sahasrasya payasa iti sā babhruḥ piṅgākṣyekavarṣodait tṛtīyena cātmanastṛtīyena ca sahasrasya payasaḥ sā yā somakrayaṇī saiva sā
<21.1.4>     tṛtīyena cāsya tasyā ātmanastṛtīyena ca sahasrasya payasaḥ somaḥ krīto bhavati ya evaṃ vidvān somaṃ krīṇāti yasmā evaṃ viduṣe somaṃ krīṇanti
<21.1.5>     te'bruvannindrāyodehi tṛtīyena cātmanastṛtīyena ca sahasrasya payasa iti sā śabalī paṣṭhauhyudait tṛtīyena cātmanastṛtīyena ca sahasrasya payasaḥ sā yendriyaiṣyā saiva sā
<21.1.6>     tṛtīyena cāsya tasyā ātmanastṛtīyena ca sahasrasya payasa indriyaiṣyā dattā bhavati ya evaṃ vidvānindriyaiṣyāṃ dadāti yasmā evaṃ viduṣa indriyaiṣyāṃ dadāti
<21.1.7>     te'bruvan yamāyodehi tṛtīyena cātmanastṛtīyena ca sahasrasya payasa iti sā jaratī kuṣṭhāśṛṅgyadaiddhūmrā vā dityauhīrmato hrasīyasī tṛtīyena cātmanaḥ tṛtīyena ca sahasrasya payasaḥ sā yānustaraṇī saiva sā
<21.1.8>     tṛtīyena cāsya tasyā ātmanastṛtīyena ca sahasrasya payaso'nustaraṇī kṛtā bhavati ya evaṃ vidvānanustaraṇīṃ karoti yasmā evaṃ viduṣe'nustaraṇīṃ kurvanti nācakṛvānmanyate
<21.1.9>     tadāhurbrahmavādino na vā amuṣmin loke sahasrayāḍaloko'stīti tadyāvaditaḥ sahasrasya gaurgavi pratiṣṭhitā tāvadasmāllokādasau lokaḥ sarasrayājī vā imān lokān vyāpnotyatho yāvat sahasraṃ yojanānyatho sahasramāśvīnān yatho yāvat sahasramahnayāni tadgavā gavā spṛṇoti samākramaṇāya vā etā dīyante
<21.1.10>     sahasraṃ yadasṛjata tasya tārpyaṃ yonirāsīdyat tārpyaṃ pratyasya nayati sayonitvāya

<21.2.1>     prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata tā asmāt sṛṣṭāḥ parācya āyannatsyati na iti bibhyatyaḥ so'bravīdupa mā vartadhvaṃ tathā vai vo'tsyāmi yathādyamānā bhūyasyaḥ prajaniṣyatha iti tābhyo vainaṃ ṛtaṃ brūhītyabruvaṃstābhya ṛtanidhanenartamabravīdīnidhanenāvayat triṇidhanena prājanayadetairha vā idaṃ sāmabhirmṛtyuḥ prajā atti ca prajanayati
<21.2.2>     adyamānasya bhūyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<21.2.3>     jyeṣṭhasāmāni vā etāni śreṣṭhasāmāni prajāpatisāmāni
<21.2.4>     gacchati jyaiṣṭhayaṃ śraiṣṭhayaṃ ya evaṃ veda
<21.2.5>     etairvai sāmabhiḥ prajāpatirimān lokān sarvān kāmān dugdhe yadācyā dugdhe tadācyā dohānāmācyā dohatvam
<21.2.6>     sarvānimān lokān kāmān dugdhe ya evaṃ vidvānetaissāmabhiḥ stute
<21.2.7>     ime vai lokā etāni sāmānyayamevartanidhanamantarikṣamīnidhanaṃ dyaustriṇidhanam
<21.2.8>     yathā kṣetrajñaḥ kṣetrāṇyanusañcaratyevamimān lokānanusañcarati ya evaṃ veda
<21.2.9>     agnervā etāni vaiśvānarasya sāmāni yatra vā etairaśāntaiḥ stuvanti tat prajā devo ghātuko bhavatyagnimupanidhāya stuvate svāyā eva taddevatāyāḥ sāmyekṣāya namaskṛtyodgāyati śāntaisstuvanti

<21.3.1>     vāgvai śabalī tasyāstrirātro vatsastrirātro vā etāṃ pradāpayati
<21.3.2>     tadya evaṃ veda tasmā eṣā prattā dugdhe
<21.3.3>     yo'lamannādyāya sannathānnaṃ nādyāt
<21.3.4>     barāsīṃ paridhāya taptaṃ piban dvādaśa rātrīradhaḥ śayīta
<21.3.5>     yā dvādaśī syāt tasyā upavyuṣaṃ śabalīhomaṃ hutvā purā vāgbhyaḥ sampravaditoryatra grāmyasya paśornāśṛṇuyāt tadaraṇyaṃ paretya darbhastambamālabhya śabali śabalīti trirāhvayedyadanyacchunaśca gardabhācca prativāśyate sā samṛddhā
<21.3.6>     yadi na prativāśyeta saṃvatsare punarāhvayeta
<21.3.7>     śabali samudro'si viśvavyacā brahma devānāṃ prathamajā ṛtasyānnamasi śukramasi tejo'syamṛtamasi tāṃ tvā vidma śabali dīdyānāṃ tasyāste pṛthivī pādo'ntarikṣaṃ pādo dyauḥ pādaḥ samudraḥ pāda eṣāsi śabali tāṃ tvā vidma sā na iṣamūrjaṃ dhukṣva vasordhārāṃ śabali prajānāṃ śaciṣṭhā vratamanugeṣaṃ svāhā

<21.4.1>     catuṣṭomo'gniṣṭoma ekaviṃśa ukthyaḥ sarvastomo'tirātraḥ
<21.4.2>     prajāpatervā akṣyaśvat tat parāpatat tadaśvo'bhavat tadaśvasyāśvatvaṃ taddevā aśvamedhena pratyadadhureṣa vāva prajāpatiṃ sarvaṃ karoti yo'śvamedhena yajate
<21.4.3>     eṣa vāva saśarīraḥ sambhavatyamuṣmai lokāyayo'śvamedhī
<21.4.4>     saraghā vā aśvasya sakthyāvṛhat taddevāścatuṣṭomena pratyadadhuryaccatuṣṭomo bhavatyaśvasya sarvatvāya
<21.4.5>     yat tisro'nuṣṭubhaścatasro gāyatrīḥ karoti tasmāt tribhistiṣṭhan pratitiṣṭhati sarvān palāyamānaḥ pratidadhāti
<21.4.6>     anto vā aśvaḥ paśūnāmanto'nuṣṭupchandasāmanto viṣṇurdevatānāmantaścatuṣṭomasstomānāmantastrirātro yajñānāṃ yadvaiplavyo'nuṣṭubhaḥ pratipado bhavanti catuṣṭoma stomastrirātro yajño'nta eva tadantaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayati
<21.4.7>     ekaviṃśamaharbhavati yasminnaśva ālabhyata ekaviṃśo vā ito'sāvādityo dvādaśa māsāḥ pañcartavastraya ime lokā asāvāditya ekaviṃśa ādityasyaivainaṃ mātrāṃ gamayati
<21.4.8>     tasya mahānāmnyaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavanti
<21.4.9>     anyadanyadvā etāśchando'nyonya ete paśava ālabhyanta etā vā etaṃ samāpnuvanti yanmahānāmnyaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavantyaśvasya sarvatvāya
<21.4.10>     pārthuraśmaṃ brahmasāma bhavati
<21.4.11>     īśvaro vā eṣo'yato'dhṛtaḥ parāṃ parāvatameto raśminā vā aśvo dhṛto yat pārthuraśmaṃ brahmasāma bhavatyaśvasyaiva yatyai
<21.4.12>     sarvastomo'tirātro bhavati sarvasyāptyai sarvasya jityai sarvamevaitenāpnoti sarvaṃ jayati
<21.4.13>     ekayūpo vaikādaśinī vānyeṣāṃ yajñānāṃ bhavatyekaviṃśinyaśvamedhasya khādiro vā bailvo vā pārṇo vā'nyeṣāṃ yajñānāṃ bhavati naicudāra ekaviṃśatyaratniraśvamedhasya nānyeṣāṃ paśūnāṃ tejanyā avadyantyavadyantyaśvamedhasya dakṣiṇato'nyeṣāṃ paśūnāmavadyantyuttarato'śvamedhasya plakṣaśākhāsvanyeṣāṃ paśūnāmavadyanti vetasaśākhāsvaśvamedhasya yūpe grāmyān paśūnniyuñjantyārokeṣvāraṇyān dhārayantyā grāmyān paśūn labhante prāraṇyān sṛjanti

<21.5.1>     trayastrivṛto'tirātrāḥ sarve ṣoḍaśimantaḥ
<21.5.2>     yo rājya āśaṃseta sa etena yajeta
<21.5.3>     rājā vā eṣa stomānāṃ rājyamevāsmin dadhāti
<21.5.4>     yadākṣārāṇi prathamamaharbhajanta ekākṣarā vai vāgvāco'nativādāya
<21.5.5>     atha yat dvyakṣaraṇidhanamājyadohaṃ bhavatyuttarayorahorabhisantatyā anvahamājyadohāni bhavantyanvahamevainaṃ paśubhiḥ samardhayanti
<21.5.6>     sarve ṣoḍaśimanto bhavantīndriyaṃ vīryaṃ ṣoḍaśīndriyeṇaivainān vīryeṇa samardhayati

<21.6.1>     caturviṃśāḥ pavamānāstrivṛntyājyāni pañcadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni saptadaśo'gniṣṭomaścatuścatvāriṃśāḥ pavamānāḥ pañcadaśānyājyāni saptadaśāni pṛṣṭhānyekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ soktho'ṣṭācatvāriṃśāḥ pavamānā ekaviṃśānyājyāni triṇavāni pṛṣṭhāni trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭoma ekaviṃśānyukthāni saṣoḍaśikāni pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiśchandomapavamānaḥ
<21.6.2>     paśukāmo yajet
<21.6.3>     paśavo vai chandomā yacchandomāḥ pavamānā bhavanti paśūnevāvarūndhe
<21.6.4>     ubhaye stomā yugmantaścāyujaśca tat mithunaṃ tasmānmithunāt prajāyate

<21.7.1>     trivṛt prātassavanaṃ pañcadaśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ saptadaśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ caturviṃśaṃ prātassavanaṃ catuścatvāriṃśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanamaṣṭācatvāriṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ sokthamekaviṃśaṃ prātassavanan triṇavaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ trayastriṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ sokthamekaviṃśaḥ ṣoḍaśī pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhirantarvasuḥ
<21.7.2>     ime lokāstrirātraḥ
<21.7.3>     astīva vā ayaṃ loko'stīvāsau chidramivāntarikṣam
<21.7.4>     astīva trirātrasya prathamamaharastīvottamaṃ chidramiva madhyataḥ
<21.7.5>     paśavo vai chandomā yacchandomā madhyato bhavantyapihityā evācchidratāyai

<21.8.1>     trivṛt prātassavanaṃ pañcadaśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ saptadaśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ ekaviṃśaṃ prātassavanaṃ triṇavaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ trayastriṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ sokthaṃ caturviṃśaṃ prātassavanaṃ catuścatvāriṃśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanamaṣṭācatvāriṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ sokthamekaviṃśaḥ ṣoḍaśī pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiḥ parākaḥ
<21.8.2>     parākeṇa vai devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāyan svargakāmo yajeta parāṅevaitena svargaṃ lokamākramate
<21.8.3>     yadvā etasyākaṃ tadasya parāktat parākasya parākatvam
<21.8.4>     na vai tatra jagmuṣe kiñcanā'kam
<21.8.5>     nāsmā akaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<21.8.6>     prajāyāstvakḷptaḥ parāṅhyevaitena svargaṃ lokamākramate tadyadekaviṃśaḥ ṣoḍaśī bhavati pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhistenāsmin loke pratitiṣṭhati
<21.9.1>     caturviṃśāḥ pavamānāstrivṛntyājyāni  pañcadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni saptadaśo'gniṣṭomaścaturviṃśāḥ pavamānāḥ pañcadaśānyājyāni saptadaśāni pṛṣṭhānyekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ sokthaścaturviṃśāḥ pavamānāḥ saptadaśānyājyānyekaviṃśani pṛṣṭhāni triṇavo'gniṣṭomaḥ sokthaścaturviṃśāḥ pavamānā ekaviṃśānyājyāni triṇavāni pṛṣṭhāni trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭoma ekaviṃśānyukthāni saṣoḍaśikāni pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhi
<21.9.2>     atrirakāmayata catvāro me vīrā ājāyeranniti sa etamapaśyat tasya catvāro vīrā ājāyantāsya catvāro vīrā jāyante ya evaṃ veda
<21.9.3>     ekaṃ stomamutsṛjyaikamabhyārabhate vīrajananaṃ vai stomo vīramevāsmai tat prajanayati
<21.9.4>     caturviṃśāḥ pavamānā bhavanti caturviṃśatyakṣarā gāyatrī prajananaṃ gāyatrī prajātyai
<21.9.5>     ubhaye stomā yugmantaścāyujaśca tanmithunaṃ tasmānmithunāt prajāyate
<21.9.6>     agniṣṭomaḥ prathamamaharūktho dvitīyaṃ ṣoḍaśī tṛtīyamatirātraścaturthaṃ nānāvīryatāyai
<21.9.7>     nānāvīryāṇyahāni karoti
<21.9.8>     gāyatraṃ vai prathamamahastraiṣṭubhaṃ dvitīyaṃ jāgataṃ tṛtīyamānuṣṭubhaṃ caturtham
<21.9.9>     tadāhuryat tṛtīye'hani ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇīyustṛtīyenāhnānuṣṭubhamāpnuyuracchandaścaturthamahaḥ syādānuṣṭubho vai ṣoḍaśīti
<21.9.10>     caturthe'hani ṣoḍaśī grahītavyaḥ sva āyatane ṣoḍaśī gṛhyate
<21.9.11>     chandobhirahāni nānāvīryāṇi karoti
<21.9.12>     naudhasaṃ prathamasyāhno brahmasāma śyaitaṃ dvitīyasya śrāyantīyaṃ tṛtīyasya traiśokaṃ caturthasya
<21.9.13>     tadāhurapabhraṃśa iva vā eṣa yat jyāyasaḥ chandaso'dhi kanīyaśchanda upaitīti
<21.9.14>     tadyadeṣā caturthe'hanyatijagatī kriyate'napabhraṃśāya
<21.9.15>     kāleyaṃ prathamasyāho'cchāvākasāma mādhucchandasaṃ dvitīyasya rauravaṃ tṛtīyasya samantaṃ caturthasya nānāvīryatāyai nānāvīryāṇyahāni karoti
<21.9.16>   "bhitvā vṛṣabhā suta"iti tṛtīyasyāhno rathantaraṃ''kastamindra tvā vasava"iti vāmadevyaṃ''yajñā yathā apūrva"iti caturthasyāhno bṛhat''edu madhormadintaram"iti vāmadevyaṃ chandasāṃ nānāvīryatāyai nānāvīryāṇyahāni kāroti

<21.10.1>     trivṛt pañcadaśo'gniṣṭomaḥ prathamamahaḥ
<21.10.2>     athottarasyāhnoścaturviṃśaṃ vahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśāni trīṇyājyāni saptadaśamacchāvākasyaikaviṃśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamānaḥ saptadaśe dve pṛṣṭhe ekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃṃ soktham
<21.10.3>     tṛtīyasyāhna ekaviṃśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ trīṇi cājyāni pañcadaśamacchāvākasya caturviṃśo mādhyandinaḥ pavamāna ekaviṃśāni pṛṣṭhāni triṇavaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ dve cokthe ekaviṃśamacchāvākasya
<21.10.4>     caturthasyāhnaścaturviśāḥ pavamānāḥ pañcadaśaṃ  hoturājyaṃ saptadaśāni trīṇyekaviṃśāni pṛṣṭhāni trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭoma ekaviṃśānyukthāni saṣoḍaśikāni pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiḥ
<21.10.5>     jamadagniḥ puṣṭikāma etamāharat sa imān poṣānapuṣyat
<21.10.6>     yadidamāhurnavā ūrvau palitau sañjānāte iti
<21.10.7>     tat sarvānevaitena poṣān puṣyati
<21.10.8>     jagatī chandobhiḥ sampadyate
<21.10.9>     jagatī vai chandasāṃ paramaṃ poṣaṃ puṣṭā paramamevaite poṣaṃ puṣyati
<21.10.10>     puroḍāśinya upasado bhavanti paśavo vai puroḍāśāḥ paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhanti
<21.10.11>     agne verhotra veradhvaramāpitaraṃ vaiśvānaramavase'karindrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.12>     devāvaśvinau madhukaśayādyemaṃ yajñaṃ yajamānāya mimikṣatamindrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.13>     deva viṣṇa urvadyāsmin yajñe yajamānāyādhi vikramasvendrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.14>     deva soma retodhā adyāsmin yajñe yajamānāyaidhīndrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.15>     deva savitaḥ susāvitramadyāsmin yajñe yajamānāsuvasvendrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.16>     deva dhātaḥ sudhātādyāsmin yajñe yajamānāyaidhīndrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.17>     devā grāvāṇo madhumatīmadyāsmin yajñe yajamānāya vācaṃ vadatendrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.18>     devyanumate'nvadyemaṃ yajñaṃ yajamānāya manyasvendrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.19>     devyadite svādityāmadyāsmin yajñe yajamānāyāsuvasvendrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.20>     devya āpo nannamyadhvamadyāsmin yajñe yajamānāyendrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.21>     sadassadaḥ prajāvānṛbhurjuṣāṇa indrāya devebhyo juhutā havissvāhā
<21.10.22>     deva tvaṣṭaḥ suretodhā adyāsmin yajñe yajamānāyaidhīndrāya devebhyo juhutā haviḥ svāhā
<21.10.23>     āgneya ekakapāla āśvino dvikapālo vaiṣṇavastrikapālaḥ saumyaścatuṣkapālaḥ sāvitraḥ pañcakapālo dhātraḥ ṣaṭkapālo mārūtaḥ saptakapālo bārhaspatyo'ṣṭākapālo maitro navakapālo vārūṇo daśakapāla aindra ekādaśakapālo vaiśvadevo dvādaśakapālaḥ

<21.11.1>     trivṛta prātassavanaṃ pañcadaśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ saptadaśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ pañcadaśaṃ prātassavanaṃ saptadaśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanamekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ sokthaṃ saptadaśaṃ prātassavanamekaviṃśaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ triṇavaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ sokthamekaviṃśaṃ prātassavanaṃ triṇavaṃ mādhyandinaṃ savanaṃ trayastriṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ pratyavarohīṇyukthāni triṇavaṃ prathamaṃ dve ekaviṃśe saṣoḍaśike pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiḥ
<21.11.2>     vasiṣṭhaḥ putrahato hīna ivāmanyata sa etamapaśyat so'graṃ paryaidyo hīna iva manyeta sa etena yajeta
<21.11.3>     yat stomāt stomamabhisaṃkrāmatyagrādevāgraṃ rohati vasiṣṭhasya janitre bhavataḥ prajātyai
<21.11.4>     pratyavarohīṇyuttamasyāhna ukthāni bhavanti pratiṣṭhityai

<21.12.1>     trivṛdagniṣṭomaḥ pañcadaśa ukthaḥ saptadaśa uktha ekaviṃśo'tirātro viśvāmitrasya sañjayaḥ
<21.12.2>     jahnuvṛcīvanto rāṣṭra āhiṃsanta sa viśvāmitro jāhnavo rājaitamapaśyat sa rāṣṭramabhavadarāṣṭramitare
<21.12.3>     bhrātṛvyavān yajeta
<21.12.4>     bhavatyātmanā parāsya bhrātṛvyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<21.12.5>     jyotirvā eṣa vihṛtaḥ
<21.12.6>     jyotiḥ prajānāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<21.12.7>     catuṣṭomo vā eṣa catuṣpādāḥ paśavaḥ paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhati
<21.12.8>     ekaviṃśaṃ stomā nātiyanti pratiṣṭhā vā ekaviṃśo'ntata eva yajñasya pratitiṣṭhati

<21.13.1>     dve trivṛtī savane pañcadaśamekaṃ dve pañcadaśe savane saptadaśamekaṃ dve saptadaśe savane ekaviṃśamekaṃ dve ekaviṃśe savane triṇavamekaṃ dve triṇave savane trayastriṃśamekaṃ pratyavarohīṇyukthāni triṇavaṃ prathamaṃ dve ekaviṃśe saṣoḍaśike pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt sandhiḥ
<21.13.2>     devāśca vā asurāścāspardhanta te na vyajayanta te'bruvan vāco mithunena vijayāmahai yatare no vāco mithunaṃ na prativindāṃste parā bhavāniti te devā eka ityabruvanneketyasurā vāco mithunaṃ pratyavindan dvāviti devā abruvan dve ityasurā vāco mithunaṃ pratyavindanaṃstraya iti devā abruvaṃstisra ityasurā vāco mithunaṃ pratyavindaṃścatvāra iti devā abruvaṃścatastra ityasurā vāco mithunaṃ pratyavindan pañceti devā abruvannāsurā avindaṃstato devā abhavan parāsurāḥ
<21.13.3>     bhavatyātmanā parāsya bhrātṛvyo bhavati ya evaṃ veda
<21.13.4>     saṃvatsaraṃ vā eṣāṃ tadvīryamannādyamavṛñjata
<21.13.5>     saṃvatsaraṃ vīryamannādyaṃ bhrātṛvyasya vṛṅkte ya evaṃ veda
<21.13.6>     pañcarātro vā eṣa pāṅktaḥ purūṣaḥ pāṅktāḥ paśavastena purūṣaṃ ca paśūṃścāpnoti
<21.13.7>     āstharirvā eṣa santato yajño dvau dvau hi stomāvaharvahato yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate tametenābhyaśnute
<21.13.8>     yatra yatra hyasthūriṇā kāmayate tat tadabhyaśnute
<21.13.9>     abhyāsaṅgyaḥ pañcāho bhavati santatyai
<21.13.10>     pratyavarohīṇyuttamasyāhna ukthāni bhavanti pratiṣṭhityai

<21.14.1>     trivṛdagniṣṭomaḥ pañcadaśa ukthaḥ saptadaśa ukthaḥ pañcadaśa ukthaḥ saptadaśo'tirātraḥ pañcaśāradīyo marutāṃ stomaḥ
<21.14.2>     yaḥ kāmayeta bahu syāmiti sa etena yajeta
<21.14.3>     marūto vai devānāṃ bhūyiṣṭhā bahureva bhavati
<21.14.4>     saptadaśaṃ stomā nātiyanti prajāpatirvai saptadaśaḥ prajāpatimevāpnoti
<21.14.5>     agastyo vai marūdbhya ukṣṇaḥ praukṣat tānindrāyābadhnāt te vajramādāyābhyapatan sa etat kayāśubhīyamapaśyat tenāśamayat
<21.14.6>     yat  kayāśubhīyaṃ śasyate śāntyā eva
<21.14.7>     saptadaśa pṛśnīnukṣṇaḥ pañcavarṣān saptadaśa pṛśnīstrivatsā apravītāstān paryagnikṛtān prokṣitānetarā labhante pretarān sṛjanti
<21.14.8>     tataḥ saṃvatsare navanītapṛśnīrarūṇā ānayanti tāṃścaivokṣṇastān paryagnikṛtān prokṣitānetarā labhante pretarān sṛjanti tataḥ saṃvatsare rājīvā ānayanti tāṃścaivokṣṇastān paryagnikṛtān prokṣitānetarā labhante pretarān sṛjanti tataḥ saṃvatsare piśaṅgīrānayanti tāṃścaivokṣṇastān paryagnikṛtān prokṣitānetarā labhante pretarān sṛjanti tataḥ saṃvatsare sāraṅgīrānayanti tāṃścaivokṣṇastān paryagnikṛtān pokṣitānetarā labhante pretarān sṛjanti
<21.14.9>     tataḥ saṃvatsare somā bhavanti
<21.14.10>     trīṃstrīnanvahamālabhante pañcottame'hani
<21.14.11>     ajo'gnīṣomīyaḥ
<21.14.12>     aindrā mārūto ukṣāṇo mārūtyo vatsataryaḥ
<21.14.13>     yadi rūdro'bhimanyotāgnaye rūdravate puroḍāśamaṣṭākapālaṃ nirūpyāthānyamālabheta
<21.14.14>     yadi saṃśīryaita bhaumamekakapālaṃ nirūpyāthānyamālabheta
<21.14.15>     yadyavasīdernairṛtaṃ caruṃ nirūpyāthānyamālabheta yadyapsu mriyetāponaptrīyaṃ caruṃ nirūpyāthānyamālabheta yadyandhasyāt sauryaṃ caruṃ nirūpyāthānyamālabheta
<21.14.16>     yadi ślavaṇo vā kuṭo vā syādbārhaspatyaṃ caruṃ nipyāthānyamālabheta
<21.14.17>     yadi palāyeta vāyavyaṃ caruṃ nirūpyāthānyamālabheta
<21.14.18>     yadi prāsahājayeyurindrāya prasahvana ekādaśakapālaṃ nirūpyāthānyamālabheta
<21.14.19>     yadyanyena mṛtyunā mriyeta prājāpatyaṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirūpyāthānyamālabheta
<21.14.20>     etena vā ekayāvā gāndamo vetasvatīṣṭvā sarvāmṛddhimārdhnot
<21.14.21>     sarvāmṛddhimṛdhnoti ya evaṃ veda

<21.15.1>     jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo gaurukthyo mahāvrataṃ gaurukthya āyuratirātraḥ
<21.15.2>     saṃvatsaro vai vrataṃ tasya vasanta ṛturmukhaṃ grīṣmaśca varṣāśca pakṣau śaranmadhyaṃ hemantaḥ puccham
<21.15.3>     tasmāccharadamoṣadhayo'bhisampacyante śaraddhi madhyaṃ saṃvatsarasya
<21.15.4>     akṛtaṃ vā ete kurvanti ye purā saṃvatsarāt vratamupayanti
<21.15.5>     etadvai vratamāptaṃ yat pañcarātre pañca hyṛtavaḥ
<21.15.6>     āptena vratena stute sarvamāyureti ya evaṃ veda

<22.1.1>     pṛṣṭhayaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ
<22.1.2>     ṛtavo na pratyatiṣṭhaṃsta etena pratyatiṣṭhan pratiṣṭhākāmo yajeta pratyeva tiṣṭhati
<22.1.3>     ṣaḍvā ṛtava ṛtuṣvevaitena pratitiṣṭhati pṛṣṭhayaḥ ṣaḍaho bhavati pratyakṣamṛdhyai
<22.1.4>     pratyakṣaṃ hyetenartava ārdhnuvannṛdhyā eva

<22.2.1>     trivṛdagniṣṭomaḥ pañcadaśa ukthyaḥ saptadaśa ukthyo jyotirgaurāyuratirātraḥ
<22.2.2>     yaḥ kāmayeta sarvamāyuriyāmiti sa etena yajeta
<22.2.3>     yat tryahaḥ purastādbhavati trayaḥ prāṇāpānavyānāsta eva tat saṃdhīyante
<22.2.4>     atha yat jyotirgaurāyuratirātro bhavati pratiṣṭhityai

<22.3.1>     abhyāsaṅgyaḥ pañcāho viśvajidatirātraḥ
<22.3.2>     anyasmai vai kāmāya sattramanyasmai yajño na tatsatreṇāpnoti yasmaikaṃ yajño na tadyajñenāpnoti yasmaikaṃ sattram
<22.3.3>     sattramiva vā etadyadanulomaṃ pṛṣṭhāni yadekadhā pṛṣṭhāni bhavantyekadhaivāsmiṃstadojo vīryaṃ dadhāti
<22.3.4>     annaṃ pṛṣṭhānyannādyamevāsmin dadhāti
<22.3.5>     paśavaḥ pṛṣṭhāni paśuṣveva pratitiṣṭhati
<22.3.6>     abhyāsaṅgyaḥ pañcāho bhavati santatyai
<22.3.7>     viśvajidatirātro bhavati viśvasyābhijityai

<22.4.1>     pṛṣṭhayaḥ ṣaḍaho mahāvratamatirātraḥ
<22.4.2>     sapta ṛṣaya etenārdhnuvaṃstenarddhistasmādetena yajanta ṛddhyā eva
<22.4.3>     sapta śirasi prāṇāḥ prāṇā indriyāṇīndriyāṇyevaitenāpnoti
<22.4.4>     sapta grāmyāḥ paśavastānetenāpnoti
<22.4.5>     vrataṃ saptamasyāhnaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavati taddhyanāptamannaṃ vai vratamannādyamevaitenāpnoti
<22.4.6>     pṛṣṭhayaḥ ṣaḍaho bhavati pratyakṣamṛdhyai
<22.4.7>     pratyakṣaṃ hyetena sapta ṛṣaya ārdhnuvannṛdhyā eva

<22.5.1>     pṛṣṭhayaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ saptadaśaṃ mahāvratamatirātraḥ
<22.5.2>     tasya trivṛcchiraḥ pañcadaśau pakṣau saptadaśa ātmaikaviṃśaṃ puccham
<22.5.3>     etena vai prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata
<22.5.4>     pra prajayā pra paśubhirjāyate ya evaṃ veda
<22.5.5>     saptadaśo vai prajāpatiryat saptadaśaṃ vrataṃ bhavati prajāpatimevāpnoti
<22.5.6>     yat trivṛcchiro bhavati nava prāṇāḥ prāṇeṣveva prati tiṣṭhati
<22.5.7>     yat pañcadaśau pakṣau savīvadhatvāya
<22.5.8>     saptadaśa ātmā bhavati prajāpatirvai saptadaśaḥ prajā patimevāpnoti
<22.5.9>     ekaviṃśaṃ puccha bhavati pratiṣṭhityai

<22.6.1>     pṛṣṭhayaḥ ṣaḍahaśchandomapavamānaṃ mahāvratamatirātraḥ
<22.6.2>     paśukāmo yajeta
<22.6.3>     paśavo vai chandāmāḥ
<22.6.4>     yacchadomāḥ pavamānā mahāvratasya bhavanti paśūnevāvarūndhe
<22.6.5>     ubhaye stomā yugmantaścāyujaśca tanmithunaṃ tasmānmithunāt prajāyate

<22.7.1>     abhyāsaṅgyaḥ pañcāho'tha trayastriṃśāmahastasya catustriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ saptadaśaṃ mahāvratamatirātrastasya caturviṃśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ trivṛcchiraḥ pañcadaśau pakṣau saptadaśa ātmaikaviṃśaṃ puccham
<22.7.2>     etena vai jamadagniḥ sarvān poṣānapuṣyat sarvānevaitena poṣān puṣyati
<22.7.3>     yadabhyāsaṅgyaḥ pañcāhaḥ purastādbhavati santatyā eva
<22.7.4>     athaitat trayastriṃśamahastrayastriṃśaddevatā devatā evāpnoti
<22.7.5>     tasya catustriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ prajāpatiścatustriṃśo devatānāṃ prajāpatimevāpnoti
<22.7.6>     caturviṃśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ bhavati mahāvratasya caturviṃśatyakṣarā gāyatrī prajananaṃ gāyatrī prajātyai
<22.7.7>     ubhaye stomā yugmantaścāyujaśca tanmithunaṃ tasmānmithunāt prajāyate
